# 751: Bond Evolution!

Gengar's body immediately burst with brilliant rainbow light, intertwined with bright white evolutionary light.

Its appearance rapidly changed within the radiance.

When the light quickly faded, Gengar had transformed into Mega Gengar!

"This..." Cynthia looked at Johan and Mega Gengar, shock intertwining with flowing light in her eyes.

She seemed to understand what had happened between Johan and Gengar.

Ultimately, the essence of Mega Evolution was still the materialization of the bond power between trainer and Pokémon.

It was just that bonds were invisible and intangible, requiring Key Stones and Mega Stones to materialize this invisible, intangible thing, thus transforming it into physical changes and strength improvements.

But since it existed, then under certain special circumstances, perhaps this power could transform without relying on Key Stones and Mega Stones?

Cynthia somewhat understood what Johan had meant earlier by "special methods."

Perhaps this was it!

"Ka-kaah!!"

Mega Gengar, riding on Spectrier's back, excitedly shouted. It knew it could do it!

Actually, whether it was Gengar or Johan, when using Key Stones and Mega Stones before, they'd vaguely felt that even without Key Stones and Mega Stones, they seemed able to trigger some kind of power.

Gengar could grasp it itself, and Johan, possessing aura power, was much more sensitive to emotions compared to ordinary people.

Moreover, Johan was originally a Pokémon psychologist, studying the "arts" of "stand up," "dodge quickly," and "hang in there."

His perception of bond power was also clearer.

This was it.

When Johan and Gengar, at a certain moment and place, had their thoughts and intentions completely align on the same frequency, this power appeared!

This change in Gengar shouldn't actually be called Mega Evolution, but rather—

Bond Evolution!

And compared to Mega Evolution using Key Stones and Mega Stones, bond evolution—whether for Gengar or Johan—felt... much clearer than using Key Stones and Mega Stones, with a much clearer spiritual connection between them.

It was as if... Johan could directly control and influence Gengar's body.

But similarly, if Gengar suffered any attacks or injuries, Johan could clearly feel them, even experience them physically.

If Gengar suffered heavy damage, Johan would probably collapse directly.

And as a Pokémon, Gengar's recovery speed was much faster than Johan's as a human.

In this situation where they were being "hunted" by Eternamax Eternatus, Johan and Gengar performing bond evolution was almost equivalent to completely binding Johan's safety with Gengar's.

Especially under Eternatus's Dynamax Cannon.

If Gengar took this blast head-on, Johan... might faint on the spot.

But at a time like this, he wouldn't consider so much.

In fact, the first thought that popped into Johan's mind after bond evolving with Gengar was—

If Gengar didn't need to carry a Mega Stone in the future, wouldn't that free up an item slot?

Maybe he could give opponents a little surprise in the "Pokémon World Championships."

"Neigh—"

Spectrier reared up on its hind legs, neighing loudly.

Run!

The overwhelming energy completely sealed off space—they couldn't enter shadows or open doors to the "Distortion World."

Running was definitely impossible.

Eternatus was determined to eliminate Gengar, this "natural enemy."

So they had to fight back!

Speed was Gengar's advantage, but it was hard to utilize now. Defense was Gengar's absolute weakness—tanking was definitely impossible.

Only counterattack!

The best defense was offense!

Johan clenched his fist, and Mega Gengar also grinned fiercely as it pulled the reins, turning to face Eternatus's massive claw-like head swinging toward them, facing the terrifying dark red glow flashing above it.

"Ninetales, Tinkaton, Victini!" Johan called out softly.

"Oowoo!!"

Ninetales erected ice walls in front of Gengar, lowering a transparent aurora veil.

After Earth Power, Ninetales' strength had also increased dramatically, plus with the "Fairy Plate's" power—except for being unable to use exclusive Z-moves, Ninetales' strength had already reached its peak.

"Absolute defense" maxed out!

"Cha!"

Tinkaton suddenly gripped its hammer tightly.

The hammer again burst with pink light comparable to sword and shield, its size seemingly changing in a trance.

"Vii~"

Victini's expression was serious. It knew this was a very crucial moment, so it unreservedly bestowed the "Victory Star" of the "Victory Pokémon" upon Gengar.

"Gengar!"

At this moment, Johan was of one mind with Gengar—no words were needed. He raised his hand and clenched his fist.

"Ka-kaah!"

Mega Gengar's body immediately filled with extremely rich black mist—some belonging to it, some to Spectrier.

Hum—

In an instant, the spreading black mist seemed to become a pitch-black night sky, with light points sporadically appearing, then shining brilliantly like a vast starry sky.

The next moment, those star-like light points gathered on Gengar's fist.

Bond evolution plus man-horse fusion plus Victini's "Helping Hand"—the instant explosive power Mega Gengar displayed at this moment was even stronger than what ordinary legendary Pokémon could unleash!

Come on!

Head-to-head!

Whoever backs down is a puppy!

Gengar's eyes sparkled with starlight, mind and will unified, delivering the strongest attack of its life so far!

Stars filled the sky, gathered in one body.

Star Crash!

In the instant when the ice wall and aurora veil shattered, brilliant radiance quietly struck out.

Black light and dark red waves collided head-on, like two massive waves crashing violently together.

Rumble!!!

Calyrex, Zacian, and Zamazenta couldn't help but turn their attention here, watching the stellar brilliance Gengar had unleashed.

[This Star Crash move... Gengar is no weaker than I was back then...] Calyrex said with some satisfaction.

Zacian and Zamazenta looked at each other and nodded in agreement.

However, unlike Calyrex whose attention was completely on Gengar, besides watching Gengar, they also noticed Tinkaton carrying its iron hammer and swaying its twin tails.

Although it was still somewhat "naive," it had the potential to become a true hero!

Crack! Crack!

Eternamax Eternatus's slender body immediately made crisp cracking sounds under this impact, countless dark red Dynamax energies escaping from its body and scattering throughout the entire Galar region.

A fierce crack also appeared in Eternamax Eternatus's energy core.

How was this possible?!

This was the only thought in Eternatus's mind.

That Gengar looked annoying, but it was clearly just annoying—how could it burst with such powerful force?

Not only did its Dynamax Cannon barely hurt it, but this opposing force...

If Johan knew Eternatus's thoughts, he would definitely scoff.

Ridiculous—your Dynamax Cannon, a move for dealing with Dynamax Pokémon, used against bond evolution?

Using a Ming Dynasty sword to execute a Qing Dynasty official?

Such arrogance?

Moreover, Eternatus only had eyes for Gengar—had it forgotten about Ninetales' Fairy Aura?

As a Dragon-type move, how much power could the Dynamax Cannon retain after being weakened by Fairy Aura, ice walls, and aurora veil?

So—we're targeting you specifically!

"Now!!" Johan shouted loudly.

Calyrex raised its hand in the air, and ice spears materialized again.

Dragonite flapped its wings, catching the leaping Tinkaton and charging straight toward the sky, instantly covered in a dragon aura.

Garchomp's feet shattered the ground beneath, similarly clad in a fierce dragon aura, charging toward Eternatus's energy core like an unstoppable force.

Boom!!!

Since Eternatus was determined to deal with Gengar, it had to be prepared for the consequences of failing to take down Gengar and being counterattacked.

Its massive energy core again let out a cracking "wail."

"Cha!"

Tinkaton stepped on Dragonite's head, leaping high into the air and swinging its massive hammer with both hands toward Eternatus's core.

Behold! Take my hammer!

Zacian and Zamazenta's eyes met, looking at Tinkaton about to strike with its hammer. From it, they saw the shadow of a young hero, saw... their former selves.

Then—

"Awooo—"

An azure sword and a crimson shield flew into the sky, circling around Tinkaton's blurred hammer that had enlarged several times.

Sword, hammer, shield!

Behemoth Hammer!

Rumble—

The brilliant energy core completely shattered.

Countless streams of Dynamax energy shot out.

Everyone in Wyndon looked up in shock at the scene in the sky.

"Eternatus—!!"

Eternatus let out an earth-shaking roar, but still couldn't prevent the energy core's collapse, couldn't prevent the gradual dimming of the black-red vortex behind it, couldn't prevent its body's collapse.

Bang!!

Its struggling five-clawed heads finally crashed heavily into Wyndon Stadium.

Was it finally over?

Cynthia secretly breathed a sigh of relief, looking toward Johan.

Johan shook his head with disgust.

I'm not capturing it!

Too ugly!

Eternatus: "..."

Thud.

Tinkaton shouldered its hammer and walked with Gengar to the motionless Eternatus.

"Cha?"

Still not unconscious?

"Ka-kaah?"

Should we add another hammer blow?

"Cha!" Tinkaton's eyes lit up.

Good point!

[Quickly seal it, Johan.] Calyrex, riding Glastrier, came to Johan's side.

"Seal... do you think I know such advanced stuff? Shouldn't you do it?"

One person and one Pokémon stared at each other.

[This king... cough cough, this king only excels at horse training and knows a bit about fighting. I don't know sealing techniques.]

[Don't humans have something... called Poké Balls?] Zacian asked.

"Emmm..."

Johan was considering how to explain when he suddenly noticed Charizard flying from the horizon, his eyes lighting up.

"Here he comes. The guy who'll capture it is here."

"Roar—"

Charizard quickly flew over, and Leon in his cape landed.

"Teacher Johan! Miss Cynthia!"

Leon, who had rushed here urgently, hadn't seen Eternatus fall.

However, on the way here, due to Eternatus's scattered Dynamax energy, he'd seen many Pokémon continuously losing control due to Dynamax energy.

This made Leon's heart extremely heavy.

Fortunately, because Eternamax Eternatus's vortex was too distinctive, Leon didn't get lost this time.

But now it seemed... everything was already over?

"That's the culprit—Eternatus, revived by your Chairman Rose..."

After Johan briefly explained, he said: "Alright, Leon, while it's still unconscious, as Galar's Champion, you should capture it."

"Me?"

Leon was stunned.

Good grief, I came all this way just to capture this thing?

"As Champion of the Galar region, I think this responsibility is yours to bear," Johan said seriously.

Just kidding.

Eternatus wasn't something you could successfully capture with just any Poké Ball. Even a high-quality Ultra Ball could at most temporarily restrain it when Eternatus was weakened.

Once Eternatus fully recovered, if it wasn't willing, a mere Ultra Ball couldn't possibly contain it.

Unless they used a Master Ball.

That might keep Eternatus in a captured state.

This was only "might"—not a 100% guarantee.

Johan did have a Master Ball, but using it to capture Eternatus...

Wasn't Groudon more appealing, or Giratina more fun, or wasn't Mewtwo's "resentment" enough?

So this was destined to be a guy that would cause trouble again. Johan chose to respectfully decline.

Moreover, Eternatus was related to Galar's Dynamax. If he took Eternatus away, the Galar region would probably gradually lose its Dynamax abilities.

For the rapidly developing Galar region now, this would definitely be an enormous blow.

Rose's vision was somewhat unrealistic, but the Galar region couldn't do without Eternatus, just like the West couldn't do without Jerusalem.

This trouble—let Leon have the headache.

At least, whether in games or anime, Leon had handled the relationship between Eternatus and the Galar region well.

Most importantly—Eternatus and his Pokémon's "art style" were too different.

In the "Johan family," which Pokémon wasn't both cute and powerful?

Elegant Ninetales, craftsman Tinkaton, Gengar, Tyranitar, and... elegant Ninetales!

Seeing Johan invoke the "Champion" title, Leon showed helplessness.

Honestly, after hearing Johan's explanation, Leon also realized the Galar region couldn't lose Eternatus.

But Chairman Rose's method of capturing Eternatus and treating it as a "tool" or something like a power plant—he didn't approve or agree with that.

But...

"It wasn't me who defeated it. I didn't even fight it. It definitely won't respect me..." Leon said.

Johan smiled and patted Leon's shoulder: "Don't worry about that. Its temper toward you will definitely be much better than when facing me."

Eternatus had been "tormented" by Gengar to the point of losing its wits. Facing the unfamiliar Leon, it would definitely be much more "well-behaved" than facing Johan.

"If..." Leon paused, "if it goes berserk again later..."

"I could beat it once, I can beat it a second time," Johan said confidently.

Bond evolution that ignored Dynamax energy suppression, the "Fairy Plate" unaffected by Dynamax energy, and Tinkaton's gradually "awakening" Behemoth Hammer...

It wasn't an exaggeration to say—we're specifically targeting Eternatus!

And Johan also sensed something.

Compared to Necrozma from the Alola region, Eternatus—aside from being bigger in size and stature—had much less oppressive presence.

Maybe it really had been "tormented" by Gengar.

But more likely, it was because Zacian and Zamazenta, who countered Eternatus even more severely, existed, not to mention the recovered Calyrex.

Eternatus was also the only one in Johan's memory... that was countered to death by two legendary Pokémon from the same region.

Like Hoenn's weather duo and Rayquaza, Unova's truth and ideals dragons with Kyurem, Alola's Solgaleo, Lunala with Necrozma—which one wasn't a one-versus-two existence?

Only Galar's Eternatus, Zacian, and Zamazenta were the reverse.

"With your word, I'm relieved," Leon breathed out lightly.

Although Eternatus was unconscious, that oppressive presence was still strong...

Against such an Eternatus, Leon admitted he was still lacking.

As he spoke, Leon rotated his Dynamax Band. With Eternatus unconscious, Dynamax power could finally be used again.

The Ultra Ball in his hand rapidly expanded in the rich Dynamax particles.

Leon hefted the enlarged Ultra Ball and threw it forcefully.

The massive Ultra Ball opened, drawing the unconscious Eternatus inside, then crashed to the ground with a "bang" and began shaking.

Everyone's gazes fell nervously on the Ultra Ball.

Until the Ultra Ball shook three times and "clicked" shut, then quickly shrank back to its original size.

Leon immediately breathed a long sigh of relief.

Johan also secretly relaxed—at least he'd managed to pass off this "hot potato."

"Well, Leon, I still suggest you go find your teacher Master Mustard. Two people have more strength..."

"Good point!" Leon nodded.

Next, he'd probably be very busy.

Chairman Rose's actions meant he could no longer continue as Galar League Chairman. He would report this matter truthfully to the main League.

And having just become Champion, he might shoulder even greater responsibilities because of this.

However, Johan suggested he could talk with Peony.

Additionally, the "Pokémon World Championships" still had to proceed as scheduled, and he definitely had to participate.

A pile of things suddenly came flooding in.

Even Leon felt overwhelmed.

"Teacher Johan, Miss Cynthia, and everyone... thank you again for your help!" Leon bowed ninety degrees, solemnly expressing gratitude on behalf of the Galar region.

"Since there are still many things to handle, I'll leave first. We'll keep in touch by phone!"

With that, Leon swished his cape and mounted Charizard.

"Charizard, let's go to the Isle of Armor first to find Master!"

Master Mustard might have ways to make Eternatus more docile—after all, he had "captured" legendary Pokémon like Urshifu and granted them even stronger power.

Master Mustard: Don't come over here!!

"Roar—"

Charizard spread its wings, bathed in the pure white moonlight that fell after the clouds dispersed.

"Leon, the Isle of Armor is that way!" Johan reminded, seeing Leon's flight direction.

"Oh, oh..."

Watching their departing figures, Johan estimated Leon would probably reach the Isle of Armor... next week.

Plus dealing with Eternatus and settling a pile of Galar League messes... he definitely wouldn't have time to climb ranks!

"Master Ball Tier bottom—that's definitely him!" Johan muttered happily to himself.

"Bottom?"

Cynthia glanced at him: "Don't you know Leon has participated in the most matches? Even if he doesn't fight anymore later, he's stable for top three in 'Master Ball Tier'?"

"Huh?"

Johan was stunned.

Leon fought many matches—didn't I fight many too?

Why don't League Conferences count for points!

"What about you?" Johan asked quietly.

Cynthia smiled gracefully: "I haven't fought as much as Leon."

"So..."

"So I'm currently ranked third."

Johan: "..."

His suspended heart finally died.

"First is Leon, third is you, so second... wouldn't be Lance, would it?"

"That's right."

Johan looked up to the sky.

When exactly did you all fight these matches?

You couldn't have been secretly climbing ranks while I was having "friendly" exchanges with legendary Pokémon, could you?

"Johan." Cynthia tugged at his clothes, nodding toward the three legendary Pokémon still present.

"Oh."

Johan snapped back to attention.

Right, Zacian, Zamazenta, and Calyrex were still here.

But before talking with them, Johan had one more thing to handle first.

"Where's Gengar?"

Looking around, he saw Gengar, having reverted from its Mega state, leading Spectrier and tiptoeing hunched over, sneaking toward nearby shadows.

Hearing Johan's call, it suddenly froze.

Turning around, scratching its head, it showed an embarrassed smile.

"Ka-kaah~ (=ω=)"

If I said I was going to the bathroom, would you believe me?

"Stop fooling around." Johan didn't want to argue with this purple fatty. When their minds were connected earlier, he understood Gengar's thoughts perfectly.

"Take out that meteorite."

The meteorite!

Hearing this, Zacian, Zamazenta, and Calyrex all reacted.

Right, that meteorite that nearly let Eternatus absorb it and greatly increase its power!

"Ka-kaah?"

Meteorite? What meteorite? I don't know anything~

Gengar was still struggling, but Johan just crossed his arms, smiling as he watched it.

"Ka-kaah..."

Seeing it couldn't fool its way out, Gengar's ears immediately drooped. It reached out and swiped through the air beside it, and a dark red stone about the size of two washbasins fell out.

Thud—

Probably because it no longer had Eternatus's power to activate it, this meteorite's glow had obviously dimmed, showing a blackish dark color with faint red tints.

With this meteorite's appearance, the surrounding Dynamax particles that had dispersed became rich again.

More importantly...

"This is... 'cosmic energy'?" Johan said somewhat uncertainly.

"'Cosmic energy'?" Cynthia looked puzzled.

"Mm... I don't know how to explain this energy either. If I had to say, I've actually encountered it on another Pokémon before."

"Another Pokémon?"

"Jirachi."

Powerstones?

# 752: Coach, I Want to Craft

"Alright, put it away for now."

After confirming the meteorite's condition, Johan nodded with satisfaction.

This meteorite indeed contained "cosmic power." This force might be able to help Jirachi end its thousand-year slumber.

Johan really missed that cute little thing, Jirachi.

Back when he went to space with Rayquaza, he had actually searched for "cosmic power"—meteorites containing similar "cosmic power."

However, the main purpose then was dealing with the giant meteorite, so after a quick search without finding any, he gave up.

Rayquaza had said meteorites with this kind of "cosmic power" were very rare, which was why Jirachi needed to wait a thousand years until the millennium comet appeared before awakening.

He hadn't expected that the meteorite summoned by Eternatus this time would contain considerable "cosmic power."

And it wasn't just "cosmic power"—it also contained even richer Dynamax particles.

It was fortunate that Eternatus hadn't managed to grab the meteorite and that Gengar had hidden it ahead of time. Otherwise, with such powerful Dynamax particles plus "cosmic power"...

Eternatus's own Eternamax state would have become even stronger, and its "cosmic power" would probably have maxed out directly.

With its defenses becoming even more exaggerated, taking it down would definitely have been more troublesome—they might have needed to call for more help.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar breathed a sigh of relief and happily put the meteorite away again.

It had thought Johan was going to "confiscate" the meteorite, but he'd just taken a look.

The Dynamax particles on the meteorite provided tremendous benefits to Eternatus, but for Gengar, they were also a rare "delicacy."

Watching Gengar store the meteorite again, Johan lightly rubbed his chin.

Gengar hadn't undergone Gigantamax yet. If it absorbed the Dynamax particles from this meteorite, he wondered what it would look like when it Gigantamaxed in the future...

He felt his Gengar was becoming increasingly unreasonable.

But his Gengar was already unreasonable to begin with. As they say, having many skills doesn't hurt—there wasn't much difference between being a little unreasonable and being extremely unreasonable, right?

"I forgot."

Johan slapped his forehead.

"What?" Cynthia asked curiously.

"I should have told Leon to find Professor Magnolia first," Johan said.

He remembered that in the anime, after Leon captured Eternatus, he entrusted it to Professor Magnolia.

After all, in the field of Dynamax and Galar particles, Professor Magnolia was the authority.

And later, Eternatus being able to roam freely in the Galar region was probably largely thanks to Professor Magnolia's contribution.

"The key point is giving Leon, that blockhead, more opportunities to interact with Sonia," Johan added.

Cynthia smiled slightly. "You still have time to worry about other people's business."

Johan took Cynthia's hand and "proudly" raised his chin. "Of course!"

"Then just call him later. At Leon's speed, he's probably still circling above Wyndon."

Cynthia had quite a bit of contact with Sonia too. Although Sonia usually acted very dismissive toward Leon, Cynthia could sense that Sonia actually liked Leon—it was just that Leon was completely focused on battles and competitions, comparable to Lance.

She and Johan didn't mind playing matchmaker.

"Good point!"

Johan squeezed her hand tighter, then said: "No wait, I should call Sonia instead. Setting two destinations for Leon is somewhat difficult for him."

Cynthia smiled, hearing Johan's implicit criticism of Leon's sense of direction.

The moonlight was bright and clear.

After Eternatus's rampage, Wyndon Stadium had become almost completely ruins.

Fortunately, thanks to Johan's advance arrangements, the International Police and League Police had set up a perimeter in time, so there wasn't much damage beyond Wyndon Stadium and the energy plant.

The black-red vortex had dispersed, and all the Pokémon that had lost control and Dynamaxed under the Dynamax particles had returned to normal.

However, quite a few Pokémon would probably have some bad psychological trauma from this inexplicable Dynamax experience.

But fortunately, all the trouble and danger had passed.

Crisis resolved!

In the ruins of Wyndon Stadium, Johan and Cynthia with their Pokémon were preparing to bid farewell to Zacian, Zamazenta, and Calyrex.

[Let it stay with you for now.] Calyrex gently stroked Spectrier's neck, speaking to Gengar.

[It still enjoys being with you, and you can now properly utilize its power.]

Calyrex used to alternate between riding both steeds, but from now on, it could only ride Glastrier.

But this was only temporary—a way for Calyrex to express its gratitude.

"Ka-kaah! Ka-kaah!"

Gengar turned its head, waving its hands repeatedly while its other hand very "honestly" took the reins Calyrex handed over.

This coy attitude made Johan's eye twitch—it looked exactly like himself during New Year's, saying "no, no" while opening his pocket to receive red envelopes.

But this time Johan didn't stop it.

On one hand, as Calyrex said, Gengar's "ghost-horse fusion" with Spectrier had become more skilled, and Gengar truly possessed the power to control Spectrier.

On the other hand, his island had been completely developed. The white and black radishes planted earlier had overflowed their containers—feeding one Spectrier should be enough.

At worst... he could have Gengar take Spectrier to participate in "horse races" in the future, winning some prize money to cover feed costs.

The only downside might be that during competitions, Gengar couldn't bring Spectrier as an "item," otherwise Gengar would truly dominate in battles.

"Ghost-horse fusion" plus Gengar's own legendary-tier strength could even stand a chance against ordinary first-tier legendaries.

Clearly in the "Pokémon battle" games, Calyrex riding a horse counted as one Pokémon.

But if it were double battles, then hehe! No fancy tricks needed—Gengar would dominate!

Pat!

Gengar patted Spectrier's neck.

"Neigh—"

Spectrier reared and neighed, clip-clopping into the shadows.

No need to worry about following issues anymore.

On the other side, Zacian and Zamazenta were thanking Tinkaton for its help.

The two large wolf-like Pokémon, one blue and one red, with scar-like patterns covering their bodies, had serious expressions but gentle gazes.

[You have the potential to become a hero.] Zacian said.

[We hope you won't disappoint the power you wield.] Zamazenta also fully approved of Tinkaton.

Tinkaton had helped them repair their sword and shield, and had also allowed its own hammer to be further strengthened.

When dealing with Eternatus earlier, their sword and shield had helped Tinkaton fully master "Behemoth Hammer."

This was the only Pokémon besides them and Eternatus to master a "Behemoth" move.

"Behemoth" moves had extremely strong striking power against Dynamax and Gigantamax Pokémon—the move's power would directly double!

If Eternatus ever summoned the "Darkest Day" again while they couldn't exert their power, Tinkaton would become the most crucial existence for subduing Eternatus.

This was power, but also responsibility.

"Cha..."

Tinkaton shouldered its hammer, swaying its pink twin tails, glancing carefully at Zacian and Zamazenta before muttering.

Coach, I want to craft...

Zacian & Zamazenta: "..."

[When the time comes for you to shoulder responsibility, you won't be able to escape.]

[We believe you can do it.]

"Cha?" Tinkaton tilted its head.

Coaches—no, teachers—did you hear what I was saying?

Zacian nodded slightly. [Very good, we believe you can do it.]

Zamazenta turned around. [Since that's the case, let's part ways here.]

"Cha..." Tinkaton gripped its hammer handle with both hands.

Oh no, did you teachers get your heads knocked around? Want me to help treat you? Don't worry, I'm fast—just one hammer blow will do.

Following Johan, Tinkaton felt it had learned a little bit of "technique."

Zacian & Zamazenta: "..."

Retreat!

Whether Tinkaton had listened or not, they'd delivered their message.

"Awooo—"

The two wolves howled and transformed into blue and red beams of light, flying away.

What awaited them might be another long slumber.

"Cha!!" Tinkaton waved its hammer in farewell.

Eh? Not getting treated? Then goodbye! Make sure to come for treatment promptly next time!

The two beams of light seemed to accelerate as they departed.

"Whew—"

Johan lowered his waving hand and breathed a long sigh. "That takes care of one worry."

With Calyrex's request resolved, it could focus more on helping him grow vegetables.

"What about Rose?" Hearing Johan's words, Cynthia asked.

Ultimately, this whole incident was caused by Rose's meddling, so he couldn't escape responsibility.

But Rose's identity was too special.

Chairman of the Galar League, president of the monopolistic Macro Cosmos Group in the Galar region...

And when Eternatus had lost control after going Eternamax, Oleana had already run off with Rose.

Johan hadn't stopped them.

He couldn't handle it.

"Let this big problem give the main League and Leon headaches," Johan said.

Even if they stripped Rose of his League Chairman position, what about Macro Cosmos Group?

Such a massive corporation, one closely related to every Galar resident—could they really dismantle that too?

Who knew how many people would lose their jobs, and the entire Galar region's economy and development would regress by years.

"True."

After thinking it through, Cynthia also felt Rose's identity was too sensitive to handle easily.

The best outcome might be finding someone to take over Macro Cosmos Group.

But with loyal subordinates like Oleana, who knew how many of Rose's die-hard supporters remained within Macro Cosmos Group.

The Eternatus incident had come to a close.

The Galar League didn't stop functioning due to Rose's disappearance, which showed Rose's management of the Galar League.

In fact, Macro Cosmos Group continued operating as well.

The Galar League immediately issued a disaster report about Eternatus—casualties were virtually nonexistent, and they expressed sincere gratitude to those who contributed during the incident.

Mainly Johan and Cynthia, the two Champions.

Of course, there were also people like Hop, Gloria, and Officer Jenny who had forcibly established a perimeter to isolate Wyndon Stadium and the energy plant.

There were also those who had subdued rampaging Dynamax Pokémon or helped others—all these people were on the Galar League's thank-you list.

But most importantly, it was for Champions Johan and Cynthia.

The value of "Champion" skyrocketed again.

Being Champion represented not just strength, but responsibility.

As many people said, if even the Champion couldn't solve a problem, then that region was doomed.

In many people's hearts, "Champion" represented... safety, represented being the tall person who would catch the falling sky.

Of course, Teacher Johan's reputation had already spread "far and wide."

He was famous for teaching and saving the world on the side.

As for Eternatus and its importance to the Galar region, the Galar League's announcement didn't mention this.

After all, it involved secrets about Galar region's Dynamax, and to avoid more people coveting Eternatus...

At this moment, above the Galar region's Wild Area:

"Where is this? Turffield should be that way, right, Charizard?" Leon pointed in a direction and asked.

"Roar..."

Charizard looked around, then back at Leon, and closed its eyes.

It didn't know when it started, but it had been led astray by Leon and could no longer tell directions.

It used to be good at navigation! Even in complex mountain forests, it could accurately find water sources within 500 meters!

"No, it's the opposite direction, Leon."

The sudden voice from behind made Leon pause.

Looking back, he saw a large Corviknight taxi flying in mid-air. In the passenger compartment it carried, Rose sat by the window, smiling and waving at him.

Oleana, who usually stayed by Rose's side, was nowhere to be seen.

"Chairman... Rose..."

Seeing who it was, Leon's expression became complicated.

After hearing Johan's account and reading the Galar League's emergency internal announcement, Leon understood what Rose had done.

But Leon had personal feelings toward Rose.

Honestly, without Rose's help, while Leon was confident he could have grown strong, the speed definitely wouldn't have been as fast as now.

But as the newly appointed Champion of the Galar League, Leon couldn't ignore Rose's actions.

Rose smiled broadly, as if the Eternatus incident hadn't affected him at all.

He said: "Leon, in the future when traveling, if all else fails, call a Flying Taxi, or... bring that little girlfriend of yours along."

Leon: "..."

The two stared at each other in mid-air.

For a moment, both fell into silence.

After a long while, fatigue and loneliness finally appeared on Rose's face.

"I've already made arrangements with the League and the company."

Leon's expression shifted slightly.

Rose continued: "From here on, it's up to you. I'm entrusting the Galar region to you, Champion!"

Using "up to you" once and "entrusting" once, Rose still had deep feelings for the Galar region he'd devoted most of his life to.

Leon's eyes filled with complex emotions.

His lips moved several times, but he still couldn't speak.

Seeing this, Rose smiled slightly.

"You can do it, Champion!"

After speaking, Rose signaled the Flying Taxi driver. The driver saluted Leon before carrying Rose away into the distance.

Leon looked down at his hands gripping Charizard, tightening and loosening them repeatedly.

He took a deep breath.

Slowly exhaled.

He shouted toward the sky where Rose had vanished: "I can do it! Because I'm the Champion of the Galar League!"

"Charizard, let's go—Turffield!" Leon said with determined eyes.

"Roar—!!"

Charizard could feel Leon's state and roared with full spirit, flying toward the direction Rose had indicated.

Rose had given him the starting direction, but the distant journey ahead... he'd have to explore slowly on his own.

Three days later.

"What use are you? Nowhere to be seen when trouble comes, first place in eating!"

Johan looked at Flint, who was busy eating, and his anger flared.

He'd brought him along specifically to attract Eternatus's aggro, but even when Eternatus was rolling its eyes, Flint was nowhere to be seen.

"You can't blame me for that! Teacher Johan, you didn't call me, and I was on my way!" Flint defended himself "righteously."

"Leon came from Wyndon and made it, but you couldn't make it from within Wyndon?"

"Leon didn't get lost?" Flint asked instinctively, then quickly added: "That's different—Leon has Charizard!"

Johan glared. "Leon can capture a Charizard, can't you capture one? Or are you saying Charizard isn't a Fire-type Pokémon?"

"I..."

Flint wanted to argue further, but facing "aggressive" Johan, his momentum weakened as he muttered quietly: "I want to capture one too, but I have to be able to afford raising it..."

For Flint, it wasn't enough to just capture Pokémon—he had to train them too.

He'd previously spent everything training one Ninetales to build his so-called "sun team." Where would he get resources to train a Charizard?

Moreover, as one of Kanto's starter Pokémon trio, Charizard was so popular—how easy did they think it was to capture?

Did they think everyone could encounter an abandoned Charmander?

And... it had been so many days, yet Teacher Johan kept bringing this up repeatedly.

Watching Johan and Flint "bicker," Caitlin hugged Cynthia's arm, pitifully recounting the entire process of being "bullied" by Johan.

Cynthia felt helpless about this.

Both were dear to her—she could only comfort the "wounded" Caitlin while listening to her complaints.

It wasn't just Johan going on about it repeatedly; Caitlin had been using Johan "bullying" her as a talking point for days.

"Speaking of which, Teacher Johan, are we staying in the Galar region? I heard quite a few contestants went to the Paldea region to climb ranks," Flint suddenly asked.

After such a major incident with Eternatus in the Galar region, plus Chairman Rose's disappearance, many contestants—especially those like Leon and Raihan who shouldered responsibilities—had to spend more energy on League management and Wyndon's reconstruction and maintenance.

More importantly, none of them—whether Johan, Cynthia, Flint, or Caitlin—had been to the Paldea region.

Everyone was somewhat curious about this unvisited region and the Pokémon they'd never seen.

"The Paldea region..."

Johan was successfully distracted by Flint.

Compared to the Paldea region, what Johan was most curious about were the ancient and future forms of Pokémon.

They weren't in the Pokédex, but there were occasional rumors about ancient form Pokémon—he wondered if they actually existed.

He was quite curious about the so-called "Area Zero."

He wondered how his Tyranitar would react to meeting the future form Iron Thorns.

Nothing else mattered, but the future form Iron Valiant looked pretty cool. If there was a chance, getting a photo together would be nice.

"It's not impossible to consider," Johan said thoughtfully.

He suddenly asked: "By the way, are Friede and the others not in the Wild Area recently?"

The few days at Lake of Outrage had made Johan recognize the safety configuration of the "Brave Olivine" and the "Rising Volt Tacklers."

Being able to stay safely at Lake of Outrage for several days at least ensured that under Friede's protection, the "Rising Volt Tacklers" could go to most wild areas in the Pokémon world.

Of course, extremely dangerous places like Mt. Silver were out of the question.

Even Johan had to be careful on Mt. Silver, or he might suffer serious injuries.

"Then when they return, let's all go to the Paldea region together!" Johan decided, with no objections.

The Paldea region had special Terastallization, another enhancement method they hadn't experienced much.

Terastallization could not only strengthen a Pokémon's type but also change its type to some extent—this ability often produced surprising effects in battle.

For example, a Pokémon weak to Fighting could directly Terastallize into Ghost type, creating a very significant type reversal effect.

However, most Pokémon's Terastallization matched their original types—for instance, when Gengar Terastallized before, it was always Tera Ghost.

Because changing a Pokémon's Tera type required special "Tera Shards."

This stuff... was fairly easy to obtain in Pokémon Scarlet/Violet, but he didn't know if it existed in the Pokémon world.

If he could change Tyranitar's Tera type to Ground, that would be perfect!

"Oh right."

Cynthia suddenly said: "Professor Sycamore couldn't reach you, so he's been calling me instead."

"Professor Sycamore?" Johan was surprised.

These past few days, Johan had put his phone in airplane mode.

On one hand, to properly spend time with Cynthia; on the other, because the existence of a legendary like Eternatus—anyone with eyes could see it was somehow related to Galar region's Dynamax—had many people wanting to understand the situation from him.

Most of these people were Pokémon research professionals.

Leading them was Professor Oak.

Professor Oak was extremely curious about the never-before-seen legendary Eternatus.

So Johan had simply turned off communications for three days.

He hadn't expected Professor Sycamore to call Cynthia's phone directly.

Probably got the contact from Diantha?

"About Eternatus?"

"No, about..." Cynthia glanced at Flint and Caitlin. "About bond evolution."

Although Johan had cleared the area, some people had inevitably captured images of Wyndon Stadium from afar.

Most people's attention was on Eternatus, but he hadn't expected Professor Sycamore to keenly notice Gengar's change.

That made sense—he was the Pokémon professor researching Mega Evolution after all.

Johan understood.

"Bond evolution?" Flint and Caitlin looked confused hearing Cynthia's words.

What was that?

"I should indeed send Professor Sycamore some data on this."

"Bond evolution" was related to further Mega Evolution research—the direction Professor Sycamore was pursuing. Johan and Gengar's appearance was equivalent to pointing Professor Sycamore in the right direction again.

Professor Sycamore should give him half his doctorate degree.

Back then, Mega Evolution had also been based on first-hand information he and Gengar provided.

This time, "bond evolution" was again pointing him in the right direction.

"Mm, contact him yourself later," Cynthia said.

"Yeah."

Brave Olivine.

"The Paldea region?" Hearing Teacher Johan's intention, Friede's eyes immediately lit up.

"I can't speak for other regions, but I'm very familiar with the Paldea region."

Friede had previously worked as a researcher at the Paldea region's largest company, Rotom Phone Company.

Additionally, his teacher Rica was also working as a teacher in the Paldea region.

"I can arrange accommodations!"

It had been a while since he'd seen his teacher or little Liko. He wondered if the child still remembered him.

Counting the time, Liko was probably about school age, right?

If she could meet an excellent teacher like Teacher Johan early, it might give Liko a very good start.

"Really? Then I'll leave it to you, Friede," Johan said.

# 753: Paldea Region!

The Paldea region was vast and expansive.

Most importantly, the Paldea region had very complex terrain. Centered around the basin and mountainous area where the Great Crater of Paldea was located, it divided the entire region into four areas: East, South, West, and North.

Pokémon like Deerling and Sawsbuck appeared in different forms across the various areas of the Paldea region, showing just how vast and complex the Paldea region's territory was.

Moreover, the four areas of the Paldea region presented different temperature environments: the south approached spring temperatures, the east approached summer temperatures, the west approached autumn temperatures, and the northern Glaseado Mountain approached winter.

At the same time, the most mysterious part of the Paldea region was undoubtedly the mysterious Great Crater of Paldea, surrounded by thick cloud layers.

It was said that the residents of the Paldea region all believed that very precious treasures slept in the depths of the Great Crater of Paldea, but due to environmental and Pokémon issues, few people dared to venture into the Great Crater of Paldea.

Additionally, the Paldea region was also famous for the "Ten Sights of Paldea"—ten areas with extremely beautiful scenery in the Paldea region.

Besides casually gaining some points, Johan's visit to the Paldea region was actually more about wanting to see these "Ten Sights of Paldea."

Of course, more importantly were the "Titan Pokémon" of the Paldea region and the "Herba Mystica" they guarded.

This was the "consciousness" of a chef.

Hearing Friede's brief introduction to the Paldea region, everyone was quite interested in it.

Especially Flint.

He looked eager to challenge the Paldea League's Elite Four.

The Galar region didn't have an Elite Four system, but the Paldea region did, which made Flint's goals more defined.

"This is the West Province of the Paldea region."

Friede stood with Johan and the others on the deck of the Brave Olivine, looking down through the clouds at the complex terrain of rolling mountains in Paldea's West Province.

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar wasn't very concerned about what the Paldea region looked like. It just tilted its head and sat beside Old Man Ludlow, curiously watching his aerial fishing stance.

Could he really catch fish like this?

Ludlow chuckled happily. "I'm fishing for peace of mind. Whether I actually catch anything isn't really important."

"Ka-kaah! (゜Д゜)σ"

Gengar pointed at the old man and looked at Johan questioningly.

Is this old man showing off?

Johan nodded expressionlessly.

Ludlow: "..."

"Ahead is Medali Town," Friede said while speaking into the radio. "Medali Town, prepare for landing."

"Roger that." Orla the mechanic's voice came through the radio.

"Medali Town?" Flint poked his head out, looking down curiously.

Although Medali Town was just a town located in the West Province of the Paldea region, its scale wasn't small at all—it even looked more prosperous than ordinary cities.

Moreover, Medali Town was a town with a complex, intricate layout. As its name suggested, it was a town that gathered various treasures.

It was most famous for the large and small distinctive restaurants throughout the town.

It could be said to gather various specialty cuisines from all areas of the Paldea region, and even specialty foods from the other eight regions could be found in Medali Town.

It wasn't an exaggeration to say that Medali Town was a massive "food city" of the Paldea region.

This was also why many people would come to Medali Town to enjoy cuisine, including Elite Four members, Gym Leaders, and even Champions from the Paldea region.

The most famous place in Medali Town was "Treasure Eatery."

It was famous for good quality at reasonable prices. It was said that the taste was quite good and the prices very affordable. Many well-known trainers from the Paldea region were loyal customers of "Treasure Eatery."

This included the Gym Leader of Medali Town.

Moreover, it was said that "Treasure Eatery" would introduce a dish that could change a Pokémon's Tera type every so often, which was one of the reasons it could attract so many trainers.

Of course, with more monks than porridge, Pokémon battles were inevitable.

So "Treasure Eatery" was also famously a Pokémon battle restaurant.

And so, hearing that Medali Town was full of delicious restaurants, Gengar could barely contain itself.

In Gengar's words—it was already famished!

"Doesn't that mean we can encounter many powerful trainers to gain points from?" Flint became even more excited, his face showing impatience.

Johan glanced at him sideways.

"Who knows who'll be giving points to whom."

"Heh." Flint didn't mind. "Winning and losing are both common. I just wonder which powerful trainers are in Medali Town now."

"The Gym Leader of Medali Town," Johan said casually.

"Is Medali Town's Gym Leader very powerful?"

Friede interjected: "Medali Town's Gym Leader Mr. Larry is also an Elite Four member of the Paldea League."

"Elite Four? Moonlighting as a Gym Leader?" Flint looked surprised.

Except for the Galar region, where some were called Gym Leaders but were more like Elite Four in name—like Raihan—other regions didn't seem to have Elite Four moonlighting as Gym Leaders, right?

Johan's expression was somewhat strange as he said: "Strictly speaking... he's a Gym Leader moonlighting as Elite Four."

"Huh?"

Flint was stunned.

Could it work in reverse too?

Friede looked at Johan with some surprise.

"I didn't expect Teacher Johan to know about Mr. Larry?"

Larry's fame wasn't great. Even as an Elite Four member, he wasn't as enthusiastic about battles as other Elite Four members. He was like... just a very ordinary, ordinary person.

Johan's mouth twitched slightly.

Honestly, calling him a Gym Leader moonlighting as Elite Four was already "flattering" Larry.

Because Larry's primary identity was an employee in the Paldea League's operations department, then Medali Town's Gym Leader, and only after that an Elite Four member of the Paldea League.

As for why he chose Medali Town... for no other reason than Medali Town's "Treasure Eatery" suited Larry's taste perfectly, and the prices were cheap.

The key point was the cheap prices.

Therefore, Larry was actually moonlighting as a Gym Leader, then moonlighting as League Elite Four.

League Elite Four was his second side job. If word got out, who knew how many Elite Four would think this Elite Four position wasn't worth having!

So Larry had truly understood the "working life," clocking in and out on time. He was also somewhat famous for being laid-back—no, it should be called having a normal mindset.

He formed a stark contrast with Galar region's Gym Leader Mr. Gordie.

As for why Johan knew so much about Larry...

On one hand, he had fought Larry when playing Pokémon Scarlet/Violet.

On the other hand, he had once read a Pokémon fanfiction whose protagonist was named "Larry," and this was even before Scarlet/Violet came out.

"I've heard some things, just some simple things..." Johan said vaguely.

"Preparing to land!"

Orla's voice rang out over the intercom.

Then the Brave Olivine descended below.

Although Medali Town was located in the West Province of the Paldea region, it wasn't near the edge of the Paldea region. It could even be said to be in an area relatively close to the center, because east of Medali Town was the Great Crater of Paldea.

West of Medali Town was the famous "Casseroya Lake," south was the Paldea region's largest desert "Asado Desert," and a bit further north was the Paldea region's highest snow mountain "Glaseado Mountain."

So Medali Town's geographical location was also an important reason it could become the Paldea region's largest "food city."

The "Brave Olivine" landed on flat ground outside Medali Town.

As soon as they got off the airship, there was Medali Town's Pokémon Center.

"Eh? The Pokémon Center is outside town?" Flint asked curiously.

"This is also one of the Paldea region's characteristics," Friede said. "Most Pokémon Centers in the Paldea region are located outside towns, to make it more convenient for traveling trainers to get timely treatment."

"On the other hand, it's probably also prepared for trainers who have Tera Orbs, right?" Johan added.

"Exactly." Friede nodded. "There's also the reason of recharging Tera Orbs faster."

"But actually, it's mainly for the convenience of traveling trainers. Most towns also have a Pokémon Center inside, after all... not every trainer can possess a Tera Orb," Friede added.

There were also ones inside towns?

This was somewhat different from Johan's understanding of Paldea region Pokémon Centers.

However, having at least three Pokémon Centers per town required considerable financial investment from the Paldea League.

Not just in terms of Nurse Joy staffing, but also the expensive equipment in Pokémon Centers.

The Paldea region truly lived up to its reputation as a region very "friendly" to trainers.

Not just inside and outside towns—even in some relatively dangerous wild areas, Pokémon Centers existed.

This placed higher requirements on the "self-protection abilities" of the Nurse Joys at Pokémon Centers.

"Tera Orbs? Those are the items that let Pokémon Terastallize, right?" Caitlin asked.

"That's right," Friede said.

Then, under everyone's puzzled gazes, Johan strode purposefully into the Pokémon Center.

Against the familiar background music, Johan walked up to Nurse Joy.

"Hello, how may I help... Teacher Johan?"

The polite Nurse Joy was halfway through her professional greeting when she suddenly recognized Johan, looking completely surprised.

Johan smiled as he took out a pitch-black orb.

It was exactly a Tera Orb.

However, its energy hadn't been recharged since the last "Hoopa Unbound" incident.

"Nurse Joy, could you please help me recharge this?"

"A Tera Orb? Oh, oh oh... certainly, please wait a moment." Nurse Joy was still in shock from Teacher Johan's sudden appearance. Only when Johan took out the Tera Orb did she react.

Tera Orbs that couldn't be recharged in other regions became very convenient in the Paldea region.

Just like Dynamax in the Galar region.

"Thank you."

Taking the fully recharged Tera Orb, Johan thanked her with a gentle smile.

Nurse Joy's face flushed slightly as she quickly waved her hands. "N-no need to be polite."

Only after Johan left the Pokémon Center did Nurse Joy finally slowly realize.

Teacher Johan had actually come to the Paldea region?

Wasn't he just dealing with that terrifying and powerful Eternatus in the Galar region a few days ago?

"Oh no, I forgot to take a photo!" Nurse Joy slapped her forehead, remembering she hadn't taken a photo with Teacher Johan. Sisters in the family group were always showing off photos with Teacher Johan—she hadn't expected it would finally be her turn!

Biting her lip, Nurse Joy still jogged after him.

"Teacher Johan, could I take a photo with you?"

"Hmm?"

Johan, who had just left the Pokémon Center, was slightly stunned, then smiled. "Of course."

Click—

After the photo, Nurse Joy returned to the Pokémon Center satisfied, posting the picture in the family group to show off.

Medali Town South Pokémon Center Joy: [Photo.jpg]

Medali Town South Pokémon Center Joy: Teacher Johan is even more handsome in person than in photos!

Oh no!

Johan, who had just taken a photo with Nurse Joy, suddenly felt a chill down his back.

Damn.

He'd forgotten his little Cynthia was there, plus Caitlin as a "troublemaker" fanning the flames.

Johan turned around.

He saw Cynthia with a gentle smile and warm expression, Caitlin whispering in her ear, Garchomp looking fierce but with its eyes covered by Gengar, and...

A bunch of "spectators" turning their heads aside, pretending to make phone calls, or crouching to count ants.

Cynthia tucked her golden hair behind her ear and smiled slightly. "Let's go, let's enter Medali Town first."

She turned and walked elegantly in front.

Entering Medali Town, Johan hurried to catch up, spending considerable effort to squeeze past the pouting and indignant Caitlin, quietly asking:

"Are you jealous?"

"You're overthinking it." Cynthia's voice remained unchanged, still gentle and elegant.

"Really?"

"Really."

"Is that... Miss Cynthia! It really is Miss Cynthia!"

As soon as they entered Medali Town, they suddenly encountered a young man in his twenties who immediately recognized Cynthia.

"I'm your fan! Could I take a photo with you?" The young man's face flushed red with excitement.

Hmm?

Johan immediately narrowed his eyes.

Cynthia looked at Johan with a smile, then said: "If you strongly insist..."

"That would be wonder—" Before he could finish, the young man suddenly felt a bone-chilling coldness rise behind him, as if he'd fallen directly from the hot Asado Desert into Glaseado Mountain.

Only then did he notice Johan beside Cynthia, wearing a "kind" smile.

"T-Teacher Johan..."

Johan said gently: "Like taking photos? I know many rare legendary Pokémon—Giratina, Groudon, Yveltal, Kyurem, Eternatus. If you're willing, I could have them pose nicely for photos with you."

Young man: Σ(゜□゜)

These... these legendary Pokémon could really take photos?

"Gulp—" The young man swallowed hard.

Cynthia gave Johan an annoyed look, then turned to the young man: "Sorry, my boyfriend is here, so photos... let's skip it."

"Boy-boyfriend?!"

The young man was struck by lightning.

Although there had long been rumors online that Teacher Johan and Miss Cynthia might be a couple, hearing this for the first time, the young man as a Cynthia fan still found it hard to believe.

Johan was different—he immediately squinted his eyes, almost letting out a "Zhang Wei-style" long "ahhhh."

This was the first time Cynthia had openly called him her boyfriend in front of a stranger.

This feeling... was incredibly fulfilling!

"Ka-kaah~" Gengar beside them showed an "auntie smile," looking like their pig had finally managed to win over good cabbage.

In contrast, Garchomp and Caitlin's expressions were quite colorful.

"Shaka..."

My cabbage that I raised for over twenty years...

Johan patted the young man's shoulder: "Fine, I won't have Gengar visit you tonight."

Young man: Σ(゜□゜)

Did... did I dodge a bullet?

After passing the young man, Cynthia asked softly with a smile: "Are you jealous?"

She "replicated" Johan's earlier words.

Johan's answer was much more "honest"—he nodded: "Yeah."

"I almost had Gengar and Giratina take him for a tour of Paldea's famous cemetery areas in the middle of the night."

"Pfft—"

Cynthia couldn't help but laugh.

"So were you jealous just now?" Johan asked again as if confirming.

"I wasn't."

"Eh?"

Cynthia's cheeks flushed as she glared at Johan somewhat coquettishly, saying word by word:

"I wasn't!"

Her reaction actually said everything. Johan smiled.

When little Cynthia was jealous... she was really cute.

Everyone behind them: We shouldn't be here, we should be under the car...

They felt like they'd been force-fed an entire tank of dog food.

"Teacher Rica!"

In Medali Town, Friede finally met the teacher who had profoundly influenced him—Rica!

She was also the mother of Liko, one of the protagonists of Pokémon Horizons, and the great-granddaughter of Rystal, one of the ancient "Explorers trio"!

She had been a Pokémon trainer and was now a teacher.

Rica wore a practical white T-shirt and navy jacket, with short hair, and a golden Squawkabilly perched on her shoulder. It called out quite imposingly, seemingly compensating for Rica's lack of "authority" as a teacher.

"Long time no see, Friede. You look really good now. These people are— Wait! Teacher Johan?!"

As a teacher, Rica was naturally familiar with Johan, who was also a teacher.

"Let me introduce everyone..."

Friede then introduced everyone to Rica one by one.

They learned that Rica's family had already moved from Medali Town to Cabo Poco, but they still arranged to meet in Medali Town...

On one hand, Medali Town was more suitable for hosting guests, allowing her and her husband to properly fulfill their host duties.

On the other hand, it was also because her husband and his friend were recently collecting materials near Medali Town.

Her husband Alex was a well-known picture book artist.

"If Alex's friend knew Teacher Johan was here too, he'd definitely be very excited."

Hmm?

Alex's... friend?

Why would he be so excited?

"Come on! I'll take you to a restaurant first. Alex and the others need a bit more time—we don't need to wait for them!" Rica waved her hand, speaking quite "boldly."

Flint and Gengar immediately cheered.

"Teacher, where's little Liko?" Friede couldn't help asking.

Besides seeing her once when Liko was very small, he hadn't seen that little one for a long time.

"Liko." Speaking of Liko, Rica's face immediately showed maternal radiance. "She's with her grandmother in Cabo Poco. She's starting kindergarten."

"I see..." Friede understood.

Johan walked alongside Cynthia holding her hand without interrupting.

But he was thinking about some things.

Liko should already be wearing Terapagos's pendant by now, right?

In the near future, when her grandmother Diana realized Liko might encounter danger, she would commission Friede's "Rising Volt Tacklers."

That would be the beginning of the Pokémon Horizons plot.

Oh, there was no more "Rising Volt Tacklers" now—it should be called the "Rising Volt Tacklers Class."

Just as everyone arrived at Medali Town's most famous "Treasure Eatery," a crisp call suddenly came from the side.

"Woof!!"

They saw a small dog that looked like it was made from fermented dough, wagging its tail and sticking out its tongue, barking while running toward Rica.

Fidough.

"Fidough, a Fairy-type puppy Pokémon. It's said that its breath contains rich yeast that can ferment surrounding objects. It's one of the favorite Pokémon of pastry chefs, Rotom!"

Rotom rarely proved useful again.

Rica crouched down, smiling as she caught the running Fidough.

This was their family's Fidough. Its appearance meant Alex had returned.

Looking up, she indeed saw two men walking quickly toward them.

Walking in front was a man with a silly smile, wearing round glasses and a navy shirt with a paint-stained apron over it, scratching his head—Rica's husband, picture book artist Alex.

Beside Alex was a well-dressed middle-aged man with golden hair, holding a sketchbook.

"Back so soon?"

Rica first expressed surprise, then introduced everyone.

Setting aside Alex, the person beside him was clearly—

"This is the Art teacher at Orange Academy, and also one of our Paldea region's Elite Four, Dragon-type Elite Four member, Mr. Hassel!"

It really was him!

The meticulous Hassel maintained a stern expression, but when Rica introduced him, his expression softened considerably, showing some smile.

"Teacher Johan, Champion Cynthia..."

Hassel's Elite Four status certainly surprised quite a few people.

But don't forget—among Johan's group were two Champions and two Elite Four members.

So Hassel's Elite Four status... barely reached average passing level.

"Mr. Hassel, no need for formalities..."

Regardless, Hassel was considerably older than Johan, Cynthia, and the others.

Although they were already Champions, proper respect for seniors was still appropriate.

Hassel nodded, his gaze falling on Johan.

His expression became serious again.

"Teacher Johan, I would like to discuss with you the way of teaching, to learn and study from each other!"

Hmm?

Everyone looked toward Rica and Alex.

Alex quietly explained: "This is Mr. Hassel's challenge invitation."

Everyone understood.

No wonder... Rica had said earlier that Alex's friend would definitely be very excited to see Teacher Johan.

Upon meeting, he immediately issued a challenge?

And his speech was somewhat literary...
