# 754: The Way of Teaching!

Paldea region, Medali Town, Treasure Eatery.

Rica and Alex's family were treating everyone to a meal.

Flint and Gengar were relatively restrained.

Although it was an affordable eatery, if these two were allowed to eat freely... Rica's family would definitely go broke.

Moreover, the Treasure Eatery's food really tasted great. Even many high-end restaurants might not match its flavor, especially some of their specialty dishes.

This made Johan quite curious. When he was tasting the food, he felt there were several seasonings he couldn't quite identify.

Maybe he could ask the owner after finishing the meal.

However, for Johan right now, the most "troublesome" thing was actually Elite Four Hassel's challenge.

Hassel wasn't like Flint, who would challenge even Champions in a moment of heated passion.

As a teacher with more seniority than Johan, Hassel was quite strict when teaching, and his actions definitely weren't impulsive decisions. His challenge must have been carefully considered.

Most likely... it was exactly as he said—he wanted to discuss the "way of teaching" with Johan.

When it involved such a serious topic, Johan really couldn't casually refuse.

But the problem was—what exactly was the "way of teaching"?

Johan felt somewhat bewildered.

I'm just someone who teaches "Pokémon Psychology," and you teach art. Our so-called "ways of teaching" are quite different, aren't they?

But...

"Teacher Johan seems somewhat confused?" Hassel, who was also dining, seemed to see through Johan's thoughts and asked with a slight smile.

At this moment, Hassel appeared much more gentle and approachable compared to earlier.

Johan didn't hide it and nodded: "Indeed, a little."

They had no previous connection. Although Johan's reputation was considerable, and he had outstanding students like Steven, N, Flannery, Roxanne, and Penny...

Honestly, even without him, these people could have grown excellently. At most, he'd just helped them avoid some detours or clarified their true inner goals.

Johan felt he couldn't quite bear the weight of "the way of teaching."

"Mainly because of three people," Hassel explained.

"Three people?"

Johan was stunned.

"The first is the principal of your Kanaz Pokémon Academy. He recommended you to me, saying that if I ever encountered particularly confusing matters, perhaps I could talk with you."

The old principal?

Johan: "..."

The old principal truly lived up to the title of "Pokémon World's Top Network King."

He had connections everywhere and could talk to anyone.

No wonder with the old principal in charge, Kanaz Pokémon Academy could develop to such a scale.

With such networking relationships... it would be hard not to succeed, right?

However, Johan also noticed Hassel's mention of "particularly confusing matters."

Confusing matters referred to...

Hassel continued: "The second is Mr. Shaga, the Gym Leader of Opelucid City."

Old man Shaga?

Johan's mind conjured the image of that Opelucid City Gym Leader who, despite his age, was built like a brick house.

"I used to teach his grandson Drayton—a very capable but rather lazy child. However, Mr. Shaga said that after Teacher Johan battled with Drayton, Drayton has become much more motivated than before."

Both Shaga and Hassel were trainers specializing in Dragon-type Pokémon, and Mr. Shaga actually had strength comparable to Elite Four level.

Plus, Drayton's Blueberry Academy had complex relationships with Orange Academy and the Paldea region.

It made sense that Hassel would listen to old man Shaga's words.

"What about the third person?" Johan couldn't help but become curious.

One old principal, one old man Shaga, and the last one... it couldn't be old man Drake, could it?

Hassel smiled slightly: "Lance."

"Lance?"

Johan silently added a question mark above his head.

When did Lance develop such strong networking skills?

Wait.

Suddenly, Johan remembered something.

Besides being a Paldea League Elite Four member and Orange Academy art teacher, Hassel had another identity... heir to the Paldea region's Dragon Tamer family!

And Lance was the heir to the Dragon Clan of Kanto and Johto regions!

The two really could connect.

Moreover, Hassel and Lance did indeed share some similarities.

Lance hadn't grown up within the Dragon Clan. His starter Pokémon was actually Charizard, and only later did he rise to prominence and return to the Dragon Clan in Blackthorn City, Johto.

Slightly different from that, Hassel had been quite rebellious in his youth, running away from home to pursue his dream of becoming a musician.

He eventually grew up to become a Paldea League Elite Four member.

To call Hassel back to the Paldea Dragon Tamer family, his father had sent a Dragon Tamer who lied about being seriously ill, wanting Hassel to return.

This had left Hassel in confusion.

It wasn't until later, through interaction with the game's "protagonist," that he realized how precious his life as a teacher at the academy was. He also saw through his father's lie, thus refusing that Dragon Tamer and declining to return to the Dragon Tamer family.

This angered both that Dragon Tamer and the Dragon Tamer family.

But thinking in reverse, it was precisely Hassel's persistence and determination that the Dragon Tamer family patriarch most needed, so he appeared quite conflicted.

Thinking of this, Johan's heart stirred.

Could Hassel currently be at a confused crossroads about whether to return to the Dragon Tamer family or continue as a teacher?

Lance's return to the Dragon Clan was one example, but Hassel was obviously more hesitant than Lance. Honestly, he didn't need the Dragon Tamer family's help now, nor did he feel he could help the Dragon Tamer family much...

Instead, he felt reluctant about his teaching profession.

Therefore, upon meeting Johan, Hassel sought the so-called "answer" to the "way of teaching."

Johan felt his guess was probably close to the truth.

And indeed, it was.

Hassel said: "Lance said you once helped Steven emerge from confusion. Perhaps I can get answers from you."

After pausing, he became serious again and continued:

"As fellow trainers, I think... nothing allows for better communication than battle."

Johan felt somewhat helpless.

What kind of situation was this?

He'd originally wanted to watch the good show of Flint, that explosive head, battling Larry, that taciturn guy, to see whether the explosive head's enthusiasm could ignite the taciturn guy or the taciturn guy's cold water would extinguish the explosive head.

He hadn't expected it would be his turn first.

However... as an Elite Four member, and the first Elite Four of the Paldea League at that, plus being active in the Paldea region, Hassel's points... shouldn't be low, right?

With the opponent so openly requesting a challenge under the banner of "the way of teaching," Johan couldn't refuse.

Since he couldn't refuse... then I'll take these points!

Facing other Elite Four members might be uncertain, but facing a Dragon-type Elite Four member...

I'll send out Ninetales—how would you like to respond to that?

Though thinking this internally, Johan still responded very seriously:

"Good. In that case... I'll also learn about the 'way of teaching' from Mr. Hassel."

Seeing Johan always show serious, earnest expressions when discussing "the way of teaching," Hassel felt respectful, thinking Johan was the same type of person as himself.

When it came to being a teacher and setting an example, both would treat it seriously.

After all, it concerned young people's futures—no room for carelessness.

Hassel felt he might have found someone who could truly discuss the "way of teaching."

This battle exchange... he had to approach it with 120% seriousness and dedication!

"Hmm?"

A middle-aged man in a wrinkled suit, carrying a briefcase with disheveled hair and looking listless, was just about to dine at "Treasure Eatery."

He saw a Rotom drone flying toward the stadium outside Medali Town.

The middle-aged man was used to this—since the "Pokémon World Championships" points stage began, people battled for points daily.

Moreover, Medali Town, famous for its cuisine, attracted many trainers from around the world to taste the food.

With so many people, naturally there were many battles.

Rather than join the excitement, it was better to order at "Treasure Eatery" early and have a small drink... Thinking of this, the middle-aged man's spirits seemed to improve somewhat.

Middle-aged men's happiness was sometimes just that simple.

But just as he turned back, the middle-aged man received a text on his phone.

"It better not be overtime again."

Somewhat annoyed, he glanced at his phone, planning to pretend he hadn't seen it and turn off his phone if it was overtime.

However, when he saw the battle information displayed on his phone, he couldn't help but pause.

Hoenn Champion Johan VS Paldea Elite Four Hassel!

As Medali Town's Gym Leader and one of Paldea's Elite Four, Larry needed to be responsible for Medali Town's security. Therefore, for relatively "dangerous" battles, he would receive information from the League system.

This was to prevent the battling parties from getting too heated and causing unnecessary damage to Medali Town.

In this regard, the Paldea region, compared to the Galar region with stadiums everywhere, was somewhat lacking.

Of course, this didn't mean Paldea region's stadiums weren't safe—it was just that relatively speaking, Paldea region's stadium barriers weren't as strong as Galar region's Dynamax barriers.

In fact, purely in terms of protective barriers, the Galar region should be the best in the entire Pokémon world.

The Paldea region... was probably about the same as Hoenn, Johto, and Sinnoh.

"Sigh," Larry rubbed his stomach and sighed deeply.

As expected, it was overtime.

He looked up at the setting sun and the reddened sky, scratching his head.

However... for a battle of this level, working a little overtime was still acceptable, right?

Thinking this, Larry hefted his briefcase and reluctantly looked back at "Treasure Eatery" before turning toward the stadium.

Actually, "Treasure Eatery" had a battle area inside. Larry often used "Treasure Eatery" as half a gym, and after battling challengers, he could dine right there.

But obviously, a battle like Teacher Johan VS Teacher Hassel couldn't be contained by the battle area inside "Treasure Eatery."

When Larry arrived at the stadium in Medali Town's outskirts, quite a crowd of people and trainers had already gathered.

Despite being an Elite Four member, Larry walking into the crowd didn't even cause much commotion.

However, Larry was quite happy about this.

He was just a "working man," not here to be some idol or celebrity. The fewer people who recognized him, the less trouble there would be.

Being ordinary and unremarkable wasn't bad at all.

"Why did Teacher Johan come to our Paldea?"

"I don't know."

"Do you think he might be here for an exchange with Orange Academy? Otherwise, why would he battle Elite Four Hassel right after arriving?"

"I think it's more likely that Teacher Johan finished 'destroying' Galar region's contestants and came to climb ranks in our Paldea..."

"Climb ranks? Oh no! Quick, notify all the 'babies' to go home! Need 'adult' supervision when going out—Teacher Johan is here!"

The trainers and audience who came here weren't unfamiliar with Johan.

Especially in Paldea, a region where the education industry flourished—the currently most famous and strongest "teacher" in the entire Pokémon world wasn't from their Paldea region. How could they not know him?

Of course, there were also audience members who came because of Hassel.

Overall... Hassel had slightly higher support.

This had nothing to do with strength, region, or personal preference—purely because the world had suffered under Teacher Johan for too long!

In plain terms: if you're going to watch excitement, watch big excitement!

"Go Hassel! Crush Teacher Johan!"

"Both are teachers—prove that Orange Academy is better than Kanaz Pokémon Academy!"

"Elite Four Hassel, did you see that girl? Yes, Miss Cynthia! So... please beat up Teacher Johan for us!"

"..."

Hearing the cheers from the gradually filling stadium audience, Hassel's expression was grave in the preparation room.

Why...

Although Teacher Johan was clearly a Champion, everyone still called him "Teacher," while although he was an Elite Four member and also a teacher, everyone called him "Elite Four."

This showed... Teacher Johan's teacher identity was deeply rooted in people's hearts, while his own... Elite Four identity seemed more notable than his teacher identity.

"Perhaps this is what I need to learn from Teacher Johan."

On the other side, in Johan's rest room, he was having a staring contest with Gengar.

"Ka... ka-kaah..."

Gengar stared with dry, bloodshot eyes. After struggling to hold on, it finally couldn't help but blink, then frantically rubbed its eyes.

"You lost!"

Johan smiled triumphantly, also rubbing his dry eyes.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar muttered while rubbing its eyes.

I was... I was letting you win! Mere human...

How could it lose to Johan in a blinking contest? Gengar was full of confusion.

That made no sense...

"Cynthia, look at him—where's the mature man's demeanor? Actually... actually having a blinking contest with Gengar?" Caitlin hugged Cynthia's arm with a disgusted expression.

Cynthia smiled gracefully and patted Caitlin's head.

The fierce Caitlin toward others only became obedient like a little kitten when facing Cynthia.

"Johan, come try on your outfit," Cynthia said.

"Outfit?"

Johan, who was about to put in eye drops, looked up at the black half-collar cape Cynthia had taken out. At first glance, it looked somewhat like a black windbreaker—very stylish and matching well with the black suit Johan was wearing.

"When did you buy this?" Johan approached, asking curiously.

Cynthia didn't answer but asked: "How is it? Try it on?"

"Sure."

Johan didn't hesitate. Cynthia helped him put the cape on his back.

It had to be said, even Johan felt stunning after putting it on.

If he added black gloves and carried a cane, people would probably believe he was descended from ancient nobility.

"Mm... it looks quite good." Cynthia didn't know if she was praising Johan or her own taste.

Johan touched the cape—the texture was quite nice too.

As everyone knew, in the Pokémon world, capes and windbreakers were part of the boss starter kit.

He hadn't expected to have a day when he'd wear a cape...

This cape, plus his little suit, plus his teacher identity—could this be the Pokémon world's "home tutor"?

Too bad this cape was a gift from his little Cynthia, so he couldn't put advertisements on it!

Johan immediately felt like he'd lost billions.

But since it was from Cynthia, Johan felt very happy.

"Now that you're a Champion, you should pay more attention to your attire," Cynthia adjusted Johan's collar, saying softly.

"Don't worry, I guarantee I won't let the cape fly away," Johan promised.

Hearing this, Cynthia smiled, knowing Johan was referring to Leon's "Champion Time."

She discovered... Johan seemed to enjoy muttering about friends' little quirks or habits. This was probably one of Johan's little quirks too?

"Ka-kaah~"

Whoosh—

Gengar pouted as it shook out its huge Spirit World cloth and draped it over its shoulders.

It looked at itself in the mirror, turned around, wiggled its rear, and nodded with satisfaction.

"Ka-kaah~"

As expected, I'm still the most handsome.

"Teacher Johan, time to go on stage!" Flint, who was getting somewhat impatient, shouted.

He'd clearly wanted to come to the Paldea region to climb ranks, so how had the first battle become Teacher Johan's?

But Champion VS First Elite Four was still quite exciting to imagine.

"Let's go!" Johan grinned.

"Mm."

Walking out of the contestant tunnel, the audience immediately erupted in exclamations.

"Holy crap! Teacher Johan actually became more handsome?!"

Johan: "..."

"A cape! Teacher Johan actually has such a cool cape?"

"It's different now—from now on, his identity is Champion Johan, plus he has a girlfriend who's also a Champion. (Lemon face)"

Die of sourness!

Johan walked toward the field with some small pride in his heart.

Entrance! Teacher Johan's Home Tutor Ultimate Outfit Form!

On the opposite side, Hassel, holding a sketchbook in one hand, also walked out of the tunnel seriously and came to the other side of the battle field.

Buzz buzz buzz—

A Rotom drone descended from the air.

Whoosh—

From Johan's pocket, his Rotom Pokédex shot out like lightning and directly kicked the Rotom drone away.

The audience's gaze followed the Rotom drone as it traced a beautiful arc through the air.

Everyone silently added question marks above their heads.

"This battle will be broadcast by this Rotom, Rotom!"

The Rotom Pokédex had become somewhat addicted to being a referee recently. The Rotom drone "working" in the Paldea region obviously wasn't networked with Galar region's Rotom drones, or that Rotom drone should have been prepared.

"Rigged! Rigged! Rigged!"

The audience who realized what happened started booing again.

Good grief, even the referee was truly one of their own—this was definitely rigged!

Of course, it was just booing for fun.

"Teacher Johan, I hope you won't hold back!" Hassel said seriously.

"Facing Mr. Hassel, I wouldn't dare," Johan smiled.

Although he was thinking about taking Hassel's points, with Ninetales and Tinkaton backing him up, the pressure of dealing with a Dragon-type Elite Four wasn't great.

But Johan didn't dare truly underestimate Hassel.

He wasn't clear about other things yet, but he knew what Hassel's ace Pokémon was.

The Paldea region's pseudo-legendary Pokémon, Baxcalibur!

Among ordinary Dragon-type Pokémon, the only Ice and Dragon-type Pokémon that didn't fear Ice-types.

If you really counted it... Baxcalibur could be considered the "Dragon Civil War King," comparable to Mega Altaria.

Johan could already feel his Dragonite becoming somewhat restless in its Poké Ball.

Facing Dragon-type Pokémon, Dragonite definitely couldn't contain itself, but Dragonite... was quadruple weak to Ice, completely countered.

Not just Dragonite.

Among his Pokémon, there was another little guy who'd been feeling quite stifled recently.

"Please prepare, both contestants!"

The Rotom Pokédex started broadcasting while taking out two flags, its voice spreading throughout the stadium.

The originally lively stadium gradually became quiet.

It was finally about to begin!

Larry sat in the front row, taking out a wrinkled bread package from his bag, tearing it open and eating with relish.

How would Hoenn League Champion Teacher Johan's strength differ from Paldea League Champion and President Ms. Geeta?

"Go, Flapple!"

On the field, Hassel threw out a Poké Ball. In the flashing red light, a Pokémon with a snake head protruding from a red apple appeared on the field.

"He's really serious now..." Larry took a bite of bread, his lazy expression becoming much more serious.

Larry understood Hassel quite well.

Usually, Hassel would send out Appletun. When he sent out Flapple, it meant he... was serious.

Johan threw out a Poké Ball at the same time.

"Please, Snivy!"

What appeared before Johan was an energetic Grass-type Pokémon, Snivy.

"Sni!"

Seeing Snivy, Hassel instinctively frowned.

He'd just said he wouldn't hold back—how could he send out Snivy, a Pokémon that... hadn't even reached its final evolution?

Audience members who knew Johan were already shouting.

"Oh no! Snivy? Teacher Johan is treating Elite Four Hassel like a 'baby'!"

Johan seemed to see Hassel's confusion and smiled slightly:

"Mr. Hassel, I believe the first principle of 'the way of teaching' is teaching without discrimination—not viewing any upright, kind student with goals and vision through colored glasses."

Hearing this, Hassel was stunned.

He looked at Snivy again.

This time, he clearly felt the intense battle desire emanating from Snivy—that was a Pokémon's pure longing for battle...

Such an attitude, regardless of form, strength, or condition, deserved respect.

Hassel suddenly understood. After seriously thinking for a moment, he bowed slightly to Johan and said sincerely:

"I've learned something, Teacher Johan."

----------

# 755: Teacher Johan is Truly Unfathomable! (Two-in-One Chapter)

Hoenn Champion Johan VS Paldea's First Elite Four Hassel!

As soon as this livestream title appeared, it immediately attracted a massive influx of viewers into the stream.

Recently, audiences following the "Pokémon World Championships" were truly having a blast.

They could constantly watch spectacular battles—Masters VS Masters, Elite Four VS Elite Four.

No wonder the "Pokémon World Championships" had such high attention and popularity, being called one of the most authoritative tournaments in the entire Pokémon world.

As audiences continued flooding into the livestream, the battle between Johan and Hassel had already begun.

"Flapple, Clangorous Soul!" Hassel commanded seriously, waving his hand to give the first order.

Thump thump! Thump thump! Thump thump—

Inspiring drum sounds emanated from Flapple's apple-like body, making everyone who heard it feel as if their hearts were beating along, blood flow accelerating, emotions becoming excited.

[Clangorous Soul: Raises the critical hit ratio of all allies except the user by 1 stage. When an ally has the Dragon type, critical hit ratio is raised by 2 stages!]

Flapple needed Applin to learn "Clangorous Soul" and then call other Applin together to complete its evolution.

This was a move very suitable for gregarious Dragon-type Pokémon, as it could greatly enhance all Dragon-type Pokémon's ability to hit opponents' vital spots and boost morale.

It was said that in ancient Dragon-type Pokémon communities, this move was the inspiring sound during Dragon Pokémon rituals.

For some Dragon-type Pokémon, this move might not be particularly important, but for Garchomp...

Garchomp: Who said Clangorous Soul isn't a dance!

However, although Clangorous Soul was a group buff, it didn't actually strengthen the user.

So many people were puzzled by Hassel having Flapple use "Clangorous Soul" first.

Could it be... Elite Four Hassel's Flapple's Clangorous Soul could strengthen Dragon-type Pokémon still in their Poké Balls?

But soon, the audience understood and couldn't help but exclaim in amazement.

As the inspiring drums continued beating, from Flapple's apple-like body, besides the largest original "worm" head, four smaller "worm" heads emerged in rhythm with the drums, flicking their tongues.

Additionally, behind Flapple, two tails also swayed gently to the beat of Clangorous Soul.

Although Flapple's "Clangorous Soul" didn't boost other Dragon-type Pokémon, Flapple itself had 7 "worms."

One provided the boost, while the other 6 "worms" received Clangorous Soul's enhancement.

This made the audience truly impressed.

Even Johan was somewhat stunned.

This was possible too?

But surprise aside, Johan wasn't going to do nothing. When Hassel commanded Flapple to use Clangorous Soul, Johan also gave his command.

"Snivy, Leaf Storm!"

Snivy with the "Contrary" ability couldn't use stat-boosting moves—those would only lower Snivy's stats.

For a "Contrary" Snivy, the best way to boost was... attack!

"Sni!"

A large number of emerald leaves spiraled around its tail, gathering into a leaf tornado that roared toward Flapple.

As a Grass/Dragon-type Pokémon, Flapple resisted Grass-type moves with 4× resistance. Plus Snivy was indeed lacking in strength, so while focused on Clangorous Soul, Flapple didn't dodge.

The roaring emerald leaves immediately swept over Flapple. The usually razor-sharp leaves could only scratch shallow marks on Flapple's waxy apple shell.

Although Snivy's Special Attack had greatly increased through the combination of "Contrary" and Leaf Storm, seeing such results still made the little one frown seriously.

The opponent was strong and heavily countered it!

"Flapple, Grav Apple!"

Grav Apple was essentially Flapple's signature move. When using this move, there was a chance its power would double directly!

Although this probability wasn't high, depending on the activity of the "worms" inside Flapple's body.

But obviously Hassel didn't want the move's power and effect to depend on randomness.

The "worms" might not be willing to come out?

Then use Clangorous Soul to make them excited!

This way, Grav Apple's power-doubling probability was greatly enhanced!

Additionally, Clangorous Soul made Dragon-type Pokémon excited, increasing their critical hit ability by 2 stages—equivalent to doubling.

Under these circumstances, Grav Apple's base power was 80, which became a terrifying 160 after doubling!

If it also hit a critical spot... it would be equivalent to taking a direct hit from a 320-power move!

Snivy might be instantly defeated!

"Hiss—"

Following Hassel's command, Flapple's "worms" immediately spat out five blue-purple energy beams in their excitement.

The power had doubled!

The audience immediately became excited.

Flapple had 4× resistance to Grass-type moves, but Snivy had no resistance to Dragon-type moves!

Were they about to witness Teacher Johan's Pokémon get one-shot for the first time?

Just thinking about it was thrilling...

Compared to the audience's excitement, Johan appeared much calmer. He waved his hand lightly and said gravely:

"Snivy, Protect!"

A translucent green barrier appeared from nowhere. Five spiraling energy beams struck the barrier, blue-purple light intertwining with green light, producing crisp sounds.

Crack!!

The Protect barrier shattered.

"Dodge." Johan frowned slightly.

"Sni!"

The moment the Protect barrier shattered, Snivy's vine whipped the ground, its body nimbly flying backward. Just as Grav Apple broke through the protective barrier but before it could attack, Snivy perfectly avoided the remaining impact.

This move's power was much greater than imagined.

After shattering the Protect barrier, it still had enough force left to attack Snivy. If Johan and Snivy hadn't been focused enough, they would have taken a hit regardless.

"Light Screen!" Johan quickly commanded again.

Mid-flight, Snivy waved its vines, and an invisible transparent wall appeared before it, capable of greatly reducing special attack moves from opponents.

But just this wouldn't be enough...

Hassel half-closed his eyes. "Flapple, once more—Grav Apple!"

Without Protect, just Light Screen couldn't stop the extremely powerful Grav Apple.

"Hiss—!!"

The five snake heads hissed again, blue-purple energy beams shot from their mouths, intertwining into a thick beam that rushed toward Snivy again.

"Create distance, Snivy—Sunny Day!"

Sunny Day?

All the audience present and watching the livestream were stunned.

Although everyone was clamoring about one-shotting Teacher Johan, they were actually thinking about what they would do if they were in Teacher Johan's position.

But no matter how they thought, they never expected Teacher Johan to have Snivy use Sunny Day!

Was he giving up?

Others might question Johan's choice, but Snivy wouldn't.

It immediately used its vines and agile body to keep retreating while using Sunny Day.

Hum—

The originally setting sunlight suddenly became bright and scorching.

But simultaneously, Flapple's Grav Apple broke through Light Screen as expected, about to reach Snivy.

Although Light Screen had reduced most of Grav Apple's power, even so, being hit would still be devastating for Snivy.

But just then, a red beam shot from behind Snivy.

Boom!!

Grav Apple hit the ground, immediately blasting a charred crater.

Switch!

At the crucial moment, Johan had recalled Snivy with its Poké Ball!

"Actually switching Pokémon? This is just the first one... doesn't match Teacher Johan's style!"

"So... the best way to handle this was switching Pokémon?"

"Elite Four Hassel truly lives up to his reputation—forcing Teacher Johan to switch Pokémon right from the start..."

It could only be said that Johan had left some deeply rooted impressions on people at certain times.

4× resistance to Grass-type, and Snivy was a pure Grass-type Pokémon, not to mention slightly inferior in strength—what kind of person wouldn't switch Pokémon in this situation?

Did they really think everyone was a type-disadvantage master?

Or that everyone could get fired up and defeat 4× resistant opponents through sheer determination?

Even getting fired up might not defeat 4× resistant opponents.

While the audience discussed the Pokémon switch, Hassel on the opposite side indicated he "understood."

"Teaching according to individual needs? Not looking down on any student for any reason, but also not blindly making them do extremely difficult or impossible things, then damaging their confidence afterward."

Snivy against Flapple had too obvious a type disadvantage.

So this kind of "teaching according to individual needs"...

Hassel had another "realization."

"You did very well, Snivy." After saying this gently to the Poké Ball, he put it back at his waist and took out another one.

Pokémon battles were a team effort. Each Pokémon doing their job well was already excellent enough.

Whoosh—

Johan threw his second Poké Ball.

"Go, Ceruledge!"

Clang—

The sword rang sharply, fire danced around its face. Bathed in ghostly blue flames, Ceruledge appeared on the field.

The strong sunlight in the air made the flames on Ceruledge burn even more vigorously.

So Snivy's Sunny Day was prepared for Ceruledge?

The audience suddenly understood.

"Let the right person do the right job..." Hassel muttered, then focused intently on the battle again. "Flapple, do you hear that inspiring music? Grav Apple!"

Dazzling light burst forth again.

The cost of switching Pokémon was often losing the initiative.

"Ceruledge!" Johan called out.

Hum—

Ceruledge's flame-wreathed blade cut through the space before it, disappearing into the shadows under black mist. Grav Apple was slightly blocked by the Light Screen Snivy had left behind, missing Ceruledge and hitting nothing.

Boom!!

So Snivy's Light Screen was also prepared for this move by Ceruledge?

"Watch your surroundings!" Hassel commanded gravely.

Ceruledge could appear on any side of the body at any time.

"Withdraw!"

"Hish hish—"

Flapple's "worms" quickly retreated into the apple shell, covered with thick wax layers that ordinary attacks could hardly break.

Especially after the "worms" withdrew, they would spit out acid liquid that could further strengthen the apple shell's defense.

Using slow against fast, like turtle versus snake—fighting for a counterattack opportunity.

Flapple's defensive ability wasn't weak to begin with.

Hum—

Space fluctuated on Flapple's left side.

"It's coming! Left side!"

It had to be said that Hassel was highly focused in this battle, immediately noticing the spatial anomaly.

But Johan was going for surprise.

Clang!!

When Flapple and Hassel focused their attention on the left side, a blade wreathed in deep purple flames struck from Flapple's right side.

On the right!

Wrong judgment.

But it didn't matter.

Flapple's defense plus Withdraw was still excellent.

Moreover, although Flapple was a Grass-type Pokémon, it didn't fear Fire-type moves.

Bang!!

The sharp blade struck the apple shell like hitting extremely solid metal. The waxy special structure on its surface made the apple shell extremely sturdy.

This was Flapple's counterattack moment!

"Grav Apple!"

Five "worm" heads quickly emerged again, blue-purple light flickering.

Johan's eyes glowed slightly blue as he waved his hand, his cape fluttering behind him.

"Bitter Blade!"

Crack!!

Flapple's apple shell suddenly made a crisp sound.

Hassel's eyes widened.

He could accept Ceruledge's attack not being blocked, but breaking through Flapple's apple shell so quickly?

It had even spat acid liquid beforehand and used Withdraw...

Suddenly, Hassel saw those shallow but dense scratches on Flapple's apple shell!

These scratches... seemed chaotic but several connected into lines that circled the apple shell!

Normally these scratches would heal quickly, but now the opportunity was seized... they became "grooves" for Ceruledge's blade to strike!

Hassel was shocked.

So... from when Snivy appeared and used Leaf Storm, Teacher Johan had been preparing and laying groundwork for this moment?

Snivy seemed no match for Flapple and had to be switched, but whether it was Leaf Storm, Light Screen, or Sunny Day, everything was preparing for Ceruledge's sword...

It had done everything it could and should do!

Was this Teacher Johan's "way of teaching"?

Each person, or rather each Pokémon, just needed to do what they could do well—that was enough...

Whoosh—

Deep purple flames like an arc, cutting in a circle, completely destroying Flapple's defense!

The heavy blow and unexpected sword forced Flapple's Grav Apple to be interrupted.

"Goka!"

Twin swords crossed.

"Flare Blitz!"

Clang!

Twin blade flash!

At such close distance, plus the broken defense and Sunny Day's boost...

Boom!!

Ceruledge bathed in deep purple fire, its form becoming a streak of light that flashed by.

Flapple's body immediately burst into roaring flames!

"Flapple is unable to battle!"

Whoosh!!

The entire venue erupted.

So fast?

The Flapple that had completely dominated Snivy was... two-shotted by Ceruledge?

The audience was shocked by Ceruledge's strength.

Only a few people like Hassel noticed how much Snivy had contributed to Ceruledge's clean victory.

It could only be said...

"Teacher Johan is really detailed!" Flint in the audience, like Hassel, was also a late realizer and couldn't help but exclaim.

Although that statement...

Cynthia, eating ice cream, couldn't help but glance at him.

But she had to admit that Johan had indeed been very detailed this time. She only guessed Johan would switch to Ceruledge after seeing Snivy use Sunny Day.

Otherwise, pure Sunny Day, while useful for Snivy, would equally benefit the opposing Flapple and couldn't play a decisive role.

Hiss—

Gengar opened a soda can, happily gulped some happy water, then let out a satisfied burp.

Burp~~

"Eh? Gengar, why are you here? Aren't you preparing to battle?" Flint couldn't help asking.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar waved its hand, then counted on its fingers.

It's not my turn!

It counted: Ninetales, Tinkaton, Dragonite, Ceruledge, Tyranitar, Snivy...

Everyone wanted to battle and all had better reasons than it did. There was no way it would get a turn.

"True..." Flint nodded.

On the field, Hassel recalled the defeated Flapple, looking at the battle-ready Ceruledge. After brief consideration, he took out another Poké Ball.

As soon as he took it out, the Poké Ball trembled slightly in his hand.

"Full of fighting spirit..." Hassel smiled slightly. "Then no one is more suitable than it!"

He threw the Poké Ball.

"Go, Haxorus!"

"Roar—!!!"

In the red light, a Haxorus wearing heavy armor with crimson fangs like giant axes, full of momentum and overflowing with battle desire, appeared on the field.

Those crimson eyes stared intently at Ceruledge, claws slowly grinding against its axe fangs, producing sharp, teeth-grinding sounds.

Hone Claws!

Pure Dragon-type Haxorus also had 2× resistance to Fire-type moves. How would Teacher Johan respond?

Johan's response...

"Ceruledge, Will-O-Wisp!"

Of course, let the momentum-riding Ceruledge continue fighting!

"Goka!"

Swinging its blade, several ghostly blue flames flew toward Haxorus.

"Haxorus, Outrage!"

"Roar!!"

Haxorus immediately spun and swung its tail, wrapped in blue-purple energy. Its thick tail "pop pop pop" scattered all the flames into sparks.

Seeing this, Johan couldn't help but want to complain.

In games, against some physical attack Pokémon, Will-O-Wisp could be called a "divine technique," along with similar moves like Thunder Wave.

But in the real world, when encountering anyone with decent strength, they could always use seemingly unreasonable but apparently reasonable methods to destroy these moves' effects.

This was the difference between the Pokémon world and games.

But fortunately, Johan hadn't planned on Will-O-Wisp breaking through Haxorus anyway.

Pop pop pop—

As ghostly blue sparks scattered like starlight, Ceruledge's form followed like a shadow right behind Will-O-Wisp, arriving before Haxorus.

Swinging its blade to strike upward.

"Haxorus!"

But Hassel seemed prepared for this too. As Ceruledge's blade struck along the scattered sparks toward Haxorus, Haxorus twisted its body at an exaggerated angle, meeting it with crimson axe fangs.

Clang!!

Axe fang and blade collided, immediately producing sharp metallic sounds, blade and axe grinding with teeth-grinding friction.

Hiss—

The blade suddenly burst with ghostly blue flames.

Haxorus wasn't to be outdone, its crimson axe fangs showing black malevolent light.

Bang!!

Blade and axe fang collided again.

Bang bang bang—

In an instant, sword and axe clashed dozens of times rapidly.

Both showed excellent mastery.

"Goka!"

"Roar!"

The two Pokémon contested in strength while their four eyes met with mutual respect.

Sword and axe fang trembled continuously.

But... Haxorus had an advantage over Ceruledge—its twin axes were teeth, so even while struggling, Haxorus's arms and claws could still move.

"Dragon Claw!"

Hassel made a dragon claw gesture with his hand, commanding gravely.

"Roar!!"

Haxorus in the struggle let out a low roar, its claws covered in rich Dragon-type energy, swiping out.

Thump—

Haxorus was very fast. Hassel's voice had just sounded when it had almost already struck, plus at such close distance...

Ceruledge was sent flying, its armor cracking, crashing to the ground with a "boom" and raising large clouds of dust.

"Haxorus, good opportunity—Assurance!"

"Roar!!"

Haxorus spread its arms and roared, then its entire body covered in black light, charging like a bulldozer toward where Ceruledge had crashed at extreme speed.

In the swirling dust, ghostly blue flames surged and danced.

When Haxorus charged into the smoke, thick smoke immediately spread.

Smokescreen!

Hiss—

The rolling, scorching black smoke made Haxorus feel burning sensations. The heat from Smokescreen quickly made Haxorus feel the scorching temperature.

Smokescreen sometimes caused opponents to become burned. The longer one stayed in the smoke, the higher the chance of becoming burned!

But in this situation, retreating clearly wasn't an option.

As long as Haxorus's Assurance hit Ceruledge, even if it couldn't one-shot it, it would leave it with minimal HP.

This was Hassel and Haxorus's choice.

"Press forward, Haxorus!"

Though reckless, this fighting style indeed suited a fierce Pokémon like Haxorus.

"Roar—"

In Haxorus's roar, it still gritted its teeth and charged forward.

Clang!!

Raised twin swords bore the direct impact of Haxorus's Assurance.

Ceruledge was sent flying again, its armor cracking once more, looking somewhat battered.

Johan's expression was grave but showed no panic or worry.

It was pushing the limit, but this hadn't exceeded his estimated range.

As for whether Ceruledge could withstand it...

Heh... was Bitter Blade learned for nothing?

Sizzle—

Burn marks appeared on Haxorus.

Staying too long had inevitably caused it to become burned!

But in Hassel's view, with Ceruledge's current stamina, even if Haxorus was burned, one touch could make the opponent unable to battle.

So...

"Haxorus, continue the assault—Dragon Claw!"

Haxorus, enduring the pain with a fierce expression, charged forward again.

But this time...

"It's our turn." Johan murmured quietly, then shouted: "Ceruledge!"

Whooom!!!

Vigorous flames continuously overflowed from Ceruledge's body and armor gaps. Strands of golden flames intertwined with ghostly blue flames, presenting a gorgeous purple-gold color.

"Whoa! Here it comes—Teacher Johan's unreasonable external 'Blaze' ability!" Flint smacked his lips.

Eyes burning with flames glanced at the charging Haxorus.

Whoosh—

Ceruledge suddenly disappeared from Hassel and Haxorus's sight!

So fast!

Seeing this, both Hassel and Haxorus were startled.

Swish! Swish!

Fire suddenly burst from behind Haxorus, followed by blade arcs.

Cross-striking blades covered Haxorus's back in flames, its stamina draining at a terrifying rate.

Bitter Blade!

With Bitter Blade plus Blaze, Ceruledge didn't need to worry about stamina at all.

This traded for the "Weak Armor" ability's speed boost, plus figuring out Haxorus's fighting style and habits, and causing it to become burned...

Enough!

Next, there was no turn for Haxorus!

"Haxorus, Outrage!" Unable to find Ceruledge, it would use area attacks for self-protection.

Haxorus's thick tail struck around itself, waiting for Ceruledge's charge.

But it only had one tail—there would always be gaps.

And seizing this gap... wasn't difficult for Ceruledge with doubled speed!

Swish swish—

Sword light appeared again, flames erupting on Haxorus once more.

"Roar—"

Haxorus cried out in pain.

Hassel knew that continuing like this, Haxorus would definitely fall, so he gritted his teeth and shouted: "Haxorus, Outrage!"

Berserk aura suddenly surged.

But Johan waved his cape.

His tone was calm: "End it, Ceruledge—Bitter Blade!"

As his words fell...

Swish—

The berserk aura surging from Haxorus suddenly stopped. Covered in wounds, it widened its eyes and fell straight backward.

"Goka..."

Ceruledge appeared before Haxorus, slowly sheathing its sword.

Sheathed.

It nodded slightly toward Haxorus.

Indeed a worthy opponent to respect.

After the battle, Ceruledge had recovered most of its stamina...

Not a drop of its HP bar belonged to itself!

Boom!!!

The entire venue erupted in enthusiastic cheers, sweeping over like a tsunami.

What kind of match was most exciting?

Critical HP comeback!

Perhaps in Johan's eyes, he knew Ceruledge wouldn't lose its fighting ability thanks to Bitter Blade, but in the audience's eyes, this was an extreme critical HP comeback.

"Holy crap! Teacher Johan is amazing!"

"I'd only heard Teacher Johan was powerful before—now I have a clear understanding."

"This counts as type disadvantage too, right? Type disadvantage and extreme critical HP comeback! My grandma asked why I was watching TV on my knees..."

"Teacher Johan: 4× disadvantage is too much, but 2× disadvantage? Easy peasy~"

If Haxorus hadn't had 2× resistance to Fire-type moves, the battle might have ended much earlier.

Hassel, realizing belatedly, said: "Understanding each student well enough, knowing where their limits and extremes are, then focusing on developing and cultivating them..."

As he reviewed the battle, he realized Johan had remained very calm throughout, commanding decisively with crisp counterattacks—clearly maintaining firm control of rhythm and limits.

No wonder everyone called him "Teacher Johan."

Teacher Johan was truly... unfathomable.

# 756: I Predict Your Prediction of My Prediction! (Two-in-One Chapter)

With Haxorus losing its fighting ability, Hassel was pushed to the brink.

Because the battle rules were 3v3 rotation format, and he had already lost two Pokémon.

But Johan's Snivy and Ceruledge were both in decent condition.

Although Snivy wasn't as strong as Ceruledge in raw power, it couldn't be ignored.

And while Ceruledge had been beaten to critical HP by Haxorus earlier, Bitter Blade plus Blaze had restored Ceruledge to a battleable state.

This situation... was very unfavorable for Hassel.

However, Hassel wasn't nervous—he even seemed somewhat joyful and excited.

He hadn't intended to definitely win this battle against Teacher Johan. As he'd said from the beginning, he only wanted to discuss the "way of teaching" with Teacher Johan.

From what he could see now, he'd gained much.

Of course, regardless, he would still give his all in this battle!

So after recalling Haxorus, Hassel unhesitatingly took out his final Poké Ball and threw it without hesitation.

"Go, Baxcalibur!"

Red light flashed as the Ultra Ball opened.

A bone-chilling aura immediately spread. In the frigid airflow, a Pokémon resembling a spiny dragon appeared on the field—covered in blue-gray scales, with snow-white ice crystal armor on its head, abdomen, knees, and elbows, and prominent massive ice crystal spines on its back.

Baxcalibur, an Ice/Dragon-type ice dragon Pokémon. It was said that the frigid breath from its mouth could instantly freeze even scorching lava into ice blocks. It was an extremely rare Pokémon living in extremely cold regions.

Baxcalibur was also the Paldea region's pseudo-legendary Pokémon!

Except for Ice Dragon Kyurem, it was the only Pokémon with the Ice/Dragon type combination—truly a "civil war phantom god"!

"Roar!!!"

The moment Baxcalibur appeared, it roared, and a terrifying chill centered on it spread rapidly in all directions.

The ground, walls, and even the air gradually showed hexagonal ice crystals as the entire stadium's temperature plummeted.

Logically, Ice-type Pokémon facing Fire-type Ceruledge should be at an absolute type disadvantage.

But Baxcalibur was different. Dragon-type had 2× resistance to Fire-type moves, and combined with Ice-type, it no longer feared Fire-type attacks.

That wasn't all.

What truly frustrated Fire-type Pokémon about Baxcalibur was its ability "Thermal Exchange"!

[Thermal Exchange: When a Pokémon with this ability is hit by a Fire-type move, its Attack increases by 1 stage and it cannot be burned!]

This was purely an ability targeting Fire-type Pokémon.

The more you hit it, the stronger it became!

And it didn't fear burn status, so its physical attack wouldn't be lowered.

Clearly built like a "civil war phantom god," it even beat up Fire-type Pokémon too.

So Baxcalibur didn't fear Ceruledge.

Additionally, its Ice typing allowed it to easily handle Snivy.

One move directly "sealed" Johan's two remaining Pokémon with decent fighting power.

"Come back, Ceruledge."

Johan was also decisive, directly taking out a Luxury Ball and recalling the still-decent Ceruledge.

Although Baxcalibur as a Dragon-type didn't fear Ice-type moves, its Fairy-type weakness still existed, and its Ice typing also made it vulnerable to Steel-type moves.

This way, Steel/Fairy-type Tinkaton would be Johan's excellent third Pokémon choice.

But just as he reached for the Poké Ball at his waist, one Luxury Ball shook excitedly.

Johan's movement paused, his expression showing some helplessness.

No, the opponent is Ice/Dragon-type...

Obviously, the shaking Luxury Ball contained Dragonite!

The "always striving" Dragonite became especially excited and battle-ready whenever it encountered Dragon-type Pokémon.

Especially when it sensed the momentum and strength of that Baxcalibur on the field.

But the problem was...

Baxcalibur wasn't called a "civil war phantom god" for nothing, especially since Dragonite was 4× weak to Ice...

If the opponent had 4× resistance, you could still fight, but being 4× weak to the opponent's type... one careless moment could result in being one-shotted.

Are you serious?

This was the ace Pokémon of the first Elite Four!

Comparing horizontally to Elite Four Drake's Salamence—after Mega Evolution, it reached Champion peak level.

With such type suppression, even legendary-tier might not win...

Rustle rustle—

The Luxury Ball shook twice more.

Feeling Dragonite's emotions, Johan helplessly held his forehead.

Gengar in the stands grinned—it knew the "predicament" Johan was in.

Gengar: You just spoil it!

Fine.

Johan sighed lightly and took out Dragonite's Luxury Ball.

"Whatever, worst case I'll forfeit these points!"

Muttering quietly, he threw the Luxury Ball.

"As you wish, Dragonite!"

"Draaa—"

In the red light, a large, round-bellied Dragonite flew out impatiently, its small wings flapping as it held its chubby fists, excitedly calling out.

"Dragonite?!"

Seeing Johan's final Pokémon was actually Dragonite, both the audience in the stands and those watching the livestream exclaimed in shock.

Who would dare summon Dragonite when facing Baxcalibur?

Although both were pseudo-legendary Dragon-type Pokémon, Dragonite was also Flying-type, making it 4× weak to Ice!

4× weak to Ice plus 2× weak to Dragon—this was even more pressure than Snivy facing Baxcalibur, right?

"Did Teacher Johan grab the wrong Poké Ball?"

"Or is Teacher Johan showing consideration and going easy?"

"Going easy? Summoning Dragonite against Baxcalibur—this is like letting the ocean out!"

"Is there a possibility that Teacher Johan... doesn't know about this Baxcalibur species? After all, this is Teacher Johan's first time in the Paldea region..."

Anyway, the audience couldn't understand.

Actually, not just the audience—even Flint, Cynthia, and Johan's opponent Hassel were puzzled.

Hassel frowned even more.

He could understand the earlier Snivy and Ceruledge, but this Dragonite...

Could Teacher Johan have some other meaning?

Johan: Heh, don't ask—I can't figure it out either.

But since it was already sent out, what else could he do?

Fight!

"Dragonite, Dragon Dance!"

Johan waved his hand broadly, taking the initiative for the first time in this battle.

No choice—the disadvantage was really huge this time. Johan, who usually preferred being passive, had to be proactive for once.

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite showed no fear facing Baxcalibur. After a long cry, airflow spiraled around its body as its momentum rapidly climbed.

Hassel temporarily stopped thinking about Teacher Johan's "intention" and decided to fight first and think later.

"Baxcalibur, Icicle Crash!"

"Roar!!"

Baxcalibur's body again spewed frigid air. Its claws lifted upward, and the cold air quickly condensed into a massive pale ice spike that appeared above Dragonite's head.

Whoosh—

The ice spike, wrapped in cold air, rapidly fell.

"Dodge, Dragonite! Use Extreme Speed!"

Hum—

Dragonite glowed white, its small wings flapping as its speed suddenly skyrocketed. Despite its seemingly chubby body, it displayed speed completely inconsistent with its appearance.

Bang!!

Baxcalibur's Icicle Crash missed.

Dragonite, spiraling in the air, charged straight at Baxcalibur.

"Dragon Rush!" Johan went with the flow.

Rich blue-purple energy surged out, forming a dragon cloak over Dragonite as it roared and bit toward Baxcalibur.

"Baxcalibur, we'll use Dragon Rush too!"

Whooom!!

Baxcalibur's body also surged with rich Dragon-type energy, quickly wrapped in a dragon cloak, directly facing the falling dragon with a roaring cry.

Boom—!!

In an instant, the two dragons—one above, one below—collided head-on, producing an enormous roar that shook the entire stadium.

"Draaa—"

Dragonite flapped its wings and flew backward rapidly, its expression excited.

While Baxcalibur's feet carved two deep furrows in the ground, sliding some distance before barely stabilizing.

Dragonite actually had the advantage?

With Dragon Dance's boost and Extreme Speed's speed enhancement, naturally Dragonite, attacking from above with the initiative, had the advantage.

Baxcalibur, defending passively and hastily, inevitably fell into slight disadvantage.

This plot development somewhat exceeded everyone's expectations.

In their view, with Baxcalibur's huge type advantage... shouldn't it be dominating?

But Hassel's furrowed brow soon relaxed, looking at Johan with some realization.

He'd fallen into Teacher Johan's rhythm...

From starting with Dragon Dance, forcing him to want to interrupt Dragonite, to the later Dragon Rush collision—this was completely falling into Teacher Johan's offensive rhythm!

Not enough type advantage, so rhythm compensation?

The higher the level of battle, the more important rhythm became.

But realizing this, Hassel immediately decided to change tactics and rhythm.

"Baxcalibur, Snow!"

Snow was a weather move, similar to Ninetales' Snowfall, changing the weather to snowy.

Under snowy conditions, Ice-type Pokémon's physical defense greatly increased, and the plummeting temperature could also affect Dragonite more severely.

"Roar!!"

The stabilized Baxcalibur roared again, cold air immediately surging from its body.

Johan half-closed his eyes.

Although his Dragonite often battled under Ninetales' snowy weather and had resistance to snow weather and extremely low temperatures no weaker than Cynthia's Garchomp...

But letting Baxcalibur initiate snow weather would make Dragonite's already very unfavorable situation even more severe.

So...

"Dragonite, Extreme Speed!"

"Draaa—"

Dragonite again became a white streak, rapidly approaching Baxcalibur. This time without Dragon Rush, so its speed reached maximum.

"Now! Baxcalibur, Glaive Rush!" Hassel shouted loudly.

Baxcalibur immediately stopped its Snow move and turned its back toward the charging Dragonite!

This is what I was waiting for—you trying to interrupt the move!

Baxcalibur exhaled frosty breath, its sharp massive back spines immediately covered in Dragon-type energy, like a giant sword's edge, advancing instead of retreating toward Dragonite.

Glaive Rush, Baxcalibur's signature Dragon-type move with a massive 120 base power—an extremely strong Dragon-type attack.

But in the Pokédex, Glaive Rush was described as a reckless, self-sacrificing attack.

Because while Glaive Rush had high power, it completely exposed the back to the opponent. The result was that after the attack ended, the opponent's next move had greatly increased accuracy and doubled power!

In other words, this was a move that, if it didn't KO the opponent, would definitely result in being relentlessly attacked—a typical double-edged sword.

And Johan really wanted to say at this moment...

I was waiting for exactly this move!

"Dragonite, block it!"

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite's form suddenly stopped, spreading its arms to directly receive the terrifying impact from Baxcalibur's back spines.

Boom!!

The massive impact and terrifying Dragon-type energy, the sharp and hard back spines and the scales on Dragonite's palms produced extremely harsh friction sounds.

Although the scales on Dragonite's palms kept cracking, Dragonite actually withstood Baxcalibur's Glaive Rush!

Because of Multiscale!

In the earlier Dragon Rush collision, Dragonite had the absolute advantage, so it hadn't suffered much stamina loss, preserving Multiscale's damage-halving effect.

This was why Johan said he was waiting for Baxcalibur's Glaive Rush.

"It blocked it?!"

"It's Multiscale!"

"Teacher Johan is still so devious—seemingly trying to interrupt Snow but actually frantically baiting, waiting for exactly this Glaive Rush from Baxcalibur!"

"Who said Teacher Johan didn't know about Baxcalibur? Stand up!"

"Elite Four Hassel is in danger!"

Danger?

"Dragonite, grab Baxcalibur—Dragon Rush!"

Feeling the impact from the back spines weaken, Dragonite's expression changed as it firmly grasped the spines, blue-purple energy surging over its body again.

Glaive Rush's side effect doubled the opponent's move power, plus Dragon-type's super effectiveness—this move would KO Baxcalibur, right?

"Draaa—"

Dragonite embraced Baxcalibur and rapidly climbed, its dragon cloak growing larger and larger.

Upon reaching sufficient height, it flapped its wings and plummeted like a meteor toward the ground.

Witnessing this, Hassel immediately took out a black orb and threw it forcefully.

Boom!!!

The meteor-like impact raised massive clouds of dust.

Crack crack—

The ground and something crisp continuously cracked and spread.

The entire venue fell silent.

Was it a KO?

Just as all the audience were still guessing whether Baxcalibur had been KO'd...

Hassel waved and shouted: "Baxcalibur, Icicle Crash!"

What?

Baxcalibur hadn't lost its fighting ability?

Boom!!

The surging cold air blew away the surrounding dust. Seeing the scene, the audience's pupils contracted.

They saw Hassel's Baxcalibur now completely covered in transparent ice crystals, its body seeming entirely made of transparent crystal, with a bright Ice-type symbol on its head.

Terastallization!

At the crucial moment, Elite Four Hassel had Baxcalibur Terastallize, and its Tera type was Ice!

Tera Ice meant that while Dragonite's Dragon Rush still had doubled power, it lost the 2× type effectiveness against Dragon-types!

So Baxcalibur withstood this Dragon Rush attack.

And Baxcalibur in turn firmly grasped the extremely fast Dragonite, frost continuously spreading from its body to Dragonite while a massive ice spike rapidly formed above their heads!

"Holy crap! So Elite Four Hassel was waiting for this move!"

"4× weak to Ice Dragonite, having lost Multiscale, plus Terastallization's boost—if this hits... it'll be a KO, right?"

"The situation instantly reversed? This is Terastallization?"

After Pokémon Terastallize, if it's an originally possessed type, moves get STAB boost increased from the original 1.5× to 2×!

Plus 4× weakness to Ice... this Icicle Crash would definitely be too much for Dragonite!

"Heh." Johan smiled.

Worthy of the Paldea region, worthy of Terastallization.

Sometimes it was just this unreasonably "game-changing."

But Terastallization... who doesn't have it?

Johan flicked his wrist, and a black orb filled with energy appeared in his hand.

Good thing he recharged his Tera Orb as soon as he arrived in the Paldea region.

"Dragonite!" Johan shouted loudly, throwing the Tera Orb to land on Dragonite's head as it was held by Baxcalibur.

Crack! Crack crack—

Crystal built up, then quickly crumbled like dust. Dragonite's body was immediately covered in transparent crystal like Baxcalibur, with a Dragon-type crystal symbol appearing on its head.

Tera Dragon!

"When I get the chance, I definitely need to change Dragonite's Tera type to Normal," he thought.

Boom!!

Icicle Crash struck down.

Countless ice crystals and fragments scattered.

"Teacher Johan has Terastallization too?!"

"Tera Dragon? Isn't it still weak to Ice-type?"

"Weak is weak, but it's not 4× weak anymore!"

"Holy crap, these two are hiding deeper than each other, predicting and calculating one after another—is this what Pokémon world teachers are like? Too complex, too complex!"

"I predict your prediction, I predict your prediction of my prediction—too deep, too deep!"

Reversal, reversal, constant reversals.

Every time the audience thought it was ending, Teacher Johan and Elite Four Hassel would pull out something new.

The two kept exchanging trump cards, seeing who calculated further, who hid deeper.

From the current results... forget it.

The audience no longer dared make casual conclusions.

Who knew what would happen next?

But this kind of battle was truly exciting.

Mutual gaming, mutual scheming.

One undeniable point was that Terastallization's appearance had indeed made tactical gaming in battles more varied and exciting.

This time both had Terastallized into their Pokémon's original types.

If they Terastallized into other types, it would undoubtedly be even more complex and variable.

Icicle Crash hit Dragonite, dealing heavy damage, but as some audience members said, with pure Dragon-type, at least it wasn't 4× weak to Ice—2× damage was still within Dragonite's tolerance!

Bang!!

Ice fragments scattered.

"Draaa!!"

Terastallized Dragonite suddenly raised its head, looking at Baxcalibur firmly grasping it, and grinned.

"Outrage!" came Johan's voice from behind.

Boom!!

In Baxcalibur's eyes, Dragonite's originally gentle smile immediately became fierce, and Baxcalibur felt immense pressure.

Dragonite's aura skyrocketed in a violent manner, again grasping Baxcalibur in return.

Oh no!

"Baxcalibur, Ice Fang!" Hassel immediately shouted.

However, it was too late.

After entering "Outrage state," Dragonite directly punched upward from below, hitting Baxcalibur hard under the chin.

Then it clenched its fist and gathered power.

A faint blue light flashed in Johan's eyes.

"Focus Punch!"

Outrage + Focus Punch!

This punch carried thirty years of power!

The fist wrapped in rich dark red airflow, the chubby arm actually showed muscle definition under its thick scales.

Bang!!

Baxcalibur's form shot backward like a cannonball, fine cracks appearing in its ice crystal armor under the crystal covering, crumbling into ice fragments.

Whoosh—

The pained Baxcalibur hadn't lost its fighting ability, but as it opened its eyes mid-flight, it showed a shocked expression.

The Dragonite that had punched it was now wrapped in green and red intertwining airflow, appearing beside it mid-flight!

What speed was this?!

Johan breathed out slowly, his tone calm:

"The final one—Dragon Ascent!"

Whoosh—

A streak of green and red light flashed past Baxcalibur's side.

Bang!!

The already flying Baxcalibur crashed hard into the stadium wall like a cannonball.

The entire venue fell silent.

That Teacher Johan's Dragonite could use moves while in "Outrage state" wasn't a secret.

In fact, not only Teacher Johan's Dragonite could do this—after the "Pokémon World Championships" began, various talented individuals always appeared.

But this time, the 4× Ice-weak Dragonite had actually defeated Ice Dragon Baxcalibur!

This battle was truly spectacular!

Both sides gamed against each other, their mutual tactical exchanges were quite educational.

Especially Terastallization.

Both used Terastallization excellently, playing situation-reversing roles.

Elite Four Hassel's Baxcalibur's Tera Ice neutralized Dragonite's doubled-power Dragon Rush.

Teacher Johan's Dragonite's Tera Dragon weakened Baxcalibur's fatal Icicle Crash.

Overall... Teacher Johan was still superior.

But Elite Four Hassel also performed excellently. Although he lost despite 4× type advantage, anyone could see it was just one move short, truly worthy of the first Elite Four title.

After all, his opponent was a Champion!

Clap clap clap—

The audience who came to their senses gave enthusiastic applause.

Rotom Pokédex also excitedly announced Johan's victory at the end.

Johan was actually curious—if he had lost, how would little Rotom announce the result?

Would it say something shocking?

Too bad he couldn't see it this time.

It would probably be difficult in the future too.

Hassel recalled the defeated Baxcalibur and walked to the center of the battle field to shake hands with Johan.

His face showed no disappointment, instead his eyes held some enlightenment as he smiled and extended his hand to Johan.

"I understand now, Teacher Johan. The final Dragonite—what you actually wanted to express was the 'joy of teaching,' right?"

Huh?

Hearing Hassel's words, Johan silently added a question mark above his head.

But his face showed a smile as he said: "Showing off before an expert—you flatter me, Teacher Hassel."

Hassel shook his head.

"On the surface, a 4× Ice-weak Dragonite at the same strength level couldn't possibly defeat Ice Dragon Baxcalibur, but Teacher Johan, through your commanding and Dragonite's performance, you reversed what many would consider a predetermined result..."

After pausing, his eyes lit up: "This ability to help students achieve the impossible through a teacher's skill, turning failure into success, letting every student exceed their potential... this is the 'joy of teaching,' right?"

Believing in your students, then working together to make the impossible possible—this was success for both teacher and student, and also the teacher's most worthwhile joy and happiness.

This was the "way of teaching" Hassel learned from that final battle.

Huh?

Johan was confused inside.

Is this what I was trying to say?

Wasn't it about having a troublesome kid at home, wanting to teach it a "lesson," but accidentally succeeding?

"Cough." Johan cleared his throat.

For a moment, he didn't know what to say.

Whatever you think it is, that's what it is...

"Thank you for your 'guidance,' Teacher Johan." Hassel bowed seriously, expressing his gratitude.

"I'm no longer confused—I know what I should do!"

Between his family and his teaching position at Orange Academy, he indeed felt the teaching profession and life were more precious!

"Good, good." Johan nodded.

He wondered if Hassel's Dragon Tamer family would come looking for trouble...

After a few more words, Hassel apologized and left, his steps steady and eyes determined.

His visit to Medali Town to find Rica and Alex was also to "escape."

Alex was like him—an artist, and Rica was like him—a teacher. He wanted to see if he could find answers from them.

That's why he'd gone out with Alex to seek inspiration.

He hadn't expected to find the answer from Teacher Johan.

Principal, Mr. Shaga, and Lance—they hadn't deceived him!

Johan held his forehead.

People with complex thoughts were truly "terrifying"...

"Wait."

How many points did he get from Elite Four Hassel this time?

"Repairing the stadium is another troublesome matter..."

Larry in the audience scratched his head in distress.

But recalling Johan and Hassel's battle, after reviewing it once, he nodded and muttered:

"Well... such a spectacular match was worth it, I guess."

He rustled as he packed his bread wrapper back into his briefcase, patted the dust off his pants, straightened his wrinkled suit, and got up to go eat at "Treasure Eatery."

Finally, he could eat!

Larry's actions still didn't attract surrounding attention.

As a fellow Elite Four member, Larry seemed to have a natural "invisibility" attribute.

But just as Larry turned around...

He saw a pancake-like black face, crimson eyes, and a mischievous smile.

Larry: !!

"Ka-kaah!"

Mister, you dropped something.

Gengar held out a small badge—Larry's work ID, which must have fallen when he was packing his bread wrapper.

"Oh, oh, thank you, thank you." Larry scratched his head and took the ID badge gratefully.

"Ka-kaah~" Gengar waved dismissively.

Larry turned and left.

Gengar watched his departing figure, scratched its head, and suddenly realized something.

Eh?

That mister seemed somewhat familiar just now?

It seemed like...

"What's wrong, Gengar?"

Cynthia noticed Gengar floating back with a thoughtful expression.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar pointed toward where Larry had left.

There was a mister named Larry just now...

Halfway through, Gengar suddenly remembered.

"Ka-kaah!" It punched its palm.

I remember! It's a wild Elite Four member!

"A wild Elite Four member?" Cynthia looked puzzled.

"A wild Elite Four member?! Where? Where?" Flint jumped up from his seat.

One Medali Town was actually hiding another Elite Four member?

Having just watched Teacher Johan battle Elite Four Hassel, Flint felt he also needed an exhilarating battle!

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar pointed with its hand.

Flint immediately gave chase.

Wild Elite Four member, don't run!
