# 757: Treasures of Ruin!

"I just want to eat..."

Looking at the fiery red explosive hair blocking his path, leaning in close with an intense stare, Larry's wooden face showed helplessness.

Why did it seem like he couldn't get his meal?

"Ah!"

Flint suddenly shouted, staring at Larry: "I remember now! You're... the Paldea League Elite Four, Hazel!"

Larry's mouth twitched as he gripped his briefcase tighter, saying flatly:

"Sorry, you've got the wrong person."

After speaking, he sidestepped around Flint and continued toward "Treasure Eatery."

"Eh?" Flint scratched his explosive hair, looking puzzled. "Wrong person?"

Larry gradually quickened his pace!

He wasn't lying, because his name was "Larry," and Flint had just said "Hazel"—that was definitely the wrong person, right?

And... quick escape!

This guy clearly didn't look very bright.

People like this were either not very smart but might burst with unexpected enthusiasm in battle, or... just simply not very smart.

But either way, battling such people might be "contagious"!

"What, didn't find him?"

At this moment, Johan, Cynthia, Caitlin and others walked over. Seeing Flint standing there constantly scratching his head, Johan asked.

Honestly, he was quite looking forward to seeing what would happen when Flint, that ball of fire, battled Larry, that block of ice.

"No." Flint shook his head. "That mister just said I had the wrong person. Where would Hazel go?"

"Idiot! The Paldea League Elite Four is called 'Larry,' not 'Hazel'!" Caitlin couldn't help but scold.

"Eh?" Flint was stunned.

Cynthia brushed her hair aside and said softly: "You really shouldn't go out representing the Sinnoh League Elite Four title in the future..."

They'd think everyone in the Sinnoh League was an idiot.

"Does he look like this?" Johan took out his phone—the League's official website had photos of Paldea League Elite Four Larry.

"Yes! That's the mister!" Flint immediately jumped up. "And he said I had the wrong person?"

Caitlin rolled her eyes unsympathetically. "You called him 'Hazel,' of course he'd say you had the wrong person."

"That makes sense..."

Everyone unanimously rolled their eyes.

They finally understood why Lucian, despite being so busy, was willing to let fellow Elite Four Flint wander around outside.

"I'll go find him right now!" Flint was eager to give chase.

Johan stopped him.

"At this hour, he's definitely off work eating dinner. You think everyone's like you, already finished eating? Go later or tomorrow."

That made sense... Flint looked dejected but could only accept this reality.

"I'll squat at the eatery entrance for him!"

Thinking of this, Flint perked up again, stuck his hands in his pockets, and walked toward "Treasure Eatery" with a swagger.

Everyone was speechless again.

"I bet ten thousand Pokédollars he won't catch him," Johan said.

If Larry knew Flint was squatting for him at the eatery entrance, he definitely wouldn't use the front door.

With his relationship with the "Treasure Eatery" owner, using the back door wouldn't be difficult.

But unfortunately, no one took his bet.

After dusk, night fell with the full moon hanging high in the black curtain.

Johan sat on the deck of the "Brave Olivine," mimicking Old Man Ludlow by fishing in the air, experiencing "solitude."

Friede had gone to Rica and Alex's house. They had invited Johan and the others too, but considering there were so many of them, they might not all fit, so they politely declined.

Flint was still squatting for Larry at the "Treasure Eatery" entrance.

Cynthia had been pulled by Caitlin to the upper deck, supposedly to admire the moon.

This left Johan "alone" sitting on the bow deck.

Of course, true solitude was impossible—at least he had his little ones keeping him company.

Gengar, Dragonite, and Urshifu playing Pokémon poker, Tinkaton and Ceruledge forging and being forged, Tyranitar eating, Ninetales and Snivy chatting...

"Sigh..."

Johan sighed softly.

"Oowoo..."

Thoughtful Ninetales wrapped one of its tails around his arm.

"I'm fine." Johan's heart warmed.

"Oowoo!"

Ninetales' tail pulled again, gesturing for Johan to look north.

Hmm?

Following Ninetales' direction, Johan was stunned.

A League helicopter was flying toward their location.

In the middle of the night...

Johan put away his fishing rod and got off the Brave Olivine.

Soon the helicopter landed on the grass beside the Brave Olivine.

A woman in an elegant black tailored suit with long black hair, wearing blue gloves, tall and graceful with a gentle smile, got off the helicopter.

Seeing this outfit and appearance, Johan immediately recognized her.

"Teacher Johan, I'm sorry for visiting so late."

"No, we should have visited you instead, Chairman Geeta," Johan said.

The visitor was clearly the Chairman and Champion of the Paldea League, Geeta!

Actually, Johan had planned to visit Geeta tomorrow.

After all, this was their first time in the Paldea region, and as Champions of two regions, he and Cynthia should visit the Paldea League's Chairman and Champion.

He hadn't expected Geeta to come looking for them first.

Geeta smiled slightly. "I happened to see your battle with Mr. Hassel online."

"I called Hassel, and he spoke very highly of you, so I couldn't contain myself and came over while it wasn't too late."

Speaking of this, Johan was actually quite annoyed.

Hassel, a dignified first Elite Four of the Paldea League, had given him fewer points than beating Caitlin!

Clearly the Paldea region was one of the "Pokémon World Championships" competition areas, clearly Hassel was also an Elite Four, even the first Elite Four, yet he had so little presence in the rankings!

This really "angered" Johan.

Because after the battle, calculating the points, he was still damn well at the bottom of "Master Ball Tier."

This was "bullying" honest people.

But thinking about it later, Hassel had probably been "tormented" by that Dragon Tamer from his family recently, with no time to farm points.

Moreover, Hassel's personality didn't seem like someone who would accept any opponent just to climb ranks.

The facts told Johan: next time before battling someone, he must check the League website for rankings and their points.

At this time, Cynthia and Caitlin, who had also noticed the helicopter, came down from the Brave Olivine.

They briefly greeted Geeta.

Then, at Johan's invitation, they boarded the Brave Olivine and went to the cabin's living room.

Johan made coffee for Geeta and poured juice for Cynthia and Caitlin. Gengar wanted to show off its tea-making skills, even bringing out Sinistcha and Poltchageist.

However, as a Paldean, how could Geeta not know about Sinistcha and Poltchageist?

No matter how good Gengar's tea was, Geeta wouldn't dare drink it.

Not to mention, Gengar was brewing Energypowder tea.

Finally, the little fatty could only sit aside sipping tea alone, ears drooping as it enjoyed its own "solitude."

"Actually, I came to find you because there's something I'd like your help with," Geeta said directly after sipping her coffee.

Johan gave Cynthia a look, then asked warily:

"What kind of matter?"

If Geeta mentioned anything about Area Zero or North Province, he'd immediately show her out.

This trip to the Paldea region was meant to be tourist-like point farming—gaining points while seeing if he could collect those extremely rare "Herba Mystica."

If he could see ancient and future forms of Pokémon, even better. He didn't want to mindlessly charge into the Great Crater of Paldea, Area Zero, or North Province.

"It's like this..."

Geeta took out a piece of decayed wood with extremely obscure and ancient writing carved on it.

The moment Cynthia saw this piece of wood, her eyes immediately lit up.

As an excellent mythology scholar, such a historically rich item with ancient writing held enormous appeal for her.

"This was accidentally dug up by a farmer while tilling his field. I'm not even sure if it's wood, because pure wood couldn't possibly be preserved for such a long time without any protection."

Cynthia nodded in agreement.

"At first the farmer didn't care, but gradually he became wrong—his temper became violent, becoming... filled with hostility and even hatred toward everyone."

Hatred?

Johan and Cynthia looked at each other, both frowning simultaneously.

"If it were just that, this matter wouldn't really be my concern. But after that, everyone in that village gradually became like that farmer, and even the surrounding wild Pokémon were all the same..."

This was becoming like a Pokémon world version of a "cursed village story"?

Why did it sound so creepy?

Geeta continued: "We suspect the problem lies with this piece of wood, but... no matter how hard our experts try, they can't decipher the content..."

Johan took the wood piece and glanced at it briefly, his expression becoming somewhat strange.

Why did this writing look somewhat familiar?

"Miss Cynthia is a famous mythology scholar, and I heard Teacher Johan also has some understanding of ancient writing and is a psychologist—perhaps you could help us find the cause."

Geeta continued with some helplessness:

"So even though it's nighttime and very rude, I came calling."

"This matter..."

Before Johan could finish, Cynthia reached under the table and gently squeezed Johan's hand.

Understanding Cynthia, Johan knew his little Cynthia... was interested.

Cynthia really had little resistance to various historical matters.

"This matter... is quite tricky." Johan no longer firmly refused.

Since Cynthia wanted to accept, he naturally couldn't refuse. But even if accepting, shouldn't he get some "benefits"?

"Indeed." Geeta nodded. "If you two could help our Paldea solve this trouble, the Paldea League would certainly reward you generously..."

Look at that!

This was a shrewd person!

Worthy of someone who could become Champion while also serving as League Chairman.

Just this emotional intelligence and "understanding" was enough to shoulder the League Chairman position.

But Johan immediately raised his hand to stop Geeta's words.

Since she was so understanding, he couldn't appear "clueless," right?

So he said: "We're all League members. Although we're Champions of Hoenn and Sinnoh, Paldea is also part of the League—we couldn't just stand by. I just have one question for Chairman Geeta."

"Please ask, Teacher Johan."

"I wonder if Chairman Geeta knows about 'Herba Mystica'?"

Geeta was stunned.

"'Herba Mystica'? Teacher Johan knows cooking?"

"A little, just a little."

Geeta nodded, pondering briefly before saying: "In that case, I'll send the Paldea League's collected information about 'Herba Mystica' locations and data to your phone."

"Then thank you, Chairman Geeta."

"Then I'll leave this matter to you both," Geeta said.

Both smiled and shook hands.

"I'm still looking forward to facing Teacher Johan in the 'Pokémon World Championships,'" Geeta added.

Johan's expression fell as he said helplessly: "I'm not very hopeful about that."

After more pleasantries, Geeta left behind the wood piece and the data they'd collected about the wood piece, the farmer, and that village.

Cynthia immediately picked up the files to read.

Johan held his coffee cup, sitting aside and happily taking a few sips.

Caitlin frowned puzzledly: "That's not right. With your personality, why didn't you take the 'benefits' Chairman Geeta offered?"

"Not take them?"

Johan raised an eyebrow with a "you don't understand" expression. "What do you mean not take? Did I say I wouldn't take them?"

"But you just..."

Cynthia, still reading files, smiled: "He skipped that topic, but Chairman Geeta showed her interpersonal style, so as long as we complete this commission, the reward will still be there."

Caitlin's eyes widened. Looking at the quietly smiling Johan, she couldn't help muttering:

"Schemer!"

"But I'm more curious—what is 'Herba Mystica' that made you actively ask for it?" Cynthia kept her head down but her tone was full of curiosity.

"Cough cough, what do you mean 'ask for'?"

Johan choked on his coffee but still said: "These are special seasonings that make food more delicious—truly rare treasures."

"I see..."

Even in cooking, Caitlin admired Johan.

No helping it—this guy was truly a chef!

"When we find them, I'll make you a feast!" Johan smiled.

"Ka-kaah!!"

Gengar, who had been pretending to be dejected, immediately perked up and was first to voice agreement.

Even Caitlin's eyes showed some anticipation.

After a brief silence, Cynthia frowned and placed the wood piece before Johan. "I've never seen this kind of writing. Do you recognize it, Johan?"

Johan glanced at the wood piece.

The writing did give him a familiar feeling, but looking carefully, he found it different.

It wasn't the writing he knew.

Johan sighed inwardly but still said: "I don't recognize it either."

After pausing, he immediately continued: "However, I roughly know where this wood piece—more accurately, this 'bamboo piece'—comes from."

"Bamboo piece?"

Cynthia was stunned and picked up the bamboo piece to examine it.

Due to time's "erosion," it was hard to distinguish whether this was wood or bamboo with the naked eye.

"But bamboo being preserved until now would be strange..."

"Because it's a treasure," Johan said.

"A treasure?"

Sitting up slightly, Johan said: "Legend has it that during the Paldean Empire era, someone brought four treasures from the distant East: a vessel, sword, tablets, and beads, which were eventually sold to the king."

"Vessel, sword, tablets, and beads?" Caitlin looked puzzled.

"The distant East?" The more sensitive Cynthia vaguely felt Johan had some strange feelings about these words.

Johan didn't explain but continued: "But these four treasures were actually four Pokémon that had long wandered various places, absorbing many desires and curses. After reaching the king, they triggered various desires deep in the king's heart."

"Then the king destroyed the empire himself?" Caitlin interjected.

Johan rolled his eyes unsympathetically.

"This isn't your Unova region—why would all kings be that stupid?"

Despite having the Black and White dragons, they were destroyed by the dragon conflict. That Unova king clearly didn't understand balance or royal governance.

"You!"

"Don't interrupt me!" Johan ignored her directly. "Later, the king found powerful Pokémon tamers who used 8 stakes each to seal them in four places throughout the Paldea region."

"So..." Cynthia spoke: "You're saying this bamboo piece actually comes from the tablets among those four treasures?"

"Most likely." Johan nodded.

"And those four Pokémon are called the 'Treasures of Ruin'!"

Cynthia and Caitlin repeated "Treasures of Ruin."

"Legendary Pokémon?" Caitlin asked for confirmation.

Johan neither confirmed nor denied, but his expression already gave the answer.

Taking another sip of coffee, he said calmly: "But don't be nervous. They're not legendary Pokémon on the level of Rayquaza, Giratina, or the Black and White dragons. If I had to compare... about the same as the three legendary birds."

The three legendary birds?

Both breathed slight sighs of relief.

With their group—two Champions and two Elite Four—even if all three legendary birds gathered, they wouldn't need to worry.

Even legendary first-tier gods might not be unbattleable.

"But there's also a problem," Johan added.

"Hmm?"

Both looked at him.

"I suspect when that king sealed the four 'Treasures of Ruin,' he sealed them together." Johan put down his coffee cup and gently rubbed the rim.

"What does that mean?" Caitlin frowned.

Can't you just say everything at once instead of acting mysterious?

But Cynthia understood Johan's meaning and also frowned: "Meaning if one seal is broken, the other three seals will gradually loosen until completely unsealed."

"My little Cynthia is so smart," Johan smiled.

Caitlin: [○｀Д○]

"And there's another problem," Johan continued.

"To solve the problem, we'd probably have to break the seal, then defeat that 'Treasures of Ruin' Pokémon, but how do we contain it afterward? Where do we put it?" Cynthia continued.

Caitlin sulked on the side.

Gengar quietly moved over a cup of brewed Sinistcha tea, eyes showing anxiety and curiosity.

The others didn't pay much attention to Gengar's actions.

In thought, Johan and Cynthia looked at each other and very synchronously spoke at the same time.

"Professor Oak's lab!"

"Your island!"

Johan: "?"

Caitlin was amused and unconsciously picked up the Sinistcha tea.

"Pfft—"

But quickly spat it out.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar shrieked.

Gotta run, gotta run~

"Professor Oak's lab?" Cynthia looked puzzled. "Why think of Professor Oak?"

Because his stamina was maxed out.

Never mind one "Treasures of Ruin"—even if all were sent there, the old man would definitely suffer but be happy about it.

But Johan didn't explain, instead asking back:

"Why send them to my island?"

Cynthia blinked her crystalline eyes cutely: "Because... I believe you can subdue it or them."

"They're all Pokémon influenced by negative emotions—their nature must not be bad. Isn't this... your specialty?"

Johan opened his mouth, moving his lips.

For a moment, he didn't know how to refute.

But his mind unconsciously conjured images of his Gengar leading four "Treasures of Ruin" Pokémon, calling themselves the "Outlaw Gang," running wild...

He shuddered involuntarily.

No way!

This wouldn't do.

Under his Gengar's "leadership"... wouldn't that cause huge trouble?

Sure enough, sending them to Professor Oak was most appropriate!

No choice—he'd have to trouble the old man.

At worst... Johan could ghostwrite a letter to Elite Four Agatha as "compensation"!

Decided happily!

Now that they knew about the "Treasures of Ruin" Pokémon, the investigation would undoubtedly be much simpler.

But compared to the "Treasures of Ruin" Pokémon whose outcome was already known, Cynthia was more curious about that history.

Even Geeta, the Paldea League Chairman and Champion, didn't know—how did Johan know?

But thinking of how Johan always knew some unknown stories—probably his legendary Pokémon friends told him?

Cynthia didn't probe deeper.

Everyone had their secrets.

"Gengar!! Stop right there!" came Caitlin's wailing from the Brave Olivine.

"Ka-kaah~"

Nyah nyah nyah~ Can't catch me, hehe, can't catch me~~

Meanwhile, Flint was still keeping watch at the "Treasure Eatery" entrance.

# 758: Gengar: Don't You Know How to Open Doors?

Early the next morning.

"Eh? South Province Area One?"

When Friede returned to the Brave Olivine and learned of Johan's group's destination, he showed some surprise.

Because his teacher Rica's home was now in Cabo Poco, and South Province Area One was where the Poco Path was located—the route from Cabo Poco to Mesagoza.

He had originally thought Johan's group would continue playing in Medali Town for a few more days, and if they wanted to continue afterward, he would first take his teacher and Alex back to Cabo Poco.

And see Liko while he was at it.

He hadn't expected Johan's group to also be heading to South Province Area One.

But the problem was, his teacher Rica and Mr. Alex still had some unfinished business...

So Johan said:

"No problem, we can go by ourselves."

Since they were already in the Paldea region, traveling within Paldea didn't require constantly using the Brave Olivine.

"We can take a Flying Taxi," Johan said.

"Will it fit everyone?" Friede looked hesitant.

Johan smiled. "Flint and the others aren't coming."

Speaking of Flint... after waiting all night at the "Treasure Eatery" entrance, he had completely locked horns with Medali Town's Larry and decided to stay in Medali Town for a while, also taking the opportunity to climb some ranks.

After all, as a famous "food city" in the Paldea region, Medali Town attracted many trainers, so while waiting for Larry, he could also battle other Pokémon trainers.

Penny, who had come with him, had already been taken away by her doting father Peony in the Galar region.

Flannery, like Flint, was someone passionate about battles and also wanted to climb ranks in Medali Town.

As for Roxanne... she was completely immersed in Medali Town's vast ocean of cuisine and couldn't extricate herself.

So those going to South Province Area One were actually just Johan, Cynthia, and Caitlin.

Actually, Johan didn't want Caitlin following along either, but unfortunately, Caitlin insisted on being this maximum brightness "light bulb."

And Cynthia spoiled her, leaving Johan helpless.

"I see..."

"We'll take the opportunity to appreciate the Paldea region's terrain and various landscapes," Cynthia said with a gentle smile.

Hearing this, Friede breathed a sigh of relief.

"Alright, I'll take my teacher and the others home first, see little Liko, then come back for you, Teacher Johan."

"No rush."

If there was a chance, Johan was also somewhat interested in meeting this protagonist who had "replaced" Ash and successfully opened a new chapter for the Pokémon anime.

Though she was still very young.

Like the Galar region, Flying Taxis were very popular in the Paldea region—quite a good industry.

However, the Paldea region's Flying Taxis were different from the Galar region's Flying Taxis.

"Excuse me, who called for Flying Taxi service?"

A helicopter-like Flying Taxi frame slowly descended, and the goggle-wearing taxi driver called out.

"Here." Johan lightly waved to signal.

"Why aren't Paldea's Flying Taxis Corviknight?" Flint called out like he'd never seen the world, seeing the seven or eight Squawkabilly standing on the Flying Taxi.

Johan's expression became somewhat strange.

The taxi driver was very good-natured and explained with a smile:

"Because... in the Paldea region, if we used Corviknight, we might... get shot down while flying."

"Shot down?" Flint was stunned.

"Cough." Johan cleared his throat and said: "Because the Paldea region is home to many Tinkaton, Tinkawk, and Tinkatink."

Having his own Tinkaton, Johan understood these Pokémon's temperament all too well.

As the Paldea region was the native region for Tinkaton-line Pokémon, you could see them in many places.

If a Corviknight flew over their heads... dozens of Tinkatink, Tinkawk, and even Tinkaton throwing rocks upward—the Flying Taxi couldn't handle that.

Back when Johan first caught Tinkaton, he was always on edge when walking outside.

Afraid his Tinkaton would grab its hammer and throw rocks, one strike sending down an airplane from the sky...

That would be impossible to compensate for.

"Oh..." Flint understood.

So that's how it was.

"But can such a group of 'little birds' really carry such a heavy taxi?"

Looking at those small Squawkabilly, Flint was doubtful.

"Squawk!!"

"Squawk—"

Hearing this, the seven or eight Squawkabilly immediately became noisy, showing angry fierce expressions—clearly Flint's doubt had made them quite indignant.

"Ka-kaah ka-kaah~" Gengar transformed into a Squawkabilly's appearance and mimicked their calls.

For a moment, it was like entering a bird market.

Caitlin muttered: "Cynthia and I are very light, okay? If anyone's heavy, it's Johan..."

The taxi driver maintained his warm smile, taking out a bag of food to feed the Squawkabilly, and they gradually calmed down.

At the same time, he said: "Don't worry, customers. These Squawkabilly are professionally trained Pokémon. Unless we encounter real trouble, they won't fall."

Your words are no different from saying professionally trained people won't laugh... Johan thought sarcastically while maintaining a neutral expression.

Calling the Flying Taxi was just for the convenience of enjoying the Paldea region's scenery while flying. Whether it was him, Cynthia, or Caitlin, flying wasn't difficult.

Of course, it definitely wasn't because Flying Taxis flew relatively slowly.

"Alright, since you're not the one riding, stop worrying about it." Johan stopped Flint, who seemed to have become a walking "hundred thousand whys."

He pulled Cynthia aboard the Flying Taxi.

Golden-haired loser Caitlin huffily followed.

Cynthia sat between them with a smile tinged with helplessness.

"Passengers, please sit tight—we're taking off—"

Flutter flutter—

The Squawkabilly flapped their wings, and the Flying Taxi took off. Destination—South Province Area One!

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar, addicted to mimicking, also flapped its hands like the Squawkabilly and flew up.

Going from Medali Town to South Province Area One required circling around the largest and most mysterious Great Crater of Paldea.

Since Medali Town was not far from the Great Crater of Paldea, after flying for some time, the Flying Taxi flew along the crater's towering rock walls that reached the clouds.

"Inside there is the Great Crater of Paldea, right?" Cynthia looked curious.

"Yes, the Paldea region's most mysterious area," Johan nodded.

"Do you want to go in, Cynthia? I can take you in~" Caitlin showed her presence.

With her psychic powers, as long as the distance wasn't too ridiculous, she could teleport directly in with Cynthia even without coordinates.

"Customers, you really shouldn't enter the Great Crater of Paldea," the taxi driver reminded them.

"Why?" Caitlin asked.

"Legend says that people who enter the Great Crater of Paldea never come back out."

The taxi driver seemed very talkative, recounting many legends about the Great Crater of Paldea.

Especially recently, the famous Paldean Pokémon professors Sada and Turo had entered the Great Crater of Paldea and never returned.

So now the Great Crater of Paldea was strictly controlled by the Paldea League. Anyone... unless they flew in from the unknowably high top of the crater, needed the Paldea League's permission.

"They say..."

The taxi driver said mysteriously:

"Some people have seen shadows of ancient and future Pokémon in the Great Crater of Paldea..."

"Didn't you say no one could come back out? How can there be 'they say'?" Caitlin couldn't help asking.

"The League has controls, you know. As long as you use the League's passages and don't go too deep, you can still get out..." the taxi driver chuckled.

So it wasn't as dangerous as imagined.

Many legends were spread through rumors.

But Johan was certain about the two professors' situation.

Actually, the real danger in the Great Crater of Paldea was at the very bottom. The outer areas were fine—the Paldea League had even established various base stations and infrastructure inside.

"Let's understand the specific situation first," Johan said softly.

Although he was quite curious about ancient and future forms.

But if nothing unexpected happened, the deepest part of the Great Crater of Paldea's Area Zero should have Koraidon or Miraidon, possibly even both.

"But customers, if you're flying in the Paldea region, you need to be careful of one type of Pokémon," the taxi driver was clearly enjoying the conversation and reminded them.

"What?"

"Bombirdier."

Johan understood.

Seeing Caitlin's "confused" expression, Johan took out his Rotom Pokédex.

"Bombirdier, the Item Drop Pokémon, Flying and Dark-type. It's a bird Pokémon that wraps things in its chest feathers then drops them, enjoying the sound they make. Quite dangerous, Rotom!"

"But as long as you fly high enough, you don't need to particularly worry about Bombirdier. But if you're in West Province Area One, you need to be extremely careful. Normally our Flying Taxi service doesn't include West Province Area One," the taxi driver added.

"Why?" Caitlin took over Flint's "hundred thousand whys" job.

"Because..." the taxi driver pondered before saying: "Because the Bombirdier there are particularly fierce, fly very high, and have very large populations."

"Oh..."

Hearing this, Johan's heart stirred.

If he remembered correctly... among the Paldea region's Titan Pokémon, there was a Bombirdier, right?

Guarding Herba Mystica.

West Province Area One?

Why hadn't Geeta sent the Herba Mystica location information yet?

Chatting casually with the Flying Taxi driver while admiring the various different terrains and landscapes of the Paldea region.

After flying past the towering rock walls where the Great Crater of Paldea was located, there was an endless great plain.

In the Paldea region's South Province, most areas were plains.

Of course, this didn't mean there were no mountains or rocks, just that compared to other areas of the Paldea region, it was relatively flat and more livable.

And the South Province was indeed the most densely populated area of the Paldea region.

But one very important factor couldn't be ignored.

That was Naranja Academy!

As an ancient academy with over 800 years of history, Naranja Academy was different from the more purely Pokémon-focused schools like Rustboro Pokémon Academy and Sinnoh Academy.

Naranja Academy could be seen as an important department of the Paldea League to some extent!

It held a high position within the Paldea League.

Geeta, as League Chairman, was also a member of Naranja Academy's board of directors.

And most of the Paldea League's historical Champions and Chairmen had also come from Naranja Academy.

Setting aside reputation, Naranja Academy was absolutely comparable to Kanto's Celadon University.

In some ways, it might even be more impressive than Celadon University.

But precisely because of its overly close relationship with the Paldea League, Naranja Academy had many elements that shouldn't appear in schools besides teaching.

For example... the now notorious Team Star within Naranja Academy.

Johan hadn't expected that even with Penny no longer at Naranja Academy, Team Star would still exist.

Facts proved that even though Penny was Team Star's boss in the games, she was only a relatively important factor in Team Star's existence, not the decisive one.

What really determined Team Star's existence was actually a very bad atmosphere within Naranja Academy—bullying!

So Johan had never held much favor toward Naranja Academy.

Although Naranja Academy would address such phenomena, it only treated symptoms, not the root cause.

After completely introducing the "competition" that belonged only to the League and society into schools, the byproducts of such competition were very difficult to eradicate.

Otherwise, why would Friede's teacher Rica, clearly teaching in the Paldea region, specifically send her daughter Liko to school in Kanto?

The Explorers' threat was one aspect.

Could the mighty Naranja Academy with 800 years of history really not handle the Explorers?

Fundamentally... the Explorers' tentacles had long reached into Naranja Academy.

And this was an unavoidable negative impact brought by Naranja Academy's excessive binding with the Paldea League.

Did the academy's leadership not know about such things?

No.

They just didn't know how to change it, lacked the ability to change it, or didn't want to change it.

No wonder they say the only lesson humanity can learn from history is that humanity cannot learn any lessons from history.

So even though the old principal had suggested Johan could visit Naranja Academy, Johan chose to avoid it.

Of course, problems shouldn't be viewed one-sidedly. Naranja Academy still had many excellent, motivated, and capable students.

"Customers, we're almost at South Province Area One. Where would you like to land?"

From morning to afternoon, the Flying Taxi's slow but comfortable journey was finally coming to an end.

"At the lighthouse."

"You got it!"

In the distance, a dilapidated lighthouse stood on a cliff by the sea.

This lighthouse had once been one of the residences of Professors Sada and Turo. After the two professors disappeared, the Paldea League moved their belongings, and the lighthouse was abandoned.

But it had become one of South Province Area One's landmark buildings.

The Flying Taxi slowly descended.

Johan paid the fare, then the three got out and stood on the cliff edge, feeling the sea breeze.

"Customers, if you need anything, you can call Flying Taxi service again!"

After saying this, the driver left with the Flying Taxi.

"This isn't near the 'bamboo piece incident,'" Cynthia looked around and checked the materials Geeta had given them.

"Mm."

Johan stood at the cliff edge. In the distance was the ocean, and directly below was a beach.

"But the source of the problem is probably from here."

Assuming he remembered correctly.

After all, the "shrines" where the Treasures of Ruin were sealed wouldn't move, unlike those Titan Pokémon that could run around, causing the Herba Mystica locations to be unfixed—that's why he needed Geeta's data.

"Here?"

Johan smiled without saying much. "Let's go. Since we're here, let's take a look first."

Because the South Province had many traces of human activity, most wild Pokémon there were generally weak.

In the dense grass, several Deerling were playing. In the pond, dark Wooper were blowing bubbles, cautiously poking out half their heads. In the lush trees, Greedent and Applin were fighting over unknown berries.

By the dirt slope under the tree, two orange Lechonk were swaying their round little bottoms while digging.

"Those are... Lechonk?"

Caitlin had crammed knowledge about Paldea region Pokémon on the Flying Taxi.

She saw three or four little black pigs gathered together in the nearby grass, apparently searching for some food.

"Yes." Cynthia smiled and nodded, being led by Caitlin's hand. "They're chubby..."

"You can tell they'd smell great roasted," Johan said.

"Ka-kaah! Ka-kaah!"

Gengar nodded repeatedly, drool shamefully sliding from the corner of its mouth.

Cynthia and Caitlin both glared at Johan.

"Just kidding, kidding. I don't eat Pokémon," Johan quickly explained.

But those fragrant Lechonk seemed to sense the "hungry gaze" from Gengar. Their fur stood up, and after looking around a few times, they quickly scattered.

"Ka-kaah..."

Gengar was unhappy.

I really want barbecue... it seems like we haven't had barbecue in a long time, right?

"I'll make it for you after this is over."

"Ka-kaah!"

Hehe!

The grinning Gengar immediately perked up again.

But it hadn't been happy long when Gengar's expression suddenly became serious.

"Ka-kaah!"

With a light call, it floated toward a direction.

This reaction...

Cynthia and Johan looked at each other and immediately quickened their pace to follow.

After some distance, several Hypno with fierce expressions suddenly appeared in their view.

Then several Clodsire crawled out of the grass, poison brewing in their mouths.

Just over a short distance, the Pokémon here had completely reversed their temperament 180 degrees from those in the previous area.

Although these Pokémon were naturally somewhat fierce, they wouldn't normally show such expressions.

"Looks like it's indeed around here," Johan said.

The wild Pokémon being affected confirmed Johan's guess.

"Yes." Cynthia's expression also became somewhat serious.

"Gengar, have them take a nap first."

Johan ignored these wild Pokémon and walked toward the nearby rock wall.

"There's definitely a problem..." Caitlin, with her psychic powers, also sensed something wrong.

"Ka-kaah~"

Accompanied by "thump thump" falling sounds, the three reached the rock wall.

"Garchomp!"

Being cautious, Cynthia still summoned Garchomp.

"Shaka!"

Garchomp's crimson scythe flashed, and the rock wall immediately burst with cracking sounds as large amounts of rock "clattered" down.

A circular stone tablet engraved with special patterns and bound to the rock wall with iron chains appeared in everyone's view.

Even without using Aura power, Johan could see wisps of black energy as thin as hair seeping from behind the stone tablet.

The seal was loosening.

"The patterns on this..."

Cynthia frowned and approached under Garchomp and Gengar's protection.

On the edge outside the circular tablet's pattern, she saw a circle of tiny text.

She recognized some of these characters.

"Shrine sealed stakes eight..."

"Pull out the eight stakes and you can open the seal here, releasing whatever's inside, right?" Johan said.

When he played the game, he had spent considerable time searching for these stakes.

"Mm... that should roughly be it..."

"But the seal here has already loosened, and the negative energy sealed inside has started leaking. We might not need to pull out all the stakes."

Johan gently rubbed his chin.

He said to Garchomp: "Garchomp, try breaking it open."

"Shaka!!"

Garchomp glared fiercely at Johan, waving its scythe.

Don't order me around!

Though it protested verbally, its body honestly moved.

"Shaka!!"

Immediately, extremely powerful deep purple Dragon-type energy enveloped its entire body. A dragon cloak appeared, and its sharp twin scythe crossed, targeting the heavy stone tablet and its chains...

Dragon Rush!

Boom!!!

A massive roar accompanied by heavy explosion sounds, rolling dust flying, the entire ground and rock wall trembling as more rocks fell.

Caitlin's psychic power protected Cynthia as they retreated, while Johan had to run on his own.

"Damn, you're 'retaliating' against me at this time!"

Fortunately, with his physical condition, mere falling rocks weren't a concern.

Caitlin made a face at him.

When the dust settled, the circular stone tablet and its chains remained intact, with a mysterious power like a barrier protecting its surface.

"Shaka..."

Garchomp's eyes widened, somewhat unconvinced and ready to try again.

But Cynthia called it back.

"Garchomp."

She looked at Johan. "Seems like... we can only pull out the stakes first?"

Johan rubbed his temples.

"Seems like that's the only way, but this makes it much more troublesome..."

He could remember the Treasures of Ruin's sealed locations, but he couldn't remember where those stakes were located.

Who knew how much time this would take.

Chairman Geeta would need to pay extra!

"Ka-kaah~"

Hearing this, Gengar curled its lip and floated to the circular stone tablet.

"Shaka!" Garchomp shook its head.

It didn't believe the stone tablet that hadn't budged when it attacked could be forcibly broken by Gengar.

But then...

Gengar looked at everyone with a "looking at fools" expression, then grabbed what looked like a wooden rivet at the junction where the two crossed iron chains met the rock wall.

With a gentle application of force...

Clatter—

The iron chains fell loose.

Johan: "..."

Garchomp: "..."

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar pointed to its own head and shook it.

Don't you know how to unlock doors? Where are your brains?

Johan: "..."

His Gengar actually knew how to open doors?

Johan felt like the sun was rising from the west.

# 759: Capture! Wo-chien! (Two-in-One, Please Vote!)

Clatter—

The metal chains fell, and immediately another surge of energy filled with negative emotions seeped from behind the circular stone tablet.

Sure enough, Gengar's perception was the most acute.

The seal here was already loose, which was why Johan had tried having Garchomp forcibly shatter the seal.

He hadn't expected that while the seal was loose, it wasn't loose enough. Under normal circumstances without Mega Evolution or enhancement, Garchomp's full-power attack left the seal completely unmoved.

But as Gengar had demonstrated, since the seal was loose, there had to be a weak point. The wedge it had just pulled out was exactly where this seal had loosened.

What surprised Johan was that his Gengar actually knew how to "open doors"!

But thinking about it, Gengar would phase through doors it could pass through, and only think about opening doors it couldn't pass through.

Gengar - No Door Can Stop Me!

But regardless, with the seal's loosening expanded, things became much easier.

At least they didn't need to pull out stakes one by one.

They retreated another distance.

They watched as gray, misty energy seeped from behind the stone tablet seal, gradually spreading outward in all directions.

"If left alone, this negative energy's area of influence will keep expanding," Caitlin said with a frown.

"Isn't that exactly why we're here to handle it?"

Johan remained calm. "Rotom, record the footage."

"Understood, Rotom!"

The Rotom Pokédex floated out, turning on its camera function to record the scene.

"Next, we completely break the seal."

Johan loosened his collar slightly and smiled at Garchomp. "Garchomp, want to try again?"

"Shaka!!"

Garchomp growled fiercely at Johan.

Again?

It wasn't stupid!

Johan shrugged, figuring Caitlin probably wouldn't listen to him either.

Looked like he'd have to do it himself.

He took out a Luxury Ball and threw it.

"Garruu zzZ..."

The snoring Urshifu appeared before Johan.

Though asleep, both its expression and posture were ready for battle.

"Wake up, Urshifu," Johan said softly.

"Garruu?"

Urshifu's sleepy eyes opened.

"Focus Energy, Sword Dance!"

"Garruu!"

Without any hesitation, Urshifu immediately took a deep breath, its eyes instantly becoming sharp. A dark red aura rose from its feet, faintly accompanied by metallic clanging as its momentum rapidly climbed.

"Shaka?"

Feeling Urshifu's changing aura, Garchomp's eyes narrowed, showing some surprise.

It also sensed considerable threat from Urshifu.

This guy...

Garchomp couldn't help but glance at the confident Johan.

His ability to train Pokémon was truly impressive.

Johan, also watching Urshifu, had a thought.

Looking at Urshifu's momentum change—that fragment from the "Fist Plate" had been pretty well "digested," hadn't it?

Recently, Urshifu hadn't had many chances to appear, but Coach Bear never neglected its training.

If asked which of Johan's Pokémon was most "hardworking," it would definitely be Urshifu.

After all, punching during the day, punching while asleep—who could out-work that?

However, compared to the other little ones, Urshifu's "luck" wasn't as good. In cultivation novels, this would be called "opportunities."

But Urshifu's foundation was among the best in Johan's team.

It was, after all, a legendary Pokémon's foundation.

What it might lack was just an opportunity.

"Urshifu, that stone tablet." Johan pointed at the seal. "Shatter it!"

"Garruu!"

Hearing this, Urshifu stared intently at the circular stone tablet, its fur dancing in the airflow.

Bang!!

An explosive sound, the ground erupted, and Urshifu instantly disappeared from its spot.

The next second—

Boom!!

Before the massive circular stone tablet, a seemingly plain and simple fist pressed solidly against it.

Wicked Blow!

Rustle rustle rustle—

Large amounts of dust shook down from the huge circular stone tablet, but the tablet seemed unmoved.

But suddenly—

Crack—

A crisp, loud sound split from the stone tablet.

Then, with Urshifu's fist as the center, spider web-like fine cracks rapidly spread until they covered the entire tablet.

It opened!

Rumble—

The massive stone tablet shook once more, immediately crumbling into stones of various sizes that scattered down.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar gave Urshifu a thumbs up, then raised an eyebrow at Garchomp.

"Shaka!"

Garchomp crossed its arms and turned its head away.

Boom!!

As the stone tablet shattered, a surge of powerful negative emotional energy immediately poured from the scattered stones.

A pitch-black massive cavity appeared in everyone's view.

"Garruu!!"

As soon as the seal opened, Urshifu, Garchomp, and Gengar's expressions immediately became serious, as if sensing some threat.

"It's coming!" Johan's expression also became serious as he spoke gravely.

"Gururu..."

An extremely dull sound came from within that pitch-black cavity, followed by scraping noises.

It was like... something was crawling over a pile of dead leaves on the ground.

Dimly visible, a three-to-four-meter-tall, small hill-like Pokémon slowly crawled out from the sealed area, accompanied by rich negative emotions, entering Johan and the others' view.

Under the heavy atmosphere, that creature gradually revealed its form.

It looked like a gigantic snail, its head and surroundings covered with numerous dead leaves. On either side of its head, two white tentacles intertwined with each other, with green eyes between the tentacles.

On its back was a shell formed by coiled bamboo strips like scrolls, nearly identical to the bamboo piece they had obtained, densely carved with text.

This was clearly one of the legendary "Treasures of Ruin"—the Tablets, Wo-chien!

"Unknown! Unknown Pokémon, Rotom!" the Rotom Pokédex called out.

"This is the Pokémon representing the tablets among the 'Treasures of Ruin'?" Cynthia said softly.

Johan nodded. "A Dark and Grass-type Ruinous Pokémon."

The typing wasn't hard to guess.

Just looking at Wo-chien's appearance and that strong evil aura, you could tell it was Dark and Grass-type.

"Garruu!"

"Shaka!"

The moment they sensed Wo-chien's aura, Urshifu and Garchomp showed discomfort and unease.

As if some mysterious and strange power was suppressing their own power.

The ability, Tablets of Ruin!

[Tablets of Ruin: When a Pokémon with this ability is on the field, all other Pokémon except those with Tablets of Ruin have their Attack reduced to three-quarters!]

A direct, unreasonable reduction of one-quarter.

This was definitely bad news for any physical attack Pokémon.

However, for Gengar, who specialized in special attack...

"Ka-kaah! Ka-kaah! Ka-kaah!"

Turning to look at Gengar, they saw it lying on the ground, rolling back and forth while hugging itself, as if on fire, looking extremely "pained."

Johan: "..."

"That's enough, stop pretending."

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar sat up straight and looked at the speechless Urshifu and Garchomp.

Eh? You guys aren't affected that badly?

Maybe my fighting skills are too amazing, so I'm affected more severely.

Everyone: "..."

"Gururu!!"

While Gengar was still fooling around, Wo-chien, having finally escaped its seal, wasn't in a good mood. After a low roar, the dead leaves on its body rustled, and a shower of malicious leaves scattered toward Johan and the others.

"Urshifu!"

Johan called out.

"Garruu!"

Urshifu immediately understood, sweeping out with one foot to create an airwave that scattered Wo-chien's falling leaves.

"Once more, Sword Dance!"

Although Wo-chien's "Tablets of Ruin" ability would suppress the power of physical attackers like Urshifu, Wo-chien itself was very skilled at special defense. Relatively speaking, physical attackers could still deal some damage.

Moreover, Wo-chien's Dark and Grass typing gave it many weaknesses.

4× weak to Bug.

Unfortunately, Aaron wasn't here.

But it was also 2× weak to Fighting. Even with Urshifu's power suppressed, under two Sword Dances plus Focus Energy enhancement, it would be enough!

"Garruu!!"

Metallic clanging accompanied sharp momentum bursting forth again.

At the same time, though the leaves Wo-chien scattered were swept away by Urshifu before getting close, after falling to the ground they quickly took root and sprouted, growing dark green vines.

And those seemingly small vines rapidly coiled toward Urshifu.

Leech Seed!

"Urshifu, Sucker Punch and get close!"

Since he chose to let Urshifu fight, it naturally needed to get in close.

"Garruu!"

Urshifu roared and shot forward again, wrapped in dark, sinister Dark-type energy, appearing before Wo-chien in an instant.

This sudden attack clearly caught the long-slumbering Wo-chien off guard.

In its memory, no Pokémon dared appear so directly in front of it, especially at such close range.

After all, it had the "Tablets of Ruin" ability—physical attackers still dared approach?

But while Wo-chien was stunned, Urshifu certainly wasn't.

"Close Combat!"

In an instant, the knotted muscles under Urshifu's thick fur bulged, and its fist unreasonably struck toward Wo-chien's face.

Bang!!

The sudden violent blow immediately brought Wo-chien back to its senses while it felt excruciating pain.

However, without giving it a chance to react, after one punch came a storm-like flurry of crazy consecutive hits.

More importantly, Wo-chien could clearly feel that in this combo, each punch seemed slightly heavier than the previous one.

Every time it gathered strength to counterattack, the tremendous force of each blow immediately shattered its accumulation.

The more it was hit, the more it hurt; the more it hurt, the more confused it became.

"Continue, Close Combat!"

Johan was somewhat surprised that Wo-chien's reaction was so sluggish—it had been hit with a full combo by Urshifu but still hadn't reacted.

Was it because of the snail's "nature"?

But Johan wouldn't waste such an opportunity.

Finish early, complete the job early, get reimbursed early, rest early.

"Garruu!"

Urshifu clenched its fist again.

Such a punching bag was rare.

Its eyes became even sharper.

It had made some gains while practicing punching recently—perfect time to test them on this guy!

Taking a deep breath, it slowly exhaled.

A wisp of white mist drifted from Urshifu's mouth, and an invisible wave of aura power spread with a "hum."

Focus Energy brought more concentrated spirit, aura power brought stronger strength, and strength combined with the surrounding aura instantly condensed.

Focus Punch?

Seeing this scene, Johan raised an eyebrow.

Trying to combine Focus Punch with Close Combat?

Good grief!

Focus Punch required highly concentrated spirit and energy. Any external interference would interrupt it directly.

So Focus Punch had to be used when the opponent wasn't attacking and gave enough time to gather energy.

Swinging the fist—

Throwing the punch!

"Gururu!!"

At this moment, the "snail-natured" Wo-chien finally came to its senses, roaring low as a terrifying oppressive airwave wrapped in thick negative energy immediately rushed toward Urshifu.

Boom!!

Energy filled with negative emotions descended like disaster, a decaying, dark energy mixed with hatred struck heavily.

The massive impact directly sent Urshifu flying.

"Garruu!!"

As the huge negative energy drilled into its body, Urshifu hit the ground while being invaded by negative energy, crying out in uncontrolled pain.

Ruination!

The signature move exclusive to the "Treasures of Ruin" Pokémon—Ruination.

Similar to the Alola region's Guardian deities' Nature's Madness.

It directly reduced the opponent's HP by half, no matter how much HP they had!

It looked very powerful.

But it only looked that way.

Because no matter how much it reduced, it could only reduce half the HP—it couldn't completely defeat the opponent.

The "Treasures of Ruin" Pokémon had finally awakened!

But... what awakened wasn't just it!

"Garruu!"

After briefly experiencing pain, the fallen Urshifu immediately forced down that extremely uncomfortable energy baptism.

Dark-type Pokémon?

Who isn't a Dark-type Pokémon!

Legendary?

Who isn't a legendary!

Standing with feet front and back, arms spread fore and aft, Urshifu took a stance that was neither Single Strike nor Rapid Strike.

Its momentum, far from being crushed, became even stronger!

Focus Energy.

Concentrate.

Clench fist—

Johan felt Urshifu's will—the will it had forged day after day, night after night.

Aura power rapidly spread.

"Aura?" Johan said softly. "I have quite a bit of that too..."

Aura!

Hum—

Johan's aura power quickly merged with Urshifu's aura power.

In an instant, Urshifu seemed to go "Super Saiyan," its fur standing on end.

Boom!!

The surrounding ground exploded again. Facing Wo-chien's Ruination attack, Urshifu showed no fear—instead, it became even more imposing.

In a flash, it was before Wo-chien.

This time, Wo-chien reacted.

The numerous Leech Seeds it had planted earlier sprouted vines that quickly gathered in front of it, weaving into a massive vine barrier.

However, for Urshifu with "Unseen Fist," such protection was like paper.

Bang—

One punch.

Wo-chien's eyes immediately widened, the piercing pain making it cry out instinctively. Its mountain-like body uncontrollably flew back like a cannonball, crashing heavily into the rock wall behind.

The next second, Urshifu closed in again, giving Wo-chien no chance to react, frantically throwing punches.

Rumble—

The rock wall and ground shook violently as large amounts of rock and dust continuously fell and rose.

The tremendous roar scared all nearby wild Pokémon into hiding.

"Garruu!!"

Urshifu roared once more, delivering a final downward punch from above.

Boom!!

The ground and rock wall immediately collapsed.

Wo-chien was unable to battle!

"Ka-kaah..." Gengar muttered.

A bit harsh...

This fighting technique was comparable to mine.

"Violent maniac," Caitlin muttered. Though complaining, she was also surprised by the strength Urshifu displayed.

Psychic-type was very effective against Fighting-type Pokémon, but the power and technique Urshifu showed...

It would be hard to beat.

"Garruu!"

Urshifu stepped back and returned to Johan's side, breathing heavily, looking somewhat excited.

Did the combination of Focus Punch and Close Combat succeed?

No.

After all, both Focus Punch and Close Combat were Fighting-type finishing moves with very high explosive power, each with demanding requirements.

Focus Punch required absolutely concentrated spirit, while Close Combat required constantly finding angles and timing. These two moves inherently contradicted each other.

But while Focus Punch didn't work, aura power could.

Combining aura with Close Combat, using aura as the "Rapid Strike Style Secret" flowing power—this worked!

Progress and growth were enough for Urshifu.

As long as it kept growing step by step, Heavenly Father would definitely "take care" of His child!

With "care," it could better protect Johan!

Wo-chien was unable to battle.

Now came the most troublesome stage.

Capturing it.

"Johan." Cynthia nodded.

She believed Johan was most suitable for handling Wo-chien, this "disaster Pokémon" that shouldn't have become a disaster.

"Throw the ball, Johan," Caitlin said with some teasing.

Never mind that Urshifu had defeated Wo-chien—the uncomfortable negative energy it constantly released was most important.

Johan's mouth twitched slightly.

Taking this one might mean taking many more...

But having accepted already...

Sighing lightly, Johan took out an Ultra Ball.

Throwing this one ball out could mean hundreds in return...

That works.

Thinking this, Johan threw the Ultra Ball at Wo-chien.

Red light shot out, drawing Wo-chien inside. The Ultra Ball shook three times continuously, then made a crisp "click" sound.

But afterward, the yellow patterns on the Ultra Ball were dyed pitch black by black mist seeping from within.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar grabbed the Ultra Ball from the ground and quickly floated to Johan.

Hey! A legendary!

Looking at the "Ultra Ball" that seemed to have become a "Dark Ball," Johan's mouth twitched again.

Johan: "..."

Though speechless, captured was captured.

"Gengar, hold onto this for me," Johan said.

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar scratched its head, but when thinking of Wo-chien's appearance and abilities, its eyes lit up.

"Ka-kaah!"

Got it!

It immediately threw the blackened Ultra Ball into its mouth.

Done!

"Rotom, send that video to Chairman Geeta," Johan said.

"Understood, Rotom!"

Rotom immediately sent the video to Paldea League Chairman Geeta.

"Oh, and attach an expense report, just use our previous... template."

"Got it, Rotom!"

This whole process was quite familiar!

"That takes care of one matter, let's..."

Johan looked at Cynthia and Caitlin, then at Gengar and the injured Urshifu.

"Have a happy beach barbecue!"

Gengar had been calling for barbecue for a while now.

Time to satisfy it.

"Ka-kaah!"

"Garruu!"

"Chairman Geeta sent materials, Rotom!"

On the beach of Paldea region's South Province Area One, Rotom, which had just emerged from the grill, received information from Geeta.

"Information about 'Herba Mystica,' general locations, and Titan Pokémon data, Rotom."

"Oh?"

Johan, wearing an apron while barbecuing, grabbed Rotom and quickly flipped through the data, finding that there happened to be a Titan Pokémon in South Province Area Three.

Not too far from South Province Area One.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar suddenly tugged at Johan's clothes, pointing toward the grass near the beach.

"What's wrong?"

Following Gengar's direction, Johan was slightly stunned.

He saw many wild Pokémon, each carrying something—berries, food, or materials—cautiously at the grass edge, looking like they wanted to approach but didn't dare.

"They're..."

"Ka-kaah!"

I'll go ask!

Just as Gengar finished speaking, Johan grabbed the horn on its head. "You go? They'll all run away! Ninetales, you go ask."

"Oowoo..."

Ninetales nodded, walking over with light steps and a very gentle attitude.

Who knew what it said to those wild Pokémon.

The next second, those little ones immediately showed happy expressions, excitedly running over together and placing all their berries, materials, and food in front of Urshifu.

"Garr... garruu?"

Urshifu looked confused.

After placing their items, the wild Pokémon didn't leave immediately but stood there hopefully.

Lechonk, Tandemaus, and Greedent rubbed their hands like little flies.

"What's the situation?"

Ninetales approached and quietly said a few words to Johan, and he finally understood.

Then his expression became somewhat strange.

It turned out these wild Pokémon were here to thank Johan and the others for helping deal with Wo-chien, the "big trouble." Many of their companions often accidentally wandered into the sealed area and became very irritable.

Now that Wo-chien was removed, those companions had returned to normal.

They were expressing gratitude.

"Garruu!"

Urshifu clenched its fist.

See? Heavenly Father would "take care of" hardworking Pokémon!

Look, didn't it come?

"Could be considered doing a good deed," Johan said to Cynthia.

Cynthia smiled and nodded.

Sometimes, you really didn't need to worry so much about gains and losses, right?

"Well then." Johan looked at the hopeful wild Pokémon, moved his lips, then said: "Since you're here... want to eat together?"

The next second, those wild Pokémon immediately rushed over.

Greedent was exaggerated, constantly stuffing things in its mouth without chewing, just stuffing—comparable to Gengar.

Most exaggerated were the Tandemaus, directly climbing onto Johan's body to grab the skewers in his hands, completely losing their earlier fearful, timid appearance.

Johan: "..."

Why did it feel like he was still at a loss?

But seeing those wild Pokémon so happy and carefree, Johan thought about it and didn't mind.

Whatever—worst case, he'd send another expense report to Chairman Geeta.

"What else do you want to eat? I'll make more?"

Whoosh—

At the same time, all the Pokémon present pointed to their favorite foods in unison.

All different, diverse and varied.

Johan: "..."

Looking skyward.

Day-who-knows-how-many since leaving Mewtwo—missing it.

"I want ice cream too," Cynthia said with a smile, looking at Johan who already had five or six Pokémon climbing on him.

Being so liked by wild Pokémon showed Johan's Pokémon affinity was still strong.

"Bubo bubo~"

A chubby Lechonk tugged at Cynthia's pants, pointing at the half-eaten skewer in her hands with starry eyes, rubbing its little hands.

Seeing this, Cynthia couldn't help but smile. "Here."

"I want ice cream too!" Caitlin also shouted.

"Roger, one more ice cream coming up!" Johan ignored Caitlin.

Caitlin: (#皿)

# 760: Gengar's "Rescue" Team Sets Out Again!

"Worthy of Teacher Johan and Miss Cynthia—I didn't expect you to resolve it in just one day."

Geeta received the video and called them, her words full of admiration.

"So has Chairman Geeta thought about how to handle this Wo-chien?"

Johan still asked somewhat "reluctantly."

"Wo-chien? I heard that Teacher Johan, your 'Pokémon Recovery Island' has been completed?" Geeta said.

The strength Wo-chien displayed could probably be suppressed by Geeta or the Paldea League's Elite Four.

But suppression was one thing—proper handling was the key.

The strong negative energy Wo-chien carried would significantly affect nearby people and Pokémon, and the longer it persisted, the greater the impact.

Eliminating the negative energy from Wo-chien wasn't something easily accomplished.

Otherwise, that king wouldn't have chosen to seal the four "Treasures of Ruin" in the first place.

At least, the current Paldea League had no means to deal with it.

And right here was an expert in "Pokémon Psychology" who had handled countless legendary Pokémon incidents, including many affected by negative emotions.

So why hesitate?

Find Teacher Johan—that's right!

Moreover, although the "Treasures of Ruin" Wo-chien was a legendary Pokémon, it wasn't like Eternatus, which concerned an entire region. This type of legendary wasn't that important to a region.

Hearing this, Johan knew he "couldn't escape."

"The problems with Wo-chien aren't easy to handle..."

Johan wasn't confident either.

How many years had it been? Who knew how long the negative energy on the "Treasures of Ruin" had accumulated? He hadn't tried yet and didn't know if it could be removed.

If not possible... he'd probably have to ask Giratina to place it in some corner of the "Distortion World."

"I understand, I understand, Teacher Johan," Chairman Geeta smiled. "Wo-chien's food expenses and such—our Paldea League will cover them, and we'll also bear the treatment costs."

Medical treatment and payment, basically.

After pausing, Geeta spoke again: "However, Teacher Johan, according to what you said, there are four 'Treasures of Ruin' total, and Wo-chien is just one of them?"

"Uh... theoretically, yes."

"Then, with Wo-chien's seal loosened, might the other three... or rather, after Wo-chien's seal was broken, might the other three also possibly escape their seals?" Geeta asked with some concern.

Great.

Indeed, "provoking" one meant getting entangled with all of them.

"I'm not certain about the specific situation, but it's possible. I can give you some general directions and locations. Chairman Geeta, you could have people investigate first," Johan said.

"General directions? Good."

Geeta also felt they should confirm the situation first.

"Oh, right. I sent 'transportation' to you, Teacher Johan. In certain situations... it should be useful and help you better search for 'Herba Mystica.'"

"Transportation?" Johan was stunned.

A helicopter?

Compared to helicopters, Johan actually preferred Flying Taxis—the leisurely swaying allowed him to see quite nice scenery.

Just as he finished speaking, a blue-feathered Squawkabilly flew over, carrying a small bag and flapping its wings.

Hmm?

"Should have arrived," Geeta smiled. "I hope you can properly enjoy our Paldea region's scenery and culture."

The call ended, and the Squawkabilly also flew to Johan's front.

"Squawk!"

Delivery!

"Ka-kaah?"

Delivery?

Gengar's eyes lit up as it floated to Johan's front, approaching the Squawkabilly.

The Squawkabilly was startled by Gengar but still took out four Poké Balls from its package and handed them to Gengar.

Without waiting for Johan to offer Energy Cubes as thanks, the Squawkabilly flapped its wings and flew away as if escaping.

"Ka-kaah?"

Poké Balls?

Looking at the Poké Balls bearing Paldea League insignia, Gengar scratched its head.

What kind of delivery was this?

Delivering Pokémon?

Johan, however, showed understanding upon seeing these Poké Balls.

"What's wrong?" Cynthia and Caitlin approached and asked.

"Oh, nothing. Chairman Geeta sent us 'transportation.'"

"Transportation? Poké Balls?" Caitlin showed the same confused expression as Gengar.

Johan took a Poké Ball and threw it with a smile.

Red light flashed.

A lizard-like Pokémon appeared, standing on two feet with dark green skin and tire-like structures on its neck, abdomen, and tail.

"Ka-kaah?" Gengar tilted its head.

What Pokémon is this?

"Cyclizar, Dragon and Normal-type Mount Pokémon. It's a gentle Pokémon that enjoys being with humans. It's said that ten thousand years ago, it already lived harmoniously with humans, Rotom!" Rotom introduced according to the Pokédex.

"Cyclizar, riding mode," Johan said.

"Gao~"

Hearing this, Cyclizar immediately crouched down, its neck and chest forming the "motorcycle's" front wheel, while its tail curved into the "motorcycle's" rear wheel.

Soon, a Pokémon version "motorcycle" appeared before Johan and the others.

"Ka-kaah?!"

Gengar's eyes lit up, immediately becoming excited as it circled around Cyclizar.

It really became a motorcycle?

Amazing! Amazing!

"Cyclizar is a very characteristic Pokémon of the Paldea region. They love being ridden, reportedly because human body temperature warms their backs, helping them resist cold and regain vitality."

Johan explained.

"I—I read the Paldea region Pokémon guide! I recognize it!" Caitlin called out stubbornly.

Cynthia patted her head.

The "fierce" humanoid Psychic-type Pokémon immediately became a "docile" golden-haired loser.

"I'd heard of them before, but this is indeed my first time seeing one..." Cynthia smiled.

"Cyclizar established friendly relationships with Paldea region humans long ago. They can adapt to various complex terrains—as long as it's not extremely cold places, they can go almost anywhere."

"Ka-kaah! Ka-kaah!"

Gengar threw out the remaining three Poké Balls, and three more Cyclizar appeared.

Though there were only three people, four Cyclizar were sent.

No wonder Chairman Geeta was shrewd—she'd obviously included playful Gengar in the count.

Sure enough, Gengar's favorability toward Chairman Geeta increased greatly!

Gengar immediately mounted one Cyclizar, professionally putting on windproof goggles and calling for the three to get on, completely forgetting about the beloved mount hidden in its shadow.

Though Cyclizar had good relationships with humans, these were clearly professionally trained ones, appearing more docile, honest, and well-behaved.

Reportedly, Elite Four Hassel's Dragon Tamer family originally rose by taming Cyclizar.

"Ka-kaah!"

Get on!

Gengar grabbed the Cyclizar's handlebars, looking like a biker youth... no, a biker old ghost.

But Gengar couldn't help clicking its tongue.

"Ka-kaah..."

Too bad—it would be even better if it could fly and glide.

Fly?

In ancient and future times... Cyclizar might really be able to fly, Johan thought.

Cyclizar was a relatively common Dragon-type Pokémon in the Paldea region, and the cover Pokémon of "Pokémon Scarlet/Violet"—purple Miraidon and red Koraidon—had great similarities to Cyclizar.

Many speculated that Koraidon might be an ancient form of Cyclizar, while Miraidon might be a future form.

After packing up, the three mounted their Cyclizar.

Honestly, Cynthia and Cyclizar suited each other well—at least she looked cool riding one.

So, three people plus one Pokémon, riding four Cyclizar, began traveling through the Paldea region.

"Ka-kaah—"

No wonder so many people like riding motorcycles—this feeling is amazing!

Johan: I didn't expect Cyclizar could ride this fast!

At sunset, Johan and the others arrived at South Province Area Three, a barren rocky area.

Though the South Province was generally flat overall, there were still areas with complex terrain. This rocky region in South Province Area Three was one of them.

"Should be around here, Rotom!"

The Rotom Pokédex showed navigation.

"In such a barren place without even grass, there's the 'Herba Mystica' you're looking for?" Caitlin looked around and asked.

"Barren doesn't mean 'Herba Mystica' won't grow."

Looking at this terrain, it should be the territory of the Titan Pokémon Klawf...

"Let's find a place to camp first," Cynthia said.

It was getting late.

Finding things in dim light would double the difficulty.

"Good."

Johan naturally wouldn't object.

Night fell.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar carefully and quietly floated out of the tent.

"Bangi?"

As soon as it emerged, Tyranitar, resting on a rock "soft bed" outside, immediately opened its eyes and sat up.

Gengar quickly floated over and covered Tyranitar's mouth.

"Ka-kaah!"

Keep it down, don't wake Johan!

"Bangi, bangi~"

Tyranitar scratched its head, showing a silly smile.

This rocky terrain was its "territory"!

So Gengar had arranged with it to go "exploring" after dark, seeing if they could find valuable ores or gems.

"Sni?"

At this time, Snivy also emerged from the tent, rubbing its eyes.

Gengar and Tyranitar stared at the suddenly awakened Snivy.

"Sni~"

Snivy disdainfully curled its lip, extended a vine, wrapped it around Tyranitar's body, then quickly contracted it, reaching Tyranitar's back in one motion.

What are you looking at? Let's go!

As long as it wasn't going to "tattle" to Johan, Gengar and Tyranitar relaxed.

Two Pokémon or three Pokémon... not much difference.

Gengar took out a prepared small shovel, put on a mining helmet, and waved.

"Ka-kaah!"

Let's go!

Thus, the three little ones formed a "temporary rocky terrain exploration team" and entered the jagged rocky area.

There were quite a few wild Pokémon in the rocky area.

However, compared to forests and grasslands, few wild Pokémon left their nests to wander or search for food in rocky areas at night.

After all, Rock-type Pokémon like Onix and Geodude mostly preferred warmth and sunbathing, following a schedule of working at sunrise and resting at sunset.

This saved Gengar's group considerable trouble.

"Bangi..."

Tyranitar lowered its head, holding two diamonds and munching them like apples while searching for mineral-rich ores.

But most Rock-type Pokémon in rocky areas fed on such ores, so finding them wasn't easy.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar rubbed its chin, feeling it had made an error.

Should have called Tinkaton too.

At least... Tinkaton's hammer could sense ores rich in metallic elements, not just focusing on gems and diamonds.

"Sni?"

Snivy noticed movement and tugged Tyranitar's ear.

As the most recently joined Pokémon, Snivy rarely went "fooling around" with Gengar at night, and being in the unfamiliar rocky area naturally brought some curiosity.

Its vine pointed toward a rocky area, saying there was movement.

So Gengar and Tyranitar tiptoed over.

Then they saw...

"Bangi?"

Tyranitar casually picked up a house-sized... Nacli.

The movement Snivy mentioned was actually a large group of Nacli's habitat.

They were currently curled up around a Naclstack, resting in clusters.

The Nacli picked up by Tyranitar seemed completely unaware it had encountered "danger," blinking sleepy eyes while looking at Tyranitar with confusion and bewilderment.

"Salt?"

Nacli blinked twice.

"Ka-kaah~ Ka-kaah~"

Gengar waved, signaling Tyranitar to put it back.

This wasn't their target.

"Bangi?"

Tyranitar scratched its head.

Should we... bring 'Salt Lord' a companion?

Gengar's eyes immediately widened.

"Ka-kaah?!"

We're all old bachelors—bring Salt Lord a companion for us to watch?

Besides, these are all babies. Do you have the heart?

Tyranitar: "..."

True.

Thinking about it, Tyranitar looked at the bewildered Nacli in its hands, stuck out its tongue to lick the delicious salt deposits, smacked its lips, and put it back in place.

Honestly, whether it was "Salt Lord's" salt deposits or these Nacli's salt deposits, they all tasted quite good, especially for Rock-type Pokémon like Tyranitar.

The returned Nacli just felt... it had been sleeping soundly when suddenly someone licked it, leaving it all wet...

Nacli: I'm not clean anymore!

Continuing deeper, they encountered and saw many wild Rock-type Pokémon. Besides Nacli and Naclstack, there were common Geodude and Graveler, plus rarer Nosepass and Dwebble.

Gengar even saw a Shuckle, but after shaking it, there was no "fresh juice" in its shell, which was somewhat disappointing.

But when they reached a cave-like area, Gengar's group suddenly discovered... there was light inside the cave!

Light purple, feeling like...

"Ka-kaah!"

Amethyst!

The three little ones excitedly ran into the cave.

They saw strange Pokémon that looked part flower, part firefly, and part tadpole, emitting a peculiar light purple glow.

Some floated in mid-air like they were wandering, some clung to rock walls absorbing nutrients, while others stared with pale yellow eyes, blankly watching Gengar's group who had suddenly entered the cave.

"Sni?"

What Pokémon are these?

"Ka-kaah?"

You ask me, who do I ask?

Gengar shrugged, suddenly feeling that besides Tinkaton, it should have brought Rotom too—at least when encountering unknown Pokémon, there'd be some hints.

If Johan were here, he could tell Gengar's group that these floating Pokémon in the cave were called Glimmet—Rock and Poison-type Crystal Pokémon.

They liked living in groups and could absorb nutrients from rocks. Their petal-like structures were actually crystalized toxins that would scatter poisonous powder like pollen for self-defense.

Encountering Glimmet in the wild was quite dangerous.

Not because they were highly aggressive or had bad temperaments, but because they constantly released toxins. The light purple, glowing, dust-like particles falling from them were actually their scattered poison powder.

And if they felt threatened or became hostile, their toxin output would become even more fierce.

As everyone knew, Poison-type Pokémon in the wild were absolutely as dangerous as fierce, powerful Pokémon—especially group-dwelling Poison-type wild Pokémon like Glimmet.

Sure enough, Gengar's group's appearance immediately made the Glimmet feel threatened.

Purple crystal-like flowers opened, and crystalline purple toxic mist surged toward Gengar's group.

If it were other Pokémon, facing this toxic mist, they'd at least dodge first. But Gengar...

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar was delighted, opening its mouth and taking a deep breath. The approaching toxic mist was immediately sucked in by Gengar like a vacuum, its body swelling up.

Seeing this scene, the Glimmet looked bewildered, blinking their golden eyes.

"Burp~"

Gengar burped, its swollen body quickly returning to normal, then waved at the Glimmet.

Thanks for the treat!

Then it prepared to retreat.

These Glimmet looked nice, but they weren't their "target."

However, the Glimmet didn't think so.

The dense Glimmet separated to both sides.

Several Pokémon obviously larger than Glimmet, with clear differences, floated out.

They were bigger than Glimmet, especially their blue-purple Poison-type crystal petals, which were obviously larger, like flowers in full bloom.

If Johan had fought Paldea League Chairman and Champion Geeta first, Gengar's group would recognize these larger Pokémon that floated out.

Glimmora!

Glimmet's evolved form, also Poison and Rock-type Crystal Pokémon, but with stronger poison abilities.

Among Geeta's ace Pokémon, Glimmora was very important.

Buzz—buzz—buzz—

The Glimmora floated in mid-air, their huge blue-purple crystal flowers opening and closing, scattering beautiful crystalline dust.

They didn't directly attack Gengar's group.

"Ka-kaah?" Gengar wiggled its butt.

What do they want to say?

"Bangi..."

Tyranitar also looked bewildered.

Seems like we can't communicate.

"Sni!"

Snivy rolled its eyes at the two guys and jumped down from Tyranitar's back.

"Sni?"

"Buzz..." The Glimmora's golden eyes flickered, seemingly narrating something.

"Sni~ Sni~"

Snivy actually successfully communicated with them.

Under Gengar and Tyranitar's bewildered gazes, Snivy didn't bother explaining, waving for them to follow.

Then, led by the Glimmora and watched by numerous Glimmet, the three little ones headed deeper into the cave.

Gengar smacked its lips.

First time experiencing how Tyranitar felt when seeing ores everywhere.

But considering they hadn't shown obvious hostility, it wouldn't be right to attack directly.

Soon, led by the Glimmora, they reached the cave's depths.

Zap—

But before reaching the deepest part, suddenly a golden lightning bolt flashed, shooting from the dark depths at extreme speed.

Fortunately, Gengar's group reacted quickly enough, dodging in time to avoid being struck.

Bang!!

But the golden arc still hit the rock wall, creating a sizeable crater with accompanying burnt black smoke.

What was that?

"Ka-kaah?"

Lured in for killing?

Then we can fight!

Gengar licked its lips, drool shamefully sliding from its mouth corner.

"Sni!"

Snivy immediately stopped it irritably, explaining it wasn't the Glimmora but the creature in the cave's depths.

The creature in the cave's depths?

Only then did Snivy finally share the information it got from the Glimmora.

Originally, some time ago, a stranger like Gengar's group had intruded into their cave, seemingly searching for something, insisting on going to the cave's depths.

So, the Glimmora clan fought it.

The result... the Glimmora didn't win, and the opponent still entered the cave's depths.

But they didn't lose either, because the opponent was poisoned by the Glimmora.

Though it didn't fall, it remained trapped in the cave's depths, unable to leave, attacking any approaching Pokémon.

So, when Gengar's group arrived, the Glimmora wondered if they were allies of that creature.

Or rather, if Gengar's group could help remove that creature from the cave's depths, the Glimmora clan would provide rewards.

"Ka-kaah?"

Rewards?

Looking at the Glimmora's crystalline blue-purple petals, Gengar perked up again.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar immediately patted its chest, saying: Leave it to us!

We're not outlaws—we're the "Gengar Rescue Team" and love helping other Pokémon!

But first, Gengar needed to see what Pokémon that creature was.

It carefully peeked over.

Zap—

Another golden arc flashed toward them.

"Kraa—"

----------

Powerstones?
