# 761: "Iron Serpent" Miraidon! (Two-in-One, Please Vote!)

"Ka-kaah!"

So fierce!

But I like it!

Gengar chuckled and slipped into the shadows, diving right in.

The pitch-black cave was the perfect cover for Gengar.

When Gengar entered the cave, it finally saw what that creature looked like.

"Ka-kaah?"

Cyclizar?

Eh?

This guy looked somewhat like Cyclizar, but seemed different everywhere...

Similar giant lizard appearance, with two tire-like structures, except its skin was no longer green but presented as blue-purple. Blue-purple lightning danced on both sides of its head and tail, giving it a... very high-tech feeling.

Compared to Cyclizar, this guy was more dragon-like.

Just as Gengar was observing this creature, despite hiding in the shadows, the blue-purple being's eyes looked directly at Gengar.

Zap—

Golden arcs jumped again, striking the rock beside Gengar with a "crack," exploding into a charred hole.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar sighed in relief, wiping its forehead.

Good thing I dodged quickly.

But Gengar was somewhat puzzled—how had this guy discovered it?

Logically, with Gengar's current shadow-hiding ability, even some legendary Pokémon would have difficulty sensing its presence, right?

Unless it was a legendary with particularly strong perception or special means...

Seeing arcs beginning to jump on the opponent again, Gengar quickly floated out from the shadows, showing a "kind" smile while indicating...

"Ka-kaah~ Ka-kaah~"

I mean no harm~

Zap—

Arcs jumped once more, but were again dodged by Gengar.

Gengar was frustrated.

Johan's "expressing goodwill" trick didn't work at all!

In fact, any normal Pokémon seeing Gengar's "kind" smile would feel creeped out.

Gengar also noticed this guy seemed very weak.

Being poisoned by Glimmora was one thing, but it also seemed to have some unknown problems of its own.

Additionally, this guy was extremely vigilant and seemed very aggressive, even able to "see" the hidden self.

Although Gengar felt taking down the opponent while it was weak wouldn't be a problem...

The opponent's hostility was for self-preservation—there was no reason to forcibly subdue it.

If it was a relatively kind, friendly Pokémon and they forcibly captured it, Johan would probably scold them.

More importantly, where would they take it if they captured it?

Send it to Johan?

If it was trouble, wouldn't Johan scold them anyway?

Gengar's little brain spun rapidly, finally deciding... let Johan handle it himself!

Thinking this, Gengar floated out and waved at Tyranitar and Snivy.

Time to retreat!

Tyranitar and Snivy looked at each other, glanced around at the Glimmora and Glimmet, then slowly retreated.

"Mmm..."

In the tent, Johan instinctively reached toward his pillow, took a Pecha Berry and bit into it. The paralysis quickly faded.

He opened his eyes, seeing the "terrifying" ghost face sticking out its tongue before him, and rubbed his face saying:

"Morning, Gengar. Did I oversleep today?"

Sitting up, Johan realized... it was still pitch black around them, and Ninetales, Tinkaton and the others were still sleeping.

Johan: "!!"

His eyes widened as he looked at Gengar's grinning expression, rubbed his temples, and carefully walked out of the tent to see Tyranitar and Snivy also standing outside.

"Huff—"

Slowly exhaling, Johan rubbed his temples.

Without asking, he knew Gengar had definitely taken Tyranitar and Snivy "exploring" in the middle of the night, then probably encountered some difficult or tricky problem and returned to find him.

After so much time together, Johan was used to this.

In the middle of the night, he didn't have the energy to be angry anymore. He rubbed his face and said:

"Alright, tell me what happened this time."

"Ka-kaah~ Ka-kaah~"

"Bangi~ Bangi~"

Gengar and Tyranitar each spoke their own version, both gesturing while talking, but neither made the situation clear.

"Snivy, you tell me," Johan ignored the two and looked at Snivy.

"Sni~"

Fortunately, Snivy managed to explain the situation clearly, and Johan understood the general cause.

"A Pokémon mistakenly entered Glimmora territory, got poisoned, hid in the depths. The Glimmora can't do anything about it, it won't come out, so they asked for your help. You were worried about hurting them, so you came back to find me, right?"

"Sni~"

Snivy nodded.

That was roughly the situation.

"Alright."

At least... since Gengar's group was trying to help other Pokémon, he'd temporarily forgive them for waking him in the middle of the night.

Looking up at the sky, the moon had moved toward the horizon and the sun hadn't risen yet, but it probably wouldn't be long until dawn.

"Fine, let's go. Let's see those Glimmora and that Pokémon that needs help."

"Ka-kaah~!"

I'll lead the way!

Since it wasn't dawn yet, he wouldn't disturb the resting Cyclizar. Johan walked with Gengar, Tyranitar and the others toward the cave where the Glimmora were.

Dawn was breaking.

Johan's group reached a cave entrance surrounded by rock walls and stones.

"Are you sure... this is the place?"

Because of the morning light, the faint purple glow from inside the cave was no longer visible.

"Ka-kaah..."

Should be...

The area all looked the same, and they'd seen at least ten similar caves on the way.

Without detailed maps and coordinates, or sufficient familiarity with the area, it would indeed be difficult to pinpoint the exact one.

This wasn't the first time Johan had asked this question.

Johan: "What do you mean 'should be'?"

He looked at Tyranitar and Snivy.

Tyranitar nodded heavily, but Snivy shook its head decisively.

Johan: "..."

Even at night, you shouldn't get lost, right?

Three of them, three different answers?

"Whatever, we'll know once we go in and look," Johan said helplessly.

Then he stepped into the cave.

The already dim cave without light didn't become any more visible with the morning light, and there were no Glimmet or Glimmora to be seen.

"Ka-kaah!"

Seeing this, Gengar immediately showed realization, pounding its fist on its palm.

This isn't the place!

It was too dark at night, this cave didn't emit blue-purple light, so the three of them never approached this cave—it was just one they passed by.

Next one, the next one for sure!

Johan shook his head.

"Next time you go out, you should at least bring Rotom along."

"Ka-kaah!"

I think so too!

Gengar agreed wholeheartedly.

Rotom: "??"

Exiting the cave—

Rumble—

Just as Johan's group walked out of the cave, they suddenly heard rumbling accompanied by falling rocks, as if something was climbing down from the rock wall beside the cave.

When they emerged from the cave, they saw...

Dense masses of orange, crab-like Pokémon with thick fur at their joints were crawling on the rock walls, staring at Johan's group with pairs of large, round eyes lacking much "intelligence."

"Ka-kaah?"

There are crabs in the mountains too?

Gengar looked at these crab-like Pokémon and couldn't help swallowing.

And they were all "cooked" crabs too.

Look how red they are—they must taste great...

Johan rolled his eyes unsympathetically.

"All you think about is food. These are Klawf, Rock-type ambush Pokémon that live in rocky areas and can freely crawl on cliffs. We seem to have accidentally entered their territory."

Klawf were relatively hot-tempered Pokémon. Unlike other crab Pokémon like Krabby and Kingler, Klawf couldn't just crawl sideways—they could also crawl vertically.

Additionally, Klawf's claws were extremely sharp. Once prey was caught by their claws, it was extremely difficult to escape.

Perhaps because of this, Klawf claws were considered a rare delicacy in the Paldea region. Many restaurants and hotels took pride in offering dishes made from Klawf claws.

Of course, most Klawf claws were naturally shed by the Klawf themselves.

But one couldn't rule out some profit-driven people engaging in illegal activities.

So in recent years, wild Klawf's attitude toward humans had grown increasingly hostile.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar seemed not to have heard Johan's words, suddenly widening its eyes and pointing at the rock wall above the cave.

There was a Klawf... several or even dozens of times larger than ordinary Klawf!

Even more exaggerated than the proportion between Galar region's Alpha Pokémon and their normal counterparts.

Those who didn't know might think it was a huge rock wall.

Such a big one... how much meat must be in those claws?

Following Gengar's pointing direction, Johan also saw this enormous Klawf. After a brief pause, he laughed.

"This really delivered itself to our doorstep..."

"Ka-kaah?"

The three little ones looked at him puzzledly.

"Titan Pokémon. This is the Stony Cliff Titan," Johan said.

Titan?

Gengar's group knew what Johan had come to find—Titan Pokémon!

And the Herba Mystica that Titan Pokémon protected!

Titan Pokémon were special Pokémon unique to the Paldea region. Their origins could be traced back two hundred years ago, when people found special plants in the depths of the Great Crater of Paldea and planted them in different locations according to their growth requirements.

These plants from the depths of the Great Crater of Paldea were the Herba Mystica Johan sought.

However, for unknown reasons, these planted Herba Mystica were never collected and became food for some wild Pokémon.

Those Pokémon that ate the Herba Mystica became today's Titan Pokémon!

Powerful and commanding large populations, they were often regional overlords.

Gengar's trio understood.

Soon Gengar proudly patted its chest.

"Ka-kaah!"

Aren't I amazing? I found a Titan Pokémon for you right away!

"Bangi!"

And me!

Johan and Snivy looked at each other.

Johan & Snivy: "..."

"Klawf! Klawf!"

At the same time, the Stony Cliff Titan Klawf rumbled low, and all the Klawf immediately sprang into action.

Dense masses of Klawf surged toward Johan's group like a tide.

Gengar, Tyranitar, and Snivy also immediately put away their joking expressions.

Gengar didn't move, while Tyranitar and Snivy each stepped forward, ready for battle.

"Tyranitar, Earthquake!"

"Snivy, Leaf Storm!"

Johan called out softly.

"Bangi!!"

Tyranitar's eyes immediately became sharp, returning to the terrifying Desert Tyrant. It roared powerfully and stomped the ground hard.

Rumble—

Instantly, the ground shook violently. The entire surface collapsed and cracked, and countless Klawf caught in the earthquake rolled their eyes and lost fighting ability.

Numbers, especially these Ground-type weak Pokémon, meant nothing before Tyranitar.

The only trouble might be...

"Tyranitar, take it easy—don't bury us too!"

Easy friendly fire.

"Sni!"

Snivy's tail swept up numerous leaves in a tornado-like sweep across the rock wall, catching many Klawf and directly making them lose fighting ability.

One attacked those on the ground, one attacked those on the rock wall.

Two moves cleared a large area like cutting grass.

The remaining Klawf stared blankly, seemingly not understanding what happened.

"Klawf!!"

Though more Klawf surged forward, the Titan Klawf couldn't sit still seeing this.

It immediately took giant steps forward with numerous Klawf.

Capture the leader first!

Otherwise, who knew how many Klawf were in this rocky area.

"Sni!"

Understanding Johan's intention, Snivy immediately took the initiative to attack.

"Bangi..."

Tyranitar looked regretful for not speaking up sooner.

But for opponents like Klawf clinging to rock walls, Snivy was indeed more suitable.

"Klawf!!"

Just as Snivy leaped toward the Klawf, the Titan Klawf roared low, and numerous rocks appeared from nowhere, rapidly flying toward Snivy.

Rock Slide!

Simultaneously, all Klawf received the Titan Klawf's command and used Rock Slide.

Dense masses of rocks appeared above Johan's group, like a terrifying rock rain about to fall.

"Bangi!"

Tyranitar showed no fear, crouching down and plunging both hands into the ground in front, then exerting force—

Boom!!

It directly lifted a huge rock layer, raising it overhead to shield Johan and Gengar.

Rubble crashed down, but Tyranitar didn't budge.

On the other side, the Titan Klawf's large Rock Slide attacked Snivy, but Snivy nimbly dodged using its agile form and vines.

Even these rocks became a faster path for Snivy to approach it.

Nimbly dodging, tail gathering leaves again, stepping forcefully on a flying boulder, Snivy leaped into the air, curled its body, spun 180 degrees mid-air, then struck down hard with its tail carrying the leaf tornado.

Bang!!

The massive Titan Klawf felt the sensation of flying through the air for the first time...

It was also confused.

No way.

That tiny little thing—how did it explode with such exaggerated power and destructiveness?

But the damage from hitting the ground and Leaf Storm followed, leaving the Titan Klawf no time to wonder why.

"Klawf—"

The Titan Klawf's eyes immediately turned red as it roared.

Crack—

Its orange-red hard shell cracked, but the Klawf's momentum rapidly climbed.

Ability: Anger Shell!

[Anger Shell: When HP drops below half from an attack, Defense and Special Defense decrease by 1 stage, while Attack, Special Attack, and Speed increase by 1 stage!]

Trading defensive ability for stronger explosive power and faster speed.

The enraged Titan Klawf immediately raised its giant claws and charged at Snivy.

"Sni..."

Snivy curled its lip.

It thought Titan Pokémon would be stronger, but they just looked bigger with more HP—what a letdown...

"Snivy, don't let it lose fighting ability. Just hurt it," Johan reminded.

Hearing this, Snivy, who had been preparing another Leaf Storm to finish the Titan Klawf, paused.

It had already used Leaf Storm twice. Combined with "Contrary," its Special Attack had received a 4-stage boost—equivalent to doubling!

With such explosive power, the opposing Titan Klawf definitely couldn't withstand it.

To keep it from losing fighting ability...

Snivy became interested.

Its tail swayed gently.

Both hands extended.

Hum—

Two emerald green leaf blades appeared from Snivy's arms.

Seeing the Titan Klawf charging like a mountain, Snivy showed no fear and charged forward instead of retreating.

Just as the Titan Klawf's giant claw fell, Snivy bent backward sharply, extending vines that wrapped around rocks behind the Klawf.

A lightning-fast slide under the Titan Klawf.

Swish!

Snivy appeared behind the Titan Klawf, crouched halfway down with leaf blades slowly retracting.

Thud—

The Titan Klawf collapsed directly, with two deep cracks on its abdominal armor.

"Klawf!"

The Titan Klawf finally panicked.

Enduring the pain, it hastily got up and ran toward the cave Johan's group had just exited.

Seeing their "boss" flee, the remaining Klawf immediately scattered.

"Let's go!"

Seeing this, Johan waved, signaling the little ones to follow.

"Gengar! Don't collect those claws—I won't cook them for you even if you do."

"Ka-kaah..."

Gengar's ears drooped as it dropped the collected Klawf claws and floated along.

Following the Titan Klawf through the complex cave system, they soon reached the depths.

A huge cavern with several holes in the ceiling let beams of light shine down.

In that direct light, a pale pink plant emitting a faint, enticing fragrance grew quietly.

Found it!

Herba Mystica!

"Gengar, thank the Klawf and let it sleep awhile."

"Ka-kaah~"

A few simple waves, and black mist enveloped the Titan Klawf. Before it could reach the Herba Mystica, it collapsed and fell into deep sleep.

"If I'm not mistaken, this should be the Sweet variety."

Looking at the pink, knee-high Herba Mystica plant, Johan rubbed his chin and muttered.

Sweet variety...

If he used this with "Rare Candy" to make a dish, he wondered what it would taste like and what effects it would have.

"Ka-kaah! Ka-kaah!"

Success! Success!

Gengar pulled out a small hoe, ready to pack and take away.

"Sni!"

Snivy spread its arms, blocking Gengar with a serious expression.

"Ka-kaah?"

Seeing this, Gengar was stunned.

Johan, also seeing this scene, raised an eyebrow somewhat surprised: "Snivy, can you... transplant?"

The Herba Mystica here had originally been brought from the Great Crater of Paldea's depths by Paldeans two hundred years ago. If they could transplant successfully, perhaps Snivy with its "Forest's Blessing" ability could too.

"Sni..."

Worth trying.

Snivy didn't guarantee success, but felt... there was a chance.

But regardless, uprooting and taking everything wasn't good.

"Alright, I'll leave it to you."

Johan agreed without much hesitation.

He'd originally wanted to find some Herba Mystica to try cooking and see what effects it would have. He hadn't planned to take everything, so if Snivy could transplant and keep it alive, that would be great.

"Sni!"

Feeling Johan's trust, Snivy nodded firmly.

Leaf Blade appeared again.

It gently touched the Herba Mystica plant, and the second-largest branch was removed, wrapped in emerald green energy, then stored somewhere unknown.

Only then did it harvest the leaves.

These leaves were the key to the seasoning.

"Job done!"

Herba Mystica +1!

Several hours later, the slowly awakening Titan Klawf looked at the bare Herba Mystica plant with only four or five leaves remaining and wailed.

But it also felt somewhat grateful.

Those guys... were actually quite nice, not uprooting everything and even leaving some leaves...

Just waiting for it to flourish again—who knew how long that would take.

"Are you sure this time?"

Johan, in good spirits after obtaining the Herba Mystica, followed Gengar's group to another cave entrance. It indeed didn't look particularly different from the cave where the Klawf lived—no wonder Gengar's group got confused.

This time Gengar got smart.

Coming to the cave entrance, it cupped its hands like a megaphone and shouted inside:

"Ka—kaah—"

Any—one—there—

Several Glimmet resting on the cave's rock walls were immediately startled, their blue-purple crystal petals immediately scattering as dust, which Gengar vacuumed up as "food."

"Ka-kaah!"

This time is right! This is the place!

The commotion wasn't small, so several Glimmora and a group of Glimmet inside floated to the cave entrance.

Seeing Gengar's group return, they understood and made way.

Following the Glimmora into the cave, Johan wasn't particularly worried about the toxic powder released by these Glimmet and Glimmora.

Never mind whether these particles could reach him with Gengar around—even if they did, compared to Gengar's toxins, these Glimmora's powder wasn't impressive.

But everything seemed to go smoothly.

Johan directly reached the cave's depths.

Zap—

A golden arc shot out. Having been prepared, it didn't cause any fright.

How to approach in this situation?

Johan had a method.

He closed his eyes.

Aura dwells within the heart!

Hum—

Invisible aura power immediately spread from him as the center.

Carrying goodwill, it approached that creature in the cave's depths.

When Johan "saw" the listless, exhausted creature curled up in the cave's depths, his eyes immediately opened.

He looked at Gengar's group with complete bewilderment.

"You said the one needing help—are you sure it's this guy?"

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar's trio nodded repeatedly, while the nearby Glimmora indicated: Actually, we're the ones needing help.

But that wasn't important anymore.

Because Johan discovered that the creature inside—this Pokémon somewhat resembling Cyclizar—was clearly Miraidon!

The cover legendary of "Pokémon Violet," the "Iron Serpent" Miraidon!

In other words, the future form of Cyclizar!

Johan: "..."

Good grief.

He'd wanted to see future and ancient forms, but encountering the "strongest" future form right away—wasn't that a bit too fast?

Buzz buzz

Sensing Johan's aura, arcs burst from Miraidon's body again.

"Kraa—"

Low calls filled with heavy wariness.

Miraidon and Koraidon were the two cover legendaries of "Pokémon Scarlet/Violet," but their personalities differed greatly.

Ancient form Koraidon was tenacious and fierce with unshakeable strong will, but also full of curiosity. Though wary of humans, it would sometimes actively approach and play with them.

Future form Miraidon was extremely calm, even cold. It possessed observational and analytical abilities that could see through personalities. Whether facing enemies or humans, it maintained machine-like indifference, rarely showing emotional changes.

Miraidon was more like a precisely designed, perfect machine.

It could sense human or Pokémon personalities, which was why it could detect shadow-hidden Gengar and Johan's aura power.

This made things somewhat difficult...

Despite Johan's aura carrying heavy goodwill, the cold Miraidon ignored such goodwill, maintaining the principle that any creature approaching while it was weak was an enemy. All who entered the cave might face its attacks.

Johan rubbed his temples.

"This couldn't be the one that escaped from the Great Crater of Paldea's Area Zero, could it? Triggered the 'game storyline'?"

According to the game's storyline, to make Miraidon feel goodwill might require...

Herba Mystica!

What a coincidence!

Actually, no.

Miraidon appearing here, weakened and accidentally poisoned by the Glimmora, perhaps it had originally come seeking Herba Mystica.

But who made the Glimmora's cave so similar to the Klawf's cave?

Whoosh—

Suddenly, Miraidon, wrapped in golden electricity, curled into a ball and shot from the cave's depths at extreme speed.

Hmm?

"Ka-kaah?"

It ran?

Gengar picked its nose, somewhat surprised.

Johan's heart stirred as he quickly followed outside.

He saw the weakened Miraidon hovering outside the cave, suddenly roaring toward the Great Crater of Paldea:

"Kraa—!!"

Terrifying momentum with extreme pressure burst forth amid scattering electricity.

Surrounding ground and rock walls were left with numerous charred marks.

This creature, which normally showed little emotional fluctuation or feeling, now had a voice carrying traces of tragedy and anger.

However, just after roaring, Miraidon fell to the ground, struggled briefly, then fell unconscious.

# 762: Rankings!

"This is... a Pokémon?"

Back at the campsite.

When the awakened Cynthia and Caitlin saw the unconscious Miraidon that Tyranitar had carried back, they were both somewhat stunned.

Caitlin immediately took out her Pokédex and started flipping through information about Paldea region Pokémon.

She had crammed information about Paldea region Pokémon but didn't remember any Pokémon like Miraidon, which looked somewhat similar to Cyclizar.

To avoid being "educated" by Johan again, Caitlin was being proactive.

But Cynthia was different.

Cynthia was at least familiar with Paldea region Pokémon, having battled some Paldea region trainers.

But it was precisely because of this that she felt confused and curious about the Miraidon that Tyranitar was carrying.

What Pokémon was this?

Johan, who had picked up a "big trouble," looked helplessly at the unconscious Miraidon and nodded:

"I don't know how to explain it all at once."

He couldn't just directly say this was a Pokémon from the future—a future Cyclizar, right?

The four Cyclizar that Chairman Geeta had sent, which had been quiet and well-behaved all along, all seemed frightened when they saw Miraidon. The four Cyclizar huddled together, looking scared.

"Take it to a Pokémon Center?" Cynthia asked.

Johan thought about it and shook his head.

"No need, I should be able to handle it."

Never mind whether the Pokémon Center could help restore a future, highly technological legendary like Miraidon.

Just the impact that Miraidon's appearance might bring would be a huge trouble.

Rather than getting involved in these troubles, it was better to pretend not to know and treat Miraidon as an ordinary Pokémon that needed help.

More importantly, the nearest Pokémon Center to this rocky area wasn't close either.

Out of trust in Johan's abilities, Cynthia didn't insist.

"Bangi—"

Tyranitar placed Miraidon on the ground.

Johan then took out some healing spray and antidote to treat some of its problems.

For wilderness exploration, healing sprays and antidotes were essential.

However, Miraidon's body was more complex than Johan had expected.

Its entire body was almost covered with a very sturdy but seemingly not very heavy special metal. From head to tail, there were almost no flesh structures visible—in fact, none at all.

It gave off a feeling like... Mewtwo wearing specially-made Mewtwo armor, full of technological sense.

It was also somewhat like Genesect, which had been modified by Colress—a combination of technological and biological power.

"Would all future world Pokémon become this technological state? Or is this an exception? Or is this just the future of one universe among countless parallel universes?"

Johan felt the possibility that Miraidon belonged to a "parallel universe" future might be higher.

After all, besides Miraidon, there was also Koraidon from ancient times.

It couldn't be that Cyclizar from both past and future were so strong, while Cyclizar of this era were stuck being "transportation tools," right?

He applied healing spray to some obvious places, but the effect was very limited.

He also removed the poison from Miraidon.

Actually, to be honest, Miraidon wasn't severely affected by the toxins from Glimmora and Glimmet. Its current state was fundamentally because its body was already very weak.

A weakness whose cause Johan didn't quite understand.

"Done."

But Miraidon still didn't wake up.

Seeing this, Johan understood.

"Looks like we still need Herba Mystica..."

Fine.

Since he'd obtained Herba Mystica and Johan had originally planned to make food anyway, then... let's make breakfast!

Gengar and the others had already set everything up.

He put on his apron.

"Rotom, oven mode. Ceruledge, light the fire!"

"Understood, Rotom!"

Whenever it was time to make breakfast, his little ones were very cooperative and self-motivated.

Following the game's method would definitely be sandwich standards, at most adding some meat sauce noodles or pasta.

But in Johan's hands...

"Do you want sweet or salty soy milk?" Johan asked.

Cynthia: "Sweet."

Caitlin: "Sweet, of course."

Hearing this, Johan curled his lip. "Heretics!"

Then he silently added fried dough sticks, pickled vegetables, and soy sauce to his own soy milk, while adding sugar to Cynthia and Caitlin's soy milk.

He lightly patted his hands, forgiving himself for his "heretical" behavior.

Soy milk was just one part—there were also fried dough sticks, baked flatbread, and steamed buns.

This was the true breakfast standard!

Ceruledge controlled the temperature of the oil pot. After pressing the kneaded dough and putting it in, fried dough sticks soon rolled up from the oil pot.

Tyranitar built an oven. After rolling the dough pieces into rounds, flattening them, sprinkling sesame seeds and sticking them inside the oven, where Ceruledge had prepared charcoal.

As for the steamed buns, they were the key to this breakfast.

"Herba Mystica" was used by Johan as ordinary seasoning, ground into powder and mixed with minced meat, mushrooms, and other seasonings to make filling, which was wrapped in the buns.

Then steamed in a steamer.

Soon, the aroma of meat buns carrying a peculiar, enticing fragrance spread out.

The little ones were already squatting in front of the steamer with their eating utensils, waiting to eat.

Gengar and Tyranitar were even banging their iron bowls, making "clang clang" sounds.

"Smells so good," Cynthia twitched her little nose.

"What did you put in these buns? They smell so good?" Even Caitlin, who usually liked to argue with Johan, couldn't help asking, carefully swallowing her saliva.

Johan smiled mysteriously.

"Eat slowly. Don't blame me later if you can't finish the buns."

There had probably been people who obtained "Herba Mystica" before, but someone like Johan who used it to make meat buns... was probably the first.

"Kraa..."

Under the temptation of the aroma, the unconscious Miraidon also twitched its eyelids twice before slowly waking up.

As soon as it woke up, its eyes fixed on the steaming basket.

But soon, it quickly calmed down, taking a defensive posture as golden arcs jumped on its body.

However, upon using electricity, Miraidon suddenly discovered...

Its body seemed to have recovered somewhat!

Though weakness remained, the poison it had contracted earlier had been removed.

More importantly...

The group of guys opposite weren't even paying attention to it!

"Kra, kraa?"

Miraidon tentatively called out again.

Still received no response.

Johan was still making breakfast, Cynthia and Caitlin were drinking soy milk and eating fried dough sticks and flatbread, while Gengar and the other Pokémon surrounded the steamer without even glancing at it.

Only the four Cyclizar barely glanced at it.

Miraidon: "..."

Fortunately, Johan finally noticed it after it stood stunned for a few seconds.

"Awake? The buns need a bit more time. Fried dough stick or flatbread? Sweet or salty soy milk?" Johan, wiping his apron with both hands, raised his eyelids and asked.

"Kraa?"

Miraidon tilted its head.

"Alright, salty then. Go wait over there." Johan pointed to beside the picnic table, where a brand-new iron bowl and utensils were placed.

A big question mark appeared above Miraidon's head.

"What are you standing around for? Don't you want to eat? If you don't want to eat, you can leave."

"Kraa!"

Miraidon immediately trotted to the picnic table. Like the four Cyclizar, it obediently waited to be "fed."

Noticing Miraidon's approach, the four Cyclizar still showed some fear.

After a while longer—

"Done, done!" Johan walked to the steamer and dispersed the surrounding little ones.

The noise wasn't small. Miraidon, who was carefully trying the salty soy milk, shuddered and half its head went into the soy milk. When it lifted up, its mouth, nose, and face were all covered with dark yellow soy milk, even with fried dough stick pieces and green onions.

This made Gengar and the others laugh heartily.

Miraidon licked its cheek but showed no anger or embarrassment, appearing extremely calm.

Opening the steamer lid, a milky white steam carrying rich aroma immediately rose. The little ones, including Cynthia, Caitlin, and Miraidon, all fixed their eyes on those snow-white buns.

Johan pinched the soft buns and nodded with satisfaction.

"Limited quantity, no fighting. However much you get is however much. If it's not enough, fill up with Energy Cubes." Johan said.

After speaking, he sighed inwardly.

He didn't know when his highest quality Energy Cubes had become mere stomach-filling rations in these "big eaters'" mouths.

Truly... life had become too comfortable.

Soon, each person and each Pokémon had three large meat buns distributed to their respective bowls.

For Gengar and the others, three meat buns weren't even enough to fill the gaps between their teeth, but for Cynthia and Caitlin, three large meat buns were almost equivalent to their daily rations.

"Kraa!"

Smelling that long-desired aroma, Miraidon also seemed to temporarily lose its composure, grabbing a large bun and stuffing it into its mouth.

Munch munch

After chewing twice, Miraidon's chewing speed immediately accelerated. Johan, who was inconspicuously observing it, could clearly sense that Miraidon's eyes seemed to brighten somewhat.

As if remembering something.

More importantly, after eating food made with "Herba Mystica," Miraidon's weakened state seemed to recover somewhat.

The unsatisfied Miraidon licked its lips and looked back toward the Great Crater of Paldea.

Johan's heart stirred.

Including when Miraidon had rushed out of the Glimmora cave earlier and roared toward the Great Crater of Paldea, this was the second time it had looked toward the Great Crater.

There was definitely something in the Great Crater of Paldea affecting Miraidon. It was just too far away for him and the others to sense it.

And anything happening in the Great Crater of Paldea... definitely meant big trouble.

If it were someone else, they might think about building a good relationship with Miraidon and riding this legendary future Pokémon—riding the cool "violet electric motorcycle."

But for Johan... it was meaningless.

Of course, it definitely wasn't because the "violet electric motorcycle" was too fast.

What a joke.

He'd even ridden Rayquaza—what was so special about a mere Miraidon?

"Are you full? If not, there are Energy Cubes here." Johan said to the group of Pokémon.

Considering Miraidon's displayed personality, he directly took Energy Cubes that Dragonite and Rotom liked and placed them in front of it.

"Kraa..."

Miraidon looked at Johan, looked at the Energy Cubes on the table, and still began eating them with "crunch crunch" sounds.

Although the Energy Cubes didn't have that special aroma of "Herba Mystica," they actually tasted quite good. Miraidon felt its body seemed to recover a bit more.

"Can't eat anymore..." Cynthia rubbed her stomach.

"Me too."

Though very delicious, Johan had indeed made the buns a bit large. Having already drunk soy milk and eaten fried dough sticks and flatbread, eating one more meat bun had far exceeded their food intake.

But there was no worry about waste.

Because Gengar, Dragonite, Tyranitar, and even Garchomp had long been waiting in front of them.

"I admit, your search for those 'Herba Mystica'... has some merit," Caitlin said, hugging Cynthia's arm with a pout.

"Heh."

Johan chuckled softly.

If it was about Pokémon battles, Johan might not have 100% confidence in himself, but when it came to food... he was absolutely confident.

"What about it?" Cynthia gestured toward Miraidon.

After eating breakfast and Energy Cubes, Miraidon had recovered quite well.

It was still weak, of course, but had almost maxed out within the weakened state.

Moreover, when Miraidon first woke up, it was very wary, subtly hostile toward Johan's group and all the Pokémon.

But now Miraidon had dropped its guard.

Look—Gengar was already circling around Miraidon with a curious, examining expression.

Even showing signs of wanting to try riding it...

"Kraa."

Miraidon looked at Gengar circling around it, seemingly guessing what Gengar wanted to do. It didn't show obvious resistance, but from its eyes following Gengar's movement, it was still somewhat unwilling.

"Let's go, Gengar."

Johan, who had finished packing, called to the bold purple little fatty and waved.

"Ka-kaah..."

Gengar hung its head and reluctantly floated toward Johan.

"You already have Cyclizar, and Spectrier too," Johan said, tapping the little fatty's head irritably.

"Ka-kaah!"

Oh right!

Gengar realized.

Right, I still have Spectrier!

It immediately whistled, and Spectrier ran out from Gengar's shadow.

Neighing—

Spectrier raised its hooves, rearing up and neighing.

"Ka-kaah!"

Behold, ghost and horse united!

Johan shook his head and mounted Cyclizar with Cynthia and the others, heading toward their next destination.

Miraidon stood in place, watching them leave.

"Not caring about it anymore?"

Johan smiled: "If it wants to follow, it naturally will. The choice is in its own hands. If it doesn't want to follow, that means it has other business."

Although Miraidon seemed to have dropped its guard, it actually instinctively felt a kind of innate resistance toward this world—or rather, toward creatures outside the Great Crater of Paldea.

That his group could make it not hostile was already quite good.

As a "Pokémon Psychologist," Johan had sufficient authority on this matter.

"It seems to have business."

Even Cynthia could see this.

This time Johan didn't respond.

Business?

What Miraidon had wasn't small business.

Compared to that, even the "Treasures of Ruin" seals could only be considered drizzle.

"Where are we going now?"

"Levincia City," Johan said without hesitation.

This was the most technologically advanced city in the Paldea region. Though not as large as Mesagoza where Naranja Academy was located, it was still the second-largest city with extremely prosperous commerce.

More importantly...

"What are we going there for?"

"To watch Iono's livestream show."

?

"No, to see Levincia City's unique 'Million Volt Night Scene.'"

He'd almost revealed his true thoughts, but fortunately Johan stopped himself in time.

Cynthia looked at him with a half-smile.

Johan wiped the cold sweat from his forehead.

"Kraa..."

Miraidon watched Johan's group leave, also feeling somewhat relieved.

Honestly, besides the two professors, it really didn't know how to interact with other humans and Pokémon.

It had been hesitating earlier—if Gengar wanted to ride it, should it allow it? If Johan invited it, should it go?

Now it didn't need to struggle with that.

However... for some reason, watching Johan's group leave, Miraidon felt a vaguely empty feeling rise in its heart. It didn't know what this feeling was.

"Kraa!"

After glancing toward the Great Crater of Paldea, Miraidon quickly rallied its spirits.

Wait for me!

Miraidon went in the opposite direction from Johan's group.

League Official Website.

As young trainers gradually took over as Champions, Elite Four members, and Gym Leaders, all nine "Master Ball Tier" contestants for the "Pokémon World Championships" were basically confirmed.

Elite Four Agatha, Lance, Johan, Cynthia, Alder, Diantha, the Royal Masked Man, Leon, and Geeta!

Except for Elite Four Agatha and the Royal Masked Man, all were regional Champions.

Additionally, two people served based on their rankings, strength, and points from the previous "Pokémon World Championships."

They were Pryce from the Johto region and Peony from the Paldea region!

Eleven people total.

Currently ranked by points, Leon was in first place!

But because Leon had recently been busy with Galar region and Rose Corporation succession matters, his points were trending toward being caught up by Lance.

As for Johan... though he'd gained some points, he was still at the bottom.

Additionally, currently in "Great Ball Tier," the top five who would successfully advance to "Master Ball Tier" in the "Master Replacement Battle" were:

Raihan, Geeta, Mustard, Lorelei, and Giovanni!

Master Mustard's strength was actually sufficient to replace Peony's position, but... Mustard hadn't participated in the previous "Pokémon World Championships," so he didn't qualify.

Or more accurately, Mustard's joy was in climbing ranks and rankings, not in achieving some final placement.

He would most likely, like in the previous "Pokémon World Championships," voluntarily withdraw after the points stage ended.

Discussion about these "Great Ball Tier" top five was very intense.

Especially two names rarely seen in the public eye: Geeta and Giovanni.

Boss Giovanni was still fine—he'd done several big things with Johan, plus "Rainbow Rocket" gradually appearing in everyone's view, so people had some understanding of him.

But Geeta... when people investigated, they discovered he was actually the chairman of the Paldea region's largest corporation, Outstanding Inc.

No, it shouldn't be called the Paldea region's largest, because Outstanding Inc., like Silph Co. and Devon Corporation, had business covering all regions with wide influence, just not as well-known as Silph Co. and Devon Corporation.

Indeed, few people would think that Outstanding Inc.'s chairman would also participate in this "Pokémon World Championships."

This made the already limited spots extremely tight.

Moreover, besides these people, there were relatively less well-known names gradually rising in the point rankings.

Like N, like Tobias.

Overall, this "Pokémon World Championships" was more intensely competitive than many had expected.

It was also seen by many as the most important link in the generational transition!

And the end date for the "Pokémon World Championships" points stage was getting closer and closer.

# 763: "Gibeon" and Spinel

Paldea region, League Headquarters building, Chairman & Champion office.

Geeta looked seriously at the report in her hands. After carefully scanning it once, she looked at her assistant standing respectfully before her.

"Didn't see who delivered it?" Geeta asked with a frown.

"That's correct." The assistant nodded helplessly. "That location was in a surveillance blind spot. The other party deliberately concealed their identity, and it seems..."

"It seems we also have their people inside," Geeta finished.

The assistant opened his mouth, then finally confirmed what Geeta said, which was actually his guess.

Geeta gently rubbed her temples.

Naranja Academy had become complex due to its close relationship with the Paldea League.

Similarly, the Paldea League had become easier to infiltrate due to its overly close relationship with Naranja Academy.

It could only be said that the intimate relationship between the League and Academy had indeed brought a steady stream of fresh blood to the League, ensuring the Paldea League always had sufficient talent reserves.

But this was a double-edged sword.

Geeta felt helpless about this.

Sighing lightly, Geeta put down the document in her hands.

Its title read: "Regarding the Origins and Status of Outstanding Inc. Chairman Gibeon," briefly describing the deeds of the "Explorers" from over a hundred years ago.

In other words, this person named Gibeon who had suddenly appeared on the "Pokémon World Championships" point rankings had been alive for... over a hundred years!

This shouldn't be a lifespan a normal ordinary person should possess.

Moreover, this hundred-plus-year-old person still wanted to participate in the "Pokémon World Championships"?

Actually, information about Gibeon being Outstanding Inc.'s president was already online, but most people didn't know this Gibeon's true age.

In fact, even Geeta, as League Chairman and Champion, wouldn't have known this without this document.

Because long before she became Champion and Chairman—no, long before she was born—Outstanding Inc. had already existed. It's just that back then, Outstanding Inc. didn't publicly announce its president's identity, always having the director handle things.

Even the Paldea League couldn't investigate Outstanding Inc.'s background without them doing anything outrageous, since... everyone got along fairly well.

Similar to the Hoenn League and Kanto League, Outstanding Inc. also had a place among the Paldea League's upper echelons.

"Chairman Geeta, here's the information Gibeon registered for the 'Master Tournament,'" the assistant handed over another document.

Due to clearance levels, he hadn't seen the one in Geeta's hands.

But when Geeta took it and briefly compared the two documents, she was stunned.

"Not the same person..." Geeta murmured.

"Hmm?" The assistant didn't hear what she said.

Geeta picked up the "Pokémon World Championships" registration information and photo for "Gibeon."

It clearly showed a young man who looked no older than thirty, with light blue hair, wearing a neat dark blue and light blue alternating suit with a small cape hanging from one side.

But based on the descriptions in both documents, these two "Gibeons" were not the same person...

What did this mean?

Outstanding Inc. had someone participate in the "Pokémon World Championships" under the name "Gibeon"?

Then someone secretly delivered information about the real Gibeon...

Internal conflict?

Geeta couldn't even be sure if both documents were real or if both had problems.

"Go investigate this person," Geeta handed the young "Gibeon's" photo to her assistant.

"Yes!" the assistant responded.

If Naranja Academy and the Paldea League's relationship brought "chronic problems," hadn't Outstanding Inc., which had operated for over a hundred years, done the same?

Geeta decided to observe further and see what Outstanding Inc.'s people actually wanted to do.

"Also, Chairman Geeta, here's what you asked me to investigate—the status of wild Pokémon and nearby residents in some areas," the assistant said, handing over the last document.

Geeta said nothing and began flipping through it.

This was an investigation she'd ordered after Teacher Johan captured Wo-Chien, looking into locations that might seal the other three disaster Pokémon.

While flipping through, Geeta asked: "The conclusion?"

The assistant pursed his lips carefully: "There might... indeed be some anomalies."

Geeta's movements paused as she slowly looked up: "Confirmed?"

The assistant nodded. "After analysis... two of the three locations have more or less affected nearby wild Pokémon."

"Sigh..."

She couldn't help but sigh.

Geeta felt mentally exhausted.

"Troubled times indeed..."

"Pokémon World Championships" matters, Outstanding Inc.'s situation, disaster Pokémon threats and influences, plus many lingering problems—even the capable Geeta felt overwhelmed.

"Is Hassel still at Naranja Academy?"

"Yes, after Elite Four Hassel refused that Dragon Tamer family member, he returned to Naranja Academy."

"What about Larry?"

"Elite Four Larry... works normal hours, but recently applied for external assignments, probably 'avoiding' Elite Four Flint from Sinnoh."

Without waiting for Geeta to continue asking, the assistant continued: "Elite Four Rika reportedly went to Cascarrafa Town—something she needed could only be bought there."

Cascarrafa Town was the Paldea region's huge market city. With the recent "Pokémon World Championships," people from around the world entering Paldea had made Cascarrafa's markets particularly bustling.

Many things usually hard to buy could now mostly be found in Cascarrafa.

"Alright. Have Elite Four Larry and Elite Four Rika go to these two locations respectively."

Larry rarely wanted to take external assignments—not letting him run this errand would be unfair to this rare opportunity.

"Also send them copies of the 'Treasures of Ruin' materials Teacher Johan provided."

With Teacher Johan's materials, she believed Elite Four-level strength should be able to handle things.

She'd watched the video Teacher Johan sent.

Wo-Chien displayed extremely strong power, worthy of its legendary status. Teacher Johan's Urshifu was impressively fierce, defeating Wo-Chien despite the "Tablets of Ruin" suppression.

Larry and Rika weren't as powerful as Teacher Johan, but... Elite Four members didn't have just one Pokémon.

With full teams, handling a legendary on the level of Articuno should be possible.

"What about the last location?" the assistant asked.

"I heard Teacher Johan went to Levincia City? And participated in Iono's livestream?"

"Uh, yes." The assistant immediately added: "Very popular, with many viewers."

He'd specifically made time to watch the livestream. Teacher Johan and Miss Cynthia appearing together in Iono's stream essentially announced their relationship, directly igniting the entire chat.

Many people were "speculating" that Teacher Johan plus Miss Cynthia, Hoenn League plus Sinnoh League, meant overthrowing the "rule" of the Kanto and Johto Leagues!

"Levincia City is closer to that last location, right? We'll have to trouble Teacher Johan and Miss Cynthia again."

With the "Pokémon World Championships" points stage winding down, Geeta wished she could clone herself—she felt understaffed.

"I'm afraid... you'll need to contact Teacher Johan personally, Chairman," the assistant said.

After all, they were two Champions who weren't even from their Paldea League. Even with the best tone and attitude, an assistant contacting them would seem impolite.

Hearing this, Geeta smiled slightly and waved: "No need. Send Teacher Johan's expense report to him, add 10% to the reimbursed items, then put the third location's information in the reimbursement materials. He'll understand."

The assistant was stunned.

Did the Chairman have such rapport with Teacher Johan?

Speaking of which... he'd heard that Hoenn League's Champion and Elite Four were all top-tier "reimbursement experts," traveling the world with "expense reports."

Of course, this was just hearsay—he didn't know who started it...

"Also," Geeta put down the report, crossed her hands, pondered briefly, then continued: "We've found two 'Herba Mystica,' right? Send them all to Teacher Johan, and have people hurry to collect the rest."

"Understood."

After handling everything, Geeta tiredly waved her hand, signaling her assistant to get to work.

The assistant nodded and left.

After the assistant left, Geeta picked up the two documents about "Gibeon" again, then opened her computer showing several cases of Pokémon suddenly becoming stronger but losing control.

Out of intuition as League Chairman, Geeta felt these incidents might be connected.

Frowning, a thought suddenly struck her.

Handling out-of-control Pokémon... wasn't this Teacher Johan's specialty?

Paldea region, Levincia City.

Johan signed rapidly, accepting the batch of reimbursed supplies sent by the Paldea League.

The quantity was at least 10% more than what he'd requested!

As the "Reimbursement Champion" with boldness second only to Elite Four Drake, this was Johan's first time encountering such a situation.

Did I request too little?

No... Johan was confident about quantities.

Moreover, not only was the quantity 10% more, there were also two additional "Herba Mystica."

Bitter and Sour varieties respectively.

If he remembered correctly, these should be from Titan Bombirdier and... Great Tusk or Iron Treads.

"So thoughtful..." Johan gently rubbed his chin. "Must want something in return."

Sure enough.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar, stuffing things into its "treasure bag," pulled out a paper from its mouth.

"I knew it..."

Looking at the paper, Johan understood Geeta's meaning.

She wanted them to go to North Province Area Two to deal with another possibly awakening "Treasures of Ruin."

This reimbursement was just this once—next time would be separate. This was just showing sincerity.

There would be additional compensation after completion.

Plus help finding the "Herba Mystica."

"Helping out on the side isn't impossible."

Having already "handled" Wo-Chien, Johan didn't find "Treasures of Ruin" Pokémon particularly troublesome.

As they say... there's only zero times and countless times.

Since he was already "raising" one, one more wouldn't make much difference.

"Let's head out once Cynthia and the others arrive."

Looking at this technologically advanced, developed, and prosperous city, Johan couldn't muster much interest or goodwill.

He didn't know why, but now he preferred the outdoors, natural environments, and the lively, cute wild Pokémon.

"Ka-kaah?"

Not here yet?

Gengar struck a watching pose, looking toward the busy street.

Originally, the group had been together.

But because Johan needed to receive supplies from the Paldea League while Caitlin dragged Cynthia shopping, they'd temporarily separated.

"Ka-kaah!"

They're here!

Sharp-eyed Gengar spotted them.

But besides Cynthia and Caitlin, Gengar also noticed...

"Ka-kaah!"

There's a man!

A man?

Johan raised an eyebrow, following Gengar's gaze.

Beside Cynthia and Caitlin—one tall, one short, both blonde—was a man with light blue hair wearing a dark blue and light blue suit with a cape on one shoulder, smiling warmly with handsome features.

Hmm?

This person... seemed somewhat familiar.

Where had he seen him before...

While Johan pondered, Cynthia, Caitlin, and the man had reached him.

"So it really is Teacher Johan. I could only see you on TV before—finally meeting you in person," the light blue-haired man said with a smile.

"Hello," Johan's face also showed a smile with little emotional change. "May I ask who you are..."

"Sorry, I was too excited seeing Teacher Johan and forgot to introduce myself. I'm 'Gibeon,'" the light blue-haired man calling himself 'Gibeon' introduced.

Gibeon?

Johan's heart stirred.

"Gibeon, Paldea region Outstanding Inc. president, currently 'Great Ball Tier'... second place, Rotom!"

The Rotom Pokédex floated out, displaying "Gibeon's" photo while introducing the man's information.

Hearing this, Johan finally remembered who this man was.

Gibeon was indeed Outstanding Inc.'s president, even one of the "Explorers" founders from over a hundred years ago.

But definitely not this man before him.

His name should be Spinel!

A top researcher at Outstanding Inc. and a powerful trainer.

However, though his strength was decent... it shouldn't let him become "Great Ball Tier" second place in the "points stage."

Spinel could barely compete with Friede.

Johan had briefly glanced at the "Great Ball Tier" point rankings. Spinel, calling himself "Gibeon," was second only to Raihan, ranking above Mustard, Lorelei, and Boss Giovanni!

Probably fought "fixed matches."

Though the "Pokémon World Championships" points stage had Rotom drone livestreams, that didn't prevent fixed matches.

With Outstanding Inc.'s scale and the "Explorers'" strength, there were plenty of ways to quickly boost Spinel's ranking.

Moreover, Spinel was someone with a cunning, changeable personality, skilled at scheming. Despite ordinary origins, he'd climbed from a small Outstanding Inc. researcher to become one of chief Gibeon's most trusted subordinates.

He'd even managed to kick Gibeon's own grandson out of the "Explorers" core.

This level of scheming wasn't something ordinary people could achieve.

Unfortunately, when Johan came to the Pokémon world, "Pokémon Horizons" hadn't finished, so he didn't know that later, after Gibeon's fall, Spinel had gained control of the "Explorers" as chief and Outstanding Inc. as president within a year, becoming a major figure in the Paldea region!

All through his so-called schemes and planning.

Additionally, under the guise of Gibeon's goal to find "Rakua elements," Spinel secretly pursued his "enhancement ball" research. After usurping power, he promoted "enhancement balls" under Outstanding Inc.'s name.

Johan really hadn't expected Spinel to connect with Cynthia and Caitlin.

Though Outstanding Inc.'s headquarters was indeed located in Levincia City.

Seemingly noticing Johan's confusion, Cynthia gave him a slightly helpless look.

"Heh, just lucky, just lucky," Spinel said modestly after hearing the Rotom Pokédex's introduction.

"I wonder what Mr. 'Gibeon' needs?" Johan pretended not to recognize Spinel's identity, maintaining his smile and asking gently.

"What a coincidence..." Spinel showed some embarrassment: "Actually, I'm a fan of Teacher Johan."

Pfft—

Johan didn't believe this one bit.

But Spinel continued: "So, if possible, could Teacher Johan, Miss Cynthia, and Miss Caitlin consider honorary positions at our Outstanding Inc.?"

Before Johan could speak, he quickly explained.

"Of course, just honorary titles with no business requirements or work obligations, not even location restrictions to the Paldea region."

Such honorary positions weren't uncommon in the Pokémon world.

For example, Leon had previously held a special battle advisor title at Rose's Macro Cosmos, Johan had a special advisor title at Devon Corporation, and even old man Drake had a position at Devon.

No responsibilities or obligations required—they just wanted the name.

But... those were cases where both sides knew each other well. Having the title or not didn't really matter.

But Spinel's behavior was clearly...

Spinel smiled and held up three fingers.

"Three hundred million Pokédollars."

How much?!

Johan's eyes widened slightly, his expression changing somewhat.

Spinel added: "Per year."

Three hundred million Pokédollars annually?

So wealthy?

This made Johan want to "raid" Outstanding Inc.

His Devon position, after becoming Champion, only got a salary increase to twenty million Pokédollars per year from old man Joseph.

After all, it was just honorary—taking more would make Johan feel bad.

This was an opening offer of three hundred million Pokédollars!

Did Outstanding Inc. have that much liquid capital?

Well... it was a century-old giant that had existed longer than Silph Co. and Devon Corporation.

Moreover, there was the "Explorers," a "military organization," cooperating with them.

Caitlin glared at Johan.

Johan also snapped back to reality.

"Cough."

Out of respect for money, he coughed and said politely: "Next time, definitely next time."

Johan gave Cynthia and Caitlin a look.

"We have other matters, so we'll take our leave."

With that, the three turned and left.

Watching their departing figures, Spinel's smile disappeared, replaced by some gloom.

"Johan, Cynthia..." Spinel muttered quietly. "...What a pity."

He turned to leave as well.

But as soon as he turned, he saw a chubby Gengar with its hand cupped to its ear, silently pressed against his side listening.

Spinel: ?!

"You!"

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar scratched its butt and shook its head.

Pity... why didn't you say more?

If you'd said just a bit more, I could've given you a "wonderful" night tonight.

No fun.

Hands behind its head, pouting and whistling, it hopped away.

Watching Gengar leave, Spinel opened his mouth, momentarily speechless.

After standing stunned for a while, he sighed and turned around.

Then... he saw Gengar again, who had clearly left earlier.

Gengar: (ノ)

Spinel: ╬!

Veins bulged on his forehead.

"Ka-kaah~ Ka-kaah~"

Gotta run, gotta run~

Gengar slipped into shadows and disappeared.

This time Spinel didn't believe it, very cautiously looking around before finally heading back to Outstanding Inc.

This day, he was destined to find Gengar under his covers at midnight.

"That person really is..."

Once they left Levincia City, Caitlin couldn't help complaining about Spinel.

He'd "stuck" to them without asking, acting overly familiar.

Though Caitlin didn't understand psychology, as a psychic, she could somewhat sense goodwill versus malice.

From Spinel, Caitlin felt uncomfortable—very uncomfortable!

So she hadn't given Spinel any good attitude.

Though Cynthia hadn't been as direct as Caitlin, her earlier look to Johan said everything.

"Compared to him, Johan is actually a good person!" Caitlin muttered.

Johan: "..."

Thanks a lot.

Comparing me to him.

And what do you mean "actually"?

Also, why are you randomly giving out "good person cards"?

Unable to complain about Caitlin, Johan shook his head: "This person isn't simple. Better to avoid contact in the future."

"Mm." Cynthia nodded obediently.

"I even suspect his identity is fake—'Gibeon' isn't him," Johan warned.

"League identities can be faked?" Caitlin asked in surprise.

Johan glanced at her.

Why couldn't League identities be faked?

Team Rocket's highest executive serving as a League Elite Four—you haven't seen that, have you?

With ability and strength, faking was simple.

Just wondering... what was Spinel trying to do by participating in the "Pokémon World Championships" under "Gibeon's" identity...

# 764: Gengar's "Empathy"

Paldea region, East Province Area Three.

"Cha!"

Tinkaton leaped high, its metal-gleaming hammer crashing down heavily.

Boom!!

In an instant, massive clouds of sand exploded from the sandy ground. A huge Pokémon crashed heavily into the dust, falling unconscious and losing its fighting ability!

It was a Pokémon that looked like an earthworm but was countless times larger, orange-red overall with a metallic sheen, its entire body wrapped in a metal shell.

Orthworm.

A Steel-type worm Pokémon unique to the Paldea region that liked living in dry deserts, feeding on sand and metals in soil layers, with a special obsession for metal.

In this regard, it was quite similar to Tinkaton.

However, this Orthworm was dozens of times larger than ordinary Orthworm—it could even compare to Elite Four Bruno's giant Onix.

Clearly, this was a Titan Pokémon—the Lurking Steel Titan Orthworm!

Now Tinkaton stood on Orthworm's head with its hammer, pouting.

"Cha..."

Looking at the dent in the Titan Orthworm's head created by its hammer.

Your metal armor isn't that great either.

And you dared to covet my hammer?

"Tinkaton," Johan held his forehead. "Why did you knock it out directly?"

"Cha!"

Only then did Tinkaton finally react, scratching the back of its head with an embarrassed smile.

Got too excited and forgot...

Originally, Johan's group had entered East Province Area Three to find this Lurking Steel Titan Orthworm—more precisely, to find the "Herba Mystica" it guarded.

They'd expected it to take some time, like with the Klawf Titan, not knowing where it stayed without getting close.

Who knew that upon entering this desert area, before Tyranitar could even act, a large group of Orthworm had surrounded them.

They'd set their sights on Tinkaton's hammer.

After all, Tinkaton's hammer was crafted with enormous effort from Tinkaton, plus Johan's massive funding and resources, along with precious metals obtained through various fortuitous encounters.

Truly speaking, Tinkaton's hammer could be said to have material quality comparable to the sword and shield of Galar region's Zacian and Zamazenta.

This "level" of metal hammer's appeal to metal-eating Pokémon like Orthworm was obvious.

Sure enough, even that giant Titan Orthworm had been lured out.

This saved Johan considerable time searching for it.

It's just that Tinkaton hadn't controlled its strength well, directly knocking out the Titan Orthworm.

Didn't that mean he still had to search for the "Herba Mystica" himself?

"Sni!"

Fortunately, Snivy indicated it might be able to find it.

The scent of "Herba Mystica" lingered on the Titan Orthworm, and with its plant perception abilities, it should roughly be able to find the direction.

Soon, under Snivy's search, they spent a bit more time and indeed found the Titan Orthworm's nesting cave and the gray-white "Herba Mystica" growing within.

Its surface was wrapped in something similar to "Salt Lord's" salt deposits, emitting a faint glow.

This was the Salty "Herba Mystica."

Same old routine.

They picked most of the leaves and a second-thickest branch section, completing the "Herba Mystica" collection.

Those sent by Geeta had been handled similarly according to Johan's wishes.

This way, they'd collected four of the five types of "Herba Mystica."

But besides "Herba Mystica," Tinkaton, Gengar, and Tyranitar had also discovered "treasure" in the Titan Orthworm's cave!

Some metals collected by the Titan Orthworm and other Orthworm!

Probably the Orthworm's food reserves.

Most were ordinary metals collected from sand and soil layers, which current Tinkaton no longer valued.

Now ordinary metals couldn't even arouse Tinkaton's desire to strike them.

Because with its current hammer quality, such ordinary metals didn't even qualify to be hammered into its weapon.

However, there were also some unknown, badly damaged metals of various strange shapes.

Tinkaton and its little follower, Meltan attached to the hammer, found several metal fragments that should be quite good quality.

Of course, they also took some ordinary metals—about half.

Tinkaton didn't need them, but Meltan still did, serving as its usual snacks.

"Remember to leave some Energy Cubes as 'compensation' after taking their things," Johan reminded.

Don't let the Orthworm population fight due to insufficient food reserves from taking their stored rations—just compensate with some Energy Cubes.

Tinkaton was quite sensible about this and had already placed them.

"Let's go. Maybe next there'll be metals you'll find satisfactory."

"Cha?!" Tinkaton was delighted, with little follower Meltan cheering.

Paldea region, North Province Area Two.

Splash splash—

The waterfall's water rushed fiercely downward from the cliff like a white silk ribbon crossing the mountain valley.

In the dense bamboo forest, numerous wild Pokémon lived, but most hid deep within the bamboo.

"This is one of the 'Ten Scenic Spots of Paldea'?" Cynthia asked softly, looking at the waterfall.

Honestly, the waterfall was quite spectacular, but having seen bigger, more impressive waterfalls, Johan didn't feel much about it.

For size, it couldn't compare to "Huangguoshu." For height, it couldn't match "Shengziyan."

Indeed, after seeing more magnificent scenery, this level seemed insufficient.

However, Rotom continuously photographed this "Ten Scenic Spots of Paldea" waterfall without stopping.

When it finally finished and looked back... all the photos showed Gengar!

Grinning Gengar, peace-sign Gengar, tongue-out Gengar...

"Rotom!!" Rotom gnashed its teeth in frustration.

"Decent enough, not bad."

Speaking, he looked toward the cliff above the waterfall. "If nothing unexpected happens, this disaster Pokémon should be up there."

Geeta had already sent people to investigate. Though they hadn't pinpointed the exact location, based on affected wild Pokémon, they could roughly lock down the seal's location.

"I heard point climbing has been fierce recently. I need to go back and climb points, or I might not even qualify for the 'Master Replacement Battle,'" Caitlin muttered.

Johan and Cynthia were both Champions and predetermined "Master Ball Tier" contestants. Even if they didn't climb points—even if Johan was at the bottom of "Master Ball Tier"—it didn't matter.

But as an Elite Four member, she was different.

As a "Great Ball Tier" contestant, she needed to climb more points to qualify for the "Master Replacement Battle."

Actually, with Caitlin's personality, she didn't really care about these so-called competitions. Whether she won or what her final ranking was didn't really matter.

But in the "Master Replacement Battle," she could challenge Johan!

Last time she lost to Johan and lost Cynthia too, which had bothered Caitlin for a long time.

So she was determined to work hard to reach the "Master Replacement Battle" and then challenge Johan!

Not to show others how great she was, but to tell others that what she'd lost, she would definitely reclaim!

Looking at the "golden-haired loser" silently grinding her teeth while thinking about something, Johan said:

"There are plenty of Elite Four who don't make the 'Master Replacement Battle'..."

Not everyone was like Flint or Raihan, willing to constantly challenge stronger opponents.

Some, like Byron, were Elite Four more passionate about other careers.

But halfway through, Johan quickly added:

"But I still admire your persistence, so if you want to go, then go for it!"

Please go!

Originally wanting to properly tour the Paldea region with his little Cynthia, experiencing Paldea's customs and culture, but she had to come be a "light bulb"—the brightest kind.

Since leaving Medali Town, Johan hadn't even held Cynthia's hand much!

How could he deepen their relationship?

How could they progress to hugging, kissing, and more intimate moments?

With Caitlin hugging Cynthia's arm constantly and sleeping in the same tent at night, Johan felt like a lonely old man.

"Mm, Caitlin can definitely do it," Cynthia smiled, gently stroking Caitlin's head with a comforting, supportive expression.

"Meow~"

Caitlin immediately closed her eyes contentedly.

Those who didn't know might think she was Cynthia's Pokémon.

The cliff was very high. Obviously Cyclizar couldn't go up there, so they had to rely on Dragonite and Garchomp to carry the three to the clifftop.

At the summit was a large lake, partially exposed and partially connected to the mountain interior. The waterfall's water originated from this lake.

"Roar!!"

As soon as they arrived, they saw a fierce Gyarados battling a Golduck in the lake.

The two Water-type Pokémon created huge waves in the lake, with rain from Rain Dance pattering on the lake surface.

However, the two Pokémon's battle was nearing its end.

Gyarados's final Hydro Pump successfully hit Golduck, washing it toward the waterfall's edge.

Though the injured Golduck hadn't lost fighting ability, it knew it was no longer Gyarados's match. After glaring fiercely at Gyarados, it swam down the waterfall.

"Roar—!!!"

The victorious Gyarados roared skyward, freely displaying its violent aura.

Then, seemingly noticing Johan's group's presence, its bell-like red eyes glared at them, showing pure ferocity.

Logically, wild Gyarados having violent temperaments was normal, and wild Pokémon fighting over territory was also normal.

But this Gyarados was clearly wrapped in negative energy that couldn't be hidden under Johan's aura.

"Rotom, Thunderbolt. Put it to sleep."

Before Gyarados could attack them, Johan chose to strike first.

"Rotom!"

Zap—

Rotom, who hadn't acted in a while, shot out electricity. Naturally, the 4× Electric-weak Gyarados couldn't withstand such an attack, immediately rolling its eyes and losing fighting ability.

"Let's go inside."

Ninetales formed ice beneath their feet.

Following the lake water into the cave.

The advantage of Gyarados being here was that there were almost no other Pokémon in the water. They encountered no wild Pokémon attacks walking from outside to inside the cave, saving considerable trouble.

The cave's interior space grew larger as they went deeper.

Clang!!

When Johan's group reached the cave's depths, metallic collision sounds came from within.

On the cave's platform, three Bisharp leading a group of Pawniard were besieging a Kingambit.

Blade clashed with blade, accompanied by scattering sparks.

"Bisharp besieging Kingambit?" Caitlin was stunned seeing this scene.

"For the 'Leader's Crest' on it," Johan was also somewhat surprised but quickly understood.

The seal stone for the "Treasures of Ruin" was behind the platform where the Bisharp were located.

At such close distance, being affected by the disaster Pokémon sealed within was too normal.

As for "Leader's Crest," this was a special item needed for Bisharp evolution.

Generally speaking, some powerful Bisharp that had been honed over long periods would have the blades on their heads forged into something called "Leader's Crest" after gathering some Pawniard.

Usually such Bisharp already had decent strength and subordinates, enabling them to compete with other Bisharp.

By seizing other Bisharp's "Leader's Crest" and their subordinates, and collecting three "Leader's Crest," they could evolve into Kingambit!

So in the wild, Bisharp evolving into Kingambit was extremely difficult—as difficult as Galar region's Farfetch'd evolving into Sirfetch'd.

Now, these three Bisharp, clearly Kingambit's subordinates, were leading Pawniard in besieging Kingambit. In "Young and Dangerous" terms, this betrayal of the boss would warrant being stabbed six times.

"Pok!"

Kingambit was a brute, half-kneeling while swinging the giant blade atop its head, sending two Bisharp flying.

But another Bisharp still left a pale scar on Kingambit's body.

"Jealousy..." Cynthia said softly.

She saw jealousy in those Bisharp and Pawniard's eyes—thick jealous emotions.

That's right.

Jealousy.

Whether the three Bisharp or that group of Pawniard, they all envied Kingambit for evolving to the final stage, being able to command a group of followers just by giving random orders.

"Chi-Yu..." Johan said quietly.

Wo-Chien brought negative emotions of hatred.

Chi-Yu brought negative emotions of jealousy!

Chi-Yu was a disaster Pokémon Johan had used for some time in "Pokémon Battle" games and was quite familiar with.

"Gengar, let them sleep for a while," Johan said.

Actually, without being told, Gengar had already appeared beside those Bisharp, Pawniard, and Kingambit.

Usually playful Gengar now had a face full of anger.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar shouted loudly, and all the Bisharp, Pawniard, and even Kingambit instinctively looked at it.

They saw Gengar "fiercely" pointing at those Bisharp and Pawniard.

"Ka-kaah! Ka-kaah!"

As "subordinates," how dare you betray and besiege your "boss"! You're unworthy of being "subordinates"!

"Pok!"

Kingambit nearly shed tears after hearing this.

A kindred spirit!

However, before Kingambit could invite Gengar to help, Gengar turned to face Kingambit with even more anger.

"Ka-kaah!"

And you!

You're even worse!

How did you become "boss"?

As a "boss," you can't even take care of your "subordinates," forcing them to betray and besiege you. You're completely unworthy of being boss!

For a moment, Gengar pointed at Kingambit, Bisharp, and Pawniard while frantically "outputting," quite the stance of "one against many" and "debating the scholars."

This made Kingambit and the others forget about fighting.

"What is Gengar..." Caitlin looked at this scene with complete bewilderment. "What is it doing?"

Wasn't it supposed to put them to sleep?

How did it start arguing?

No, it was Gengar unilaterally "outputting."

Johan turned his head, unable to bear watching.

Could only say... Gengar was "empathizing."

As the "Outlaw Gang Boss," as the "boss" of stray Pokémon in Rustboro City, seeing this scene, Gengar had put itself in their position.

Think about it—Gengar had many "subordinates" not just in Rustboro City but in many places.

Those guys were absolutely "obedient" to it, but Gengar considered itself quite good to them. Throughout Rustboro City, whenever any disturbance occurred, Gengar could know everything within five minutes.

Yet here was fellow "boss" Kingambit being besieged by subordinates?

This was simply disgracing all "bosses"!

However, after being lectured, Kingambit and the Bisharp came to their senses and immediately wanted to tear apart Gengar's chattering mouth.

But just as they were about to exert force, they felt their bodies weaken.

Then one by one, they rolled their eyes and collapsed, losing fighting ability!

Seeing this scene, Johan's mouth twitched. After a long while, he slowly uttered one word:

"Perish Song..."

Combining lecturing with Perish Song—Gengar had pioneered a new use for Perish Song?

Mental and physical double damage.

Trash Talk Song?

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar crossed its arms and snorted white puffs from its nose, angrily turning away.

Though the Bisharp had all fallen, Gengar still felt unsatisfied.

Being "brothers" to this extent—you guys are something else!

Suddenly, Gengar's eyes rolled.

It showed a mischievous smile.

It confiscated all the "weapons" of the fallen Kingambit, Bisharp, and Pawniard!

Especially Kingambit's great blade.

This was the "Leader's Crest."

"Ka-kaah!"

Let's see how you fight now!

I'll have Tinkaton forge me a suit of armor with these later!

Decided happily!

"Alright, alright, Gengar, calm down. See if you can unseal this?" Johan soothed.

The loosening degree of this seal differed from Wo-Chien's. Only after Wo-Chien's revival had this seal begun loosening, but clearly the affected range wasn't large, indicating the seal's loosening wasn't severe.

Perhaps forcing it open would be quite troublesome.
