# 765: Capture, Chi-Yu!

"Ka-kaah~"

Looking at the circular seal stone tablet before it, Gengar gently rubbed its chubby chin.

Its nose twitched lightly.

Its eyes brightened.

It quickly found the crack where negative energy was seeping from the seal and located the seal's weak side.

But even finding the seal's weak side, there were quite a few wedges on the iron chains. Gengar wasn't sure which one to pull to unlock the seal.

"The third one."

At this moment, Cynthia reminded.

Hmm?

Johan and Gengar looked at their little Cynthia with some surprise.

Cynthia smiled sweetly. "I barely deciphered some of the previous seal stone tablet. This one is similar. With Gengar pointing out the general location of the loosening... it should be correct."

This was mythologist Cynthia?

Worthy of being a Champion who specialized in historical research.

Caitlin hugged Cynthia's arm, proudly raising her chin as if she had deciphered the seal herself.

However, Johan had no intention of contradicting Caitlin, instead saying with a smile:

"Worthy of my little Cynthia."

"Mm..." Cynthia's cheeks reddened slightly, while Caitlin's expression immediately took a sharp downturn, her face flushing red with anger.

Seeing Caitlin's expression change, Johan smirked.

What a joke—what competitiveness could a mere "golden-haired loser" have?

At the same time, at Cynthia's indication, Gengar pulled the third wedge from the iron chain on the seal's loose side.

Jingle jingle—

With the wedge's fall, the entire iron chain immediately loosened and fell.

Then—

Rumble—

The entire circular seal stone tablet made rumbling sounds. Large amounts of power filled with jealous negative emotions surged from behind the seal, and "bang bang" sounds came from behind the stone tablet.

It seemed that compared to Wo-Chien's snail temperament, the disaster Pokémon sealed here was undoubtedly much more irritable.

With one corner of the seal opened, Gengar, at Cynthia's indication, tried three times before finally removing another wedge from another iron chain.

At this point, the seal was mostly removed.

Boom!!

Another heavy rumbling, and the huge circular seal stone tablet fell away.

The next second, a scorching, restless aura immediately spread and swept over, as if an invisible, unseen "jealous fire" surged from within the seal.

Then they saw a Pokémon only as long as an arm, shaped like a goldfish, with most of its body orange-yellow and its tail and fins flame-red like fire, swimming out from the cave behind the stone tablet with very agile movements.

Its most special and eye-catching feature was undoubtedly its eyes.

More precisely, the two seemingly incomplete jade pieces surrounding its eyes.

Chi-Yu!

This was Chi-Yu, representing the "jade" among the "Treasures of Ruin"!

Its appearance was small and its movements agile. Even in the air, it could swim freely as if in water.

It looked harmless, but as its tail swayed gently, that invisible jealous fire surged again. A mysterious power immediately spread from Chi-Yu as the center, covering and affecting Gengar and the others.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar tightly covered its chest, looking "uncomfortable."

This time, it wasn't entirely an act.

Because Chi-Yu's ability was "Beads of Ruin."

[Beads of Ruin: When a Pokémon with this ability is on the field, all Pokémon except those with 'Beads of Ruin' have their Special Defense reduced to three-quarters!]

Similar to Wo-Chien's "Tablets of Ruin," except "Tablets of Ruin" suppressed Attack ability while "Beads of Ruin" suppressed Special Defense ability.

So this time Gengar feeling "uncomfortable" was quite real.

Not just Gengar—even Caitlin, with her powerful psychic abilities, felt her psychic power being suppressed.

Compared to Wo-Chien's sluggishness, though Chi-Yu had also been sealed for so long, it appeared very agile and proactive.

Especially when it saw Gengar.

Due to the power of "ancient jade," Chi-Yu was very sensitive to Pokémon's Special Attack abilities.

So it immediately sensed from Gengar the Special Attack ability that made it envious and jealous.

Therefore, while Gengar was "uncomfortable," Chi-Yu launched an attack without hesitation.

Malicious energy quickly gathered before Chi-Yu, forming a pitch-black wave that surged toward Gengar.

Dark Pulse!

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar, who was covering its chest, blinked and immediately returned to normal.

Attacking me?

Gengar waved lightly at Chi-Yu, then leaped into the shadows in a diving posture and disappeared.

"Garchomp, Earthquake!"

Cynthia waved her hand, and a fierce "god of death" immediately charged out, its powerful feet heavily stomping the ground.

Boom!!

An earthy yellow shock wave immediately spread rapidly in all directions from Garchomp as the center. The entire ground, the entire cave, and even the lake water within all shook.

Though Chi-Yu floated in mid-air, when Earthquake's power gathered beneath it, the ground exploded open, and raised earth layers and rocks rushed toward it.

This wasn't the first time dealing with "Treasures of Ruin" Pokémon. Having lost the "novelty," Johan and Cynthia didn't insist on civilized one-on-one combat with Chi-Yu.

Quick victory and problem resolution were the objectives.

However... though this cave was fairly sturdy and Garchomp's main target was also Chi-Yu, if such earthquakes came a few more times, Johan's group might end up buried like Chi-Yu today.

"Tyranitar, Sandstorm."

Johan also spoke up.

He didn't have Gengar fight Chi-Yu, instead choosing Tyranitar, which had better synergy with Garchomp.

"Bangi!!"

Rolling sand dust immediately spread through the spacious cave.

Most of the sand dust, under Tyranitar's control, adhered to the surrounding rock walls and ground beneath their feet, providing some degree of reinforcement.

At the same time, Garchomp's Earthquake didn't hit Chi-Yu.

Though with Earthquake's special technique, Garchomp's Earthquake could attack Chi-Yu floating in mid-air, Chi-Yu was very alert and quickly gained altitude when the earthquake shock waves spread.

Therefore, Chi-Yu successfully avoided it before Garchomp's Earthquake could hit.

But the ensuing Sandstorm still made Chi-Yu uncomfortable.

The rolling sand dust blocked vision, and these sand particles felt like sharp little knives scraping painfully against its body.

But for Garchomp and Tyranitar, Sandstorm brought many benefits.

"Shaka!"

When Tyranitar's Sandstorm spread, Garchomp was ready. It immediately swung its scythe, rolling sharp sand particles to envelop Chi-Yu.

Sand Tomb!

Whirlpool-like sand particles formed a sandstorm tornado, making the already uncomfortable Chi-Yu even more uncomfortable. Sand constantly battered its body, impossible to shake off.

"Tyranitar, Rock Slide."

While Chi-Yu was restricted within a certain range by Garchomp's Sand Tomb, Tyranitar immediately grabbed rocks that had crumbled from the earlier earthquake and hurled them heavily at Chi-Yu.

Bang!!

The rock hit Chi-Yu head-on from a completely unexpected angle, the rock shattering into pieces.

Along with pain came Chi-Yu's fury.

"Tch."

Seeing this, Johan clicked his tongue.

Even hitting its head like that didn't cause Chi-Yu to flinch.

Indeed, he couldn't trust his own luck.

But after Tyranitar's Rock Slide drew Chi-Yu's attention, Garchomp immediately appeared before Chi-Yu at Cynthia's signal, striking down with dragon claws condensed from blue-purple energy.

Bang—

Chi-Yu's small body was heavily slammed against the rock wall.

It had to be said—Johan and Cynthia commanding their respective Pokémon, despite having no communication between them and this being their first time commanding Garchomp and Tyranitar together in battle, showed extremely deep coordination.

Perfect cooperation between them.

When Garchomp dispersed Chi-Yu's attention, Tyranitar would launch fierce attacks. When Tyranitar drew Chi-Yu's attention, Garchomp could make Chi-Yu pay a heavy price.

Moreover, as the "Desert Tyrant" Tyranitar and "Land Dragon" Garchomp had very compatible synergy, their cooperation left Chi-Yu completely disoriented.

"Glub glub..."

Chi-Yu, spitting flame-like bubbles from its mouth after such a beating, couldn't even find its opponents. Fury instantly flooded its mind.

Boom!!

Its small form immediately surged with scorching black-red flames, carrying thick negative energy that burst outward.

The Sand Tomb surrounding it and the sand dust filling the entire cave were immediately scattered by this power, creating a vacuum zone filled with negative energy within the cave.

But this was just the beginning.

That fury mixed with jealous fire's negative energy rampaged wildly. Both Garchomp and Tyranitar sensed the threat within.

Ruination!

Like Wo-Chien, as a disaster Pokémon, Chi-Yu also possessed the move Ruination, which could forcibly reduce an opponent's HP by half.

"Tyranitar."

"Garchomp."

Johan and Cynthia spoke almost simultaneously.

But their respective actions were completely different.

Tyranitar, stepping on sand at considerable speed, immediately rushed before Garchomp and roared.

"Bangi!"

Its tail struck the ground, and immediately thick, sharp rock spikes rose from the earth. Sand dust and Stone Edge's rock spikes quickly constructed a hard and sturdy wall.

Under Sandstorm, Rock-type Pokémon's Special Defense increased by 1.5 times!

Sandstorm could enhance Pokémon's Special Defense abilities.

Though Chi-Yu's Ruination was unreasonable, it was still a Special Attack category.

Boom!!

Negative energy struck the "wall," with most of the force blocked.

At the same time, while Tyranitar constructed the "wall," Garchomp calmly sharpened its scythe as if no one else was around. Accompanied by metallic clanging, its momentum rapidly climbed!

Swords Dance!

When Ruination's impact gradually faded, Garchomp suddenly shot skyward, wearing a blue-purple fierce dragon cloak.

Dragon Rush!

Though Johan and Cynthia spoke simultaneously, Garchomp and Tyranitar, understanding both their intentions, didn't simultaneously defend or attack.

One defended, one powered up.

Perfect coordination!

With Tyranitar's resistance, the Swords Dance-enhanced Garchomp launched Dragon Rush without hesitation, heavily impacting Chi-Yu.

Even though Dragon-type had no advantage against Chi-Yu, Dragon Rush after Swords Dance enhancement was no joke.

Boom!!

Chi-Yu, sent flying by Garchomp again, was somewhat confused.

No way.

You two... why do you coordinate so well?!

Why was it all alone?

Roar!!!

Scorching flames immediately rose from Chi-Yu's body. All its power and the jealous fire in its heart became substantial flames—surging, scorching, filled with destructive aura.

Overheat!

One of Fire-type's strongest finishing moves, with unparalleled instant explosive power.

But after using Overheat, that Pokémon's Special Attack would decrease by 2 stages!

For any Pokémon relying on Special Attack for battle, this was a very deadly blow.

Almost equivalent to being unable to fight back without defeating the opponent.

Somewhat similar to Dragon-type's Draco Meteor or Grass-type's Leaf Storm.

As Chi-Yu channeled all its power to raise flames, a huge figure directly stepped into those surging flames.

When Chi-Yu's jade-surrounded eyes looked up, it saw...

A huge foot wrapped in rich Ground-type energy stomping toward its face.

A foot?

Where did this foot come from?

Boom!!

Stomp!

The heavily falling foot directly stomped Chi-Yu into the sand dust accumulated on the ground, stomping it underground.

"Bangi!!"

Tyranitar roared.

The owner of this foot was undoubtedly Tyranitar. Since the earlier Stone Edge as defense hadn't hit Chi-Yu, this Stomp's power was directly doubled!

One stomp crushed Chi-Yu's accumulated flames and shattered its gathered power.

Flames dispersed.

Chi-Yu lay in the sandy ground.

"Shaka!"

Garchomp walked beside Tyranitar. The two big guys looked at each other, both showing smiles and extending their claws.

High five.

Slap—

Chi-Yu, not yet completely unable to battle, saw this scene and immediately felt jealous fire surge to its head again. Its eyes rolled back.

It completely lost fighting ability.

"Bangi! Bangi!"

Tyranitar kept blowing on its palm.

Forgot that Garchomp's palm was a claw—so painful, so painful—

Seeing Chi-Yu lose fighting ability, Johan and Cynthia looked at each other and smiled, then threw out an Ultra Ball.

Without surprise, after several shakes and a "click," the Ultra Ball locked.

Capture successful!

Taking the Ultra Ball that Tyranitar handed over, Johan wondered... after capturing Pokémon, should he also strike a pose and say something...

No help for it.

Though Johan had grown to become Hoenn League's Champion, he truly hadn't actively captured Pokémon many times.

But regardless...

Job done!

Hum—

Once the Ultra Ball was in hand, Johan belatedly realized—

"Hiss—so hot!"

After capturing Chi-Yu, the Ultra Ball transmitted Chi-Yu's temperature.

Johan quickly handed the Ultra Ball to Gengar.

Better let Gengar store it.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar emerged from the shadows with a mournful face and took the Ultra Ball, tossing it into its mouth.

"What's wrong? Why the long face?"

"Ka-kaah..."

Gengar pulled out a pile of metal melted into one piece.

"Pfft—"

Johan laughed heartlessly.

Though Chi-Yu's final Overheat wasn't fully executed, the flames it released had still melted the metals Gengar had collected from the Bisharp into one piece.

"No problem, no problem. You were going to have Ceruledge melt them first anyway for Tinkaton."

Gengar blinked.

Oh right!

"Rotom, remember to send the video and expense report to Chairman Geeta," Johan instructed again.

"Already sent, Rotom!"

Rotom immediately indicated that as soon as Chi-Yu was captured, it had already sent everything to Chairman Geeta.

Nobody in the Johan family was inactive about expense reports.

"Well done." Johan smiled and gave a thumbs up.

"According to what you said, there should be two more 'Treasures of Ruin'—Wo-Chien, Chi-Yu, plus Chien-Pao and Ting-Lu..."

Following Johan out of the cave, Cynthia said softly.

"Chairman Geeta should have found others to handle the remaining two," Johan said with a stretch, quite confidently.

Otherwise, Geeta should have sent location data for all three disaster Pokémon, not just Chi-Yu's.

"After all, it's their Paldea's own business. While it might be big trouble for ordinary trainers, for Elite Four level, it's actually not difficult."

They couldn't have all of Paldea's problems handled by just his group, right?

Might as well make him Paldea League Chairman and Champion instead!

If it involved first-class legendary level, that would be different.

Mere third-class legendaries...

"Mm, that's right." After thinking, Cynthia nodded.

If she put herself in Geeta's position, she probably wouldn't commission outsiders to help after the first Wo-Chien.

At most, after capturing all four "Treasures of Ruin" Pokémon, she'd find experts like Johan for placement.

"Alright, let's return to Medali Town." Caitlin said.

"Wait." Johan stopped her. "We?"

Caitlin immediately hugged Cynthia's arm and raised her chin: "Of course. You wouldn't expect me, visiting Paldea region for the first time, to go back alone, would you?"

Cynthia patted Caitlin's head and gave Johan an apologetic look, saying:

"I promised Caitlin I'd see her back..."

Facing Caitlin's private pleading, Cynthia sometimes had no choice.

Johan: "..."

Looking at Caitlin's raised chin, Johan was so frustrated his teeth itched.

But thinking about it, whatever.

Return to Medali Town then. Worst case, he'd take Cynthia to Casseroya Lake afterward.

After all, it was Paldea region's largest inland lake with quite nice scenery.

Moreover, Casseroya Lake was home to many Dragon-type Pokémon, especially those two "False Dragon Titans."

The "Herba Mystica" they guarded happened to be the only type Johan currently lacked.

"Fine then. Let's return to Medali Town together."

Paldea region, on the Flying Taxi.

"Cynthia, look! Lance and Lorelei are fighting!"

Caitlin held up the latest Rotom phone, pulling Cynthia and calling out.

Lance and Lorelei?

Johan raised an eyebrow and leaned over to look.

Indeed, it was Lance and Lorelei battling, and the battle location was in Medali Town in the Paldea region.

On screen, Lance was commanding his red Gyarados against Lorelei's Dewgong.

Dewgong and Lorelei were at a disadvantage.

After all, though Gyarados was a Water and Flying-type Pokémon, it could learn Electric-type moves, which was crucial.

"Go Lorelei!"

Seeing this, Caitlin couldn't help cheering for Lorelei.

From her way of addressing Lorelei, it wasn't hard to see that Caitlin and Lorelei had a surprisingly good relationship?

Was it because they both liked dolls?

"Lorelei's Dewgong restraining Lance's Gyarados is actually already successful from a tactical standpoint."

After all, among Lance's Pokémon, aside from his rarely-used Kingdra and one of his aces, Charizard, only Gyarados wasn't so afraid of Ice-type Pokémon.

And Lorelei's Dewgong could only be considered one of her main Pokémon, not yet at ace level.

Additionally, Lance liked using Gyarados as his Mega choice. If Mega Evolution was forced out at this time, Lorelei would have established part of her advantage.

"Lorelei is still very capable," Caitlin said.

Johan nodded in agreement but still added: "However... Lance will most likely still win."

Caitlin looked unconvinced.

But Cynthia also expressed agreement with Johan's judgment.

"Why..." Caitlin muttered.

Because Lance's hard strength was indeed much stronger than Lorelei's.

If not for the overly obvious type advantage, it would be difficult for Lorelei to even push Lance to the brink.

In the livestream, Gyarados defeated Lorelei's Dewgong, but then Lorelei's Lapras defeated Gyarados.

Afterward, Lance summoned his ace Dragonite!

Seeing this, Lorelei immediately switched, replacing Lapras with Ninetales.

Ninetales!

With absolute type advantage over Dragonite!

But the next second... Johan raised an eyebrow.

"Oh no!"

"What's wrong?"

Caitlin and Cynthia looked at him puzzledly.

Johan said with a strange expression: "Lance got his biggest 'cheat.'"

Cheat?

Cynthia and the others looked confused.

What kind of cheat could make even Johan so surprised?

Then they saw—

Lance took out a "Tera Orb" unique to the Paldea region and directly had Dragonite Terastallize!

And the Terastallized type was...

Normal-type!

"Normal-type?" Caitlin asked in bewilderment.

Johan's mouth twitched as he muttered: "Rejoice! The strongest in the Pokémon world, Dragon Master Lance's final form—the legendary 'Normal-type Elite Four!'"

Who could have imagined that his casual joke about 'Lance would be strong if he made Dragonite Normal-type' had been deeply remembered by Lance.

Sure enough, Lance had really converted Dragonite's Tera type to Normal-type!

"Dragonite, Extreme Speed!" came Lance's voice from the phone.

# 766: Dragon Tamer Family

Paldea region, Medali Town.

Returning to Medali Town once again.

Though they'd only been away for a few days, Johan clearly felt that Medali Town had become even livelier.

The arrival of "Pokémon World Championships" contestants like Lance and Lorelei would inevitably attract many spectators and participants.

He didn't know when it started, but Medali Town was vaguely trending toward becoming the Paldea region's major battle town for the "points stage."

It might be because Johan and Hassel—a Champion and a top Elite Four member—had battled here.

This attracted many people, including some powerful or highly-ranked trainers, creating a ripple effect that made Medali Town increasingly bustling.

Sure enough, besides Medali Town's largest arena, you could see young trainers battling everywhere on the streets.

Some had Rotom drone referee livestreams, others were unofficial matches.

The entire town's battle enthusiasm was almost higher than the Galar region's.

The "Pokémon World Championships" organized by the Pokémon League had genuinely sparked battle enthusiasm among trainers throughout the Paldea region.

"Larry must be swamped," Johan said teasingly.

At the Pokémon Center, they sent their Pokémon to Nurse Joy for simple treatment and recovery, while recharging their Tera Orbs.

"Larry isn't busy at all," Nurse Joy said while operating the equipment.

"Hm?"

"Larry took an assignment and went out," Nurse Joy explained.

Johan's expression became strange.

He'd originally thought Larry was like Lucian—working overtime for the League's division system.

Now it seemed... Larry was more like an ordinary "worker" from his past life, always finding time to slack off when possible and seeking "emergency shelter" when encountering big trouble.

Wait.

Larry couldn't have been "tricked" by Chairman Geeta into dealing with one of the remaining two "Treasures of Ruin," could he?

Hard to say which trouble was bigger.

"Speaking of which, what powerful trainers have come to Medali Town recently?" Johan asked curiously.

From one perspective, Pokémon Centers were the "information bureaus" of the Pokémon world.

After all, most trainers couldn't avoid Pokémon Centers, especially League-registered ones.

Besides needing Pokémon medical treatment, the free food and lodging provided by Pokémon Centers was also necessary for many trainers.

Like Johan, Cynthia, and Caitlin—they'd checked into the Pokémon Center's free accommodation immediately upon arriving in Medali Town.

However, Johan only needed one room, while Cynthia and Caitlin were still organizing things in their room.

"Mm..." Nurse Joy thought, then said: "Johto League Champion Lance, Kanto League Elite Four Lorelei and Bruno, Sinnoh League Elite Four Flint..."

Johan wasn't surprised by these people.

Nurse Joy continued: "Plus Kalos League's Byron..."

"Byron?" Johan was stunned.

Would Byron prioritize "points stage" enthusiasm over culinary arts, given his personality?

"I heard Byron came to study—culinary arts, that is."

Johan understood.

Right, Medali Town was a major "food city" in the Paldea region. If it was cooking-related, that made sense.

"There's also Alexa from the Kalos League, recently conducting exclusive interviews at the arena."

"Quite a gathering of heroes," Johan sighed.

Adding himself, Cynthia, and Caitlin, Medali Town now had three Champions and five Elite Four members!

With such strength, they could conquer two regional Leagues.

"Johan?"

At this moment, the Pokémon Center's automatic doors opened, and a young man with red crew-cut hair, wearing a cape with stern features, walked in.

"Lance?"

Seeing the newcomer, Johan looked slightly stunned.

It was Lance, whom Johan's group had watched on livestream earlier, battling Lorelei and winning with his Tera Normal-type Dragonite!

"Hey." Lance smiled and approached. "When I first arrived in Medali Town, I heard you'd left. How come you're back?"

While speaking, Lance handed his Poké Balls and Tera Orb to Nurse Joy. "Please take care of these, Nurse Joy."

"It's my job."

Taking the Poké Balls, Nurse Joy also ended her chat with Johan and called Indeedee to help restore Lance's Pokémon.

Johan looked him up and down. "What? You don't want to battle me, do you?"

Sorry, but he was staying one night at most and leaving tomorrow.

Lance waved his hand with complete confidence: "No, my battle with you is destined for the Master Tournament's final showdown!"

Johan's mouth twitched.

He didn't know whether Lance was confident in himself or in Johan.

With this "Pokémon World Championships" lineup of veteran and new-generation powerhouses, he really wasn't sure what ranking he could achieve.

If he encountered some monsters in the first round, even falling in round one was possible.

"Then why are you looking for me?" Johan asked.

Lance's smile faded as he said: "I heard you helped Hassel confirm himself and make a decision?"

Johan's thoughts stirred.

He vaguely guessed something but still said: "I only battled him once. The decision was Hassel's own."

Lance scratched his head. "But some people don't think so."

"Who? Paldea's Dragon Tamer family?"

Lance nodded slightly.

Johan curled his lip. "What they think is their business. They can't possibly come cause trouble for me, right?"

Just some Dragon Tamer family...

Oh right, what time was the next flight again?

Lance chuckled. "Of course they can't cause trouble for you. You're Teacher Johan, Hoenn League's Champion, representing the entire Hoenn League, and to some degree, even the entire League."

He continued:

"Hassel's Dragon Tamer family has some connection with our Blackthorn City's Dragon Clan. They know you and I have a good relationship, so..."

After pausing: "So they want me to ask if you're interested in visiting their Dragon Tamer family as a guest?"

"As a guest?"

Johan was stunned. "A trap?"

"What trap?" Lance looked confused.

"Nothing, nothing." Johan waved his hand. "Why would they suddenly want to invite me to dinner?"

Celebrating that we "convinced" their designated next family head to become a teacher?

"They said they want to chat with you," Lance shrugged.

Blackthorn City's Dragon Clan, Hoenn's Draconids, Sinnoh's Celestic Town, Unova's Village of Dragons, Paldea's Dragon Tamer family...

These were all famous, historically long-standing, and deeply-rooted families related to Dragon-type Pokémon in the Pokémon world.

Such families all had somewhat "reclusive" attributes with unfathomable foundations. No one knew whether these families might hide powerful trainers or dragon tamers.

Even without that, with Dragon-type Pokémon's long lifespans, those dragons that had lived hundreds or even thousands of years often had frighteningly powerful strength.

"I don't know them, and we don't have much to chat about," Johan said helplessly.

"That's what I told them too."

Lance had somewhat figured out Johan's personality.

"But they mean, if possible, they'd still like to chat with you, or... seek your help."

"Help?" Johan was stunned.

A historically long-standing Dragon Tamer family would seek his help?

But thinking about it, Lance's Dragon Clan had also approached him before.

"What do you think?" Johan asked.

"Go," Lance said. "I'll go with you."

Regardless, the Dragon Tamer family couldn't possibly cause trouble for Johan, right?

They weren't stupid to that degree, right?

This was Teacher Johan!

If they really angered Teacher Johan, with his current strength, how many ancient families could withstand that?

Moreover, Teacher Johan was the legendary "Legendary Pokémon Man"!

Casually calling a few legendary Pokémon... forget ancient families—even the entire Paldea League might not withstand it.

The Dragon Tamer family contacting Hassel to seek his return showed they weren't completely isolated from the world like the Draconids, knowing nothing about outside news.

"I'm coming too."

At this moment, Cynthia and Caitlin, who had just finished organizing their room, also came out. Cynthia said seriously.

Great—three Champions.

If something really happened, overthrowing a family would be simple.

"Alright then."

Johan nodded. "Let's go freeload a meal."

After resting one night at the Pokémon Center.

The next day, on the way to the Dragon Tamer family, Johan felt something wasn't quite right.

How had he gotten rid of the light bulb Caitlin, only to not reduce the number but add one more?

Besides Lance, there was also someone who came with him—Clair.

As fellow Dragon Clan younger generation, Clair, as Blackthorn City's Gym Leader, had also made quite a name in this "Pokémon World Championships."

Currently ranked 43rd in "Great Ball Tier," higher than some Elite Four rankings.

Actually, Clair's strength improvement was rapid. Honestly, she could match some weaker Elite Four members.

However, the Kanto and Johto regions had intense competition for Elite Four and Champion positions.

Plus, with fellow Dragon Clan member Lance already Champion, Clair was at a disadvantage competing for Elite Four.

Fortunately, Clair didn't care much about Elite Four competition.

That was normal.

Girls grew up, and maybe they cared more about...

Clair looked at the hand-holding Johan and Cynthia, then glanced at her cousin beside her who seemed oblivious and only knew how to act cool, getting increasingly annoyed.

"Speaking of which, Lance, after beating Lorelei, you're ranked first in points now, right?" Johan suddenly asked.

"Yeah." Lance smiled and nodded.

In the original anime, when the eighth generation introduced "strongest Champion" Leon, Lance lost to Leon in the Eight Masters tournament.

Though many complained about Lance's obvious "holding back," it was undeniable that after full growth, Leon's win rate against Lance would be relatively higher.

But now... if Leon faced Lance, Lance's win rate would be higher.

After all, whether compared to Lance or Johan, Leon was somewhat younger.

"However, the 'points stage' doesn't really matter much for the later 'Master Tournament,'" Lance said nonchalantly.

Johan couldn't help but internally complain.

If it doesn't matter much, why are you constantly pushing for first place?

What should someone at the bottom like me say?

"Where is Hassel's Dragon Tamer family?" Johan changed the subject.

Such families more or less had historically long-standing ancestral lands for better Pokémon training and breeding.

"Casseroya Lake," Clair said before Lance could speak.

Though both Lance and Clair were Dragon Clan members, and Lance had even become Johto League Champion, the current Dragon Clan leaders were actually Clair and the elders.

Lance obviously wasn't someone willing to manage the family. As Blackthorn City's Gym Leader, Clair might be the Dragon Clan's future helmsman.

The Dragon Clan had also put their eggs in two baskets.

"Casseroya Lake?" Johan was bewildered.

"Not many people know," Lance said. "It's indeed hidden on a mysterious small island within Casseroya Lake."

These dragon-taming families never seemed to separate from lakes, water, or waterfalls.

"Then we can't ride Cyclizar there..." Johan clicked his tongue.

"Cyclizar?"

Outside town, Lance looked at Johan puzzledly and threw out a Poké Ball.

"Draa~~"

Dragonite appeared before them.

Lance mounted Dragonite's back, then extended his hand toward Clair.

Clair rolled her eyes.

Sometimes she really couldn't tell if her cousin "understood" or "didn't understand."

Johan sighed and also summoned his Dragonite.

"Draa~~(ˊˋ)"

Compared to Lance's Dragonite, Johan's was more than a size larger around the waist.

He stopped Cynthia from summoning Garchomp. At her puzzled look, Johan whispered:

"I'm scared..."

Cynthia laughed helplessly.

But still withdrew her hand from her Poké Ball.

Casseroya Lake—the largest freshwater lake in the Paldea region, covering even one-fifth of the entire region.

Various islands were scattered throughout, with numerous Water-type Pokémon living in the lake, occasionally even Dragon-types.

Many explorers actually entered Casseroya Lake.

Some for rare Dragon-type Pokémon, others for those untouched islands.

On such islands, either no Pokémon lived, or if they did, most wouldn't be weak.

More importantly, such hard-to-reach islands had good resources. With luck, you might find rare, precious materials or items.

Two Dragonite, one large and one small, flew over Casseroya Lake.

With Cynthia sitting behind Johan, smelling the fragrance from his breath, Johan suddenly felt... flying slower was indeed better.

His little Cynthia was not only fragrant but also soft...

Cynthia's face was flushed red.

Meanwhile, on the other Dragonite, the scene was different.

Lance sat in front while Clair sat behind with an exasperated expression, gripping Lance's cape.

"Hold tight, don't fall. When you were little..."

Before finishing, Clair viciously pinched Lance's waist, grinding her teeth.

"Hiss—"

Lance sharply inhaled.

Johan shook his head watching this.

Clearly, last time going to Blackthorn City and the "Dragon's Den," riding Gyarados together had made Johan feel like he was watching a couple.

Now it seemed... as Lance himself said, he was purely worried about Clair falling.

After flying a while longer, they'd nearly crossed from Casseroya Lake's easternmost to westernmost edge.

"We're here," Lance indicated to Johan.

"So fast?"

Johan felt time passed too quickly—he'd said they should fly slower.

Following Lance's gaze, Hassel's home—no, the Dragon Tamer family—was at the foot of the highest mountain on Casseroya Lake's western edge.

Not hidden at all.

That made sense.

Though the Dragon Tamer family had long history, it didn't mean they were completely isolated from the world like the Draconids.

Reportedly, the Dragon Tamer family had close cooperation with the Paldea League.

Somewhat similar to Lance's Dragon Clan with the Johto League, though not as close.

The two Dragonite descended.

The Dragon Tamer family seemed prepared, with several people waiting on the entrance platform. Leading them was an elderly man who appeared to be in his sixties with graying hair, slightly hunched.

"Why did the old master come personally?" Seeing the elder, both Lance and Clair looked surprised.

Old master?

This couldn't be the current Dragon Tamer family patriarch—Elite Four Hassel's father?

"Lance, Clair." The old master, supported by a girl who looked about twenty, wore classical but simple robes and smiled as he approached.

"These must be Teacher Johan and Miss Cynthia?" The old master looked at Johan and the others.

"Hello, sir," Johan and Cynthia politely greeted.

Regardless, elders came first.

"So sorry to have Lance bring you both here..."

The old master had a kind smile and seemed easy to talk to, not showing the bad attitude toward Johan—the one who "led" his son down the "wrong path"—that Johan had expected.

Several minutes later, Johan's group sat with the old master in the courtyard of the ancient residence, with tea quickly served.

This Dragon Tamer family house was somewhat like Master Mustard's dojo on the Isle of Armor.

However, instead of students or fighters moving about, there were young trainers with various Dragon-type Pokémon.

Johan and Lance's Dragonite walked into the courtyard flanking them, curiously looking around while being watched by passing youngsters and their Dragon-type Pokémon.

With gazes of curiosity, surprise, envy, and battle enthusiasm.

Meanwhile, the old master chatted with Johan's group while walking, mentioning some topics about Hassel's childhood.

When young, Hassel was indeed quite mischievous—or rather, rebellious.

Born into a Dragon Tamer family with the patriarch as his father, he nevertheless loved music and rock.

After arguing with the old master, he ran away from home.

Once he left, he never returned.

Ultimately, the Dragon Tamer family elders believed that with Hassel's current strength, he'd be an excellent next patriarch candidate.

But for the old master, he probably just wanted to see his son who'd run away decades ago.

Cynthia gave Johan a meaningful look.

Asking if he should...

But Johan gestured to wait.

He couldn't just run and persuade Hassel to visit based solely on the old master's words, right?

With Hassel's current character, if things were truly that simple, he'd probably have returned to visit his elderly father long ago.

In any case... even officials couldn't judge family matters.

The issues between Hassel and the Dragon Tamer family were for them to decide.

He'd only battled Hassel once. The one who found and confirmed his true feelings was Hassel himself.

Even without Johan, Hassel would eventually confirm his heart.

"Regardless, I must thank Teacher Johan. I'm proud that Hassel can stick to his beliefs," the old master said with a slightly hoarse but sincere voice.

Johan waved his hand and smiled:

"Actually, sir, seeing Hassel doesn't necessarily require him coming back, right? With technology so advanced and Pokémon so capable..."

Hearing this, the old master was stunned—stunned for quite a while.

As patriarch, rigid or even inflexible clan rules wouldn't allow the patriarch to see a clan member who'd been away for decades in an almost "apologetic" manner.

But as a father...

The old master sighed and bowed to Johan.

"This old man was narrow-minded."

This gesture greatly improved Johan's impression of the old master and the Dragon Tamer family.

"I wonder why you've invited us here, sir?" Johan finally brought the topic back.

The old master also reacted, showing some embarrassment on his aged face.

"Speaking of which..."

Thinking about it, the old master felt it was better to have Johan's group see for themselves.

"Please follow me."

After tea and chat came business.

Following the old master, they passed through the Dragon Tamer family residence to a back mountain behind the courtyard, where a cave appeared before them.

Johan couldn't help glancing at Lance.

Do all you Dragon-type people like squatting in caves?

"Roar—!!!"

Just approaching the cave, they heard roaring from inside, carrying inexplicable anger and agitation.

Johan and Lance's Dragonite both changed expressions.

They sensed a threat.

Entering the cave, they found another world inside—a waterfall within the mountain.

In the river below the waterfall, a bipedal Pokémon with two tails, flowing purple mane, and blue gem ornament on its forehead—never seen yet somehow familiar—appeared in everyone's sight.

Seeing this Pokémon, everyone was stunned.

Lance even unconsciously voiced his confusion: "Sui, Suicune?"

"Seems... a bit different," Cynthia said with a frown.

Unlike their confusion, Johan's face showed more surprise than anything.

Suicune?

Well, strictly speaking, it counted.

Just a "counterfeit" one.

# 767: Rakua Elements!

Suicune?

Just from appearance, the Pokémon roaring beneath the waterfall indeed bore seven to eight-tenths similarity to the legendary "Avatar of the North Wind," Suicune.

But it wasn't Suicune—it was...

"Walking Wake," Johan said softly.

"Walking Wake?"

Cynthia, Lance, and Clair all looked at Johan with confusion.

The old master of the Dragon Tamer family, Elite Four Hassel's father, also looked toward Johan.

However, his eyes showed not confusion, but surprise.

"Teacher Johan also recognizes this Pokémon?"

This truly surprised the old master.

If nothing else, if this Walking Wake went outside, over ninety percent of people would mistake it for Suicune. The remaining ten percent, though knowing it wasn't Suicune, probably wouldn't know what Pokémon it was either.

Because theoretically, this was an ancient Pokémon—or rather, a Paradox Pokémon.

Uh... Johan, who had unconsciously said the name "Walking Wake," paused, then smiled:

"Also my first time seeing one. Previously... saw it mentioned in an ancient text."

"The Scarlet Book?"

The Scarlet Book was written by a naturalist named Heath, who was also the first to enter the deepest parts of the Great Crater of Paldea over two hundred years ago.

It recorded vast amounts of incredible information, making people question whether Heath was creating sensational stories. For a long time afterward, the Scarlet Book became an obscure bizarre novel in the Paldea region.

Few people remembered the Scarlet Book now. Johan wasn't sure if the book was currently in Naranja Academy's library or possessed by Heath's descendant, Briar.

Johan even wasn't certain whether there was only the Scarlet Book or if there was a corresponding Violet Book, representing Pokémon's "Scarlet and Violet."

Johan smiled without giving a definite answer.

He couldn't very well say he'd played the "Pokémon Scarlet/Violet" games, could he?

But in the old master's eyes, this was almost equivalent to confirmation.

Teacher Johan's knowledge is truly broad—worthy of being a teacher, the old master thought admiringly.

This way, his son choosing to remain a teacher after communicating with Teacher Johan might not be a bad choice.

"That's right, this indeed isn't Suicune, but Walking Wake—a Dragon-type Pokémon," the old master sighed.

Dragon-type Pokémon?

Walking Wake?

Lance and Clair looked at each other.

Walking Wake, a Water and Dragon-type Paradox Pokémon, most likely from the Great Crater of Paldea. But seeing Walking Wake appear here...

Perhaps long ago, the Dragon Tamer family had already encountered Walking Wake.

After all, over a hundred years ago, adventurer Lucius, one of the "Explorers" founders, had six Pokémon including Gouging Fire—an ancient Pokémon resembling Entei.

Looking at the enraged Walking Wake, Johan frowned:

"What's wrong with it..."

"Teacher Johan has already noticed?"

The old master nodded slightly and explained: "Though Walking Wake hasn't been captured by our Dragon Tamer family, it actually established a very friendly relationship with our family. Usually when it had nothing to do, Walking Wake would live here..."

Similar to the Dragonite in Lance's Dragon Clan's "Dragon's Den," right?

The difference was that the Dragonite belonged to Pokémon left by the Dragon Clan's older generation, while Walking Wake was a Dragon-type that had established friendly relations.

The old master continued:

"But after Walking Wake went out last time and returned... it became like this. Not only did its temperament become extremely irritable, but it also became very difficult to communicate with. Though not impossible to communicate, just..."

Johan continued:

"Just instinctively following its original lifestyle, but probably no one can approach, and though it hasn't randomly destroyed things, anyone who gets close might be attacked."

"Exactly!"

The old master looked at Johan with amazement.

Just one look and he could understand Walking Wake's general situation?

Was this the person Professor Oak called the "Pokémon Heart Master"?

"Does Teacher Johan know what's wrong with Walking Wake?"

Johan's expression was strange.

Based on this description, Walking Wake completely fit one condition—being in heat.

But well...

"Cough, I need to observe more specifically."

The old master continued: "Actually, part of the reason I wanted Hassel to return was for Walking Wake."

"Want Hassel to capture Walking Wake?" Johan used a questioning tone but seemed quite certain.

Hearing this, the old master nodded. "I don't know if this could calm Walking Wake down, just... it's another option to try."

This was somewhat reasonable.

Otherwise, Hassel had been an Elite Four for so many years—why only now think of calling him back...

"If Teacher Johan can calm Walking Wake, our Dragon Tamer family will be greatly grateful!"

Calm it?

If Walking Wake was really in heat, how would you calm it?

Should they neuter it?

Of course, if it wasn't in heat, the problem might be... more serious.

At the same time.

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar had already quietly crept near Walking Wake.

Because sharp-eyed Gengar had discovered that in the pool where Walking Wake lived beneath the waterfall, there seemed to be several shed scales!

If it was a Dragon-type Pokémon, wouldn't these be Dragon Scales?

Good stuff...

"Roar—!!"

But just as Gengar bent down to pick them up, it found Walking Wake's bloodshot eyes had suddenly locked onto it.

"Ka, ka-kaah~"

Gengar put its hands behind its back, grinned, and gently waved at Walking Wake.

Well, hello there~

"Roar!!"

Walking Wake roared, the water surface immediately broke into waves, rolling up massive splashes as it charged toward Gengar.

In just a blink, Walking Wake had already reached Gengar.

So fast?

Fortunately, Gengar was faster, diving directly down.

Glub glub glub—

Scooping up several scales from underwater, it disappeared into shadows.

"Roar!!"

Walking Wake roared furiously.

Hum—

The temperature in the cave began soaring, and a Sunny Day feeling suddenly permeated the cave.

Sunny Day!

Quite interesting.

Walking Wake was clearly a Water and Dragon-type Pokémon, but regarding weather, it preferred sunny conditions.

The reason was simple—Walking Wake's ability was "Protosynthesis."

[Protosynthesis: When a Pokémon with this ability holds "Booster Energy" or during "Sunny Day," its highest stat except HP receives a thirty percent boost!]

And if the strongest stat was "Speed," the boost would be fifty percent!

But if Johan remembered correctly, Walking Wake's strongest stat should be Special Attack.

But given the speed it just displayed...

Something was wrong.

Very wrong!

Johan frowned.

The old master also seemed surprised, looking at Walking Wake and opening his mouth: "How..."

"It got stronger?" Johan seemed to ask but appeared certain of the answer.

The old master took a deep breath: "Yes..."

Though Walking Wake had displayed its strength when it first returned, it quickly went back to the cave. The old master wasn't present then, and those who were couldn't distinguish Walking Wake's power changes, so they really didn't understand this point.

While Walking Wake was still controlling the weather, venting anger, and searching for Gengar, Johan seriously asked:

"Old master, do you know where Walking Wake went after leaving the Dragon Tamer family?"

Hearing this, the old master smiled helplessly: "We... really don't know much, only that Walking Wake would enter Casseroya Lake periodically..."

"As for where it went after entering Casseroya Lake, or whether it left Casseroya Lake, we have no idea."

Casseroya Lake wasn't just the largest inland lake in the Paldea region—its waterways were very complex, with various branches spreading throughout the entire Paldea region.

Even following waterways, Walking Wake could go anywhere.

Not to mention if Walking Wake went ashore, it could travel much farther.

Knowing nothing about anything.

Johan was even speechless.

Not knowing anything, yet daring to invite people to handle the problem.

Now it seemed Walking Wake probably... wasn't in heat, so the problem might be more troublesome.

But regardless...

Subduing it first couldn't be wrong!

Johan's palm swept across his waist, throwing out a Luxury Ball.

"Go, Ninetales!"

"Oowoo!!"

The snow-white Ninetales appeared in the cave.

Though the Water and Dragon-type Walking Wake was a Dragon-type that didn't fear Ice-type attacks much, Fairy-type's advantage was still obvious.

Especially when Walking Wake's Water-type moves couldn't threaten Ninetales much either.

Of course, the more important point was...

"Oowoo—"

Ninetales raised its head and sang softly. Instantly, the cave that had warmed due to Sunny Day became filled with cold air, and snowflakes began falling.

Snow!

Ninetales' "Snow Warning" ability brought Snow that successfully "covered" Walking Wake's Sunny Day.

After losing Sunny Day, Walking Wake's "Protosynthesis" ability could no longer function.

"Roar!!"

Walking Wake, sensing Sunny Day's disappearance, immediately turned its attention to Ninetales.

Hiss—

Scorching steam immediately rose from Walking Wake's body. The burning steam melted surrounding falling snowflakes, then formed scalding water rushing toward Johan.

Steam!

"Ninetales," Johan waved lightly.

"Oowoo—"

Ninetales looked stern, its nine white ribbon-like tails swaying gently as it opened its mouth slightly, breathing out white air.

Hiss—

Burning steam collided with extreme cold air, immediately producing "hissing" sounds.

The next second, cold air instantly overwhelmed the steam, carrying Snow's chill directly past Walking Wake.

When the cold air dispersed, they saw...

Walking Wake's body surface was covered with a thin layer of ice crystals.

Frozen!

Though Walking Wake was also strong, current Ninetales... was legendary!

However, soon, before Ninetales could reinforce the ice crystals, steam surged again from within Walking Wake's body.

Boom!!

Ice crystals melted!

"Roar—"

Walking Wake roared again, its form disappearing in a blink.

Again, obviously abnormal speed.

But...

Bang!!!

The extremely fast Walking Wake suddenly appeared at Ninetales' left side, but its head seemed to hit solid, transparent glass, stunning itself silly.

"Roar..."

Walking Wake held its head, eyes pure and clear, constantly shaking its head and patting itself before barely standing steady.

Good grief...

How many Aurora Veil layers was that?

It actually blocked everything?

Walking Wake was completely confused.

The Dragon Tamer family's old master's expression was also quite spectacular.

Though he knew Teacher Johan was strong and had seen some livestreams, seeing it up close versus on TV gave completely different feelings and shock.

The old master clearly knew Walking Wake's strength, plus some mysterious reasons had made Walking Wake stronger than usual.

Though Sunny Day was suppressed by Snow...

Not breaking through defenses was too ridiculous!

This was...

"Legendary?" the old master whispered.

Legendary—above Champion level!

And it was an Ice and Fairy-type Ninetales.

Just this one Ninetales alone, their Dragon Tamer family trying to defeat it would probably...

Johan raised his hand lightly.

When fighting "Treasures of Ruin" Pokémon, Gengar and Ninetales hadn't really acted much. Now using type advantage against Walking Wake was somewhat bullying.

So, better end this quickly...

"Ninetales, Freeze-Dry!"

"Oowoo!"

Instantly, ice crystals formed at Ninetales' feet while frosty white air slowly blew from its mouth.

Whoosh—

Frosty cold air swept over Walking Wake.

Walking Wake's body surface was again covered with ice crystals, more solid and transparent than before.

Though Freeze-Dry was an Ice-type move, against the Water and Dragon-type Walking Wake, it was four times effective!

Generally, Water-type Pokémon resisted Ice-type moves by half, but Freeze-Dry was an exception—it could deal double damage to Water-type Pokémon!

Plus Ice-type's advantage over Dragon-type...

So Ninetales' Freeze-Dry was four times effective against Walking Wake.

After this Freeze-Dry attack, Walking Wake was directly frozen into an ice sculpture.

It lost fighting ability!

"Oowoo~"

Ninetales breathed out again, reinforcing the ice crystals on Walking Wake.

The old master recovered from his shock at Ninetales defeating Walking Wake instantly, taking a deep breath: "Worthy of being called the most promising to become the strongest Cham—"

Before finishing, Ninetales breathed out again.

"Oowoo~"

Crack crack crack—

The ice crystals on Walking Wake were reinforced again.

Old master: "?"

"Cough." Johan coughed lightly, stopping Ninetales. "Ninetales, that's enough."

You see, old master, some things really shouldn't be said carelessly. He didn't dare claim the title of "strongest Champion."

"Oowoo."

Ninetales nodded seriously.

Though it was still somewhat worried about what would happen if Walking Wake broke free from the ice crystal restraints, hearing Johan's words, it obediently stopped.

Looking at the lifelike Walking Wake, Lance clicked his tongue in amazement.

"This guy really looks like Suicune. Who would have thought it was actually a Dragon-type Pokémon..."

Not just Lance—Cynthia and Clair were equally curious about Walking Wake.

However, the most important thing now was finding the reason for Walking Wake's current condition.

Johan closed his eyes. Cynthia beside him said:

"Johan, be careful."

"Mm."

Aura dwells within the heart!

Johan used his aura power to try sensing Walking Wake's condition.

But soon, Johan's eyes suddenly opened as he grabbed Cynthia's hand and quickly retreated, shouting:

"Lance, retreat!"

Hearing this, Lance's expression changed as he immediately disregarded everything else, directly scooping up the bewildered Clair and retreating.

Old master: "?"

Crack!!

A fissure actually appeared in the ice crystals encasing Walking Wake.

"Oowoo..."

Ninetales, who had retreated with Johan, widened its eyes.

It knew it should have cautiously added a few hundred more reinforcing layers earlier!

However, Johan was being somewhat overly nervous, because after the crack appeared, Walking Wake didn't break free—only a... pink mist escaped.

"Pink?"

Seeing that mist, Johan was stunned.

Pink...

"Johan, what's wrong?" Cynthia asked softly, her other hand already gripping a Poké Ball at her waist. If the situation turned bad, she'd immediately summon Garchomp and the others.

With three Champions present, forget Walking Wake—even if a first-class legendary came, it wouldn't gain any advantage.

"Pink mist..."

Johan didn't answer Cynthia's question but lowered his head, rubbing his chin thoughtfully.

He felt like... he had some impression of this.

Seeing Johan thinking, Cynthia knew not to press and waited silently.

The old master, seeing Walking Wake hadn't broken free from the ice crystal restraints, also relaxed slightly and withdrew his hand from his Poké Ball.

Huff—

"I know!"

Johan suddenly looked up, saying gravely.

Under Cynthia's gaze, he said seriously: "It's Rakua elements!"

"Rakua elements?" Cynthia looked confused.

The old master was equally bewildered.

What were Rakua elements?

Johan didn't explain, because this matter... was quite complex.

If he really explained, it would involve the story of the "Explorers" trio from over a hundred years ago—Lucius, Gibeon, and Lisiantha.

Lucius, Gibeon, and Lisiantha formed the "Explorers" with the goal of reaching the paradise "Rakua."

Lucius was the trainer of the "Six Heroes," including a black Rayquaza.

Gibeon was currently Outstanding Inc.'s president and the "Explorers'" current leader, with his strongest Pokémon being a white Zygarde, also targeting Rakua elements.

Lisiantha fell in love with Lucius during exploration. Liko, one of the protagonists in "Pokémon Horizons," was her descendant. The legendary Pokémon Terapagos, related to Paldea region's Terastallization legend, was actually her Pokémon.

These three each had a legendary Pokémon, two of which were even shiny!

Black Rayquaza, white Zygarde, and Terapagos.

Such a lineup was practically invincible.

The "Explorers" they formed eventually found the paradise "Rakua" and discovered abundant Rakua elements within.

It was precisely because of Rakua elements that the three "Explorers" had conflicts.

Because Rakua elements made Pokémon irritable. Though their strength increased, there were unknown hidden dangers.

So Lucius believed Rakua elements shouldn't leave "paradise" and appear before the world.

But Gibeon thought Rakua elements could make Pokémon stronger. As long as they controlled the negative effects, it could benefit the entire Pokémon world—they should utilize it.

Thus, the two fought.

Ultimately, Lucius and Lisiantha joined forces to seal the "paradise" and its Rakua elements, with only a small portion taken out by Gibeon.

During this, Gibeon's Zygarde, sensing the threat of Rakua elements, ignored Gibeon's commands and separated its cells to prevent Rakua element spread, further stabilizing the seal.

Later, through Gibeon's research, he discovered Rakua elements could not only make Pokémon stronger but also extend lifespans!

This was why Outstanding Inc.'s Gibeon was still alive.

Walking Wake's previous behavior indeed resembled being affected by Rakua elements.

More importantly, the pink mist it released after being defeated by Ninetales made Johan almost certain of this.

But... how did Walking Wake get infected with Rakua elements?

The elements had clearly been sealed by Lucius and his six Pokémon, plus all of Terapagos' power...

Could it be that Lucius and Terapagos' seal had loosened?

Causing Rakua elements to escape again?

But Zygarde's cells would prevent Rakua element escape...

Or perhaps Walking Wake encountered Gibeon or Outstanding Inc. people?

Johan felt quite frustrated—"Pokémon Horizons" hadn't finished updating before he came to the Pokémon world.

"It's fine now." Johan pressed his hands down, saying to Ninetales: "Ninetales, release Walking Wake."

With many doubts in his heart, perhaps Walking Wake could give him some answers when it woke up.

"Is it... really fine?" the old master was still somewhat uncertain.

But Cynthia and Ninetales absolutely trusted Johan's words one hundred percent.

"Oowoo~"

Ninetales sang softly, and the ice crystals freezing Walking Wake rapidly melted.

"Is the problem serious?" Seeing Johan's serious expression earlier, Cynthia finally asked.

"I don't know either." Johan shrugged.

This time, he truly couldn't be certain.

But... involving Rakua elements, involving the "Explorers," involving Gibeon—it definitely wouldn't be easy to handle.

However, after questioning Walking Wake and getting a general direction, there wouldn't be need to worry too much.

Speaking of which, he looked at Lance and Clair, teasingly joking:

"Oh, still holding her?"

Earlier Lance had instinctively scooped up Clair while retreating and still hadn't put her down.

Clair's face was instantly covered in pink, her hands firmly locked around Lance's neck.

"Oh, I was focused on staying alert and listening to you—forgot about it." Lance laughed heartily, setting Clair down while scratching his head.

Clair: "?"

Just put her down like that?

Cynthia couldn't help laughing at Lance's behavior.

Johan shook his head even more, saying with "disdain":

"No wonder you're single, Lance."

# 768: Hidden Dangers

Paldea region, Casseroya Lake, Dragon Tamer family territory.

The ice crystals covering Walking Wake gradually melted.

Lance, Cynthia, Clair, and the Dragon Tamer family patriarch had been temporarily asked to leave by Johan.

"Ka-kaah~ Ka-kaah~"

"Draa~"

Gengar and Dragonite happily searched for scales beneath the waterfall.

These naturally shed scales were all Dragon Scales, and given Walking Wake's displayed strength, these Dragon Scales wouldn't be poor quality—quite rare treats for Dragonite.

Of course, if sold for money, they'd probably earn quite a bit.

The ice crystals completely melted.

But perhaps due to being frozen too long, or being affected by Rakua elements too long, Walking Wake's body remained stiff after breaking free, its eyes vacant, mouth agape.

It looked like drool was about to fall.

"Cha?"

Did it become stupid?

Tinkaton waved its hand in front of Walking Wake—no response.

"Cha!"

Tinkaton straightened its expression and grabbed the hammer handle on the ground.

Let me give it one hammer blow—one hit and it'll wake up.

"Draa!!"

Dragonite, searching for scales in the pool, suddenly raised its hand and called out, patting its cheek as electricity sparked between its antennae.

Let me do it! I'm best at "wake-up services"!

As Tinkaton and Dragonite debated who should try "awakening" Walking Wake, Walking Wake's body suddenly trembled.

"Roar..."

Its eyes quickly regained clarity, blinking twice.

Johan quickly raised his hand to stop Tinkaton and Dragonite. "No need, it's awake."

"Roar..."

Walking Wake looked at Johan with some "resentment."

If it hadn't woken up, given those two's attitude, it feared it might never wake up again.

Seeing Ninetales beside Johan, Walking Wake's eyes filled with deep wariness.

Good grief.

One Ninetales, one Freeze-Dry had almost instantly defeated it.

Though Walking Wake had been affected by "Rakua elements," that didn't mean it hadn't remembered what happened earlier.

Seeing Walking Wake's wariness toward Ninetales made Johan very happy.

Of course, not because Walking Wake was bullied... cough cough, at least not entirely.

Mainly because, since Walking Wake could remember Ninetales defeating it, it likely remembered why it had been affected by "Rakua elements."

"Eat something first, recover your strength."

Johan didn't immediately question Walking Wake but first took out some Energy Cubes.

"Roar..."

Walking Wake looked at Johan, then at the Energy Cubes he produced, then back at Gengar and the others searching for scales in the pool.

It silently lowered its head and began eating.

Johan also took out several bottles of "Heal Spray" to treat some frostbite wounds on Walking Wake's body.

After Walking Wake finished eating, Johan inquired about the main matter, using his aura power: "Walking Wake, do you remember where you were infected with 'Rakua elements'?"

"Roar?"

Rakua elements?

Walking Wake looked confused.

It didn't know what "Rakua elements" were.

"Rotom."

Johan beckoned to the Rotom Pokédex. "Show it what happened earlier."

Fortunately, Rotom had a photography hobby. While Gengar was busy collecting scales, Rotom's filming hadn't been affected much by Gengar.

Soon Walking Wake saw that after being defeated and frozen by Ninetales, pink energy had escaped from its body.

"This is 'Rakua elements'—also the reason you became like that before."

Walking Wake frowned, lowering its eyes in thought.

Johan didn't disturb it, just waited silently.

After a long while, Walking Wake slowly spoke.

"Roar..."

I... actually aren't sure. I only know that last time I left here, I followed Casseroya Lake's currents eastward. Many things happened, and though I encountered some people, most didn't notice me...

Johan frowned slightly.

All the way east?

The journey was too long with too many events—who knew where it had contracted "Rakua elements"?

No, that's not right!

Johan quickly asked: "So why did you return? Because you'd reached your destination?"

"Roar..." Walking Wake hesitantly spoke again.

I vaguely remember, it seemed like I was almost at the ocean, and human cities blocked the way, so... I inexplicably became irritated, then turned back.

Ocean? Human cities?

Johan caught the key points. "So you felt yourself becoming irritated then?"

"Roar..."

Walking Wake nodded.

All the way east, passing rivers and mountains, about to reach the ocean with human cities blocking—these elements combined pointed to only one answer.

Levincia City!

Coincidentally, the person possessing "Rakua elements" was only Gibeon and his Outstanding Inc.!

This matched up.

But... Johan didn't understand whether Walking Wake had accidentally encountered Outstanding Inc.'s research, or if Outstanding Inc. had discovered Walking Wake and used it for experiments.

If the latter, that was manageable, but if the former... it meant Outstanding Inc. was conducting indiscriminate entity research!

More Pokémon might be affected.

Regardless, targeting Outstanding Inc. definitely wouldn't be wrong.

"Do you remember encountering anyone in that area?" Johan asked again.

"Roar..." Walking Wake tried hard to remember but finally shook its head.

I don't remember. That power made me very irritable then. I might have remembered things at the time, but as time passed, I can't recall.

"Would you mind if I examined your body?"

Walking Wake instinctively stepped back, looking at Johan with some alarm, clamping its legs together.

What do you want to do?

Johan: "..."

Where did you learn that reaction?

So that's why you shouldn't wander human areas—easy to be influenced.

Walking Wake seemed to realize its overreaction, lowered its head, stepped forward, and mumbled:

"Roar..."

Johan shook his head and placed his hand on Walking Wake's back, immediately feeling a cool sensation transmitted from its body.

Like putting his hand into cool spring water.

Aura dwells within the heart!

Hmm?

Johan furrowed his brow.

For ordinary-born trainers like Johan who had walked this path alone to achieve current strength, many aspects required self-reliance.

For example, Energy Cube production, cooking three meals daily, daily care and massage for Pokémon, plus emergency treatment when Pokémon were injured.

So though Johan couldn't compare with Nurse Joy professionally, he could understand Pokémon conditions to some degree.

Especially when using aura power.

Therefore, Johan quickly noticed abnormalities in Walking Wake's body.

Honestly, it wasn't really a major abnormality, just...

Walking Wake's body seemed to be undergoing rapid transformation.

Normally, anyone's body was constantly "transforming."

Like human cells constantly aging and replacing—after several years with all cells replaced, it was essentially a new person.

Pokémon were the same.

But Walking Wake's current "transformation" speed was obviously abnormal.

Though Johan's aura power couldn't sense cellular levels, he could sense the general situation.

So this was why Walking Wake had shed so many scales in the pool?

Johan withdrew his aura power and removed his hand, saying to the confused Walking Wake: "Wait here for a moment."

Then he strode out of the cave.

The Dragon Tamer family patriarch, Cynthia, Lance, and Clair were still waiting outside the cave.

Except for the patriarch's somewhat serious expression, Cynthia and the others were admiring Casseroya Lake's scenery.

"Teacher Johan, how is it?" the patriarch asked eagerly upon seeing Johan emerge.

"No major problems."

Hearing this, the patriarch relaxed.

"However..."

Johan's next words made him tense again.

"Sir, your Dragon Tamer family has professional doctors, right?"

This further intensified the patriarch's expression, but he still said: "Of course. Teacher Johan, what exactly is..."

"Do you have advanced equipment? Or doctors more skilled with herbal medicine?"

In the Pokémon world, treating Pokémon was divided into herbal medicine and technology approaches. The Joy family primarily used equipment, supplemented by Pokémon healing methods and medications.

But some famous non-Joy family doctors were more skilled with herbal medicine.

Not knowing Walking Wake's exact problem, the patriarch honestly answered:

"They're all experienced doctors. Whether people or Pokémon in our family get sick, they can basically handle it."

That would be troublesome.

Johan shook his head.

"Forget it, I'll take Walking Wake to a Pokémon Center."

"A Pokémon Center? No need—I'll have a Nurse Joy come here. What equipment do you need, Teacher Johan?"

Johan was stunned.

Have a Nurse Joy come?

With equipment?

Oh right, he'd forgotten—the Dragon Tamer family was among the top three families in the Paldea region.

Heh, wealthy folks!

"Bring..." Johan roughly stated his needs, and the patriarch immediately arranged it.

Before long, a professional medical helicopter landed on the plaza behind the Dragon Tamer family residence.

Following Johan's instructions, with Nurse Joy and Indeedee's help and Walking Wake's cooperation, they entered the professional equipment inside the medical helicopter.

Though neither Nurse Joy nor League databases had much information about Walking Wake with no usable data, basic physical information could still be examined.

Soon, Nurse Joy found Johan with the results.

"Teacher Johan, here's Walking Wake's physical condition. Overall, except for weakness, it's actually quite healthy, just..."

Taking the results from Nurse Joy, Johan quickly found the item he was concerned about and got a definitive answer.

"I understand. Thank you, Nurse Joy," Johan said.

Seeing Teacher Johan immediately find what she wanted to mention, Nurse Joy didn't continue.

But she was somewhat surprised internally.

Teacher Johan wasn't just a famous "Heart Master" and Hoenn League Champion, reportedly excellent at Energy Cube production and cooking... who knew he had medical knowledge too?

Such a person, even for the Joy clan, was...

"What's wrong?"

Seeing Johan's serious expression, Cynthia couldn't help asking.

Wasn't Walking Wake mostly recovered?

Were there still hidden dangers?

Not just Cynthia—Walking Wake itself thought so, looking nervously at Johan.

"Roar..."

Don't tell me I have a terminal illness?

The Dragon Tamer patriarch was also uneasy.

Walking Wake didn't really have hidden dangers, did it?

Hearing Cynthia's question and seeing Walking Wake and the patriarch's expressions, Johan suddenly realized he seemed... too serious.

He laughed helplessly.

Pressing his hands down: "Don't be nervous. I said Walking Wake doesn't have major problems. At most, it's just..."

Walking Wake and the patriarch became more nervous.

Could you hurry up with this transition?

"Cough cough, at most it'll just live a few years less."

Live a few years less? No problem then.

Huh?!

Wait!

Walking Wake's eyes widened.

What do you mean "just"?

A few years less life!

But thinking about it, considering how long it had lived, a few years really didn't seem like much.

Walking Wake glanced at Johan somewhat resentfully.

This guy scared it badly.

"So this is..." Lance nearby looked at Joy's helicopter.

Such a big production just to check Walking Wake would live a few years less?

As something resembling Suicune, Walking Wake could at least count as half a legendary?

"That's not the point." Johan waved his hand. "The point is why Walking Wake will live fewer years."

Huh?

Everyone was stunned.

Johan continued: "More importantly, losing a few years might not matter for Walking Wake, but what if it affects us ordinary people or ordinary Pokémon?"

"Ka-kaah?" Gengar pointed at itself.

Me?

Johan irritably pushed his Gengar back into the shadows.

But Johan's words made everyone react. After several expression changes, they gradually became serious.

Right...

What if Walking Wake's situation happened to them and their Pokémon?

"You mean 'Rakua elements' reduce organisms' lifespans," Cynthia said after thinking, summarizing Johan's behavior and previous factors.

Johan gave Cynthia a thumbs up.

Worthy of his little Cynthia—so smart.

Simultaneously, he said: "Strictly speaking, 'Rakua elements' don't exactly reduce organisms' lifespans, but rather accelerate cellular division and renewal speed in exchange for more powerful strength."

This was why he insisted on professional equipment to examine Walking Wake.

His aura could only determine Walking Wake's body "transformation" speed was wrong, but combined with Gibeon's longevity and professional instruments, he reached this conclusion.

"Rakua elements" exchanged power for accelerated cellular transformation speed, and faster cellular transformation naturally meant reduced lifespan.

After all, whether organisms lived long or short, cellular division and transformation times were always limited.

Hearing Johan's conclusion, everyone finally understood why he was so serious.

Reduced lifespan!

How was this different from making organisms slowly commit suicide?

Moreover, they didn't know where these so-called "Rakua elements" came from or how much existed.

If there was enough to permeate the entire Paldea region, then...

"Since Teacher Johan knows about these so-called 'Rakua elements,' do you know where they come from, or how to prevent or counter them?" the patriarch asked.

Johan spread his hands.

"Sorry, I only saw information about 'Rakua elements' in an unknown ancient text. I only know they come from a place called 'Rakua Paradise.' As for prevention and countermeasures..."

After pondering briefly, he continued:

"Just like dealing with Walking Wake—defeat those infected with 'Rakua elements' to resolve it."

Defeat?

Lance and Cynthia understood.

Lance: "That's simple then."

Cynthia: "Yes."

The patriarch and Clair looked at each other.

Simple?

Those infected with "Rakua elements" obviously gained significantly enhanced strength. Only Champions like you would act like it's nothing...

"As for prevention..."

Johan shook his head.

He didn't know either.

However... in the storyline, the "Explorers" ultimately used Terapagos sharing power with Lucius's "Six Heroes," who used Terapagos's power to seal "Rakua Paradise."

So most likely, Terapagos, as the origin of Paldea region's Terastallization, could probably prevent "Rakua elements."

But current Terapagos was probably still just a pendant, sleeping.

The "Six Heroes" with Terapagos's power could be considered.

Additionally, Gibeon's white Zygarde had split into cells to prevent "Rakua elements" spread.

Perhaps Zygarde could do it too.

Thinking this, Johan felt much better.

Though Gibeon's Zygarde was shiny, it obviously wasn't a true legendary, while the real "Order God" Zygarde in the Kalos region still owed him several favors.

Worst case... call the real "Order God" over.

No big problem.

"Anyway, no major issues," Johan said.

Patriarch: "?"

Earlier you looked so serious, said it was hard to prevent, then suddenly no major issues?

Plus Lance and Cynthia's "indeed so" expressions.

The patriarch suddenly felt... was he really getting old?

So tired...

Was this Champions' confidence?

"But this matter should be reported to Chairman Geeta—let her worry about it," Johan added.

The three of them weren't Paldea region Champions. Discovering this was already a huge favor to Chairman Geeta.

"Yes." Cynthia nodded.

They'd already found and solved many troubles for Chairman Geeta during this Paldea trip.

Plus helped the Dragon Tamer family solve a problem.

Wait.

They hadn't eaten yet!

The patriarch seemed to finally react, hurrying: "This time really thank you, Teacher Johan. I've prepared food..."

Before finishing—

"Ka-kaah!!"

Gengar raised both arms in celebration.

"Draa!!"

"Draa~~"

Johan and Lance's Dragonite also cheered.

"Shaka!"

Garchomp glanced at them disdainfully, curling its lip.

What's the excitement?

Despite saying this, its claw-sharpening motions didn't stop.

"Bangi?"

Dinner? Dinner? Where's dinner?

Tyranitar ran out of its Poké Ball, its huge head looking around.

The initially cheerful patriarch, seeing these "massive" hungry creatures, somehow started sweating profusely.

However... just a few Pokémon couldn't possibly bankrupt the vast Dragon Tamer family, right?

"Ka-kaah~"

Such good people~

Gengar, picking its teeth, waved goodbye to the Dragon Tamer family members while distributing "good person cards."

Of course, even with Gengar, Tyranitar, Garchomp, two Dragonite and other Pokémon completely opening their stomachs, they couldn't bankrupt the Dragon Tamer family in one meal.

Unless Gengar ate and took extras, or ate continuously for months.

But though they didn't bankrupt them, they exhausted all the Dragon Tamer family's chefs.

After eating their fill, they "embarrassedly" accepted gratitude gifts from the Dragon Tamer family.

Johan had Dragonite, Cynthia had Garchomp, Lance and Clair had many Dragon-type Pokémon, so the gifts were naturally materials and food useful for Dragon-types—Dragon Scales, Dragon Fangs, etc.

What made Johan happy was avoiding "stubborn" and arrogant trainers who'd arrogantly challenge him upon seeing him.

Great families were smarter without so many hotheads.

Another thing that made Johan happy was that after learning he sought "Herba Mystica," the Dragon Tamer family gave him Casseroya Lake's share.

After all, it was in Casseroya Lake—the Dragon Tamer family couldn't not know.

Though somewhat curious about Altaria and Noivern's "fusion evolution," saving trouble was good.

This way, Johan had collected all "Herba Mystica" from the Paldea region.

When opportunity arose, he'd make a feast for his Pokémon and little Cynthia!

"Where are you going next?" Johan asked before leaving the Dragon Tamer family.

"Us?" Lance patted Dragonite, laughing: "Back to Medali Town for more battles with strong opponents."

Then he asked back: "What about you?"

"Us..." Johan smiled, taking Cynthia's hand: "We'll tour the Paldea region, see different scenery—maybe wait until the 'points stage' ends."

He'd given up struggling.

Bottom of "Master Ball Tier" was still "Master Ball Tier."

Rather than fighting many battles now for points, better to fight once in the "Master Replacement Battle."

He'd figured out the math.

"Great! See you in the 'Master Tournament'!" Lance said confidently. Terastallization seemed to give him more confidence, completely missing Clair's resentful gaze beside him.

Others held hands to see scenery and play, while her cousin had to return to battle.

Such suffering...
