# 769: Awaken, Jirachi!

Paldea region, League Headquarters.

"Rakua elements?"

Geeta looked at the letter signed by Teacher Johan, Cynthia, and Lance, unable to help but take it seriously.

A letter jointly signed by three Champions!

The importance of its contents was obvious.

When Geeta read about the events described in the letter and the "Rakua elements," her expression immediately became serious.

As a fellow Champion, she clearly understood what kind of trouble these "Rakua elements" would bring—if they could truly exchange lifespan, or accelerated cell division, for explosive improvements in Pokémon strength—and how many criminals they would attract.

Pokémon Hunters, evil organizations, or extremists would almost inevitably want to obtain "Rakua elements" upon learning of their power!

If it were just that, it might be manageable.

Though there were many such people, they could still be targeted.

The biggest trouble was when ordinary people used them blindly without knowing the side effects, or were subjected to them—the impact would be enormous.

"'Rakua elements,' power related to cell division, suspected to appear in Levincia City..."

Levincia City, Outstanding Inc., Gibeon...

Geeta naturally, or rather instinctively, connected these factors.

But it was just blind connection—there was no inevitable link between them.

"Troubled times indeed..." Geeta couldn't help but sigh deeply.

The disaster Pokémon issue wasn't completely resolved, the "Pokémon World Championships" was approaching, the depths of the "Great Crater of Paldea" seemed to be growing restless recently, plus these "Rakua elements"...

Even someone as capable as Geeta felt particularly overwhelmed, with a sense of not knowing where to start.

Over the following period, Johan and Cynthia were finally able to truly enjoy touring the Paldea region.

With their Pokémon, eating and playing along the way.

Mainly visiting the "Ten Sights of Paldea."

They'd already been to Levincia City's "Million Volt Nightscape" and North Province Area Two's "Dalizapa Passage" waterfall.

Afterward, they saw Casseroya Lake's "Socarrat Trail" and "Casseroya Falls."

They went to South Province Area Two's "Olive Roll" to eat fresh olives, with Johan stocking up on freshly made olive sauce and olive oil.

They camped at South Province Area Five's "Asado Desert," enjoying fresh seafood together.

They saw South Province Area Six's "Alfornada Cavern."

They had a "thrilling" adventure in West Province Area Two's "Dalizapa Passage," where the wild Pokémon were thoroughly scared by Gengar.

Among the "Ten Sights of Paldea," what satisfied Johan most was actually Glaseado Mountain's "Glaseado Gym" and the peak of Glaseado Mountain, called "Paldea's highest peak."

Looking down from Glaseado Mountain's summit, almost the entire Paldea region could be seen.

Even the mysterious "Great Crater of Paldea" was visible.

However, thick mist always surrounded the "Great Crater of Paldea." Looking down from Glaseado Mountain, only a rough outline was visible—the internal scenery couldn't be seen clearly.

While Johan and Cynthia toured Paldea hand in hand, the "Pokémon World Championships" was also proceeding intensely.

The "points stage" was nearing its end, and rankings were gradually stabilizing. Except for a few situations, most contestants who could qualify for the "Master Replacement Battle" were basically confirmed.

With promotion and extensive "points stage" livestreams and broadcasts, more and more people began anticipating the official start of the "Pokémon World Championships."

This "Pokémon World Championships" was a rare case where young master trainers and veteran master trainers competed together.

This was mainly because young trainers had emerged and grown in recent years.

Some representative examples included Kanto and Johto's Lance and Lorelei, Hoenn's Teacher Johan and Steven, Sinnoh's Cynthia, Flint, Lucian, and Aaron, Unova's Caitlin and N, etc.

People seemed to see an even brighter future for the League.

Therefore, this "Pokémon World Championships" was seen by many as a generational transition tournament. Missing it might mean never seeing such spectacular matches again.

So the League, to some extent, prepared a "holiday" for all audiences anticipating the tournament.

Students got vacation time, office workers had mandatory work hours, etc.

After all, in a world centered on Pokémon, such an event—arguably the highest level in the entire Pokémon world, covering nine regions—was naturally significant.

After Johan and the others finished touring the Paldea region, they'd had enough fun.

Though Johan didn't care what ranking he might achieve in the "Pokémon World Championships," he still needed to show proper attitude.

So after discussing with Cynthia, they decided to use the remaining time for simple special training!

Well, rather than special training, it was more like recovery practice.

After all, there hadn't been many battles recently, but they'd eaten plenty.

Johan felt his Dragonite seemed to have gained another size around the waist, making him genuinely worried.

Since it was special training, they naturally needed to choose an appropriate location.

So the two discussed the location issue.

"Mm..." Cynthia sat at the picnic table, chin in hand, listening to Johan's words.

Johan, wearing an apron while cooking, also spoke: "Actually, I think Glaseado Mountain is a great place. The wild Pokémon levels there aren't low, and most are Ice-types..."

Glaseado Mountain was considered a relatively dangerous area in the Paldea region.

The only shortcoming might be inconvenient accommodation.

In the icy environment, without proper warmth measures, you might wake up the next day as an icicle.

However, this wasn't a problem for Johan and Cynthia.

Through Ninetales' "training," Johan's Ice-type resistance was basically maxed out, while Cynthia had been training on Sinnoh's Mt. Coronet before challenging the "Champion's Road."

Mt. Coronet's danger level was no less than Glaseado Mountain's.

Worst case... they could spend days on the mountain and nights flying down.

They weren't in a rush anyway—just don't fly too fast.

Hearing Johan enthusiastically describe how good and suitable Glaseado Mountain was, Cynthia blinked and said softly:

"But Glaseado Mountain is similar to Mt. Coronet, and I've already trained on Mt. Coronet for a long time."

Johan's movements paused as his mouth twitched. "What do you think then?"

Cynthia smiled. "The Great Crater of Paldea, or... Kitakami, what do you think?"

Johan: "..."

He'd been enthusiastically recommending Glaseado Mountain precisely because he didn't want to go to these two places.

Though the Paradox Pokémon deep in the Great Crater of Paldea should be interesting—Johan was curious about Iron Valiant—there was also a high probability of encountering "Guardians," which would be very troublesome.

As for Kitakami... that was just a weird place.

A group of people were completely fooled by Pecharunt's story, treating Ogerpon as the villain instead.

Though Ogerpon might be very interesting, they might also get involved in "Kitakami's strange tales" troubles.

Of course, Paldea had the "Ten Sights of Paldea," and Kitakami had the "Kitakami Six."

But after seeing the "Ten Sights of Paldea," Johan wasn't really interested in these so-called "sights" and "selections."

"How about... Galar?" Johan asked tentatively.

Cynthia smiled sweetly, took out her phone, switched to an interface, and handed it to Johan. "Here?"

Taking the phone for a glance, Johan was stunned.

"Okoya? Mossui? Where's this?"

It felt somewhat familiar, but he couldn't recall immediately.

Scrolling through the screen briefly—

Mossui Town was an ancient town with a long history, located in vast mountain forests.

Any forest area with extremely large coverage would have high-level wild Pokémon in its depths.

Actually, the Pokémon world didn't only have nine League regions—there were many other large and small continents.

Some, like the Alola region, once didn't belong to the League but gradually became part of it as exchanges with the League increased.

Many regions had been like this.

Some regions, though related to the League, were never incorporated into League territory, like Orre and Fiore regions, etc.

Of course, some areas chose to join certain regions due to insufficient size, strength, and other factors, becoming part of them.

The most famous were naturally the Orange Islands, Sevii Islands—even Master Mustard's Isle of Armor counted, plus Unova's Blueberry Academy and Paldea's Kitakami region.

The region where Mossui Town was located was also one of these.

Strictly speaking, Mossui Town's region was even connected to Kitakami, though separated by vast forests with great distances.

So Mossui Town was also considered part of the Paldea region.

Continuing to scroll, Johan showed understanding.

"Mossui Town Fireworks Festival!"

So that's it.

These days were when Mossui Town held its autumn fireworks festival to celebrate harvest joy.

"Let's go there then," Johan decided.

They could train in the forest, bully wild Pokémon—cough, have "friendly" exchanges with wild Pokémon—and watch the fireworks festival.

By the time the fireworks festival ended, the "points stage" would be nearly over too.

"Okay~" Cynthia "obediently" agreed, playfully winking at Gengar.

"Ka-kaah~ (Д)b"

Gengar secretly gave a thumbs up.

Cynthia could still handle Johan.

Johan was thinking about something else.

Since there was a large forest outside Mossui Town, would it benefit Serperior?

His Snivy had evolved into Serperior and confirmed the existence of "Forest's Blessing," but seemed to have never been to a large forest...

"But before going to Mossui Town, I plan to return to Hoenn first," Johan suddenly said.

"Return to Hoenn?" Cynthia was stunned.

Johan looked toward distant Hoenn, saying softly: "Because... a little friend has been waiting for us for a long time."

Little friend?

"Ka-kaah!!"

Gengar and the others knew who Johan meant, immediately becoming excited.

On the plane returning to Hoenn.

"Steven VS Raihan?"

Looking at the livestream title pinned on the League website's homepage, Johan couldn't help clicking in.

The location was Wyndon Stadium in the Galar region.

Though Johan had kept Steven in Hoenn to "train" him and help him adapt to Champion duties early, Steven couldn't possibly miss this "Pokémon World Championships." Half a month ago, Steven had temporarily returned duties to Elite Four Glacia and rushed to Galar to gain points.

Steven's strength was indeed formidable. In just half a month, he'd already qualified for the "Master Replacement Battle."

Though his point ranking wasn't as high as Raihan's, no one dared underestimate Steven's strength.

Honestly, if Hoenn hadn't produced the "monster" Teacher Johan, Steven would likely be Hoenn League's Champion now!

So Steven was someone with Champion-level strength without Champion status.

Coincidentally, Raihan was similar.

Without Leon, Raihan would undoubtedly be Galar League's Champion.

In the anime, Raihan was originally one of the "Eight Masters," though he lost to protagonist Ash in the "Master Replacement Battle."

This proved Raihan's strength was Champion-level.

However, due to contact with Johan, current Steven had grown much faster than in the original story with a higher strength ceiling, so... in raw power, Steven was still stronger than Raihan.

Moreover, Raihan specialized in weather and was called the "Weather Prince," but Johan was also a weather "expert." As Johan's student, Steven had more or less mastered methods to counter weather.

For example...

"Cradily, Sandstorm!" In the livestream, the handsome Steven swung his hand sideways with full confidence!

Combined with Cradily's "Storm Drain" ability, he directly converted Raihan's Rain into Sandstorm.

"Steven has gotten stronger," Cynthia, also watching the livestream, couldn't help saying.

The two had battled before, more than once, and it was hard to determine a real winner, so Cynthia was quite familiar with Steven's strength.

"He hasn't been idle in Hoenn either," Johan laughed.

Steven's talent and ability were unquestionable.

While managing Devon Corporation and collecting stones, he could still become Hoenn League Champion, plus Devon's resources...

If Steven focused entirely on Pokémon, reaching legendary level would be normal.

For this "Pokémon World Championships," besides some Champion daily management for the League, Steven had truly focused on Pokémon training and battles.

He'd even held back from stone hunting for two months.

Plus being in a period of rapid strength growth, his improvement was naturally obvious.

"I heard that when Steven has free time, he even pulls Drake, Norman, and Phoebe into battle practice," Johan added.

Cynthia was speechless.

A serious Steven... was truly "frightening."

Johan continued with a smile: "I estimate many people will get nervous after watching this battle."

Especially those who might be challenged by Steven in the "Master Replacement Battle"—Lance, Professor Kukui, Leon, etc.

Cynthia nodded seriously, her expression becoming more serious.

Wasn't she also someone who might be challenged by Steven?

Johan was also possible.

In the Pokémon world, students defeating teachers wasn't shameful—it was glorious.

But for Johan... losing to Steven would be fine too.

He could conveniently pass the Champion position to Steven.

His island was already built anyway, and Steven was now much more familiar with Hoenn League affairs than him.

"Johan," Cynthia said softly.

"Mm?"

"When we reach Mossui Town, train with me in battles."

"Ah, huh?" Johan was stunned.

"Is that okay?"

Johan's expression fell as he forced a smile: "Sure..."

Hoenn region, the island where the Battle Frontier was located.

Johan and Cynthia didn't enter the Battle Frontier but walked directly toward the mountain forest in the opposite direction.

The familiar stone pillar forest.

The night sky was filled with brilliant stars, but compared to their last visit, it was missing that comet with a long tail.

"Ka-kaah..."

Mischievous Gengar somehow became dejected, drooping its ears as if remembering something beautiful but seemingly imperfect.

Cynthia also noticed the mood changes in Johan, Gengar, and several Pokémon.

Not understanding the specific situation or knowing how to comfort them, she could only silently hold Johan's hand tightly.

"Huff—"

Johan exhaled deeply, patted Gengar's head, and said:

"Don't be sad. Don't forget why we came here."

"Ka-kaah!" Gengar slapped its forehead.

Right! They almost forgot they came to wake up that little guy.

"Ka-kaah!"

I know the way—follow me!

In the night, Gengar impatiently floated toward somewhere in the stone pillar forest.

Soon, they reached that familiar place again.

The babbling stream, shores covered with pebbles, and beneath tall stone pillars was an excellent outdoor camping site.

Arriving here, both Johan and Gengar seemed to hear that familiar voice—

[Johan, let's play together, Johan! I love Johan the most...]

Whether the meteorite Eternatus had summoned was powerful enough remained to be seen.

"Gengar, take it out," Johan said.

As he spoke, the little ones emerged from their Poké Balls one by one.

Except for Tyranitar and Serperior who looked confused, the other Pokémon showed expectant expressions.

Hopefully, it would work...

"Ka-kaah!" Gengar didn't hesitate, immediately tearing open space beside it and pulling out a meteorite radiating misty light from the crack.

Cosmic Power!

"Ka-kaah?"

Gengar held the meteorite, placing it left and right, shaking it.

No effect?

Johan frowned.

That shouldn't be right...

The meteorite Eternatus had summoned not only contained rich Galar particles but possessed extremely strong cosmic power, no less than the original comet's cosmic power.

"Ka-kaah!!"

Gengar put down the meteorite, cupped its hands like a megaphone, and shouted loudly, startling a large flock of birds in the nighttime forest.

Jirachi!!

Seeing this, Dragonite, Tinkaton, Urshifu, and others all began calling out.

Johan immediately cupped his hands like a megaphone and shouted:

"Jirachi, I've come to take you home!!"

Hum—

A special energy fluctuation flashed by.

The meteorite Gengar had put down suddenly floated up, radiating cosmic power full of mysterious aura, gathering toward somewhere in the stone pillar forest.

It's coming!

Johan immediately perked up, spreading his aura power.

[Mm...]

A low moan carried the laziness of waking up, transmitted through aura.

[It's Johan, it's everyone, calling me...]

The telepathic connection through aura sounded again.

Just then, a crystal radiating weak light suddenly floated up. The cosmic power rippling from the meteorite was completely absorbed by this crystal.

The next second, the crystal suddenly burst with dazzling brilliance, brilliant light shimmering.

"Oowoo~"

The little ones immediately surrounded it.

"This is..." Cynthia looked at the floating crystal, hearing Johan's earlier words, finally understanding.

Jirachi?

Johan and the others came here to awaken the legendary mythical Pokémon, Jirachi, who could only wake for seven days every thousand years?

Looking at the meteorite Eternatus had left behind, Cynthia showed understanding.

So that's it.

The power of the cosmos?

At this moment, the crystal blazed with light.

Rotom immediately set up filming.

"Eh? Haven't I filmed this scene before, Rotom?"

Simultaneously, the dazzling light receded like a tide. As the light faded, a familiar, cute little one appeared in Johan and the others' sight again.

It looked like a star, wrapped in a golden cloth bag, with wish tags stuck to its head, eyes closed, appearing very cute.

Jirachi!

Its eyelids trembled lightly as Jirachi slowly opened its innocent big eyes, blinking twice.

When it saw clearly that it was Johan and Gengar and the others, joy immediately showed in its eyes.

[Johan!]

[Everyone!]

The golden ribbons loosened as Jirachi stretched its little hands and feet while floating up, then rushed toward Johan.

[Johan! Johan!]

Johan was directly knocked to the ground by the little one but still smiled while holding it.

Finally, they'd awakened Jirachi.

That fight with Eternatus was worth it!

[Johan! Johan!]

Jirachi's voice was full of joy.

[Has a thousand years passed already? So fast~ Johan is indeed still alive after a thousand years!]

Johan: "..."

A thousand years?

Actually, not even a year had passed.

"Cough, Jirachi, only about half a year has passed, at most ten months."

[Months?]

Jirachi looked up at the moon in the sky, showing confusion.

[Ten moons have changed?]

Johan: "..."

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar held its stomach, pointing at Johan and laughing heartily.

Johan smiled and shook his head, pulling Jirachi into his embrace.

"Regardless, it's good that you can wake up, Jirachi."

Jirachi also hugged Johan, giggling.

[Good! Good!]

# 770: The Power of the Forest

[Johan, wake up! Johan, wake up!]

Early in the morning, Johan was awakened from his sleep by an energetic voice.

He instinctively reached for the Cheri Berry by his bedside, then realized he probably didn't need it.

Opening his eyes, he saw a cute little face close to his, looking full of energy.

Seeing Jirachi like this, Johan couldn't help but laugh.

How rare.

The morning "wake-up service" wasn't from Gengar for once.

Last night they'd awakened Jirachi, and the little one became full of energy upon waking, not only being affectionate with Johan but also meeting many new friends.

Like gentle Cynthia, tough-talking Garchomp, simple Tyranitar, Serperior with its queenly dignity, Victini with its fangs, shy Marshadow...

This made Jirachi incredibly happy.

Plus, having slept for so long, it seemed to have limitless energy, which meant Gengar and the others' "adventure plan" hadn't succeeded last night.

However, this also proved from the side that the cosmic power carried by the meteorite Eternatus had summoned could indeed serve as Jirachi's power, allowing it to recover and awaken.

The only question was how long the cosmic power in that meteorite could keep the little one awake...

Thinking this, Johan couldn't help rubbing the little one's head and pinching its soft, bread-like cheeks.

[Jo-han, time to get up, Jo-han (face pinching)]

Johan laughed heartily. "Alright, getting up!"

Regardless, for however long Jirachi stayed conscious, he'd play with it properly and show it things it had never seen before.

If worst came to worst... since Eternatus's meteorite worked for Jirachi, he could just find it again!

Beat it up a couple more times, and maybe it could summon another such meteorite.

As a last resort, they had so many Pokémon friends who could enter space—like Rayquaza, Deoxys, Solgaleo, Lunala, etc.

Someday they'd find another meteorite rich in cosmic energy.

When that time came... Jirachi might no longer be "once every thousand years" but every few years, months, or even shorter!

[Johan, everyone, let's play together!]

Jirachi floated out of the tent, happily calling to everyone.

Except for the rather shy Marshadow who kept hiding in shadows, the other little ones got along quite well with Jirachi.

After all, it was such a pure and lively little one that wouldn't persistently bother just one Pokémon but would play with this one, then go play with that one.

The one who got along best with Jirachi was naturally the equally mischievous and lively Gengar.

It could be said that many of Jirachi's "little quirks" were learned from Gengar.

Additionally, Jirachi and Victini also got along very well, perhaps because both had once tasted loneliness, or perhaps because... both were full of energy.

"Jirachi, Victini, Marshadow, plus Tinkaton's little follower Meltan—that's already four mythical Pokémon," Johan counted on his fingers.

Unknowingly, through capturing or establishing friendly relationships, he'd already raised four mythical Pokémon?

Fortunately, these little things didn't have big appetites—much easier to feed than those legendary Pokémon.

Rayquaza, Giratina, the Black and White Dragons—each one ate more than the last.

"So energetic," Cynthia said with a smile, watching Jirachi and Victini floating around.

"Well, they sleep for a thousand years just to wake up for seven days."

Johan said after finishing breakfast preparations.

"Mm..."

Thinking about such a lively, cute little one only being able to wake up for seven days every thousand years, Cynthia also felt sorry for it.

[Let's play together! Johan, Cynthia, come play with us!]

Jirachi happily waved at them both.

Johan smiled: "Breakfast first, Jirachi. After breakfast, we'll take you out to play."

Hearing this, Jirachi's eyes immediately sparkled like stars, its little head nodding like a pecking chicken.

[Yay! Going out to play! Going out to play!]

Last time, because Jirachi woke up suddenly, Johan had only taken it to play in the Battle Frontier for a few days.

This time, they'd go farther away, see more different scenery, and encounter more different, interesting Pokémon.

This didn't conflict with Johan and the others' special training plans either.

The first stop was Mossui Town.

Autumn Fireworks Festival!

Mossui Town.

[Wow—]

Jirachi looked around curiously.

Mossui Town was an ancient historical town where buildings with old historical atmosphere could be seen everywhere, though most had actually been renovated.

Moreover, though Mossui Town was surrounded by vast forests, the town's various facilities were complete.

After all, vast forests meant many wild Pokémon, and many wild Pokémon meant many trainers, which meant many Pokémon-related institutions and facilities.

It was said that Mossui Town had several sizable companies—some researching wild Pokémon, some studying forest plants, and others researching various forest products.

Mossui Town was most famous for its fireworks festival.

Because the fireworks festival was approaching, many trainers came to see it.

Plus the recent "Pokémon World Championships," battles between trainers could be seen almost everywhere on streets and training grounds.

Of course, with more people came more vendors and stalls.

[What's that?]

Jirachi, who had been curious about everything since boarding the plane, immediately floated over upon seeing a boy on the street cleaning up trash.

"Wh-what is it?"

The person obviously didn't recognize Jirachi and was startled by its sudden appearance.

Fortunately, Jirachi didn't look scary, so he quickly recovered and asked with a smile:

"Do you have trash to throw away? You can give it all to my Trubbish."

"Trub~"

A simple Trubbish beside him waved at Jirachi.

[Trash?]

Jirachi bit its finger, looking at Trubbish's "eager" eyes, then at its other hand.

[Do you want trash?]

As it spoke, Jirachi's hand began moving gently, mysterious power spreading from its body.

Seeing this, Johan's eye twitched as he quickly ran over and hugged Jirachi.

Interrupt the casting!

If Jirachi made this wish come true and summoned a huge pile of trash, that would be troublesome.

"Sorry." Johan dug into Gengar's mouth and pulled out a bag of camping trash from before, feeding it to Trubbish.

"Trub~"

Trubbish stuffed it directly into its body, looking very happy.

Seeing this scene, Jirachi looked enlightened.

So that's what kind of trash it was.

"Don't casually use wish-granting abilities, Jirachi," Johan instructed.

[Oh...]

Jirachi remembered what Johan had told it when it last woke up.

Wishes should be achieved by oneself to have meaning.

Wishes granted out of thin air wouldn't be treasured.

"Of course, if someone really needs help, within our capabilities, we can provide some assistance," Johan taught the innocent little one.

[Really needing help...]

Jirachi became lively again, randomly picking a passerby to follow and asking:

[Do you need help?]

[Does anyone need help?]

Johan held his forehead.

"Um... excuse me, are you Teacher Johan?"

At this moment, the boy with Trubbish collecting trash seemed to finally recognize Johan, asking with surprise and uncertainty.

"Well, you've got the wrong person."

Johan covered his face and "fled."

"Wrong person?" The boy didn't pursue, just continued collecting trash.

To others, it might just be garbage, but to the boy and his Trubbish, it was Trubbish's food.

"Having a Trubbish is really nice," Johan couldn't help thinking.

Extremely low food requirements, plus it helps handle trash...

Except for Trubbish's smell and potentially toxic gas it could release, everything was so "perfect."

"Johan~"

When he returned to Cynthia's side, she suddenly called softly.

"Mm?"

"Want some."

Cynthia indicated a nearby stall.

The busy vendor and his Vanilluxe were busily making ice cream.

Seeing this, Johan smiled helplessly: "I'll go buy some for you."

Soon, Johan, Cynthia, and Pokémon like Gengar and Jirachi all had ice cream and continued touring Mossui Town.

Walking along, they reached Mossui Town's central plaza.

Many devices prepared for the fireworks festival were already set up here, with staff and Pokémon busily working among them.

The fireworks festival was still two days away, but preparations had already begun for perfect execution.

Many people gathered around, all anticipating the festival's start.

After Johan's group looked around and toured Mossui Town with Jirachi for a while, they went to the Pokémon Center and checked in.

Afterward, they prepared for outdoor special training.

The next day.

Outside Mossui Town, Okoya Forest.

If they were academic trainers, going into the wild would inevitably require caution—any disturbance could affect their nerves.

But for Johan and Cynthia, two Champions who had conquered the "Champion's Road," no matter how dangerous the wild environment, it wouldn't pose much obstacle.

Not long after leaving Mossui Town, they went directly to the depths of Okoya Forest.

"Let's begin special training."

After reaching a clearing in the forest depths and setting up their tent, Cynthia invited Johan to train.

Compared to training with wild Pokémon, Cynthia actually valued training with Johan more.

Johan's face showed bitterness.

Was this special training for Pokémon or for himself?

"Serperior," Johan called.

"Sni!"

Serperior flashed to Johan's side.

Seeing Johan's first choice was actually Serperior, Cynthia was slightly stunned.

She'd thought it would start with a battle between Gengar and Garchomp, not expecting Johan to lead with Serperior.

However... there was still plenty of training time ahead, so starting with Serperior wasn't wrong.

Taking a light breath, Cynthia set aside her girlfriend demeanor, becoming serious and showing her Champion qualities perfectly.

Her coat fluttered as she threw a Poké Ball.

What appeared was Togekiss!

Johan grimaced.

He was at a disadvantage.

They should have summoned Pokémon together—now he was at a type disadvantage.

The question was whether Serperior could resonate with this forest and withstand Togekiss's Air Slash.

If Serperior got a chance to use Leaf Storm twice for power-ups, even Togekiss would struggle.

The two looked at each other.

They spoke almost simultaneously.

"Togekiss, Thunder Wave!"

"Serperior, Grassy Terrain!"

Their special training began.

Meanwhile, Pokémon not participating in training wandered around the area, especially Gengar and Jirachi.

[Gengar, let's play! Let's play together!]

Jirachi called out.

This forest was full of Pokémon it had never seen.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar thumped its chest.

Let's go! I'll take you on an "adventure"!

[Adventure! Adventure!]

Gengar led Jirachi as they quietly disappeared into the forest.

Bang!!

Before long, sounds of Pokémon collision suddenly came from the forest.

"Ka-kaah~" Gengar gestured "shh" to Jirachi, using shadows to hide them both.

[Hehe!]

Jirachi covered its mouth, showing Gengar's trademark mischievous smile with thief-like joy.

When the two little ones got closer to look, they found a Grapploct and a Pangoro fighting, surrounded by many wild Pokémon who seemed to be trying to mediate?

Hidden in tree shade, Gengar and Jirachi watched with interest.

Clashes between wild Pokémon were common—over territory, food, mates, and other reasons.

However, while Gengar and Jirachi were guessing why they were fighting, a figure swinging on vines quickly approached from the distance, landing beside Pangoro and Grapploct.

"Ka-kaah?"

[A person?]

They'd expected another Pokémon, but both Gengar and Jirachi were surprised to see a human.

However, the person looked like a savage—bare-chested with unknown white paint, wearing tattered hemp clothing, wrists wrapped with withered but tough vines, appearing about ten years old.

The person quickly ran between Pangoro and Grapploct, grabbing both with one hand each, trying to forcibly separate them.

While shouting, "Zarude! Zarude!"

Eh?

Gengar and Jirachi looked at each other.

This felt even stranger.

Though clearly human, if speaking human language, they... couldn't understand.

But what he spoke was actually Pokémon language that both Gengar and Jirachi understood!

What surprised Gengar and Jirachi more was that the human's small frame actually seemed capable of separating Pangoro and Grapploct.

Such strength!

"Zarude!"

In the tree shade.

"Ka-kaah..." Gengar rubbed its chin.

This person's strength was almost comparable to Johan's.

[Johan has great strength?]

Jirachi asked in surprise.

Did Johan have such strength?

Gengar nodded seriously, indicating that Johan could lift something as big as itself with one hand.

Jirachi looked shocked, fully satisfied emotionally.

Bang! Bang!!

At this moment, the boy finally separated Pangoro and Grapploct, asking:

"Why are you fighting?" (Translated by Gengar and Jirachi)

Following Pangoro and Grapploct's indication, he noticed the mess on the ground—completely destroyed berries and food.

"Zarude..." Seeing this scene, the boy stood frozen.

Though the forest had abundant food, it also had more wild Pokémon, so food was always a scarce resource.

This area had many wild berry trees that were almost ripe for everyone to share and feast on, yet they'd been destroyed like this.

More importantly... the boy picked up a berry that had been bitten once then discarded and trampled, immediately understanding who had done this.

"Roar!!"

"Grappl!!"

Pangoro and Grapploct began arguing again.

"Zarude!" The boy stopped them again.

Remarkably, though clearly human and not appearing particularly strong besides his strength, he could make Pangoro and Grapploct stop arguing.

"Ka-kaah?"

Suddenly, Gengar in the shadows looked up toward the tall forest not far from the boy and others.

There were several Pokémon resembling apes with black fur, gray fur on chest and back forming special patterns almost identical to the boy's chest pattern, green vines wrapped around their wrists, and seeds on their backs and heels.

They watched from a distance, mockingly looking at the boy and wild Pokémon.

Their laughter was loud enough to attract the boy's attention.

"Zarude!"

It's you!

The boy glared angrily.

If Johan were here, he'd recognize these black ape-like Pokémon as the mythical Pokémon, Zarude!

The several Zarude showed contempt for the boy's angry stare, with the particularly strong leading Zarude stepping forward.

"Zarude!"

Coco, this is a lesson we "elders" are teaching you—this is the forest's survival law!

Coco was the boy's name.

"Zarude!" Coco was furious, moving forward in an ape-like posture, ready to fight.

This behavior actually suited the Zarude's intentions.

It grinned wickedly and stepped forward again.

"Zarude!"

Want me to teach you how to fight!

At this moment, a vine flew swiftly over, and another Zarude arrived, though this one wore a pink cape, looking quite strange.

It positioned itself between Coco and the leading Zarude.

The leading Zarude seemed somewhat wary of this pink-caped Zarude, showing fierce expressions but not rashly advancing.

"Zarude!" Seeing the pink-caped Zarude, Coco immediately approached and called out.

Dad!

Gengar and Jirachi looked at each other again.

Did they hear wrong?

A human was actually calling a Pokémon... Dad?

Bang!!

As they stared in surprise, the pink-caped Zarude had already collided with the strong Zarude.

"Ka-kaah..."

This show is getting quite interesting...

"Sni!"

Serperior panted heavily, using vine agility to barely avoid Togekiss's Air Slash.

Type disadvantage, strength advantage, plus aerial advantage made the battle between Togekiss and Serperior easier than expected.

But Serperior showed no signs of giving up.

Breathing out white air, it faintly inhaled some air mixed with greenish color.

Hum—

Serperior's body gradually radiated energy full of healing and forest auras.

Connected?

Johan raised an eyebrow.

But at this moment, Serperior looked surprised and confused, standing up and looking in one direction.

"Sni?"

Seeing this, Cynthia and Togekiss didn't take advantage of this opportunity.

After all, this was training, not about determining winners—the process was much more important than results.

It's just that Serperior's sudden distraction during focused battle surprised them.

Johan was the same. Following Serperior's gaze, he could only see vast forest and misty haze in the distance—too far to see clearly.

"What's wrong, Serperior?" Johan asked.

"Sni..." Serperior murmured.

The power of the forest...

----------

Powerstones?

# 771: Zarude's Natural Law!

"What were you two up to?"

Johan, who was treating wounds on Serperior and Togekiss, saw Gengar and Jirachi sneaking back and called out to them.

The two little ones immediately startled and spoke simultaneously.

"Ka-kaah~" Gengar picked its nose.

Nothing much, just wandering around nearby~

[We went to watch a fight! Really amazing guys, whoosh whoosh whoosh—]

Jirachi looked excited, hands flailing.

Looking carefully, one could see that small vines were wrapped around Jirachi's wrists.

Hmm?

The two little ones hadn't coordinated their stories. Johan showed a knowing smile.

Gengar and Jirachi looked at each other.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar immediately pulled Jirachi behind it, then grinned mischievously:

"Ka, ka-kaah~"

Went out for a stroll, saw some wild Pokémon clashing, so we watched for a little while~

As it spoke, its fingers made a "tiny" gesture.

Johan shook his head, not pursuing the matter further.

Clashes between wild Pokémon were common—you'd encounter them anywhere in the wild. As long as Gengar and the others didn't harass those wild Pokémon, it was fine.

"Alright."

He gently patted Togekiss's wing, indicating it was fine now.

"Chato~"

Togekiss flapped its wings affectionately to thank Johan, then flew away happily.

For sustained outdoor training, trainers needed the ability to treat basic Pokémon injuries.

Fortunately, both Johan and Cynthia knew restraint. Training wasn't about determining winners—they stopped appropriately, so the Pokémon's injuries weren't severe.

Togekiss still needed Johan's help, but Serperior...

It only needed to sit on the ground for a while. The power of forest and plants, sun and light could all help it recover.

Sure enough, Serperior sitting on the grass patted its knees and stood up. The grass around its body had grown several times more lush than the surrounding grassland, grown several cycles taller.

"Sni..."

Serperior looked in one direction again.

For some reason, it felt... something in that direction seemed very attractive to it.

"What's wrong, Serperior? You've seemed distracted since the battle," Johan asked.

"Sni~ Sni~"

Serperior didn't hide anything, expressing its feelings.

"Oh?"

Hearing this, Johan was stunned.

That direction in the forest?

He and Cynthia had just camped here, and planned to watch Mossui Town's fireworks festival the day after tomorrow...

"Did Serperior sense something?" Cynthia also noticed this and asked proactively.

"Mm," Johan nodded. "But I don't know how far it is."

He looked in the direction Serperior sensed, where distant mountains and forests still lay shrouded in mist—who knew how far this forest extended.

"Want to take a look?" Cynthia suggested.

Though Johan had promised to train with her, as a Champion, Cynthia clearly understood that if such sensing could bring benefits, it would definitely be more valuable than training.

"But what about here..."

Setting up camp was troublesome too.

If it were just Johan, it would be fine—just packing up the tent. But Cynthia, being a girl... cough cough, inevitably prepared more things.

Cynthia smiled slightly. "You go and come back quickly. I'll stay here and train with Garchomp and the others."

Going alone?

How considerate...

Johan didn't hesitate, nodding: "Alright, we'll be quick."

Speaking, Johan called his Pokémon, picked up the confused Jirachi, and climbed onto Dragonite's back.

"Sni!"

Serperior also jumped onto Dragonite's head, vines wrapping around to secure itself.

"Dragonite, let's go!"

"Draa~!"

Dragonite called out joyfully, spread its wings, and soared skyward.

"Slow—slow down a bit, no need to rush—"

Hearing Johan's calls growing distant, Cynthia smiled sweetly and called Garchomp and Lucario to continue training.

"Sni!"

Serperior stood on Dragonite's head guiding direction, crossing unknown distances of forest to reach a basin area deep in the forest.

Through the swirling mist, they saw a massive ancient tree rooted in the basin.

In the basin hundreds of meters deep, the ancient tree's canopy was almost level with the basin's edge.

This was almost the largest tree Johan had ever seen, second only to the World Tree where Mew lived!

Moreover, from a distance, they could see light purple liquid flowing down from the tree's canopy into the lake below.

Streams of water fell like waterfalls.

Logically, around such a naturally vibrant giant tree, large numbers of wild Pokémon should live, but this entire basin seemed exceptionally quiet.

Except for the "splash splash" of flowing water and the "rustle rustle" of wind through leaves, there wasn't much other commotion.

When Johan saw this giant tree, he instinctively froze.

"This is..."

Damn, wasn't this the "Sacred Tree" where the mythical Pokémon Zarude lived—from the movie "Pokémon the Movie: Secrets of the Jungle" with the "wolf child" Coco and his dad?

"Mossui Town, Okoya Forest..."

He'd wondered why it sounded familiar before.

So it was here...

Now Johan understood why Serperior had sensed this giant tree.

This tree was the "Sacred Tree" of the mythical Pokémon Zarude clan. Similar to the World Tree, within its thick trunk lay extremely powerful and mysterious energy.

The light purple water flowing from the canopy was the diluted product of the trunk's mysterious energy.

People who discovered this water's effects called it the "Healing Spring."

It possessed mysterious power to heal injuries in Pokémon and humans.

Without exaggeration, as long as injuries weren't severe, Pokémon could fully recover by soaking in the spring water under the tree.

Somewhat similar to... the spring water with powerful healing abilities that Celebi soaked in.

This held enormous appeal and could provide tremendous help to any human or Pokémon.

However, the Zarude clan occupied this basin and the "Sacred Tree" they inhabited. Any approaching creatures, whether wild Pokémon or humans, would be considered enemies and driven away.

Moreover, the Zarude clan believed in "natural law"—that the strong possessed everything, survival of the fittest.

Actually, this wasn't problematic.

After all, in the wild, most Pokémon believed in such "law."

Being powerful, they naturally could occupy this basin protected by the "Sacred Tree."

But the problem was that a group of humans had also targeted this basin—more precisely, the "Sacred Tree" within it.

"Healing Spring"!

If they could possess it, what enormous profits would it bring?

It might even challenge the Joy family's established position.

Moreover, what if they researched the "Healing Spring" further?

Could it extend human and Pokémon lifespans, even achieve immortality?

No one could refuse immortality's temptation!

Of course, immortality was just talk—whether it could be researched was another matter, but the healing ability was real.

"Let's go down," Johan patted Dragonite's neck.

Serperior with "Forest's Blessing" could definitely benefit from this "Sacred Tree."

As for whether the Zarude agreed...

Agreement would naturally be best. If they disagreed, then he'd make them agree!

Since the Zarude believed in "natural law," Johan would naturally use their "reasoning" to have a good "chat" with them.

Johan always considered himself very reasonable.

"Draa~(ˊˋ)"

On a tree outside the basin, "wolf child" Coco angrily jumped on the tree.

The Zarude occupying the "Sacred Tree" was one thing—why did they also destroy everyone's food?

Papa Zarude looked helpless.

Honestly, as a Zarude, it understood the Zarude clan's style and... didn't think there was much wrong with it.

In nature, fists naturally represented everything.

Their fists were hard, so they could do whatever they wanted.

But its son had completely different ideas from the Zarude.

He hoped everyone could coexist peacefully, share food, share the "Sacred Tree"—with a child's innocence.

Papa Zarude couldn't explain these things clearly, just thinking that perhaps when the child grew older, he'd understand.

Suddenly—

"Zarude!" Papa Zarude's eyes sharpened, standing on the treetop looking toward above the basin.

It saw... a Dragonite descending toward the "Sacred Tree."

Very fast.

"Zarude?"

Papa, what's that?

Following Papa Zarude's gaze, Coco also noticed Dragonite.

Hearing this, Papa Zarude shook its head.

It didn't know either.

But it felt... the newcomers weren't friendly.

"Zarude!" Papa Zarude solemnly placed its hand on Coco's shoulder, indicating he should stay here and not run around.

Then it swung its arm, shooting a vine from its wrist that wrapped around a thick branch, swinging toward the "Sacred Tree."

Watching Papa's departing figure, then looking at Dragonite about to disappear in the mist, Coco ultimately didn't follow his papa's wishes.

He untied the withered vines from his wrists and followed Papa Zarude's direction in the same manner.

Bang!!

Dragonite landed on the "Sacred Tree's" thick trunk.

As soon as it landed, Zarude of various sizes immediately appeared on surrounding trunks!

The leading ones were robust and strong.

"Zarude!"

A fierce-faced Zarude stepped forward, snarling at Johan.

Who are you people! What are you doing here!

Before Johan could speak, little Jirachi in his arms spoke up fiercely:

[Why are you being mean!]

?

Question marks appeared above Johan's head.

The opposing Zarude was also stunned by Jirachi's words.

Having witnessed Zarude destroying wild Pokémon food with Gengar, causing many wild Pokémon to go hungry, Jirachi had no good feelings toward the Zarude.

As for being afraid... little Jirachi didn't know what fear meant.

"Cough." Johan coughed lightly, calming the fierce little Jirachi, then smiled:

"We mean no harm. It's just that my partner Serperior sensed this tree might help it. Could we please approach and take a look?"

Though knowing the Zarude wouldn't agree, out of habit, Johan still asked politely first.

The Zarude looked at Serperior.

When they heard Johan wanted to approach the "Sacred Tree," their fur stood on end, snarling with fierce expressions.

"Zarude!"

You're not welcome here—get lost!

Speaking, the leading Zarude swung its arm, sending a thick vine striking toward Johan.

As expected—they attacked without discussion. Johan sighed inwardly.

However, you attacked first.

"Sni!"

Whoosh—

A vine whip flew from behind Johan, colliding with Zarude's vine. Both vines bounced apart.

"Zarude?"

The leading Zarude looked surprised.

It hadn't expected a mere Serperior to match it?

This human...

Zarude narrowed its eyes at Johan.

The newcomers weren't friendly.

Stomp! Stomp! Stomp—

Suddenly, the Zarude on surrounding trees began stomping their feet rhythmically while calling out.

"Zarude! Zarude! Zarude!"

Like some strange ritual.

[Eh?]

Jirachi looked curiously at this scene, not knowing what these guys wanted to do.

But the next second, in this special rhythmic stomping, the previously attacking Zarude's expression hardened as it rushed toward Johan and Serperior.

"Serperior!" Johan called out lightly.

Though he didn't understand what the Zarude were doing, he guessed it was probably some kind of dueling ritual.

"Sni!"

Serperior understood, vine whips flying from behind it as it charged toward Zarude without retreat.

Slap! Slap—

Vine whips and vines constantly collided.

Both sides seemed evenly matched.

However, when Serperior and Zarude closed to a certain distance, Zarude roared low. The vines from its wrists glowed green, rapidly weaving into a huge fist that struck toward Serperior's face.

Power Whip!

Use vine whips to confuse the opponent, then suddenly change tactics to catch them off guard?

Seeing this, Johan raised an eyebrow.

These Zarude had richer battle experience than he'd imagined.

They even used tactical changes.

But regarding battle experience...

"Sni!"

Serperior's vine whip flew out, not attacking Zarude but wrapping around higher branches.

Using the vine's contraction and its own agile jumping, it perfectly avoided Zarude's Power Whip.

Simultaneously, while leaping high, its tail gathered surrounding leaves into a leaf tornado, counterattacking Zarude.

Leaf Storm!

Bang—

The leaf tornado directly caught the unprepared Zarude, sending it flying.

Thud.

Serperior landed smoothly, chin slightly raised, provocatively beckoning to Zarude.

"Sni~"

Come again~

This action completely enraged Zarude.

"Zarude!"

Roaring, it charged Serperior again.

Seeing this, Serperior curled its lip.

Not calm at all.

What use was reckless charging?

Having strength but not fully utilizing it.

Back then, if it had half this Zarude's strength, it could have beaten Tyranitar and Alcremie so badly their mothers wouldn't recognize them.

Strength—not bad.

Technique—terrible!

Johan naturally knew what Serperior was thinking and couldn't help but smile.

Did it really think every Pokémon was as talented as it?

"Here it comes again—Leaf Storm!"

Johan said quietly.

"Sni!"

Seeing Zarude approaching again, Serperior immediately put away its disdain.

Strategically despise the enemy, tactically respect the enemy. Though scorning Zarude's fighting style, Serperior only showed surface contempt while taking it very seriously internally.

Leaves gathered again, forming an even fiercer leaf tornado that rushed toward Zarude.

"Zarude!!"

Zarude's fury erupted, its entire body seeming to expand a size. Arms crossed, numerous green-glowing vines continuously emerged from its wrists, weaving into a huge cone-shaped shield.

It charged straight through Serperior's Leaf Storm.

Good grief.

This fighting style—not ordinary recklessness.

Actually, though Zarude had decent battle experience, in this forest they were like local tyrants. Few dared challenge them, much less fight them evenly.

Current Serperior could at least spar with Elite Four main Pokémon. This Zarude matching Serperior said everything.

Such strength was enough to dominate this forest.

Not to mention there was a whole group of Zarude here.

Facing this hard-charging approach, Serperior immediately turned its tail, letting countless leaves envelop itself.

Among the scattered leaves, Serperior watched the approaching Zarude with a stern expression.

Bang!!!

Countless leaves scattered as Zarude forced its way through, breaking apart the Leaf Storm.

But when the leaves dispersed, Zarude was stunned.

Where was Serperior?

"Zarude!!" The other Zarude called out loudly.

Behind! Watch behind!

Behind?

Zarude was stunned, then quickly realized and immediately turned around—but it was too late.

Once again—

Leaf Storm!!

Boom!!!

Countless leaves carried tornado-like whips, fiercely striking Zarude and sending it flying again, solidly hitting the tree trunk.

Momentarily, the entire scene fell silent.

Only the "rustle rustle" of wind through leaves remained.

This Zarude could be considered one of their strongest.

Yet... it was so easily defeated by the opponent?

Watching their companion stuck against the tree trunk, struggling to stand, the Zarude were stunned for a while before finally reacting.

"Zarude!"

Bang!

"Zarude!"

Bang!

A roar, followed by the dull sound of fists heavily pounding tree trunks.

Angry!

The entire Zarude clan was furious.

They couldn't remember how long it had been since any Pokémon dared provoke them, even attacking their companions.

The first rule of "natural law" was survival of the fittest.

Then the second rule of "natural law" was strength in numbers!

They were a clan!

Were they afraid of just one human and a couple of Pokémon?

Seeing this scene, Jirachi rubbed its chubby cheeks.

[They're going to fight! They're going to fight!]

Johan was both amused and exasperated.

"Fighting's not your turn anyway." While holding Jirachi to prevent it from running around, Johan's palm swept across his waist.

Ninetales, Tinkaton, Urshifu, Ceruledge, and Tyranitar, plus Gengar hiding in shadows, belly-patting Dragonite, and battle-tested Serperior.

Full battle force deployed!

Without exaggeration, with everyone out, Johan would dare face even first-class legendaries now.

Much less just a group of Zarude.

"Zarude!!"

Some Zarude suddenly shouted, and all surrounding Zarude leaped high simultaneously, rushing toward Johan's group.

"Ninetales, Urshifu."

Johan half-closed his eyes. "Don't make too much noise—don't break the tree."

Though he'd summoned all his little ones, Johan still decided... to let the relatively restrained Ninetales and Bear Coach act.

Otherwise—whether it was Tinkaton's hammer blow, Ceruledge's fire, or Tyranitar's "Rock Spear," something here would probably break.

"Oowoo~"

Ninetales' tails danced, snow-white crystals falling from its body as ice crystals rapidly formed underfoot while breathing out cold air.

Crack crack crack—

The leaping Zarude were almost instantly frozen into ice sculptures.

"Garruu zzZ"

Urshifu had a snot bubble hanging from its nose. Its form shot out like lightning, flowing like water with continuous strikes, instantly sending several Zarude flying.

Overwhelming!

Unsurprising overwhelming victory!

Numbers seemed rather redundant now.

Just then—

"Zarude!"

A huge fist formed from vines struck the nearly frozen Zarude, sending them flying.

But the vine-formed fist was completely frozen by Ninetales' cold air.

However, it still saved several Zarude.

Bang!!

A Zarude wearing a pink cape, baring its teeth, appeared opposite Johan's group.

Seeing this Zarude, Johan instinctively said:

"Papa?"

"Ptui!"

Coco's papa?

Its strength definitely ranked among the top in the entire Zarude clan.

More importantly, it wasn't a brute—at least it understood tactics and fighting techniques.

It actually came back to help these Zarude?

Right—though it had left the clan for Coco, ultimately... it was still a Zarude.

"Zarude!!"

Papa Zarude looked grave.

It had only arrived this little bit late, yet the opponent had already defeated so many Zarude?

This human... was terrifyingly strong!

Thump!!

"Zarude—"

An aged, elderly voice came from atop the "Sacred Tree."

An old Zarude leaning on a staff, supported by two young Zarude, emerged.

Seeing this Zarude, the others all bowed.

"Zarude!"

Elder!

Johan stopped Ninetales and Bear Coach.

The decision-maker had finally appeared.

Otherwise... he might really have to beat all the Zarude here before helping Serperior approach the "Sacred Tree."

# 772: Sacred Tree and Healing Spring!

"Elder."

Johan called off his little ones and bowed slightly.

Regardless, he should show some respect to elders and seniors.

Moreover, from the Zarude's reactions, it wasn't hard to see that this elder held a significant position among the Zarude clan.

For Zarude who preferred to speak with fists, a relatively wise "sage" was quite valuable.

If he could get what he wanted through peaceful means, Johan wasn't someone who liked violence.

His aura power spread out, releasing his goodwill.

"Zarude?"

Feeling Johan's aura and the goodwill within it, the elder was stunned.

Looking at Johan, this handsome young man clearly looked like a "good person"...

So why had he fought with the Zarude in the clan?

Oh~

The elder understood.

It knew the temperament of these Zarude in its clan best—they definitely provoked the other party first, then fought, discovered they couldn't win, ganged up and still couldn't win...

But this was just the Zarude's usual style, which it understood.

However, this approach was fine against some people or Pokémon they could "bully," but if they kicked an "iron plate"...

The elder approached with young Zarude supporting it.

The other Zarude surrounded them, watching Johan warily.

Though Papa Zarude wanted to do the same, considering it had already left the Zarude clan, it ultimately didn't.

Moreover... it had noticed Coco following all the way.

While showing helplessness in its eyes, it immediately ran over and protected Coco behind it.

"Zarude!"

What are you doing here! Didn't I tell you not to follow? Don't you know how dangerous this is?!

"Zarude~ Zarude~" Coco gestured explanations while looking at Johan with curiosity.

I, I was worried about you, Papa.

Who, who is this guy?

Young Coco discovered that he seemed more similar to this person than the difference between himself and Zarude was much greater.

Regarding whether he was a Zarude, as he grew older, Coco increasingly doubted it.

Papa Zarude's expression immediately tightened upon noticing Coco's expression.

Was what he most feared about to happen?

"Za, Zarude!" Papa Zarude forcibly explained.

He, he is a very powerful Zarude! Very dangerous!

"Zarude?" Coco looked at Johan with deeper confusion.

He's also a Zarude?

I see... so there really are Zarude who look like this!

Coco immediately changed from confusion to delight.

Papa Zarude secretly sighed in relief, gently wiping its forehead.

You're the Zarude, your whole family are Zarude!

"Listening" to their exchange, Johan rolled his eyes irritably.

However, he didn't expose Papa Zarude's words.

As a Zarude who could take on fatherly responsibilities and raise Coco to this size, Johan still admired it.

The elder, supported by young Zarude, had already approached.

"Zarude." The elder's staff struck the ground heavily, stopping the Zarude's calls, then bowed slightly to Johan.

Powerful being, I wonder... what brings you here...

See, without showing your fists, how could they speak properly with you?

Johan suddenly remembered something a celebrity from his past life said—"I study martial arts so that some people can speak properly with me."

This point was particularly effective when applied to Zarude clans.

Johan showed a warm smile. "Elder, I have no malicious intent. It's just that my partner Serperior sensed that your 'Sacred Tree' might help it. Could we possibly go in and take a look?"

Sacred Tree?

The elder's expression immediately tensed.

This concerned their entire Zarude clan's lifeline.

Johan quickly explained: "Don't worry, Elder. We're not here to destroy the Sacred Tree, nor will we cause any permanent damage to it. You... forget it, Serperior."

Speaking, he gave Serperior a look, indicating it should demonstrate its abilities.

"Sni." Serperior curled its lip, somewhat disapproving of the Zarude elder.

However, since Johan said so, it still took a light breath and tried to resonate with the "Sacred Tree" using the feeling it had when interacting with the "Miracle Berry" tree.

Hum—

A peculiar, light cyan energy aura spread from Serperior's body.

Rustle rustle rustle—

The leaves on the "Sacred Tree" began swaying rhythmically.

Numerous surrounding trees, as if summoned by some power, also began swaying their leaves.

Momentarily, the entire forest sea rippled with waves.

Moreover, the light cyan glow on Serperior became more obvious, and its aura visibly strengthened.

Forest's Blessing!

"Zarude?!"

Seeing this scene, the elder's eyes widened, nearly pulling out its beard.

This, this is... a Child of the Forest?!

Not just the elder—the other Zarude, upon seeing this scene and feeling the thick forest aura from Serperior, all showed shocked expressions.

Even Papa Zarude was the same, staring blankly at Serperior, feeling... something inside its body stirring restlessly.

Only Coco looked confused, not understanding what was happening.

"Sni."

After "demonstrating" for a while, Serperior restrained that power.

Previously, it didn't even know it possessed "Forest's Blessing," much less how to use it.

But as its abilities strengthened, evolved, with Calyrex's guidance, and after multiple "interactions" with the "Miracle Berry" tree, Serperior gradually mastered some techniques for invoking this power.

When it called to the forest, the forest would respond.

"Zarude, Zarude..."

I see, I see...

The elder paced back and forth excitedly.

The Zarude prided themselves as "blood of the forest"—their existence made the forest more vibrant.

But clearly, Serperior was more favored by the forest than they were.

More importantly... a Serperior with such power wouldn't destroy the forest or Sacred Tree without reason.

Taking a deep breath, the elder stepped aside slightly and made a welcoming gesture to Johan and Serperior's group.

The nearby Zarude opened their mouths as if wanting to say something, but remembering the power Serperior had just displayed and seeing the elder's prohibitive gaze, they held back.

What else could they do?

The other party had shown their purpose, demonstrated sincerity, and most importantly, displayed strength!

Even if they refused, with the opponent's and those Pokémon's strength, they could completely enter the Sacred Tree without their permission.

Johan smiled and said "Let's go," carrying the increasingly "restless" Jirachi and leading his little ones toward the "Sacred Tree."

Though the Zarude no longer opposed them, they still followed with fierce expressions.

Johan didn't mind these Zarude.

What a joke—with so many "tough guys" beside him, would he fear them?

However, Papa Zarude and Coco were stopped by several Zarude.

"Zarude."

Let's go, Coco.

Knowing it could no longer enter the Sacred Tree, Papa Zarude tactfully beckoned to Coco and turned to leave.

Coco watched Johan walking toward the "Sacred Tree."

For some reason, even though Papa said that guy was also a Zarude, Coco felt that person and himself seemed to be the same species.

Moreover... the elder and other Zarude had clearly said that "Zarude" like himself who couldn't even use vines could never gain the "Sacred Tree's" recognition or enter it.

So... why could that guy do it?

Clearly, he didn't seem to have vines either.

"Zarude!"

Coco, keep up!

Learning from earlier, Papa Zarude immediately called when he saw Coco wasn't following.

Inside the Sacred Tree.

Among the intricate branches and twigs, light purple water flowed from the Sacred Tree's crown center, forming streams that flowed downward.

Here, even just taking a breath felt...

"Sni..."

The closer to the "Sacred Tree," the more Serperior could feel the powerful energy contained within it.

"Zarude."

This is the place.

The Zarude elder stopped. Before Johan's group appeared a pool of light purple spring water, with the spring source connected to the giant tree's thick trunk.

"Here?"

Johan was slightly stunned.

They weren't going inside the Sacred Tree?

Seeing the Zarude's matter-of-fact expressions, Johan understood.

It seemed the Zarude, out of absolute reverence for the "Sacred Tree," wouldn't live inside it.

However, this should be enough.

"Serperior?" Johan looked at Serperior—it was still up to its decision.

"Sni."

Serperior nodded, walked to the spring water, had leaves fly from the forest to gather into a small platform before it, and sat on it.

Then the leaves floated, carrying Serperior to the spring source in the water's center.

It slowly closed its eyes.

"Zarude?"

The nearby Zarude looked at Serperior at the spring source, exchanging glances.

Why did they have a feeling... the "Sacred Tree" seemed very welcoming of its approach?

"To show gratitude, I'll treat everyone to food."

Meanwhile, Johan smiled and took out quite a few Energy Cubes—average quality but quite delicious.

"Zarude."

Initially, the Zarude were very disdainful of these cube-shaped things.

This was supposed to buy them off?

Were they better than their berries?

But when they smelled the Energy Cubes' aroma, when Jirachi showed off by waving Energy Cubes in front of the Zarude, they realized they seemed wrong.

When one Zarude began trying them and was immediately "captured" by the Energy Cubes' taste, the other Zarude were all conquered.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar beckoned lightly to Dragonite.

"Draa?"

While the Zarude were immersed in Energy Cubes, Gengar and Dragonite came to the spring water.

"Ka-kaah?"

Do you think if Johan drank this stuff, would he become immortal?

Gengar asked quietly.

"Draa?" Dragonite spread its little chubby hands.

I don't know.

Gengar rolled its eyes.

"Sacred Tree," "Healing Spring"—it had heard these terms when listening to the Zarude communicate earlier and understood the "Healing Spring's" effects.

A few breaths could help injured Pokémon recover.

Such miraculous spring water extending lifespan would be normal, right?

Anyway, there was so much here... taking a few tons back wouldn't be noticed—cough, no one would dare object, right?

Then, giving Johan a cup daily—it'd be hard for him to die!

"Ka-kaah~"

Help me—let's collect a tiny bit of spring water for Johan.

"Draa~"

Oh.

Dragonite nodded.

However, just as Gengar and Dragonite prepared to collect water—

Hum—

The spring water suddenly rippled.

Then, under all the Zarude's shocked gazes, Serperior sitting by the spring source glowed with light blue aura, and the crown's spring water rippled accordingly.

The next second, amid countless "rustle" sounds, abundant green light like scattered stars formed stream-like flows toward Serperior, entering its body through its back.

Hmm?

Seeing this scene, Johan was also stunned.

How was this somewhat like the Zarude clan's Jungle Healing?

Not exactly the same, but similar.

"Zarude..." The elder gripping its staff appeared excited, red eyes showing some crystalline glint—whether from excitement or something else.

Child of the Forest...

Worthy of an existence blessed by the forest.

The Zarude were also stunned.

Their clan had long-standing rumors that when a forest-protected Zarude appeared, it would gain the "Sacred Tree's" recognition and become "King of the Forest."

Unexpectedly, such an existence had indeed appeared.

But not within their Zarude clan.

Had their Zarude clan done something wrong...? The Zarude elder thought sadly.

Moreover, Lord Celebi hadn't appeared for a long time...

Johan gently rubbed his chin.

With such commotion, would Serperior evolve?

Though Serperior having "Forest's Blessing" was good, it also constrained it, making its evolution extremely slow.

As a Pokémon, though it could continue growing stronger, being unable to evolve to its final form had significant impacts from any perspective.

Hum—

The changes in Serperior came quickly and left quickly.

When all the glow faded, Serperior slowly opened its eyes, stood up, and bowed slightly to the "Sacred Tree."

"Sni."

Thank you.

Finished?

Thud—

Serperior jumped directly from the spring water to Johan, speaking somewhat embarrassedly.

"Sni..."

Couldn't evolve...

Though Johan didn't say it and it didn't mention it, actually both human and Pokémon had the same thought.

Perhaps the "Sacred Tree" could help Serperior/me evolve.

But the result was obvious.

Johan reached out and patted Serperior's head, saying with surprise: "Oh my! You seem taller? More beautiful?!"

"Sni! (∩)"

Even if you praise me like this! I won't be happy~~

Though saying this, the blush on its face and irrepressible smile betrayed it.

"Haha!"

Johan laughed twice.

Evolution or not... it didn't matter!

Things would happen naturally.

His and his Pokémon's happiness was most important.

"Elder, thank you. These things are a token of gratitude." Johan took out more Energy Cubes and said to the Zarude elder.

Then he led his Pokémon away from the "Sacred Tree."

The Zarude elder opened its mouth, reaching out to call Johan and Serperior back, but not knowing what excuse to use, just watched them leave and sighed softly.

"Zarude!!"

Suddenly a Zarude cried out.

What?

That Zarude pointed at the crown's central spring water.

Why was the spring water so much lower?!

"Faster than expected."

At the camp, seeing Johan return under cover of night, Cynthia said with some surprise.

"Encountered some minor trouble, but fortunately nothing major," Johan smiled.

Then asked: "Did you eat dinner?"

"Mm, ate," Cynthia looked at Johan expectantly. "But didn't eat enough."

Johan laughed helplessly and took out an apron. "Alright, I'll make something. I haven't eaten much either. What do you want?"

"Udon noodles!"

[Me too! Me too! Udon! Udon!]

Hearing this, Jirachi immediately shouted excitedly.

"Alright, alright."

Johan began cooking.

Serperior, looking hesitant, suddenly approached him.

"Sni..."

"What's wrong? You want some too?"

"Sni." Serperior shook its head.

After hesitating slightly, it began explaining.

Actually, earlier at the "Sacred Tree," it had felt it actually had a chance to evolve.

However... some mysterious feeling told it that if it chose to evolve using the "Sacred Tree's" power, perhaps... it could never leave the "Sacred Tree" again.

This was unacceptable to Serperior.

So it "refused."

The feeling wasn't strong then, but the further from the "Sacred Tree," the stronger the feeling became.

After hearing this, Johan's first thought was of Mew and the World Tree.

Like Mew and the World Tree?

Mew was one with the World Tree but also couldn't leave it...

"Do you want to stay?" Johan asked.

"Sni!" Serperior's head shook like a rattle.

How could that be?

Though it joined the team latest, it never thought of leaving Johan. It wanted to become stronger, but the premise was becoming stronger together with Johan.

"Then that's it."

Johan smiled, crouched down, looked directly into Serperior's eyes, and said softly: "Your choice and judgment were correct. We must become masters of power, not be controlled by it and become slaves."

Speaking, he vigorously rubbed Serperior's head.

The "Sacred Tree's" power obviously looked so strong—even if Serperior couldn't evolve, the benefits it gained should be greater than now.

Serperior's explanation made sense.

"It's fine, we have plenty of time."

"Sni! (▼皿▼)" Serperior bared its teeth, symbolically struggling twice to escape Johan's hand, but only symbolically.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar suddenly appeared, patting its round belly and laughing.

Right! We have plenty of time!

Hmm?

"Gengar, why is your belly so big?"

Hey!

Gengar grinned, suddenly having light purple liquid trickle from its mouth.

Johan: "..."

Instantly almost understanding Gengar's intention, Johan nearly cursed.

You purple fatty, you don't expect me to drink what you spit out, do you?!

Moreover, this "Healing Spring" actually lost its effects over time without special preservation methods after leaving the "Sacred Tree."

At worst... "Healing Spring" was just healing, right? Did it think it was "Rakua elements"?

It could even extend lifespan.

Johan rolled his eyes.

However... what if "Healing Spring" and "Rakua elements" were combined?

Mossui Town.

In a research facility.

"Dr. Jedo, we're here to help you."

"Help me?" Dr. Jedo, wearing a suit, looked at the young man lounging in the chair before him, especially the logo on his chest—Outstanding Inc.'s logo!

Though his research facility and company were sizeable, compared to Outstanding Inc. with business across multiple regions, they were far inferior.

Dr. Jedo just didn't understand why Outstanding Inc. would suddenly approach him.

"We've seen your 'Healing Spring' thesis, but research couldn't proceed due to inability to find the 'Healing Spring' in the vast forest."

The Outstanding Inc. young man grinned confidently:

"And we can help you find the 'Healing Spring' you want."

"Really?!"

Hearing this, Dr. Jedo became excited.

Researching the "Healing Spring" was his life's greatest dream.

He had even killed his two "good friends" for this!

"Of course." The Outstanding Inc. young man snapped his fingers. "Please trust Outstanding Inc.'s capabilities."

"However, since this is cooperation, please have Dr. Jedo sign this contract first."
