# 773: Zarude's Request!

Bang! Bang! Bang!

Brilliant flowers bloomed in the night sky, illuminating Mossui Town's evening with dazzling radiance.

Johan and Cynthia sat on the grass, watching the blossoming fireworks as colorful light reflected on their faces.

[Wow...]

Jirachi was completely conquered by the fireworks festival display. This was a scene it had never witnessed before—even the stars in the sky seemed less beautiful than this fireworks show.

[It's Pikachu! And there's Gengar! And! And...]

Gengar stood proudly on the grass with hands on its hips.

[Why isn't there a Jirachi...]

Jirachi looked longingly at the blooming fireworks.

Though not every Pokémon had a fireworks pattern, it still held some hope.

Johan held Cynthia's hand, smiling.

Bang!!

Another firework bloomed.

[Jirachi! It's Jirachi! Johan, look! Johan, look!]

There really was a Jirachi-patterned firework, making the little one shout with delighted surprise.

"It seems to really like it," Cynthia laughed.

This was naturally Johan's surprise for Jirachi.

The little one watching "itself" in the sky, that feeling of anticipation becoming reality...

A wish came true!

Before meeting Johan, Jirachi had always helped others realize wishes, though "accidents" always happened after wishes were fulfilled.

But after meeting Johan, Jirachi experienced the feeling of others making its wishes come true...

And the kind that wouldn't have "accidents" afterward.

The little one's small face showed irrepressible joy.

"It cost quite a bit," Johan said.

Mossui Town had only agreed to add the Jirachi firework pattern for Teacher Johan's sake.

After all, there was no Jirachi pattern in Mossui Town's fireworks catalog—it needed custom production on short notice. With the fireworks festival so close, the labor costs naturally...

"But it was worth it," Cynthia said, holding one knee.

Johan had actually wanted to ask about making Cynthia firework patterns too, but... it was too rushed, no time.

But Johan still ordered them.

Not only Cynthia's, but firework patterns for all his Pokémon as well.

If this time's effect was good... next time, he'd hold his own fireworks festival on his island!

Then... heh.

"Tomorrow we continue training," Cynthia added.

Johan's expression immediately soured.

They'd actually been training for two days already. Except for when Johan took Serperior to find the "Sacred Tree," most of their time was spent training.

Cynthia was usually quite gentle, but once training began... tsk.

Now Johan felt somewhat "sorry" for Flint, Lucian, and Aaron who had to train with Cynthia.

No wonder Lucian was so "willing" to work overtime, no wonder Flint was so happy to run around with him everywhere, no wonder Aaron would quietly space out in forests when alone...

So that was the reason.

No wonder Cynthia possessed such strength.

However, this was also Johan's most rewarding training since becoming a trainer.

Not just in terms of Pokémon strength, but mainly in battle "instinct" or intuition.

At this rate... Cynthia probably planned to keep training until the "Master Replacement Battle" began.

But there was no choice.

Even schools were making way for the "Pokémon World Championships"—Johan had no excuse to "escape."

Consider it... preparing for the competition together.

Unlike the lively Mossui Town, the nighttime forest was completely quiet.

Except for occasional insect chirping and nocturnal wild Pokémon flashing by, there was rarely any major disturbance.

Rumble—

But at this moment, a giant mechanical robot completely at odds with the forest's atmosphere rampaged through with crushing force.

Many people in practical clothing rode small flying motorcycles ahead as scouts, followed by several large off-road jeeps filled with numerous researchers and various precision instruments.

In one large jeep, researcher Karen, wearing researcher clothing with long blue hair, asked worriedly:

"Dr. Jedo, should we really cooperate with these self-proclaimed 'Outstanding Inc.' people?"

Compared to Karen's worry, Dr. Jedo appeared much calmer, even showing barely contained excitement in his eyes.

"Don't worry, Karen. Our Biotope Company's cooperation with Outstanding Inc. will better promote our research results and help more people!"

More importantly, without Outstanding Inc.'s help, they could never find the "Healing Spring's" location.

Though Biotope Company was sizeable, it mostly consisted of professional researchers with few combat personnel, making free movement in dangerous wilds difficult. That's why they'd been unable to find the "Healing Spring" for so long.

But Outstanding Inc.'s people were all top trainers, plus superior technology. In just a few days, they'd almost pinpointed the "Healing Spring's" location.

Now they just needed to find it and research its secrets!

Hearing Dr. Jedo's words, Karen finally suppressed her worry and nodded.

Regardless, Dr. Jedo's intentions were good—for humanity, for Pokémon...

She just hoped the wild Pokémon in the forest would suffer less disturbance.

Night gradually passed, stars faded, and dawn broke in the east.

"We're here."

A dull male voice came through the radio.

Found it!

Dr. Jedo could no longer contain his excitement, immediately taking a flying motorcycle to the front of the convoy.

From afar, he could hear waterfall sounds.

Through misty vapor and forest cover, he saw a huge basin.

In the basin's center, the towering "Sacred Tree" continuously dripped light purple liquid—almost identical to the "Healing Spring" in Dr. Jedo's imagination.

"Finally, found it!"

Seeing this scene, Dr. Jedo's face showed some madness.

Meanwhile, the convoy finally arrived completely. Researchers from the jeeps got out and went to nearby lakes to collect liquid samples.

At this moment—

"Zarude!"

Coco and Papa Zarude, swinging on vines, were first to arrive.

Such a large convoy with giant robots made considerable noise moving through the forest.

Several wild Pokémon surrounded Coco, pointing at the machines and people, chattering about something.

Obviously these wild Pokémon had first discovered Dr. Jedo's team and informed Coco.

"Zarude!"

Seeing humans again, Coco couldn't help but question whether he was really a Zarude.

But this clearly wasn't the time for such concerns.

Dr. Jedo's team had destroyed much forest and vegetation along the way, injuring many wild Pokémon who tried to stop them. Coco came seeking accountability.

"Where did this kid come from?" Everyone was surprised to see a human child living with wild Pokémon.

Dr. Jedo initially didn't want to pay much attention, but glancing at Coco, his gaze was immediately drawn to the ring necklace around Coco's neck.

"This is..." His pupils contracted, looking at Coco with disbelief.

This was the ring of his former partners, the Molisson couple!

After he killed the Molisson couple, he'd only taken the "Healing Spring" sample without finding this ring containing "Healing Spring" information, nor seeing their child.

Could it be... this savage-like child was the Molisson couple's son, Al?!

"Zarude! Zarude!" Coco waved his arms.

What are you doing! Don't destroy the forest!

Unfortunately, Dr. Jedo couldn't understand Coco's words.

However, Dr. Jedo was very curious about the ring around Coco's neck. It contained not only the Molisson couple's location information about the "Healing Spring" but also their research results—exactly what Dr. Jedo needed.

He had to admit that in their three-person research team, the Molisson couple had been the main contributors.

So Dr. Jedo quickly showed surprised delight.

"Are you... Al?!"

"Zarude?" Coco was stunned.

Dr. Jedo took out a tablet, showing the Molisson couple's information and photos, pointing at the rings they wore.

This stunned Coco, who instinctively looked at the necklace around his neck.

Smart Coco seemed to understand many things at once.

Dr. Jedo struck while the iron was hot, describing their three-person cooperation, though hiding his murder of the Molisson couple.

"So it's Dr. Molisson's child..."

The other researchers also showed surprise.

"Zarude!" Papa Zarude became anxious seeing this.

Dr. Jedo stepped forward first, extending his hand to Coco gently: "Al, come. I'll take you home."

"Zarude..."

Coco looked at Dr. Jedo, then at Papa Zarude who seemed to understand and looked somewhat melancholy. His heart became confused.

Unable to decide, he turned and ran into the forest.

"Zarude!" Papa Zarude glanced at Dr. Jedo's group and quickly gave chase.

Though it had long known this day would come, it hadn't expected... it would come so suddenly.

Watching the quickly departing Coco and Papa Zarude, Dr. Jedo was stunned, then sneered coldly.

"Never mind."

Even without the ring's data, he'd already found the "Healing Spring." As for results... he'd indeed been inferior to the Molisson couple initially, but after all these years, he was no longer his former self.

"Dr. Jedo, confirmed—elements basically match!"

A researcher checking lake water elements ran over excitedly to report.

This meant this place was indeed the "Healing Spring" they sought.

Any observer could see the key to the "Healing Spring" might be... that giant tree!

"Karen, scan that tree."

"Yes."

Karen took out instruments and scanned the "Sacred Tree" from a distance, quickly getting results.

Looking at the data and patterns displayed, Karen said in shock: "That tree contains powerful and terrifying energy!"

"As expected!"

Dr. Jedo could no longer restrain himself, immediately ordering the machine operator while pointing at the "Sacred Tree": "Use spears to blast that tree open!"

Hearing this, all researchers' expressions changed.

The machine operator also hesitated: "Dr. Jedo, this... might not be good..."

Karen also advised: "Dr. Jedo, that tree surely has many wild Pokémon living on it..."

But Dr. Jedo said: "For all humans and the vast majority of Pokémon, some sacrifice is understandable. Besides, we can help them rebuild their homes after completing research."

Speaking with stern expression to the operator: "Blast it open!"

"Y-yes!"

Bang!! Bang—

The robot's two equipped spears immediately fired, directly bombing the "Sacred Tree's" trunk.

Boom!!

Instantly, the entire "Sacred Tree" let out a rumbling wail. Where it was blasted open, massive amounts of liquid gushed out like a broken dam.

From outside, one could see the "Sacred Tree's" trunk actually glowed with green light, seemingly containing mysterious power.

However, while Dr. Jedo was excited, this huge impact and "Sacred Tree" destruction would surely enrage the entire Zarude clan.

Sure enough—

"Zarude!"

"Zarude!"

"Zarude!"

Numerous Zarude immediately poured from the "Sacred Tree," swinging toward Dr. Jedo's group with boundless fury.

A few days ago, a human had suddenly come wanting to approach the "Sacred Tree." They couldn't defeat him, but fortunately he hadn't caused much damage to the "Sacred Tree."

This time, a whole group came and blasted the "Sacred Tree" open!

This was almost like attacking the entire Zarude clan's lifeline!

Absolutely furious!

"Seems like quite an obstacle, Dr. Jedo." A dark-skinned Outstanding Inc. member with long black braids wearing white clothing said.

Dr. Jedo also noticed the approaching Zarude, glanced at the woman, and said:

"I'm afraid we'll need to trouble Outstanding Inc.'s people, including Ms. Agate."

Agate—ostensibly an Outstanding Inc. researcher, actually one of the Explorers' executives, possessing a calm mind and excelling at data collection and analysis, highly valued by Gibeon.

Hearing this, Agate smiled. "Onyx."

"Got it." A deep, rumbling voice came from a burly man.

Onyx—also an Explorers executive with extreme strength, captain of the Explorers' combat forces, equally valued by Gibeon.

While speaking, Onyx threw two Poké Balls.

In flashing red light, a Garganacl and an Aerodactyl appeared before him.

Seeing this, other Explorers combat team members also threw Poké Balls, summoning their respective Pokémon.

"Garganacl, Salt Cure!"

"Aerodactyl, Stone Edge!"

Onyx excelled at preemptive strikes. Before the Zarude arrived, he began preparing.

Massive amounts of white salt scattered from Garganacl throughout the area. Any Pokémon accidentally stepping on it would enter "Salt Cure status."

Aerodactyl flapped its wings, sharp rocks appearing around its body. Any Pokémon approaching first would suffer fierce attacks from these sharp stones.

Soon, the furious Zarude arrived.

What greeted them was frenzied attacks from Onyx and the Explorers combat team.

"Zarude!!"

Hearing the "Sacred Tree" being destroyed, Coco and Papa Zarude who had left earlier also returned.

Perhaps Coco was still conflicted about whether he was a Zarude, but no conflict could overcome the current crisis.

Papa Zarude was even more so—with fierce expression, it directly attacked Aerodactyl.

Bang bang bang—

Instantly, battle sounds erupted throughout the forest.

But under the Explorers' "protection," Dr. Jedo and numerous Biotope Company researchers approached the "Sacred Tree."

Facing the Zarude's counterattack, researchers like Karen looked pained.

But Dr. Jedo kept comforting them with "for everyone, sacrifice is inevitable" rhetoric. For a moment, they couldn't think of better solutions.

The Zarude, facing Explorers' attacks plus the advanced robot's siege, retreated steadily with continuous injuries.

After all, not all Zarude had the strength of Papa Zarude and other powerful Zarude.

Even Papa Zarude was accidentally hit and injured by Aerodactyl while protecting Coco.

Fortunately, the Zarude had "Healing Spring" support—quickly recovering to rejoin battle.

But as the "Sacred Tree" was destroyed, the "Healing Spring" water became increasingly diluted with weakening recovery effects.

"Zarude!" Papa Zarude leaned against tree roots in the spring water, listening to continuous explosions in the forest.

This wouldn't work!

The opponents were not only powerful but had that machine that wasn't easily destroyed.

The Zarude's strength alone couldn't stop them.

They needed help!

Papa Zarude first thought of countless wild Pokémon in the forest.

These wild Pokémon were actually strong. Individual power might not make much difference, but if they grouped together...

However, Papa Zarude's heart was bitter.

How much had the Zarude bullied these wild Pokémon normally?

Though the "Sacred Tree" was important, the Zarude usually didn't let other wild Pokémon approach, much less soak in the "Healing Spring."

Now with the "Sacred Tree" destroyed, it actually didn't affect these wild Pokémon much.

Why would they be willing to help the Zarude...

"Zarude!" Coco thought the same as Papa Zarude.

Papa! I'll go ask for help. Everyone... might listen. No, I'll definitely get everyone to help!

Usually the Zarude didn't get along with wild Pokémon, but Coco's relationships with everyone were actually quite good. He might be able to persuade them.

But... Papa Zarude looked at those powerful, well-coordinated Explorers members.

"Zarude..."

I'm afraid the strength still wouldn't be enough...

What to do!

What to do?

Suddenly, a human figure flashed in Papa Zarude's mind.

That's right!

That person!

With just his own power—no, just two or three Pokémon—he'd defeated the entire Zarude clan.

If they could get his help, perhaps...

But where was he?

And how could they get him to help the Zarude?

That's right, the "Sacred Tree"!

That Serperior could sense the "Sacred Tree." Having contacted it, it surely carried the "Sacred Tree's" aura. If Papa Zarude could sense it...

No, it MUST sense it!

It MUST seek that person's help!

Papa Zarude took a deep breath and closed its eyes.

"Training people, training souls, after training you're above others..."

"Pfft—"

Hearing Johan's muttering, Cynthia covered her mouth and laughed.

But she was serious about training.

Not just for the upcoming "Pokémon World Championships," but to have an all-out battle with Johan!

Last time she lost.

But after becoming Champion, they hadn't truly fought.

At least "fight" once more for family status!

"We're here, let's go down." Johan lightly patted Dragonite's neck.

This was their previous temporary camp.

Now besides training, maybe Jirachi's situation needed more consideration.

The cosmic power in Eternatus's summoned meteorite was abundant—little Jirachi shouldn't just be awake seven days this time.

Johan estimated that as long as Jirachi didn't casually use power to fulfill others' wishes or overconsume energy, it could persist at least a month.

However, cosmic power would eventually be exhausted—they needed to prepare in advance.

Johan had already contacted the two Deoxys in space through his methods, asking them to help find meteorites containing cosmic power.

If successful... the little one might never need to sleep again, becoming the first Jirachi to stay permanently awake!

"Draa~~"

Dragonite landed.

Johan and Cynthia began setting up camp again.

They summoned all their little ones.

"Sni?" Serperior looked puzzled, turning toward the "Sacred Tree's" direction.

But this feeling was vague, so Serperior didn't pay much attention.

Just as Johan's group finished setting up camp and prepared to continue training—

"Zarude!"

Papa Zarude in his pink cape, clutching abdominal wounds, suddenly appeared in Johan's camp.

"Hmm?"

Seeing Papa Zarude, Johan looked surprised.

"This is..." Cynthia was also seeing a Zarude for the first time.

Thud!!

Papa Zarude said nothing, just seriously knelt on one knee before Johan, crystalline tears in his red eyes.

Seeing this, Johan's heart jumped.

He suddenly had a bad feeling.

"Zarude!" Papa Zarude spoke heavily.

[Johan! Johan! I sense a strong desire! It needs help!]

Jirachi felt Papa Zarude's intense wish.

The Wish Pokémon inexplicably felt power surging from its heart.

# 774: Teacher Johan's "Mercy"

"You..."

Looking at the Zarude kneeling on the ground, Johan opened his mouth.

"Zarude..." Zarude opened its mouth to explain but was stopped by Johan's raised hand.

"Wait, let me guess."

"Zarude?" Zarude looked confused.

Guess?

What... time was it for guessing?

But Johan said: "A group of people surrounded your 'Sacred Tree,' even destroyed it?"

Hearing this, Papa Zarude was stunned, looking at Johan in shock.

Johan continued: "The leader is an old acquaintance of Coco's parents, and they have a very powerful robot."

Papa Zarude looked completely bewildered, staring blankly at Johan who was rubbing his chin with a "mystic" air.

The reason Johan could guess correctly was mainly because...

This damn thing was exactly the plot from the movie "Pokémon the Movie: Secrets of the Jungle"!

Johan now muttered quietly: "That's not right, logically this plot should wait for Ash..."

Though previous plots had "accidents," they were all within acceptable ranges.

But without Ash and Pikachu, how would Coco resolve his inner conflict?

"Zarude?" Papa Zarude snapped back to attention and couldn't help calling out.

If Johan hadn't entered the "Sacred Tree" last time without causing any damage, Papa Zarude would really start suspecting whether Dr. Jedo's group was arranged by Johan.

Otherwise, how could he know so clearly?

"Oh, it's fine." Johan came back to his senses, showing some confusion.

Logically speaking...

According to the movie's development, even if Dr. Jedo's group had a powerful robot, as long as the Zarude and wild Pokémon united, plus Papa Zarude's "awakening," it shouldn't be difficult to handle.

Though that robot was portrayed as very powerful in the movie, even having arena-like "energy barriers," as long as it sustained heavy attacks, the barrier would eventually give way.

With or without Ash and Pikachu, the result should be the same.

"You... can't defeat that robot?" Johan asked with confusion.

Papa Zarude didn't understand why Johan, clearly knowing about the robot and Dr. Jedo, would ask such a question.

But it still spoke with some reluctance:

"Zarude!"

If it were just that group and one robot, the Zarude naturally wouldn't retreat continuously—the main problem was another group!

"Another group?"

Johan was stunned.

Then asked with interest: "What people?"

Papa Zarude thought about it, not knowing how to explain since it didn't know who those people were either.

So after thinking, it tore off a small section of vine from its wrist, rubbed it into crystalline powder, and scattered it down.

Outlining several Pokémon silhouettes.

"Garganacl and... Aerodactyl?"

What kind of situation was this?

"What's wrong?" Cynthia asked.

"Something happened..."

Johan then briefly explained.

After hearing, Cynthia nodded. Though she also had doubts, she still called Garchomp over and said:

"Let's go."

"Go?"

Cynthia smiled: "What? A Pokémon has come asking for your help. With your personality, would you ignore it?"

Moreover... Cynthia glanced at Jirachi clenching its little fists.

This little one had sensed strong wishes from Papa Zarude.

Johan was speechless.

"True."

Not mentioning that Serperior had gained some benefits from the "Sacred Tree," just having a Pokémon come seeking help—when had he ever refused when capable?

Not to mention... this matter seemed to have no difficulty.

Much easier than fighting first-class legendaries.

Johan grabbed Gengar, pried open its mouth, and pulled out a black cape, draping it over his shoulders with a "whoosh."

His entire aura and feeling immediately underwent tremendous change.

"Then let's go!"

Now it was... Teacher Johan's Champion moment!

"Garganacl, Aerodactyl, Stone Edge!"

Onyx displayed his fierce side completely.

Though Rock-type Pokémon had no type advantage against Grass-type Zarude, he won because Onyx wasn't alone—he had an entire combat team for coordination.

Moreover, besides him, there was another "Explorers" executive—Agate!

Agate specialized in Psychic-type Pokémon, with a Medicham and Xatu as her main forces. Against Dark-type Zarude, she couldn't exert much combat effectiveness.

But Agate's greatest strength lay in her information gathering and analysis abilities.

Though encountering Zarude for the first time, after several clashes, Agate had figured out this Pokémon species' various strengths and weaknesses.

Strengths: powerful, agile, and very familiar with surroundings.

But corresponding weaknesses: too reckless in battle, decent experience but poor coordination, and most importantly—no helpers or backup.

"Their recovery ability is getting slower. Hold out a bit longer and we can break through," Agate said calmly.

"Zarude!"

"Zarude!"

"Zarude!"

The Zarude's roars rose and fell.

But besides roaring, they had no other options.

"Zarude!"

At this moment, Coco finally returned again, bringing quite a few wild Pokémon!

There were Flygon, Grapploct, Pangoro, Vileplume, and other relatively powerful Pokémon.

With these wild Pokémon joining, Dr. Jedo's group's advance finally slowed.

However, these wild Pokémon couldn't do much against Biotope Company's robot.

This was a robot Dr. Jedo had built at enormous cost, using Galar region stadium energy barrier technology. Wild Pokémon alone could hardly break through.

If the Zarude could coordinate with them, perhaps they could.

But the Zarude were being suppressed by Onyx, Agate, and others.

Seeing danger approach the "Sacred Tree" closer and closer...

Bang!!

The Zarude elder dropped its staff, blew its beard, and prepared to enter battle.

The elder was warming up!

Elder!

Elder!

Several young Zarude quickly stopped the elder.

Don't you know your own physical condition?

Not helping would be fine, but you might even become a burden!

The Zarude elder looked bitter, watching the robot approach closer as the "Sacred Tree" became more endangered.

"Zarude..."

Had their Zarude clan really committed some wrong to suffer consecutive tribulations...

Just then, the Zarude elder seemed to sense something, suddenly looking up with contracted pupils.

"Zarude..."

That is...

Agate also changed color, her calm demeanor immediately becoming extremely grave as she urgently shouted:

"Onyx, watch out!"

The next second—

"Draa—!!!"

A massive form wrapped in draconic energy descended at terrifying speed toward Onyx's Garganacl and Aerodactyl.

Dragon Rush!

Onyx's expression also changed drastically.

Boom!!!

"Eh?"

A voice with some surprise came from the air.

A young man in black suit with fluttering cape slowly descended from above.

"Shaka!!" Garchomp bared its teeth fiercely.

When did this guy get so good at showing off?

When Dragonite launched Dragon Rush, Johan lost his riding Pokémon and naturally grabbed its arm.

Garchomp had originally wanted to secretly pretend to accidentally drop Johan, but unexpectedly he let go himself.

Only little Jirachi, hugging Johan's arm and gritting its teeth, kept trying to pull him up.

Thud—

Johan landed smoothly.

"Teacher Johan!"

Agate recognized Johan almost immediately, her expression changing drastically.

Hearing this, Johan looked up and immediately noticed the logo on her clothing—Outstanding Inc.!

Recalling her identity, he immediately understood.

Explorers, executive, Agate and Onyx!

How had the Explorers gotten involved in this?

Could it really be as he thought—Outstanding Inc. wanted to use "Healing Spring" to alleviate "Rakua elements'" side effects?

Wait.

Had he accidentally set a flag?

Johan's appearance made Agate very nervous.

Precisely because she excelled at data collection and analysis, Agate knew how terrifying Teacher Johan was.

Without exaggeration, among current regional Champions, Teacher Johan was definitely in the front ranks, arguably at least top two!

How could such a person suddenly appear in this forest?

While Johan wondered why the Explorers appeared, the Explorers equally wondered why Johan appeared.

"Draa—"

Dragonite flapped its wings, flying back through the dust.

Onyx's two Pokémon—Aerodactyl directly lost fighting ability!

Though Garganacl hadn't lost fighting ability, its salt blocks became severely damaged with almost no fighting power remaining.

Actually withstanding Dragonite's Dragon Rush—this was why Johan had exclaimed "eh" in surprise earlier.

Johan wasn't satisfied with this result, but for Papa Zarude who had followed, it was pure shock.

So, so strong?

Back at the "Sacred Tree," the opponent was still holding back?

"So what if you're a Champion!"

Onyx looked at the fallen Aerodactyl, gritting his teeth viciously.

"Onyx, don't be impulsive..." Agate tried to stop him, but saw Onyx had already taken out a black sphere filled with energy.

Tera Orb!

"Release your power, Garganacl!"

Onyx threw the Tera Orb toward Garganacl.

Crack crack crack—

Terastallization power released as crystalline crystals built up from Garganacl's feet, completely enveloping it.

With a "bang," crystals shattered as Garganacl completed Terastallization!

And it was Tera Rock!

Seeing this, Agate angrily glared at Onyx.

This muscle-brained fool!

But Onyx had clearly shown his intention to fight Teacher Johan. Agate couldn't sit idly by.

After all... whether they fought or fled, they seemingly had to get past Teacher Johan first.

"Shaka—"

At this moment, Cynthia and Garchomp also landed.

"Two Champions?!" Agate's expression froze.

How was this playable?

A mere forest outside Mossui Town actually had two Champions?

"Cynthia, handle that side. Leave this to me," Johan said.

Dr. Jedo and Biotope Company's robot were getting closer to the "Sacred Tree."

Cynthia glanced at Onyx and Agate, nodding.

"Alright."

Without hesitation, she left with Garchomp.

As for worrying about Johan facing two opponents... he was Johan!

For Agate and Onyx, Cynthia's departure brought relief.

Fortunately, Champion Cynthia left.

Otherwise... no matter how confident they were, they couldn't face two Champions simultaneously!

But now... Agate immediately analyzed the situation clearly.

This was an opportunity!

Though Teacher Johan was very powerful, if they used all means, they might not be unable to hold out or even defeat one or two of his Pokémon.

Then, before Champion Cynthia returned, they'd quickly retreat!

Continue attacking the "Sacred Tree"?

Agate had abandoned such bold thoughts, now only thinking of minimizing losses while retreating.

Understanding this, Agate hesitated no more, immediately taking out her Tera Orb and throwing it toward Medicham.

"Be one with my mind, Medicham!"

With crystal condensation and scattering, Agate's Medicham also completed Terastallization—Tera Psychic!

Both enhanced their Pokémon's original types through Terastallization.

Seeing those two Terastallized Pokémon, Johan raised an eyebrow.

Daring to fight back?

Interesting...

He casually threw out two Poké Balls.

"Serperior, Tyranitar."

"Sni!"

"Bangi!"

One Grass-type, one Dark-type—quite respectful toward Onyx and Agate.

But Agate called out:

"Onyx!"

Onyx glanced at her, immediately understanding Agate's meaning.

Both looked grave, simultaneously taking out strange spheres resembling but not quite Poké Balls.

Hmm?

Then Agate and Onyx each threw these strange spheres toward Terastallized Garganacl and Medicham respectively.

Enhancement Orbs!

Click—

The spheres opened.

Pink mist drifted from within, inhaled by Garganacl and Medicham.

The next second, both Pokémon's expressions and states changed dramatically!

Their auras rapidly surged.

Seeing that pink mist, Johan's expression also changed slightly.

This was Rakua elements?!

"Enhancement Orbs" were Outstanding Inc.'s most secret research product—no, should be said as Spinel's exclusive research achievement, products Spinel had developed while hiding from "Explorers" leader Gibeon.

What they contained was indeed "Rakua elements"—improved and refined "Rakua elements."

They caused inhaling Pokémon's strength to surge greatly without completely losing reason, still able to follow trainers' commands.

However, these "Enhancement Orbs" were still experimental, only prototypes. Throughout Outstanding Inc. and the "Explorers," fewer than five people secretly possessed them.

All were closely related to Spinel, even considered confidants.

This was something even "Explorers" leader Gibeon didn't know.

Of course, Johan didn't know these details.

But Johan recognized "Rakua elements" and felt the changes in Onyx's Garganacl and Agate's Medicham.

Combined with the previous Walking Wake at the Dragon Tamer family...

He quickly reached a conclusion.

Walking Wake had indeed been used as a test subject by Outstanding Inc. or "Explorers," whether intentionally or not.

"Quick battle!" Agate said grimly.

Not hoping to completely defeat Teacher Johan—defeating his Serperior and Tyranitar would provide escape opportunity!

Agate knew Serperior and Tyranitar were among Teacher Johan's Pokémon that joined latest and were theoretically weakest.

The Champion's "arrogance" was their greatest chance.

So Agate and Onyx held nothing back.

"Medicham, Meditate!"

"Garganacl, Salt Cure!"

Both immediately acted with tacit coordination.

Agate and Onyx were both "Explorers" executives trained from childhood, very familiar with each other, so naturally they coordinated extremely well.

Garganacl positioned itself in front of Medicham, spraying abundant salt.

Medicham assumed meditation pose as its aura began surging again.

Johan recovered from his surprise at "Rakua elements," seeing this scene and couldn't help chuckling.

Champion's arrogance?

No!

This was a Champion's absolute confidence!

Did they really think they could compete with him using mere "Rakua elements"?

"Then let me show you what a 'Champion' is!"

Johan's eyes suddenly sharpened as he waved his hand.

"Serperior, Grassy Terrain!"

"Tyranitar, Dragon Dance!"

Good thing Garchomp was taken away by Cynthia...

"Sni—"

Glowing grass spread from Serperior's feet as surrounding forest leaves suddenly rustled. An aura belonging to the forest seemed to manifest on Serperior.

The salt scattered by Garganacl was almost instantly covered and disappeared under the grass.

"Bangi!!"

Tyranitar roared fiercely. Though it controlled the sandstorm without unleashing it, the Desert Tyrant's aura remained the same even outside deserts!

No!

I am the desert!

The intimidating terrifying aura made Agate and Onyx's breathing hitch.

Both suddenly felt... their judgment might be somewhat wrong.

Though commanding later, Johan and his two Pokémon's reaction speed was faster and... exceptionally fierce.

Quick battle?

Then he'd fulfill their request!

"Leaf Storm!"

"Stone Edge!"

Agate: "???"

The battle just started!

You're already using ultimate moves?

What about protecting the forest...

"Sni!"

Amid forest leaves' "rustling," stern-faced Serperior's tail immediately gathered massive forest leaves, unleashing an unprecedentedly huge leaf tornado.

The "Sacred Tree" being severely damaged didn't just anger the Zarude and wild Pokémon—the angriest was this vast forest itself!

And now, the forest's power was Serperior's power!

"Bangi!!"

Tyranitar roared skyward as the entire ground shook violently. Terrifying huge fiery rock spikes immediately erupted from the ground.

Those violent rumbling sounds were earth's roar, the Desert Tyrant's howl, the strongest response to opponents' contempt!

Looking down on Champions? Or Champions' Pokémon?

Then they'd use strength to tell them!

Johan becoming Champion was the result of every single one of them working together with Johan!

Boom!!!

Instantly, the leaf tornado devastated Garganacl while Stone Edge charged straight at Medicham.

The massive commotion and eclipse-like scene directly enveloped this area.

Not far away, Papa Zarude stared blankly at this scene, unconsciously swallowing.

Gulp—

Mercy!

Papa Zarude felt "mercy" from Johan for the first time.

If these two moves had been used at the "Sacred Tree" days ago, then probably...

Teacher Johan?

Papa Zarude recalled Agate and Onyx's address for Johan.

This was a "merciful" teacher...

Rumble—

The trembling continued for who knows how long.

When the massive commotion gradually subsided and leaves and dust slowly settled, Garganacl and Medicham had long since fallen in ruins, having lost fighting ability.

Agate and Onyx were also currently dazed.

For the first time, the proud pair understood the strength gap between themselves and Champions.

Even as "Explorers" executives, they seemed very small before the word "Champion."

Of course, this might also be their last time understanding this.

"Zarude, they're yours." Johan pointed at the two and numerous "Explorers" members, addressing Papa Zarude.

Other "Explorers" members had long since been "played" unconscious by Gengar.

Each foaming at the mouth with convulsing bodies.

"Ka-kaah..."

Gengar shook its head with an expression of "you guys are just this much, not good enough."

"Za, Zarude!" Papa Zarude snapped back and immediately nodded.

Just... why did it feel like Teacher Johan and his Pokémon's "destruction" of the forest was greater than those people combined?

An illusion, definitely an illusion!

Meanwhile, on the other side—

Boom!!

Garchomp emerged from flames, disdainfully curling its lip.

A robot?

If robots could truly completely suppress Pokémon, what would Pokémon be needed for?

Dr. Jedo stared blankly at Garchomp and the indifferently standing Cynthia with hands on hips.

Who, who was this person?

How did she suddenly appear, suddenly attack once, and the robot suddenly exploded?

The robot he'd invested so much funding and resources to create was just... gone like that?

"No! No! No!" Dr. Jedo's eyes turned red.

Seeing his life's goal about to be completed, just one step away, would it fail?

Right! That's it—there was still Al!

If he could capture Al!

Dr. Jedo looked viciously at Coco, when suddenly a twisted ghostly face appeared before him.

"Spiriii—"

"Para, paralyzed..."

Spiritomb showed a "proud" smile.

Finally learned a trick from Johan and Gengar!

Heh! Hehe...

# 775: Evolution! Serperior!

"Resolved?"

Cynthia looked at Johan appearing on the massive root system beneath the "Sacred Tree" and asked.

Johan grinned. "Just some small fry."

We're Champions after all!

However, he still briefly explained the "Enhancement Orbs," "Rakua elements," and Outstanding Inc. identities that Agate and Onyx had used.

Cynthia pondered thoughtfully: "Looks like Chairman Geeta will be busy."

Johan shrugged.

Let Geeta worry about the "Rakua elements" situation. They'd already done enough, whether intentionally or not.

Regarding Paldea region matters, Johan chose to observe from the sidelines.

No choice—he didn't understand it well.

Besides, logically speaking, Paldea region's strongest legendary should be little turtle Terapagos.

But little turtle's nature... was basically a "turtle version" of Jirachi—harmless to humans and animals. At most, another shiny Zygarde might pop out, but they had shiny Rayquaza to handle that.

Better not get involved in this commotion if possible.

Successfully completing the "Pokémon World Championships" would be enough!

"How's this side?"

Johan looked up at the towering "Sacred Tree." After taking two spear hits from Biotope Company's robot, the "Sacred Tree" had two huge gashes with peculiar liquid seeming to have flowed out by more than half.

"Look."

Cynthia gestured with her chin, indicating Johan should look at the Zarude.

The Zarude elder, leading a group of Zarude, was looking at Johan expectantly, appearing to want to say something but not quite daring to.

Enemies that their entire Zarude clan couldn't stop despite their full efforts, Johan and Cynthia had easily resolved.

If last time the Zarude clan's understanding of Johan's strength was insufficient, this time... they recognized it.

The Zarude were also relieved that Johan had held back a few days ago—otherwise, they wouldn't know if their clan would see the next day's sun.

Simultaneously, while revering Johan and Cynthia's strength, the Zarude also looked at Johan with some pleading.

Perhaps this extraordinarily powerful human could help them restore the "Sacred Tree."

Actually, Johan was already planning to do this without them asking.

Because Serperior had once again resonated with the "Sacred Tree."

And unlike before, last time Serperior had been proactive, but this time the Sacred Tree was "proactive."

Serperior's ability to immediately get the forest's response during the previous battle was actually a sign.

Sure enough—

"Sni..."

Serperior stood beneath the tree, looking up blankly at the damaged "Sacred Tree."

Rustle rustle—

Forest leaves rustled without wind.

A light green breeze drifted up from behind Serperior, as if the forest's power was gathering toward it, helping it restore the "Sacred Tree."

Johan nodded slightly.

Last time, though Serperior gained much, it hadn't met evolution conditions. This time... might be different.

Even without using aura power, Johan could still feel that forest aura.

Such a vast forest, plus the "Sacred Tree's" power interaction...

"Sni!"

Serperior called out low as numerous blue-green transparent vine-like patterns appeared behind it.

All the Zarude present gaped seeing this scene.

"Zarude!" The elder Zarude was extremely excited, constantly striking the ground with its staff, making "bang bang" sounds.

Child of the Forest!

Child of the Forest!

Child?

Johan glanced disapprovingly at the elder Zarude.

Believe it or not, Serperior will beat you up later?

She's a girl, you know.

"Ka-kaah..."

Gengar's eyes rolled around, looking at the staff in the elder Zarude's hand with some cunning.

Last visit, Gengar had taken a liking to this staff. This time...

[It's the forest's wish! A wish from the forest!]

Jirachi called out excitedly.

A "wish" from the forest!

As the Wish Pokémon, Jirachi clearly felt it.

And to fulfill this wish... depended on Serperior.

"Sni~"

Serperior remained calm, approaching the thick "Sacred Tree" and gently placing her hand on its trunk.

The next second, tender shoots sprouted from the trunk beneath Serperior's feet, quickly budding and growing.

The blue-green vines behind Serperior grew longer, rapidly spreading. In the previously destroyed forest, numerous plants continuously sprouted and took root.

"Bii~~"

At this moment, a ethereal, crisp sound came from atop the "Sacred Tree."

Everyone looked toward the sound.

The Zarude immediately became excited.

Celebi!

It's Celebi!

They couldn't remember how long since they'd seen Celebi—this legendary existence that protected forests had actually appeared now!

Could it be...

"A shiny Celebi," Cynthia said softly.

What appeared was a pink-colored Celebi, moving gracefully and nimbly, its sparkling eyes curiously watching Serperior.

"Cha?"

Tinkaton looked at Celebi, then at itself, then pointed at Celebi and called to Johan twice.

Hey! It's the same color as me!

Johan looked at the appearing shiny Celebi, then at the various "phenomena" Serperior was bringing about, his expression somewhat strange.

His Serperior wouldn't evolve into a legendary, would it?

Really, this commotion didn't seem like what ordinary Pokémon could cause.

"Bii~"

Celebi playfully winked at Johan, looking like it recognized him.

However, it didn't linger, instead flying rapidly around the forest, constantly waving at Serperior.

The forest power already stirred by Serperior became even more surging.

"Sni!"

Serperior suddenly called out lightly.

Hum—

The damaged areas on the "Sacred Tree" began glowing faintly as the broken trunk visibly recovered.

"Oowoo..."

Ninetales sensed "life energy" aura.

When the "Sacred Tree" gradually recovered, when the forest's power reached some extreme...

Hum—

Serperior's body suddenly burst with brilliant white light!

"Here it comes!" Johan collected all scattered thoughts and focused intently on Serperior.

Evolution light!

Though his Pokémon had evolved many times, Johan inevitably felt nervous and excited whenever a Pokémon evolved.

Evolution... was probably one of Pokémon's most charming abilities.

Under the white light's envelope, Serperior's appearance gradually changed.

Her arms slowly disappeared, her body became thicker and longer, overall size growing larger. Vine-like patterns slowly appeared on her body, seeming to belong naturally while also seeming... to originate from the entire forest!

Rustle—

The recovered "Sacred Tree's" thick trunk and lush branches seemed to sway, and a power belonging to the "Sacred Tree" fed back to Serperior.

Just like Serperior with their "Miracle Berry" tree.

However, this energy was more massive and pure.

When the brilliant white light faded, Serperior—no, should be called Serperior now—appeared before Johan and others in a brand new form.

[Wow—]

Jirachi's little eyes lit up, emotional value maxed out.

"Sni..."

Serperior struck a cool pose, expression aloof, chin slightly raised, red eyes showing royal pride without reservation.

Finally!

Serperior had successfully evolved into her final form!

However, when Serperior saw Johan's smiling face, that prideful color quickly disappeared.

"Congratulations, Serperior. You've become even more amazing," Johan smiled.

He'd been wondering when Serperior would evolve into her final form. "Forest's Blessing" brought huge benefits while making evolution far more difficult than other Pokémon.

The previous "Sacred Tree" visit hadn't enabled evolution.

Unexpectedly, this time the "Sacred Tree" being damaged actually let her successfully accumulate the power needed for evolution.

"Sni, sni!"

Serperior's cheeks reddened slightly as she called out with bared teeth.

Even, even if you praise me, I won't be happy!

"Haha—"

Johan had figured out Serperior's personality—she needed gentle handling. Though she looked fierce, that was just her shy "disguise."

"Bii~"

Celebi waved at Johan and Serperior, turning to enter a space-time tunnel.

"Give my regards, I miss it very much. Come play together when you have time~" Johan also waved at Celebi.

The "it" he mentioned was naturally the Celebi he knew.

"Bii~~"

The shiny Celebi nodded happily and disappeared.

Johan rubbed his chin lightly.

He wondered if Celebi understood correctly. By "you both" he meant this shiny Celebi and the previously known Celebi—two total.

Don't let thousands of Celebi suddenly appear... that would be fun.

The elder Zarude, leaning on its staff, walked "resolutely" to Johan. When directly facing Johan's gaze, its eyes trembled slightly, but it forced courage and took a deep breath.

Johan's expression was strange.

This old Zarude's expression kept changing—who knew what it was thinking.

"Za, Zarude!"

Could, could you please have the 'Child of the Forest' stay!

Stay?

Johan and Serperior exchanged glances.

Too lazy to even speak, they turned and left.

Stay here yourselves.

The surrounding wild Pokémon that Coco had brought followed Serperior step by step as they left.

In such a short time, Serperior had already "conquered" all the wild Pokémon.

"Teacher, Teacher Johan..."

Quite a few Biotope Company researchers recognized Johan, as his reputation in research circles wasn't small.

Karen, as representative, approached.

When Dr. Jedo wanted to ignore all wild Pokémon and force-attack the "Sacred Tree," Karen and others had realized Dr. Jedo's true purpose wasn't so simple, nor was he as "kind" as he appeared.

And now, Dr. Jedo, Onyx, Agate, and numerous Outstanding Inc. members were unconscious or tied up.

Karen wanted to ask what to do with these people.

"Oh right." Johan slapped his forehead. "There's still one matter to wrap up."

He gave Gengar a look.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar hypnotized Dr. Jedo without any difficulty.

Dr. Jedo gradually told everything about persecuting Coco's parents back then.

Karen and others looked shocked.

They hadn't expected Dr. Jedo to actually commit murder?

And against his two best "friends"!

How many veteran researchers in current Biotope Company had joined because of Coco's parents?

Of course, most shocked was definitely Coco.

Not only confirming he wasn't a Zarude, but learning his parents had already died...

But this was left to Papa Zarude—it could certainly comfort him well.

Perhaps... future Coco would still leave this forest and return to human society.

The way he'd developed of interacting with Pokémon in youth, plus his ability to communicate directly with Pokémon, destined him to become a very remarkable trainer.

Perhaps comparable to N.

"Please hand this Dr. Jedo over to Officer Jenny," Johan told Karen and others.

"Yes!"

Understanding what Dr. Jedo had done, Karen and others' last trace of sympathy disappeared.

As for Agate, Onyx and others... they could only call Chairman Geeta to send people to take them away.

"Whew—"

Johan stretched lazily.

Matter finished!

This "plot" had no difficulty and even let Serperior evolve.

If only all encountered matters could be this "simple."

Noticing Cynthia's gaze, Johan smiled: "Now we can train properly."

Serperior's evolution was perfect timing for training to adapt to post-evolution bodily changes.

"Zarude..."

Watching the departing Johan and Cynthia, watching the reluctant wild Pokémon, the elder Zarude sighed sadly and pounded its back.

Pity they couldn't keep the "Child of the Forest."

However... this "Sacred Tree" attack and wild Pokémon rescue made the Zarude realize...

Perhaps "Forest's Blood" didn't just refer to them, but all forest Pokémon.

Hmm?

"Zarude?"

The elder Zarude suddenly felt like something was missing, looking around. What was missing?

Right.

My staff!

Where's my staff!

Time passed.

The "Pokémon World Championships'" "points stage" concluded amid audiences' rising enthusiasm.

The top five from "points stage" would directly advance to "Master Ball Tier," filling sixteen "Master Ball Tier" spots.

The following sixteen would qualify for "Master Replacement Battle."

Of course, considering some might not have sufficient time for points, there was still "Ultra Ball Tier" double-elimination tournament before "Master Replacement Battle."

Meaning... excluding the thirty-two who advanced to "Master Ball Tier," the top sixteen would have "two lives"—still qualifying for "revival matches" after defeat, while the bottom sixteen had only one chance.

The final sixteen determined could participate in "Master Replacement Battle."

This gave many opportunities while making competition more suspenseful.

Johan and Cynthia officially ended training, preparing to head to Galar region to watch "Ultra Ball Tier" tournaments while preparing for "Master Replacement Battle."

Additionally, many long-unseen friends and elders could meet at this event.

Johan's thinking was simple.

Don't get challenged in "Master Replacement Battle," meet Diantha or Professor Kukui in the round of sixteen—that would be success!

----------

# 776: Master Replacement Battle Begins!

Galar region, Wyndon City.

Through Leon's efforts, most of the "parasites" within the Galar League had been basically eliminated.

The Galar region's largest corporation, Macro Cosmos, had also initially completed integration with the Galar League under Leon's supervision.

Macro Cosmos was simply too massive, affecting every aspect of Galar region residents' lives. Removal was impossible—integration with the Galar League was the best choice.

Actually, when Rose was chairman, Macro Cosmos and the Galar League were already closely connected, which reduced the difficulty for Leon.

Leon had been this busy, yet after the "points stage" ended, he still ranked third in "Master Ball Tier."

This showed just how many battles he'd fought and how many points he'd accumulated.

At this moment, in a villa within Wyndon City, Johan, Cynthia, Steven, Flint, and many others had gathered.

In the villa's garden, many long-unseen friends rarely had a chance to gather together.

"Hehe! Lucian didn't make the 'double elimination tournament,' hehe—"

Flint laughed with obvious smugness.

Lucian sat cross-legged under a nearby tree, holding a book. Hearing Flint's smug laughter, he glanced up slightly without much expression change.

He hadn't really participated much in the "points stage." With his Sinnoh League Elite Four status, he barely maintained a position in the top 200 of "Ultra Ball Tier."

Unless he had cloning techniques, with so many Sinnoh League matters to handle, where would he find time for "points stage" battles?

Fortunately, Lucian wasn't particularly interested in such battles—he wasn't a battle maniac.

Only because the "Pokémon World Championships" was starting did he reluctantly take time to watch.

"You've been grinding points for so long, yet didn't directly make 'Master Ball Tier,' and in the 'double elimination' you only have one life," Lucian smirked.

Flint had indeed grinded points for a long time, fighting many spectacular battles.

But... Flint called it point grinding, but actually just sought strong opponents to battle, so he won some and lost some. This not only prevented him from directly reaching "Master Ball Tier" but left him with only one life in the "double elimination" stage.

"Just say whether I made it or not," Flint said with hands in pockets, smugness overflowing.

Lucian adjusted his glasses, nodded slightly, and replied perfunctorily:

"You made it, you made it."

Wait until this "Pokémon World Championships" ends—then we'll see if I don't deal with you.

"Come on, Flint, try this new dish I developed," Johan suddenly approached wearing an apron.

"Coming!" Flint rushed over excitedly to see Johan holding an exquisitely presented dish that was entirely fiery red, as if about to burst into flames.

"This is..." Flint asked puzzledly.

"Charizard Special."

"Charizard Special?"

Flint took the spoon and put a mouthful in his mouth.

"How is it?" Johan couldn't help asking.

Flint bit the spoon, lips pressed together, saying nothing, but his face visibly reddened as his body trembled continuously.

The next second, Flint opened his mouth and breathed out "flames" like a Charizard.

"So spicy! So spicy! So spicy—"

"Ku?" Infernape looked strangely at its trainer.

Flamethrower?

Oh no!

The trainer had mastered Flamethrower too?

Watching Flint frantically search for water, Johan rubbed his chin lightly.

"Seems like the spicy 'Herba Mystica' really isn't suitable for solo use—too spicy."

Through "guinea pig" Flint's trial, Johan confirmed the spice level of the spicy "Herba Mystica."

He just didn't know if some spice-loving Pokémon could handle it.

"Teacher Johan, anything good to eat?"

Leon arrived late after a busy day. Seeing the food in Johan's hands, he rubbed his stomach and asked.

Teacher Johan's cooking was famous among their group.

"Oh, Charizard Special. Want to try?" Johan asked with an unchanged expression.

"Charizard? I definitely have to try that!" Leon immediately became interested hearing "Charizard."

But he paused while picking up the spoon.

"Right, Teacher Johan, I have an idea. Help me think it through."

Seeing Leon stop, Johan could only say: "Go ahead."

"Look." Leon raised his hand, pointing toward Wyndon's tallest Rose Tower. "Mr. Rose's Rose Tower is Wyndon's tallest and most iconic building."

"But now that Macro Cosmos has integrated with the Galar League, using such a tall building as Galar League headquarters would be too ostentatious. But demolishing or abandoning it would be too wasteful, so... I want to renovate Rose Tower."

With Rose gone, Rose Tower still towering there was indeed somewhat inappropriate.

"Then renovate it," Johan shrugged.

"Into what? That's what's troubling me now," Leon smiled helplessly.

Into what?

"Battle Tower," Johan said without hesitation.

In the storyline, after Rose resigned as League Chairman due to the Eternatus incident and left the Galar League, the succeeding Leon converted Rose Tower into a Battle Tower.

He even invited top trainers from around the world to serve as Tower Masters.

Even Blue was invited by Leon to serve for a period.

"Battle Tower?" Leon's eyes immediately lit up. "Great idea!"

Then he dropped the spoon and ran out of the villa.

Watching Leon leave, Johan holding the plate opened his mouth.

Forget it.

One "guinea pig" Flint was enough—he'd spare Leon.

Both the "double elimination" and "Master Replacement Battle" were held in Galar region, so Leon as Champion and Chairman would be quite busy.

"What did Teacher make?"

When Johan returned to the table with the plate, Steven asked curiously.

Like Leon, no—Steven trusted Johan's cooking even more than Leon did.

"Children can't eat this," Johan immediately dumped the "Charizard Special" in the trash.

Steven: "..."

He opened his mouth, momentarily unsure what to say.

He'd clearly seen Flint eat it earlier.

"Speaking of which, Steven, what's your ranking?"

Though Steven, like Lucian, had to handle Hoenn League affairs, he was better off than Lucian since he had Elite Four Glacia helping cover for him, making things much easier.

Moreover, Steven had also joined the point-grinding queue in the last half month before the "points stage" ended.

Another point was that Steven, like Cynthia and Leon, had participated in many tournaments before, with many rankings contributing Master tournament points.

After all, people like Johan who played in the "baby cup" were still a minority.

So Steven's ranking in "Ultra Ball Tier" shouldn't be low.

Steven smiled and said: "Didn't have time for too many battles, finally reached 20th in 'Ultra Ball Tier.'"

20th place?

Excluding five who directly entered "Master Ball Tier," Steven was 15th in "Ultra Ball Tier," having one mistake allowance in the "double elimination."

However, with Steven's strength, entering the "Master Replacement Battle" was definitely no problem.

As long as he didn't pick the most abnormal opponents in the "Master Replacement Battle," advancing to the real Master tournament wouldn't be an issue.

Johan patted Steven's shoulder with satisfaction.

It was time.

Time to pass the Champion position to Steven.

His student—no problem giving him more responsibility.

As a teacher, one must learn to let go...

Future days would be sunshine, beaches, Cynthia beach exclusives...

Tsk tsk, just thinking about it felt comfortable.

"It's out!"

"The 'double elimination' bracket and schedule are out!"

Red-faced Flint with fiery explosion hair, drenched in sweat, suddenly ran into the garden again.

"League information received, Rotom!"

The League had announced the "double elimination" battle contestant list.

It included not only Flint and Steven, but many well-known Elite Four members like Bruno, Karen, Caitlin, etc.

Of course, besides Elite Four members, the "double elimination" also featured many Gym Leaders, such as Janine from Kanto, Clair from Johto, Norman from Hoenn, Volkner from Sinnoh, Marnie from Galar, etc.

All were extremely strong among Gym Leaders.

In fact, to some degree, these Gym Leaders actually had qualifications or ability to compete for Elite Four positions in their regions.

Besides Elite Four and Gym Leaders, the "double elimination" also had "ordinary" trainers.

Like N, who somewhat surprised Johan, and the expected Tobias.

Scanning this list at a glance, not just the audience, but even Johan couldn't help looking forward to it.

It felt quite like... a celebrity battle royale.

"Lucian, the schedule is out!" Flint ran to Lucian again to show off.

Looking at Flint with his swollen face, sweating profusely, and most importantly, lips swollen like sausages, Lucian lowered his sunglasses, showing an exaggerated expression, and asked:

"Who are you?"

Flint was stunned, pointing at himself: "Me! Flint!"

Lucian shook his head definitively: "Nonsense, Flint isn't nearly as handsome as you!"

The "Pokémon World Championships'" points stage ended, and the "double elimination" proceeded intensely under audiences' eager attention.

Contestants decided the qualifiers for "Master Replacement Battle" through battles.

Some were within Johan's expectations.

Like Steven, Caitlin, Bruno, etc.—nothing surprising there.

As long as they didn't coincidentally face each other, advancement probability was relatively high.

Some were beyond Johan's expectations, or unexpected.

Battle Frontier's Brandon, with his three Regi legendaries, fought with overwhelming dominance!

Also like Paldea region Gym Leader Poppy.

Johan had thought Poppy was just playing around like Master Mustard, but unexpectedly she unleashed her full strength without reservation, successfully advancing to "Master Replacement Battle."

And Hoenn League's Drake!

The old man had actually secretly snuck to Paldea region to compete.

Thinking of both Steven and Drake participating while Elite Four Glacia maintained her icy expression, Johan couldn't help shivering.

Hopefully those two would fare well upon return.

What surprised Johan most was undoubtedly N's advancement!

How long had it been?

From Team Plasma's fall to now, N had already grown enough to compete with Elite Four-level trainers and even win.

Of course, due to tournament rules prohibiting "first-class legendary" Pokémon in battle, N couldn't bring Black Dragon Zekrom to the arena.

But precisely because of this, N's progress surprised Johan.

As for why tournament rules established "prohibiting first-class legendary Pokémon in battle"...

Cough cough.

N was probably "collaterally damaged."

However, this rule wasn't restrictive for Tobias with Darkrai and Latios.

Tobias was somewhat like... someone crazily testing BUG boundaries and profiting.

But his progress was also surprising enough.

"It's not just us who are improving..." Johan leaned against the railing, looking at the portraits on the big screen, sighing involuntarily.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar, Jirachi, Victini, and Marshadow—four little ones sat in a row on the railing from big to small, swinging their feet leisurely.

Regarding Johan's sigh, Gengar didn't completely agree.

Gengar indicated... no matter how fast others improved, could they be faster than it?

Don't worry!

Opponents were just some Champions and Elite Four members.

Weren't they just like babies?

Hearing Gengar's smugness, Johan was exasperated.

But sometimes... "Being slightly confident isn't bad," Johan smiled softly.

"Now let's welcome the final two contestants of the 'double elimination'! Both are from Sinnoh region! Respectively Elite Four Flint! And Gym Leader Volkner!"

Roar—

The arena erupted.

Interested parties had already secretly investigated Flint and Volkner's history.

Unexpectedly, these close friends and rivals would meet in the "double elimination's" final battle!

"Volkner, bring it on!" Flint's face showed pure excitement.

Facing Volkner excited him more than any previous opponent.

Compared to Flint, Volkner appeared much gentler.

However, the "fourth generation" still smiled at his close friend: "Don't cry when you lose."

"Hey!"

Both smiled at each other.

"Flint's got this..." Johan looked at Victini. The little one frowned—it couldn't tell whose victory aura was stronger.

Rubbing its head, Johan said: "Trust me."

"Vii?"

"In this state, Flint... believe it or not, he and Infernape could take down two or three Champion-level Pokémon?"

"Vii..." Victini seemed to understand.

The "Journey School" vs "Academy School" debate still hadn't reached a conclusion.

Actually, in Johan's view, so-called "Journey School" and "Academy School" could be simplified as "Ash faction" and "Paul faction"—namely "nostalgia faction" and "strength faction."

Honestly, normally the "Paul faction" was stronger than the "Ash faction."

But regarding upper limits... only one thing could be said—Ash rocks.

And Flint, in Johan's view, was like Ash's "replica."

Though Volkner wasn't as cold as Paul, he was much calmer than Flint.

Perhaps Volkner would have early advantages, but ultimately... Flint would definitely achieve comeback victory through "stand up," "hang in there," "dodge quickly," "our bond," and such methods.

So battling hot-blooded guys like Flint meant either extinguishing his passion from the start, having overwhelming strength, or being even more passionate.

The final result was as Johan predicted.

Flint, with Infernape, made a comeback against Volkner in two consecutive games, finally earning "Master Replacement Battle" qualification!

The sixteen-person list for "Master Replacement Battle" qualification was officially announced:

Steven, Flint, Wallace, Caitlin, Drake, Brandon, Raihan, Marnie, Bea, Bruno, Janine, Poppy, Agatha, Karen, Tobias, and N!

Sixteen total.

With the "double elimination's" conclusion, the "Master Replacement Battle" was about to officially begin!

"Time to battle..." Johan said softly.

"Ka-kaah!" Raising a juice cup, calling out fearlessly.

Then let's crush them!

----------
