# 777: Johan Being Challenged!

On this day, the world's attention focused on the Wyndon Stadium in the Galar region.

In this stadium that could currently be called the world's largest, audiences from around the globe had gathered—describing it as a sea of people would be an understatement.

All because the "Pokémon World Championships'" "Master Replacement Battle" was about to commence here!

If the previous "double elimination tournament" was the warm-up for the "Pokémon World Championships," then reaching the "Master Replacement Battle" stage meant entering the main tournament.

The sacred flame burned fiercely above the stadium. Under countless gazes and audience cheers, the large screen hanging over the arena listed sixteen "Master Ball Tier" contestants and sixteen "Ultra Ball Tier" contestants!

Cheers erupted like a tsunami. Even before the contestants appeared, the audience was already boiling with excitement.

"Please don't choose me," Johan sat in the waiting room with arms crossed, silently praying.

Not that he was afraid—it was just troublesome.

"Leon isn't here yet?" Lance, wearing his cape and red-blue Champion outfit, looked around the waiting room and noticed someone missing.

"Probably got lost again," Raihan said calmly while watching the livestream on his phone.

Leon being late was standard procedure by now. Anyway, he always arrived before the deadline in his special way.

The contestants in the waiting room had also formed several groups.

Young people like Lance, Johan, Cynthia, Diantha, Lorelei, and Raihan sat together.

Veterans like Agatha, Alder, Pryce, and Mustard rarely met and were chatting.

Others like Professor Kukui disguised as the Royal Masked Man, former Paldea Champion Peony, and Geeta stayed close together.

Professor Kukui wanted to learn from former Champion Peony about being a regional Champion, and from Geeta about managing a region well.

However, Professor Kukui's calculations were clearly wrong.

When Peony was Champion, the Galar League was entirely controlled by Rose, so there usually wasn't much to do.

As for Geeta, her attention was more focused on "Gibeon."

Such a young "Gibeon"?

Seeming to notice Geeta's attention, "Gibeon" smiled slightly at her.

In the waiting room, only "Gibeon" and Giovanni sat alone in their respective seats.

Giovanni even had his eyes closed in a meditative posture.

Johan had originally wanted to chat with Boss Giovanni, but seeing him like that, he temporarily chose to give up.

Better wait until evening when no one's around...

Under the audience's gaze, the central big screen showed changes.

"Here it comes! The opponent selection is starting!"

"The first one... is Elite Four Bruno, right?"

"Elite Four Bruno is unfortunate—ranked sixth in 'Ultra Ball Tier,' but the top five directly advanced to 'Master Ball Tier.'"

"Who will Elite Four Bruno choose?"

The order for selecting opponents in the "Master Replacement Battle" was also based on points stage rankings.

"The Kanto region is really strong," Johan clicked his tongue, looking at Bruno's portrait on screen.

The Kanto League only had three Elite Four members total: Agatha, Lorelei, and Bruno.

All made it to the World Championships.

Moreover, Agatha and Lorelei directly entered "Master Ball Tier."

If you added Lance, who held a Kanto League Elite Four position but was actually Johto League Champion, then all of Kanto's Elite Four had at least reached the "Master Replacement Battle" stage!

Quite exaggerated.

"Hoenn isn't bad either," Cynthia said in good spirits, wearing an elegant black coat with legs crossed.

Hoenn?

Johan was stunned.

Then he realized.

Himself, Steven, Drake, and Wallace—that was four people too!

Moreover... counting himself, three of the four were Champions!

Hoenn really was full of talent, Johan sighed.

Meanwhile, on the big screen, "Master Ball Tier" contestant portraits disappeared one by one, until the last remaining was... Mustard!

"Master Replacement Battle," first round: Bruno VS Mustard!

The crowd erupted.

No one expected Bruno would choose Master Mustard.

Johan hadn't expected it either.

"I thought it would be Lance," Johan glanced at Lance with arms crossed, then at Mustard wearing an Alolan beach shirt doing basic stretches.

Everyone had previously thought Mustard would, like before, give up subsequent matches after finishing the "points stage" and achieving his ranking.

Unexpectedly, Mustard chose to continue competing this time.

Lance laughed: "Bruno was one of Mustard's idols when he was young."

Only then did Johan remember that Bruno was a martial artist, and among martial artists throughout the Pokémon world, Master Mustard held an extremely high position.

"But... it won't be easy."

Johan had battled both Bruno and Mustard. Though neither had used full strength then, Mustard's Urshifu was indeed something special—strength-wise, even if not legendary level, it was very close.

More importantly, among Bruno's main Pokémon, besides Fighting-types, there were quite a few Rock-types.

Type disadvantage.

Without unnecessary chatter, under global focus, the "Master Replacement Battle" officially began at Wyndon Stadium.

The battle between Bruno and Mustard had already entered a heated stage.

"Bruno has sent out his signature Pokémon, Machamp!!"

The commentator shouted hoarsely: "Facing Master Mustard's storm-like continuous attacks, how will Bruno and Machamp respond!"

Bruno's choice was naturally—

"Machamp, let the opponent taste why you're called 'Machamp'!"

Facing "Rapid Strike Style" secrets, Bruno decisively chose direct brutal assault.

Defense?

Didn't exist!

Single Strike Style was both the worst and best way to break Rapid Strike Style.

"Hahaha—"

Seeing Bruno's passionate enthusiasm, Mustard laughed heartily.

He knew this "Pokémon World Championships" would be interesting.

He hadn't expected to encounter such an exciting battle already in the "Master Replacement Battle" stage.

Though that heart was old, the blood flowing within it was still boiling hot!

"Go, Urshifu!" Mustard recalled his Rapid Strike Urshifu and sent out Single Strike Style Urshifu!

Since it was Single Strike Style, naturally they should compete in Single Strike Style.

"Old man will give you the most direct response!" Mustard declared loudly.

"Machamp, Dynamic Punch!"

Bruno's signature—"No Guard" ability Machamp's Dynamic Punch.

"Urshifu, Protect!"

Bruno: "?"

Johan: "?"

Audience: "?"

Mustard grinned: "Oops, getting old..."

Then waved his hand: "Wicked Blow!"

Trusting opponents' words in battle was taboo!

Didn't Johan teach you that, Bruno?

Even "No Guard" ability couldn't penetrate Protect's barrier. Mustard was indeed cunning and experienced.

However, ultimately, there was still some strength difference between them, especially when both sides used Fighting-type Pokémon.

Mustard lived up to his "Master" title, teaching Bruno some techniques while successfully defending his "Master" position.

The final score was 3:6, with Mustard having a clear advantage.

After their battle, neither held grudges—instead, they felt like friends of different generations.

"Master Mustard! Let me treat you to... food!"

"Lululu~ Bruno is indeed generous!"

"Hahaha—Of course!"

"All you can eat?"

"Yes!"

"Great!"

After determining the winner, both left the arena together.

The first "Master Replacement Battle" officially concluded, setting a good precedent for this tournament.

However, Johan estimated... Mustard wouldn't be able to eat much "angry bread" before having to return.

Because he saw Poppy's expression on screen.

When someone wanted to cut someone, their gaze couldn't be hidden.

Afterward, the "Master Replacement Battle" continued.

Most of the time, "Master Ball Tier" contestants could defend their positions.

"Another 'Master' has successfully defended their 'throne'!"

The commentator's voice was hoarse, but the audience remained in shock, unable to recover for a long time.

Only because... Wikstrom from the Kalos region's Elite Four had challenged someone who advanced as "Ultra Ball Tier's" third place—Giovanni!

Boss Giovanni!

Then was directly beaten by Boss Giovanni... six to one!

Johan seriously suspected Boss Giovanni had shown "social courtesy" at the end—otherwise Wikstrom might have been completely swept.

Johan quite admired Wikstrom's courage.

Truly worthy of his "knight" title—just brave.

Wikstrom's result also made some people with designs on the "unknown" Giovanni abandon their thoughts.

Honestly, people like Mustard, Giovanni, Raihan, and Lorelei who advanced to "Master Ball Tier" as top five "Ultra Ball Tier" were initially considered easier opponents.

But as Mustard and Giovanni successively showed their strength, many had to reassess their choices.

Of course, some fought the "Master Replacement Battle" to advance to the top sixteen, while others fought purely to battle desired opponents.

Like Bruno, Caitlin, Poppy, etc.

Janine leaned against the wall, hidden in corner shadows, watching the screen's results with narrowed eyes.

Challenge Boss Giovanni?

Legendary and Champion weren't even the same level, much less compared to Elite Four.

Janine also admired Wikstrom's courage.

Her gaze swept through the sixteen "Master Ball Tier" contestants from top to bottom.

Boss Giovanni, Teacher Johan, Agatha, Pryce...

"What a terrifying tournament..."

By her estimate, at least three people in this "Pokémon World Championships" had legendary-level Pokémon!

Compared to those she "knew," Janine's gaze finally rested on the young "Gibeon."

Outstanding Inc. president?

So young...

"Challenge successful! This is the first contestant to successfully challenge in the 'Master Replacement Battle'!"

The entire venue erupted.

Someone actually pulled a "Master" down?

"Congratulations to Steven for successful replacement!"

Steven!

"Sigh, suddenly feel old," Peony said with hands on hips, striking a "lamenting" pose.

But couldn't suppress his smile.

"Thanks to Mr. Peony's mercy," Steven said modestly.

As fellow Steel-type specialists, Steven's victory over Peony undoubtedly showed his hard strength exceeded Peony's.

It also meant Steven truly had Champion-level strength.

Peony waved his hand and "sighed" again: "Fine, I'll go accompany my little Peonia and Penny."

Then prepared to leave.

Whether he was truly happy or truly happy was unclear.

Steven's successful challenge gave challengers a shot of adrenaline.

Johan in the waiting room patted his chest.

He'd thought Steven would challenge him.

That would be awkward.

After all, if he wanted to pass the Champion position to Steven... should they have a real battle?

However, Johan wasn't surprised by Steven's advancement.

With Steven's strength, few in "Master Ball Tier" dared claim they could definitely beat him.

"Teacher!"

In the audience seats, seeing Johan welcoming him, Steven smiled and called out.

Johan patted Steven's shoulder with satisfaction.

Perfect.

"Master Ball Tier" as Champion was enough!

In the stands, green-haired N watched Teacher Johan and Steven, gently pressing his cap brim.

"Teacher..."

Wyndon City.

The "Master Replacement Battle" had thirty-two people total, at least sixteen battles, all 6V6 full battles—it couldn't be finished in a day or two.

On the fourth day of the "Master Replacement Battle," Johan was preparing to leave his residence when he met Galar League staff who informed him of news.

He was being challenged!

This was the "Master Replacement Battle" procedure—contestants could know challenge situations in advance, while audiences only learned at match start.

As for who challenged Johan... it was N from the Unova region!

N had also made quite a name during this "Pokémon World Championships'" "points stage."

His handsome appearance and gentle personality were one aspect—this only earned some "looks-focused" fans.

But N also gained many "battle fans."

Only because... N had displayed at least ten different tactics during the "points stage," including the four mainstream weather tactics: sun, rain, sandstorm, and snow!

Just to fully present these tactics, N commanded different Pokémon—over forty total!

Completely without repetition!

This made many secretly speculate how many Pokémon N had trained and how much he'd invested.

Later, some tried to investigate N's identity and background, but obtained limited information, finally only learning this N was Teacher Johan's student!

As everyone knew, Teacher Johan was not only a "Heart Master" but also called a "Tactical Master."

As a "Tactical Master's" student, N's deep tactical reserves made sense.

However, when Johan learned N chose to challenge him, he was somewhat surprised.

Really came without warning.

"Being challenged?" Cynthia, arm-in-arm with Caitlin, asked with interest upon seeing the League staff.

Caitlin appeared somewhat gloating.

She'd already finished her "Master Replacement Battle" by directly challenging Cynthia, then... unsurprisingly lost.

"Mm."

Johan's mouth twitched.

"What? Teacher Johan is being challenged?!"

Flint burst out.

"Damn, someone beat me to it!"

Lucian walked past Flint holding books, saying flatly: "It's not like each 'Master' can only be challenged once. You can challenge Teacher Johan afterward."

"Right." Flint slapped his forehead but quickly realized: "Wait, if Teacher Johan loses..."

Before finishing, everyone looked at him like he was an idiot.

Didn't they hear the League staff?

The challenger was N, that genius who considered himself Teacher Johan's student, being trained by Champion Alder as the next Unova League Champion.

This was a challenge?

Obviously coming to test learning results.

Johan was already thinking about something else.

Flint also wanted to challenge him?

Should he... eliminate him at midnight?

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar, knowing Johan's thoughts, patted its chest.

This job—I'm good at it!

October 5th, sunny weather.

Galar region, Wyndon City, Wyndon Stadium.

The big screen already showed today's challenger and the challenged party.

N VS Teacher Johan!

Teacher Johan was finally being challenged!

And the challenger was student N.

A teacher-student battle sounded very interesting.

However, what really made the entire audience erupt was the two huge shadows appearing above Wyndon Stadium before the battle began.

"Wh-what is that?!"

"Dragons! Seems like dragons! Two dragons!"

When did Galar region have such two powerful Dragon Pokémon?

Couldn't be... Eternatus?!

Crimson flames dyed half the sky red, while the other half was shrouded in black clouds and thunder.

"Those are legendary Pokémon from Unova region! Reshiram the Dragon of Truth and Zekrom the Dragon of Ideals!"

"The Black and White Dragons! Aren't those Teacher Johan's friends?"

"Holy crap... Teacher Johan isn't planning to field these two, is he?"

"But... didn't tournament rules say legendary dragons like the Black and White Dragons couldn't be used?"

"Ridiculous! If Teacher Johan really wanted to borrow these legendaries' power, who could stop him?"

Bang! Bang!

The Black and White Dragons, Reshiram and Zekrom, landed on opposite sides of the stadium's edge, glancing at each other with serious expressions.

However, Reshiram and Zekrom only appeared without doing anything excessive—they even seemed quite well-behaved.

"Stop guessing! Contestant N is not only Teacher Johan's student but also recognized by Zekrom the Dragon of Ideals!"

"Ah this..."

"So the Black and White Dragons came to..."

"Watch the match?!"

The audience was completely confused.

Legendary first-class gods, two of Unova's "Tao Trio," actually traveled from Unova to Galar to... watch live?

So exciting?

"I heard Teacher Johan was also recognized by Reshiram the Dragon of Truth?"

"Good lord! Truth vs Ideals battle?"

"Truth vs Ideals? You probably don't know how that ancient Unova kingdom fell, right?"

"So exciting..."

The appearance of the Black and White Dragons, Reshiram and Zekrom, even Johan hadn't expected.

So when he reached the arena and saw the twin dragons above the massive stadium dome, he couldn't help being stunned.

"You two..."

Johan looked toward the dragons.

Zekrom: He won't notice, right?

Reshiram: Don't worry, this meal-mooching is perfectly reasonable! He can't guess!

Johan: "..."

You two forgot I'm communicating with you through aura?

He shook his head.

Whatever.

He'd use them as "guinea pigs" later.

"Let's go. Let me see what kind of surprise N will give me." Johan was somewhat curious which Pokémon N would use this time.

"Ka~kaah~UU"

Seeing Gengar's appearance, Johan smiled:

"Then let's go, Arcanine."

# 778: Teacher Johan VS N! Truth VS Ideals Battle!

"Ladies and gentlemen, welcome to the live 'Pokémon World Championships' 'Master Replacement Battle' venue!!"

The "Master Replacement Battle" had been going on for several days, with some succeeding and others failing, but the excitement level needed no words.

This could be seen from the cheers echoing through the sky at Wyndon Stadium.

Moreover, this match had not only regular spectators but also two legendary Pokémon from the distant Unova region—Black Dragon Zekrom and White Dragon Reshiram!

This made this battle even more anticipated and topical.

After confirming the twin dragons wouldn't suddenly fly down, the host secretly sighed in relief while his voice became excited again.

"The contestants facing off today are Johan from the Hoenn region and N from the Unova region—"

"Let's welcome both contestants to the stage!"

The audience erupted in stadium-shaking cheers again, completely drowning out even the entrance background music.

In the contestant tunnel, a green-haired young man playing with a Rubik's Cube heard this, exhaled softly, then smiled and said to his companions beside him:

"It's time to go. I'll be troubling you all again this time."

The Pokémon surrounding N called out affectionately, transforming into red lights and flying back to the Poké Balls at N's waist.

Honestly, N didn't actually like using Poké Balls to carry Pokémon, but tournament rules required contestants to register Pokémon in Poké Balls—at least there had to be capture records.

So when N learned of this situation, he had considered withdrawing from competition.

But all the Pokémon he knew very actively touched the Poké Balls to be captured, which moved N greatly.

However, after the tournament ended, he would also release these Pokémon.

Taking a deep breath, N steeled himself and walked onto the green field under spotlights, accepting cheers from the audience.

But N was still somewhat shy, only waving somewhat stiffly as his response to the audience's cheers.

Perhaps because of this, many girls screamed for N.

On N's opposite side, Johan in a black suit with a grinning Gengar beside him also entered the arena.

Compared to N, the already-famous Johan undoubtedly received more cheers...

Though not all for Johan—some were for Gengar too.

"Ka-kaah~ヾ('▽')"

"How can there be such a cute Pokémon as Gengar!"

"Ahhh—Gengar!"

"I want to take Teacher Johan's Gengar home!"

Go ahead and try—guaranteed one hug, one silent treatment, Johan silently complained.

"Roar—"

"Roar—"

As both entered, the Black and White Dragons standing atop Wyndon Stadium's dome also moved to the dome's edge, looking down from above.

White Dragon Reshiram stood above Johan's side, while Black Dragon Zekrom stood above N's side.

This gave Unova region spectators quite a feeling of... historical recreation.

"Eh? The Black Dragon is on N's side?" Flint asked in surprise from the audience.

In his impression, didn't both Black and White Dragons have good relationships with Teacher Johan?

"You want both Black and White Dragons to go to Teacher Johan's side?" Lucian glanced at him.

Flint understood and said with realization: "I see!"

They couldn't leave N to face everything alone, right?

But Flint quickly added: "Isn't it worried about Teacher Johan giving it a hard time?"

Rustle rustle—

People sitting near Flint all moved away from him, looking at him with "fearful" gazes.

You dare say such "brutal truths"?

"Hey, Teacher Johan isn't here," Flint waved casually.

Then everyone looked at little Jirachi nestled in Cynthia's arms.

Flint's expression changed.

Oh no!

Little Jirachi held a feather pen from who knows where, "writing furiously" on paper, recording Flint's every word and action.

Flint quickly leaned over to look, discovering...

Little Jirachi wasn't writing words but drawing—a very realistic explosion-head figure, then an open mouth saying something, with stick-like things coming out...

"What's this? Scallions? Eggplant? Bananas?" Flint pointed at Jirachi's drawing and asked.

Little Jirachi glanced at him, unhappily turned its head away, unwilling to acknowledge him.

Tattling on Johan behind his back, hmph!

Cynthia held little Jirachi, smiling as she explained: "Those are tusks."

"Tusks?" Flint was stunned.

What did that mean?

Lucian teased: "Saying you can't spit ivory from a dog's mouth."

Flint: "..."

And indeed... Black Dragon Zekrom was also worried about exactly that.

After all, Teacher Johan was the "Person of Truth"—sometimes just that truthful.

Meanwhile on the field, Johan and N smiled at each other.

"Teacher, I don't actually like battling much, but... I've been looking forward to this battle for a long time," N said.

With his personality, battling indeed wasn't what he enjoyed.

But if he wanted to achieve his "ideal nation," wanted to help more Pokémon, wanted to help those in Team Plasma with true ideals, he had to step forward and show sufficient strength and ability.

Become Unova League Champion!

Only then could he do more.

Battling Teacher Johan, receiving his teacher's evaluation, confirming his ideal's direction—N saw this as a necessary step.

"Then let me see your growth, N!"

Though Johan didn't want to be challenged... since he was already on the battlefield, there was no need to think about irrelevant things. Just enjoy the battle!

The referee, wearing regulation uniform and riding Aegislash, entered the arena.

The match used 6v6 full rotation format. According to both sides' drawing results, the first half's field would be grass field. When one side's Pokémon losses reached three, the field would change to rock field.

Both contestants and spectators were already familiar with these rules.

Rumble—

After both sides confirmed, the entire stadium was covered with thick arena barriers to ensure spectator safety.

The referee raised two flags.

"Contestants, please take your positions!"

N somewhat awkwardly took a Poké Ball from the chain at his waist.

"Please, Ninetales!"

Red light flashed as a Ninetales with golden fur and flowing tails appeared on the field.

Hum—

The sun in the sky immediately became scorching as intense sunlight baked the field, wisps of water vapor gradually rising and disappearing.

Sunny Day!

At the same moment, Johan's Poké Ball also landed on the field, opening with a "bang." What appeared was Urshifu with closed eyes and a snot bubble.

"Garruu zzZ"

"Ninetales? Sunny Day team?" Seeing the appearing Ninetales, Johan raised an eyebrow.

During the "points stage," N had demonstrated all four weather tactics plus other tactical displays and reserves, earning the nickname "little Johan."

So no one knew what tactics N would use in this "Master Replacement Battle."

Sunny Day team...

Champion Alder seemed quite skilled with Sunny Day teams too, right?

It seemed Champion Alder was indeed properly training N.

Seeing both had summoned their respective Pokémon, the referee's flags fell heavily as he shouted:

"Battle begin!"

The battle officially started!

N's expression immediately became serious. The Rubik's Cube at his waist rotated with his body movements as he swung his arm and shouted:

"Ninetales, Will-O-Wisp!"

"Oowoo—"

Ninetales' nine tails spread like fans, each tail tip manifesting blue-purple ghostly flames. As the tails swayed, they all shot toward the sleeping Urshifu.

A very standard opening move.

As one of the Sunny Day team enthusiasts, if this were a game, Johan could guess Ninetales' nature, moves, and EVs with his eyes closed.

Facing a physical attacker, Will-O-Wisp as an opener was indeed a very good choice.

But the problem was... this wasn't a game!

Johan had more than once or twice had opponents directly eliminate Will-O-Wisp.

So when N gave his command, Johan wasn't much slower.

"Urshifu, Aqua Jet!"

Splash—

Water surged from around Urshifu's feet, flowing upward, immediately enveloping Urshifu and rushing toward Ninetales at high speed.

Those ghostly Will-O-Wisps were directly extinguished upon touching the water surrounding Urshifu.

Bang!!

With the speed boost from "Aqua Jet," Urshifu directly reached Ninetales.

The water's impact sent Ninetales flying.

Unfortunately, under Sunny Day's boost, Aqua Jet's power was greatly reduced.

However, Urshifu didn't rely on this move for damage output anyway. Moves like Aqua Jet and Sucker Punch were more about giving it mobility to approach opponents faster.

Once Urshifu got close, opponents would face storm-like attacks!

And now, N's Ninetales had already been approached by Urshifu.

"Surging Strikes!" Johan showed no mercy.

Since N wanted to test his progress, then as his teacher, Johan naturally had to show sufficient respect.

"Garruu zzZ"

As Ninetales was knocked back, the grass beneath Urshifu's feet exploded with soil as it leaped forward, water swirling around its fists.

However, before Urshifu's fist could hit Ninetales, a red beam enveloped it and recalled it.

Pokémon switch!

Catching Ninetales' Poké Ball, N exhaled slightly with a grave expression.

Teacher was indeed teacher. Just the first round and he'd almost seen through his strategy. If his movement had been even slightly slower, Ninetales would have lost fighting ability under Urshifu's storm-like attacks.

Even with halved Water-type move power under bright sunlight, it probably couldn't stop Urshifu.

No matter how well he planned before battle, when actually facing Teacher Johan, it was hard to proceed exactly as envisioned.

Looking at Urshifu on the field with water still swirling around its fists, N pondered briefly before throwing his second Poké Ball.

Thud!

On the big screen, a Poké Ball next to N's portrait also showed a Pokémon's appearance.

"Please, Talonflame!"

"Contestant N's second Pokémon is the Fire and Flying-type Scorching Pokémon, Talonflame!" the host's voice was high and excited.

However, as soon as Talonflame appeared, it would face Urshifu's Surging Strikes!

"Talonflame, Tailwind! Fly up!" N urgently shouted.

Whoosh—

Blade-like blue winds appeared around Talonflame as it flapped its wings rapidly, displaying extreme speed!

Gale Wings!

[Gale Wings: When HP is full, Flying-type moves have priority +1]

This allowed Talonflame to display such fast speed.

This ability was actually quite similar to "Prankster."

Once Talonflame successfully used Tailwind, combined with its Gale Wings ability, that speed...

So after Talonflame appeared, the water swirling around Urshifu's hands disappeared as its eyes suddenly opened.

"Snarl!"

"Garruu!!"

Urshifu roared at the ascending Talonflame, rolling black sound waves that Talonflame couldn't avoid no matter how it dodged.

The resulting damage broke Talonflame's "Gale Wings" ability.

However, for N, Talonflame's "mission" was already mostly complete.

Enough.

"Talonflame, U-turn!"

"Screech—"

The screeching Talonflame rode Tailwind currents, bathed in light blue glow, struck Urshifu once, then immediately withdrew, transforming into red light and returning to N's Poké Ball.

Another Pokémon switch!

"Why keep switching Pokémon? This is almost the third one, right?"

"What do you know? Sunny Day, Tailwind—all necessary preparations are done. N's Sunny Day team setup is almost complete."

"But... Teacher Johan seems to have done no preparation?"

"Just watch. Teacher Johan is the 'Tactical Person' and N's teacher too. He surely won't lose to his student, right?"

"Let him lose! Let him lose!"

"..."

Meanwhile, in the contestant viewing area:

"This N has made quite thorough preparations," Lucian, also skilled in tactics, was qualified to comment.

"Why isn't Johan reacting much?" Caitlin blinked innocently while asking with "malicious" intent.

Steven leaned forward against the railing, smiling: "Because he's the teacher."

"Hm?" Caitlin was stunned.

Cynthia smiled and said: "As a teacher, he should first see the student's 'answer sheet.'"

"Ultimately," Lucian said with crossed legs and a book on his lap, "N's Pokémon's raw strength still has quite a gap compared to Teacher Johan's Pokémon's raw strength."

Everyone nodded.

Indeed.

Though N "owned" at least thirty to forty Pokémon that weren't particularly weak, they couldn't be called very strong either.

In terms of individual raw strength, these Pokémon might be somewhat weaker than any person present here.

Others didn't know the reason, but Johan was clear.

Because N hardly trained those Pokémon—he purely viewed Pokémon as his companions and family.

That these Pokémon could possess their current strength, Johan speculated, was perhaps because they trained themselves to help N.

This point was confirmed by Champion Alder.

"Hahaha—" Champion Alder laughed happily while somewhat smugly bragging to his old friends: "That child N, his Pokémon all voluntarily followed him. He has great talent in Pokémon affinity!"

Drake pursed his lips: "Our Hoenn falls far short—only three talented ones: Johan, Steven, and Wallace. Sigh, so worrying."

Stare!

A group of "old folks" stared at him with "resentment."

Keep bragging!

Who could brag more than you?

Granny Agatha struck her cane, face grim, voice hoarse: "Tactical literacy is acceptable..."

"Right? Hahaha, I put quite some effort into it," Alder laughed.

Among the elderly present, only Alder and Drake were most smug.

Granny Agatha glanced at him sideways, finishing her unfinished words: "But too shallow. Against that Johan boy, it's quite insufficient."

Pryce in his wheelchair smiled gently: "Little Agatha, don't be so harsh. The young man has done very well. With a few more years of growth, he'll surely stand on his own."

Alder knew Granny Agatha spoke truth, but Pryce also spoke truth. He said frankly: "Strength-wise, it's a bit lacking, but I should be able to persist a few more years."

"Today's young people are really something, lou~" Mustard put his hands in his pockets with a "Kizaru scared" expression.

About time to hand things over to the young folks.

This was the thought of several older people here (excluding Agatha).

On the field:

"You did very well, Talonflame."

After speaking to the Poké Ball, N threw his third Poké Ball.

"Then next, please, Leafeon!"

Thud!

On the big screen next to N's portrait, after Ninetales and Talonflame, the third Pokémon was Leafeon!

One of Eevee's evolutions, Grass-type Leafeon.

"Leafeon~"

Under the scorching sunlight, Leafeon's body immediately emitted a green glow, radiating a natural aura.

Chlorophyll!

Obviously Leafeon's ability was "Chlorophyll."

[Chlorophyll: Pokémon with this ability have their Speed doubled under intense sunlight!]

Combined with the Tailwind left by Talonflame, Leafeon's speed was almost quadrupled!

Under Sunny Day, Leafeon was indeed one of the very good physical attacker choices.

Insufficient strength, make up with buffs.

"N's tactics..." Johan couldn't help wanting to complain, "...why do they also give me a dirty feeling?"

Though it was also one of Sunny Day team's standard tactics, Johan vaguely sensed something.

"Come back, Urshifu."

Johan took out a Poké Ball and recalled Urshifu.

Actually, even with Leafeon's speed quadrupled, Urshifu could still fight, but...

Johan wasn't the type to stubbornly stick to opponents' tactics in battle—that might bring unnecessary harm to his Pokémon.

He was better at "borrowing" opponents' weather.

Nice tactics.

Let me borrow them!

"Your turn, Serperior!"

After Serperior evolved from Servine, this was its first appearance on the battlefield!

"Sni—"

Serperior raised its chin high, looking down condescendingly at the opposing Leafeon.

"Contestant Johan has also switched Pokémon. His second Pokémon is—Serperior!"

"Using Unova's 'sword' to cut Unova's 'officials'—my goodness, Teacher Johan has such authority!"

# 779: Master, Johan! (Two-in-one Chapter)

"Zoroark, unable to battle!"

With the referee's announcement, N smiled helplessly.

Teacher was indeed teacher.

He believed he and Zoroark had performed excellently, completely following the initial tactical arrangement and deployment.

But who could have expected that even switching moves like U-turn could be forcibly interrupted?

Perhaps... this was what teacher had said before—tactics were "basic," and unreasonableness was the real room for improvement.

N took a deep breath.

However, wasn't this exactly why he challenged teacher—to test himself and his companions?

"Thank you, Zoroark. You did very well. It was my misjudgment."

The Tailwind on the field had already disappeared.

"I'm counting on you again, Talonflame!"

"Contestant N has summoned Talonflame again!" the host shouted.

"Talonflame, Tailwind!"

As soon as Talonflame appeared, N immediately gave his command.

"Screech—!!"

But at this moment, the effect of Johan having Urshifu use Snarl earlier became apparent.

Having taken damage, Talonflame was no longer in peak condition, losing the speed boost from "Gale Wings."

Without Tailwind, without "Gale Wings," except for flight, Talonflame's previous speed advantage had completely vanished.

Moreover, Ceruledge's "Weak Armor" had already triggered twice—while its physical defense dropped 2 levels, its speed increased an exaggerated 4 levels!

This was comparable to the speed boost from Tailwind plus "Chlorophyll."

"Talonflame, quickly ascend!"

Boom!!!

The spot where it had just appeared immediately exploded violently, flames rolling up with dust.

"Screech—"

Amid the churning air currents, Talonflame "broke free" from the flames and smoke, spreading its wings toward the sky.

Wyndon Stadium's tall energy barrier did provide Flying-type Pokémon more room to maneuver.

Seeing this, N secretly sighed in relief.

Ceruledge's speed was simply too fast, too exaggerated.

Talonflame had nearly suffered a terrible blow the moment it appeared. At this speed, without Tailwind support...

It would surely be difficult to resist.

Fortunately, Talonflame still had aerial advantage.

Red feathers swayed as wings beat forcefully, creating another swirling air current on the field.

Tailwind was renewed!

Talonflame's "mission" was complete.

Considering Zoroark's earlier fate, N no longer dared let Talonflame switch using U-turn.

First, U-turn dealt pitiful damage to Ceruledge—4x resistance to Bug-type plus it not being Talonflame's STAB move meant negligible damage, and it would further trigger Ceruledge's "Weak Armor," which would be truly troublesome.

Second, U-turn being interrupted had already been demonstrated—trying again would likely yield the same result.

So... N took out a Poké Ball.

Hum—

Suddenly, the rolling flames in the field center expanded in a strange and peculiar way before rapidly contracting, as if being absorbed by something.

The billowing dust scattered under fierce air currents.

Ceruledge, originally amid flames and dust, had now changed form.

Fiery red armor, golden cannon barrel, cannon continuously condensing and compressing chilling firelight...

Golden flames flickered on its helmet, its entire body radiating scorching heat. Heat waves caused the grass on the field to visibly wither, while the blazing sun overhead added the final touch.

Johan's black hair swayed as heat waves struck his face. He waved his hand and spoke three words softly:

"Armor Cannon!"

Armarouge!

N's pupils suddenly contracted as he urgently shouted:

"Talonflame, Brave Bird!"

Talonflame flew high to avoid Ceruledge's attacks, but the negative effect was... it would take longer for a Poké Ball to recall it.

Now that Armarouge was ready, all Talonflame could do was counterattack.

Head-on collision!

It had to be said, N indeed had talent for battling.

His judgment was accurate.

It couldn't dodge.

Because whether it was Armarouge's psychic power or aura force, both had already locked onto Talonflame.

As everyone knew, all missile cannons had radar and targeting systems.

As for... using Protect for defense.

With Armarouge's current speed, blocking one shot wouldn't stop the second, and Protect's barrier might not even hold.

"Screech!!!"

Talonflame spread its wings, azure wind power swirling around it. As it beat its wings and cried, it formed a giant bird-like coat covering its entire body.

Boom!!!

The audience's gazes trembled.

From Armarouge's golden cannon barrel burst crimson-gold flames.

Its entire body, under the recoil, forcibly carved two charred furrows in the ground.

That cannonball-like flame rushed toward Talonflame at terrifying speed, with peculiar power seeming to surge within—sacred and solemn, seemingly more brilliant than the sun itself.

"Sacred Fire! That's Entei's Sacred Fire, right?"

"Entei? Who are you looking down on? That's clearly Ho-Oh's Sacred Fire!"

"Ho-Oh? Who are you looking down on? Don't you know Ho-Oh came after Fire, and Armarouge came before Ho-Oh?"

"Ho-Oh: Are you being polite?"

The crimson-gold fireball collided head-on with the light blue bird, erupting in an incredibly violent roar.

The referee's face went pale, feeling he might have set some flags earlier.

Boom!!!

The entire stadium's energy barrier continuously flashed white under the impact's aftershocks, rumbling constantly.

In the midair roar and thick smoke, a blackened Talonflame trailing black smoke fell vertically, eyes closed, having lost fighting ability.

"Talonflame!" N was about to rush onto the field when he saw—

"Goka!"

Armarouge raised its arm, psychic power enveloping and catching the falling Talonflame.

Simultaneously, a strand of crimson-gold flame entered Talonflame's body, immediately stopping its injuries and even showing slight recovery.

It delivered Talonflame to N.

N said gratefully: "Thank you."

"Goka~" Armarouge nodded.

Courage to face danger and difficulty head-on—this was a respectable opponent.

"Talonflame, unable to battle!" Only then did the referee react and announce the result.

Instantly, the entire venue erupted in cheers.

The audience looked at the big screen—N had lost three Pokémon!

Teacher Johan had given his student a 3-0 start without holding back at all.

"Perfectly normal. This is Teacher Johan—students are just babies, right? Beating babies 3-0 is Teacher Johan's standard operation."

"Actually N has performed excellently, but Teacher Johan doesn't act human and doesn't even follow basic principles."

"This is telling his student that 'quality tactics' are indeed powerful, but the essence of 'quality tactics' is breaking basic principles!"

"N's strength compared to Teacher Johan still has quite a gap."

"However, N hasn't used his enhancement yet—wonder what it'll be. Mega Evolution? Z-Move? Dynamax? Or Terastallization?"

The audience discussed among themselves.

N still received considerable recognition.

Undeniably, N was indeed quite lacking in strength.

Though tactics could sometimes compensate for strength deficiencies... tactics also followed basic principles.

Instead, the audience didn't discuss much about Ceruledge switching to Armarouge mid-battle, acting quite calm as usual.

Could only say everyone was used to it.

With one side losing three Pokémon, it meant entering halftime.

Rumble—

At the referee's signal, the field changed from grass to rock field.

The audience watched the screen, analyzing what N's next Pokémon would be.

Ninetales, Talonflame, Leafeon, and Zoroark—N still had two Pokémon that hadn't appeared.

"Though Talonflame lost fighting ability, Tailwind was successfully renewed," Wallace said.

As purely a Tailwind support, Talonflame had actually done everything it could.

Leafeon was one of them, Zoroark was side support, so theoretically there should be at least one more...

"But whether it's 'Chlorophyll' ability Grass-types or Fire-types, facing Ceruledge/Armarouge, they might not have much advantage," Steven said.

"Sunny Day is still active," Wallace looked up at the sky. "But not as intense as before."

Steven nodded. "Indeed, so next will probably be restoring weather, then the final battle."

"For restoring weather... Torkoal? Might be more suitable than Ninetales."

Generally, weather teams had two weather setters.

N had already shown one Ninetales—among the remaining two, there was probably another.

Actually, Sunny Day weather setters weren't numerous: Ninetales, Torkoal, and Charizard. Charizard needed Mega Evolution, which might not be the best choice. Torkoal had broader applicability.

Moreover, compared to Ninetales, Torkoal knew many Ground-type moves and had decent physical attack, able to target Armarouge with reduced physical defense by 3 levels (Weak Armor 2 levels, Armor Cannon 1 level).

"If he can force teacher's Armarouge to withdraw, that would be profitable," Steven also thought Wallace's judgment was sound.

Whether Armarouge or Ceruledge, the "Weak Armor" ability had already triggered twice—speed was too exaggerated. Without defeating or forcing it down, N would suffer greatly afterward.

The two spoke in turns, seemingly revealing N's next "optimal solution."

In fact, many people who understood Sunny Day team tactics judged similarly.

But N's choice was unexpected.

"Come out again, Ninetales!"

"Oowoo—"

Golden tails, graceful agile body—N's Ninetales appeared on the field again.

The previously much gentler sun became scorching again.

Sunny Day!

It was actually Ninetales?

"Contestant N has sent out Ninetales again. Wonder what method he'll use to try recovering from his disadvantage? Let's wait and see!"

N quickly provided the answer.

"Ninetales, Imprison!"

Imprison?

Hearing N's words, all spectators paused, then silently said a number in their hearts.

Six!

Though Ninetales was a Fire-type Pokémon, it could also learn many Psychic-type moves.

This Imprison directly "caught" the Fire and Psychic-type Armarouge.

Of course, real-world "Imprison" differed from games. Games only allowed four moves, while real-world Pokémon had no move limitations.

So "Imprison" naturally couldn't seal all overlapping moves—what was sealed and what wasn't, you'd have to guess.

This greatly tested opponents' understanding of Pokémon and their learnable moves.

Moves that didn't overlap with Ninetales naturally had no possibility of being sealed.

"Oowoo—"

Ninetales' eyes glowed with psychic light as Armarouge immediately felt invisible psychic power placing certain limitations on its strength.

"Armarouge," Johan half-closed his eyes and called out.

"Goka!"

Armarouge took a deep breath, rotating its armor as flames immediately rose to envelop it, then from bottom to top, blue-purple firelight gradually covered the crimson-gold flames.

Seizing this opportunity to strengthen itself.

"Ninetales, Nasty Plot!"

"Oowoo—"

Ninetales' eyes showed cunning as its aura rapidly surged.

Simultaneously, Ceruledge swung its blades to cut through flames, feet crushing the ground as it charged straight toward Ninetales.

Fire and Psychic-type moves might be sealed.

But Bitter Blade and Ghost-type moves had lower probability.

"Shadow Claw!"

"Goka!"

Ceruledge was instantly upon Ninetales, speed so fast it put tremendous pressure on N and Ninetales.

"Ninetales, Feint Attack!"

Swoosh—

Ceruledge's blade almost grazed Ninetales' body, heavily striking the ground and carving a charred, ghostly furrow.

Bang!!

At the last moment, Ceruledge stopped its sword.

Otherwise, this Feint Attack from Ninetales would have dealt significant damage.

"Now, Ninetales, Dark Pulse!"

"Oowoo!!"

Ninetales immediately condensed surging Dark-type energy, seizing Ceruledge's missed attack opportunity to strike.

"Sucker Punch!"

Ceruledge passing by Ninetales swung its sword first, striking Ninetales' weak point.

Bang!!!

Ceruledge's body made a dull sound as it retreated.

Ninetales also whimpered, rolling several times on the ground before barely stopping.

Even with a missed attack, being counterattacked by the opponent, Ceruledge could still react with its exaggerated speed.

At least... no one could have it easy.

In terms of trading damage, Ceruledge with "Sacred Fire" and Bitter Blade wasn't afraid.

But after Ninetales created distance from Ceruledge, N said again:

"Ninetales, Baton Pass!"

"Oowoo—"

Ninetales was instantly enveloped in white light, quickly returning.

Having learned from the previous U-turn lesson, this time N waited until Ninetales created distance from Ceruledge before using Baton Pass.

Even though compared to moves like U-turn and Volt Switch, Baton Pass had much higher flexibility and initiative—even pursuit couldn't chase Pokémon using Baton Pass.

Ninetales successfully returned to its Poké Ball.

"Whew—"

N lightly exhaled, completing the Poké Ball switch.

Compared to his initial restraint, N was now much more at ease.

But after truly battling Teacher Johan, N acutely felt the gap between himself and his teacher.

This gap would probably take a very long time to close...

So he was betting the real decisive factor on this one Pokémon!

"Go!"

N threw his Poké Ball, opening in midair as red light shot out, forming a moth whose entire body seemed to burn with flames.

"Volcarona!"

"Voom—"

Volcarona flapped its wings, aura surging wildly upon appearance, receiving the special attack boost from Ninetales' Nasty Plot.

Volcarona?

Seeing N summon Volcarona to receive Ninetales' Baton Pass, Johan was also stunned.

Not just him—everyone in the contestant viewing area unconsciously looked toward Champion Alder.

Speaking of Volcarona, no one could ignore Alder's existence.

At this moment, Alder's expression was somewhat complex.

Facing everyone's gazes, he struggled to calm his heart, voice slightly hoarse and low, nodding: "Yes."

A seemingly meaningless answer that actually made several veteran trainers feel complex.

"Yes? Yes what?" Flint asked with complete confusion.

"Shut up," Lucian glared at him, then quietly explained: "Champion Alder means N's Volcarona is the child of his former partner."

Former partner?

Flint was stunned again, then realized.

Champion Alder's deceased Volcarona?!

No wonder the older generation all had those expressions.

They saw the shadow of Champion Alder's former partner in N's Volcarona, which was why Alder answered that way.

Then Flint realized he might have been somewhat presumptuous.

Alder's expression softened considerably as he exhaled: "It was that child's own choice. N is qualified enough to be its trainer—he's not like I was before."

Pryce gently patted Alder's arm.

Having also lost Pokémon, Pryce could understand Alder's feelings.

"This is perhaps the meaning of inheritance," Pryce said.

"Yes," Alder regained his smile. "Hahaha—"

This Volcarona was very strong!

At least stronger than all of N's previous Pokémon!

Its strength had reached ordinary Champion main Pokémon level, and with Baton Pass boost, it could even battle ordinary Champion ace Pokémon.

This was a real threat.

After all, Johan's Ceruledge was currently between ordinary Champion main and ace Pokémon levels—still one step from Champion ace.

Champion Alder's training of N was truly unreserved... Johan sighed internally.

"Contestant N's Pokémon replacing Ninetales is—the Sun Pokémon, Volcarona!"

In the Pokémon world, there weren't many Pokémon with legends but weren't legendary Pokémon. Arcanine was one, and Volcarona was another.

Even in Unova region, Volcarona's legend was more convincing than some legendary Pokémon's legends.

Legend said that as the sun's incarnation, Volcarona could burn the earth with a gentle wing flutter. Angering Volcarona meant angering the sun, invoking the sun god's wrath!

The sun's flames...

"Goka..."

Ceruledge looked at the appearing Volcarona, feeling Volcarona's heat as if the sky had two suns.

Actually, Tyranitar would be a better choice than Ceruledge now.

After all, Tyranitar could not only override Sunny Day but Rock-type had 4x effectiveness against Volcarona.

But... Johan saw Ceruledge's eyes and its continuously ringing blades, knowing he needn't think about switching—the little one definitely wanted to continue fighting.

"Sigh..."

Johan lightly sighed.

Between respecting Pokémon's wishes and preventing injury, he ultimately chose respecting Pokémon's wishes.

Regardless, Ceruledge with "Sacred Fire" could mostly recover from any injury after sleeping.

The only insufficiency might be that Ceruledge had already fought three battles. Though it gained speed, its physical and special defense had also decreased considerably.

He lightly patted his cheek.

Since it wanted to fight, then fight!

"Ceruledge!"

"Volcarona!"

Johan and N spoke almost simultaneously, each grasping a black energy orb.

Instantly, the livestream and arena big screen split into left and right halves.

Identical movements, identical items.

Explosive!

The audience immediately became excited.

Here it comes!!

Johan and N looked at each other and threw their respective black orbs.

Terastallization!

Both unexpectedly chose Terastallization from Z-Moves, Dynamax, and Terastallization.

Moreover... even their Tera-type choices were identical!

Crack crack crack—

Under countless crystal coverings, Volcarona and Ceruledge's bodies were covered in transparent ice crystals, with Fire-type symbols appearing above their heads.

Tera Fire!

"Voom—" Volcarona flapped its wings, black spots like sunspots on its fire-red moth wings radiating terrifying heat.

"Goka!"

Ceruledge's blade trembled continuously, blue-purple flame ripples vibrating around it, with golden glints flashing in its originally blue-purple burning eyes.

Fire VS Fire!

Sun's Fire VS Sacred Fire!

The venue's air conditioning seemed to instantly collapse. Even with Dynamax barrier separation, spectators felt like entering a sauna, sweat instantly soaking their backs.

"Volcarona, Fiery Dance!"

The usually calm N, called "Mathematical Magician," was now emotionally ignited, forcefully swinging his arm and shouting.

This was a battle to prove himself to teacher!

"Voom—"

Volcarona flapped its wings as countless crystalline scale powder scattered down, instantly forming fluttering fire moths that filled the sky densely.

The entire battle field seemed completely filled with fire moths.

[Fiery Dance: Volcarona's signature move. When attacking, there's a certain probability of boosting its own special attack!]

As Volcarona flapped its wings again, the sky full of fire moths surged madly toward Ceruledge.

"Ceruledge, Fire Spin."

Though Johan didn't appear as excited as N, he was clearly energized too.

Whoosh!!

Fire Spin rose but didn't attack Volcarona—instead rapidly enveloping itself.

However, facing Volcarona with +2 special attack, even if Fire Spin could block part of it, it couldn't stop everything.

Boom!!

Firelight shot skyward.

Countless fire moths continuously surged down like moths to flame, instantly drowning everything in flames.

"Could it end like this?"

Volcarona with +2 special attack, Ceruledge with -1 special defense—though both were Fire-type, it wasn't impossible.

Under the audience's tense gaze, Johan's voice came:

"Ceruledge!"

In the roaring flames, a figure in blue-purple armor swung twin swords, cutting through flames to emerge.

"Goka!"

Anyone, any method, could defeat me.

Only... flames could not!

"Flare Blitz!"

Though Ceruledge wasn't in good condition, one thing couldn't be ignored—its speed!

Red flames and blue-purple flames seemed to form two opposing heat waves on the field.

"Volcarona, once more, Fiery Dance!"

Countless fire moths danced again as Volcarona's aura surged accordingly.

The second Fiery Dance finally triggered special attack enhancement!

But before these fire moths fell, Ceruledge with focused, serious eyes had already broken through with unstoppable momentum, reaching Volcarona.

Twin swords crossed, blades trembling, blue-purple and gold interweaving as flames collided to bloom gorgeous flowers.

Twin swords, magnificent slash!

Bang!!!

Another head-on collision.

But this time Ceruledge used speed to gain some advantage.

Volcarona's body flew back as blue-purple flames attached to its wings, difficult to remove.

"Fly up Volcarona, continue Fiery Dance!"

Volcarona seemed to overlap with the sun, countless scattered scale powder like universal sunlight with nowhere to hide.

Ceruledge was fast, but Volcarona had aerial advantage.

Even if Ceruledge switched to Armarouge using Armor Cannon, it might not one-shot Volcarona also using Tera Fire.

And Ceruledge's condition and reduced special defense might not withstand another +3 special attack Fiery Dance.

The situation was clearly more favorable to Volcarona.

"Will Teacher Johan lose his first Pokémon in the opening battle?"

"Listen to yourself—this is Master tier!"

"Master tier? Can't Master tier be baby cup?"

"..."

Johan and Ceruledge simultaneously looked up at Volcarona alongside the sun, at the fire moths formed from scattered scale powder.

All flames...

"Ceruledge," Johan's eyes flickered with blue light. "Clear Smog!"

"Goka!"

The sword flipped, picking up mud from the ground and throwing it at airborne Volcarona.

Clear Smog could eliminate all opponent stat changes!

"Dodge, Volcarona!"

"Voom—"

Volcarona reacted quickly, sidestepping to avoid Clear Smog.

Johan and Ceruledge's eyes flashed simultaneously.

Now!

"Bitter Blade!" Johan shouted loudly.

Bitter Blade?

Was Ceruledge going to fly up and hit Volcarona?

This was most people's first thought.

But soon, someone noticed—

Ceruledge lightly touched the ground with its toes.

The next second, it vanished into thin air!

Vanished?!

Where did it go?

"The, the sky! Behind Volcarona!"

Ceruledge had appeared above Volcarona, twin blades crossed with interweaving firelight.

"It's Ally Switch! Ceruledge switched places with the Clear Smog mud!"

Whoosh—

The entire venue erupted.

This was possible too?

Slash—

The blade swept across Volcarona's back, leaving deep scratches while slashing Volcarona downward.

N's expression tightened as he urgently called: "Volcarona, Overheat!"

The falling Volcarona showed extreme body toughness, forcibly twisting its form and flapping terrifying flames at Ceruledge above.

A giant Overheat instantly formed.

Johan's brow furrowed—N's Volcarona was indeed excellent.

But at this point, the more one retreated, the weaker one's momentum became, and the more likely to fall into trouble.

So Johan immediately shouted:

"Ceruledge, cut through it!"

Cut through it?

The entire venue erupted.

But for Ceruledge—

This was exactly what it wanted!

"Goka!"

Wind-cutting sword cuts wind, flame sword naturally cuts flames!

Forge ahead courageously!

This is my way of the sword!

Ceruledge faced Volcarona's Overheat, slashing out its flame-wreathed twin swords, just like countless training sessions before.

Rumble—

Sword edges drowned in surging flames.

The audience's hearts jumped.

Failed?

The next second—

Hum—

In the roaring flames, two arcs of light appeared, then became brighter and more obvious.

Even... Volcarona's surging flames seemed to be... devouring the flames?

Your flames are good—let me borrow them?

Bathed in firelight, those two arcs suddenly brightened as the massive Overheat was suddenly split into four parts!

Boom!!

Red and blue flames crashed into the field from above, accompanied by roaring while raising great clouds of dust.

The audience waited tensely for results.

Soon, the dust settled.

The field left a huge charred crater as if hit by a meteorite.

Volcarona had reverted from its Terastallized form, lying motionless in the crater covered in dust.

Outside the crater, Ceruledge lay spread-eagle on the ground, equally motionless.

This, this was... mutual defeat?

The referee immediately used equipment to check the situation and, amid surprise, announced the result:

"Volcarona, unable to battle!"

"Ceruledge... unable to battle!"

The audience immediately erupted.

"What's the difference between 'unable to battle' and 'unable to battle'?"

"Ceruledge cut through the flames, right? It could even cut through such a big Overheat?"

"'Unable to battle' means Volcarona lost consciousness, 'unable to battle' means Ceruledge still has consciousness but is too exhausted to continue fighting."

"Isn't that the same thing?"

"Too lazy to explain—Teacher Johan rocks!"

Actually, given a bit more time, with "Sacred Fire" Ceruledge could definitely stand up again.

But... enough.

Ceruledge had done enough.

It lay on the ground looking at the sun overhead, mind continuously flashing through previous scenes, slowly smiling under its mask.

The sun's flames... nothing special.

Actually, Ceruledge lying there without struggling to stand was also Johan's intent.

When Ceruledge cut through Volcarona's +3 special attack Overheat, it also cut through the shackles hindering its further progress.

Current Ceruledge, like Tinkaton, had reached Champion peak level!

In the future... legendary level wasn't impossible!

Thud! Thud!

Both Volcarona and Ceruledge's portraits dimmed simultaneously.

N had one injured Ninetales remaining, plus one Pokémon yet to appear.

Actually, that unrevealed Pokémon was indeed another weather setter as Steven and others guessed—Torkoal.

So... N announced forfeiting the remaining battles, withdrawing.

No need to make Pokémon suffer more for his persistence.

What he wanted, he had already obtained.

"Thank you all," N said silently in his heart.

Thud—

The final score was set at 6:1!

"N!!"

"N!!"

"N!!"

N received tremendous cheers.

Scoring against Teacher Johan, this N was already excellent enough!

Honorable in defeat!

Johan lightly touched his nose.

Wait... I'm the one who won, right?

"Teacher." During the handshake, N reverted to his previous somewhat shy, not-good-with-words appearance.

Johan smiled and patted his shoulder: "Excellent, N. I'm proud of you."

N scratched his head embarrassedly, showing a somewhat silly but heartfelt smile.

Having teacher say this was enough!

"Just, whose tactics did you learn? Champion Alder? Don't learn bad things—next time don't use such tactics—"

N said quietly: "I learned from watching your videos, teacher, and articles you wrote."

Johan's expression froze as he immediately coughed twice and continued:

"These tactics are... very good! Well learned, well learned, haha..."

So that's why it felt so familiar—that Pokémon switching, alternating weather setters and Tailwind support, all gave a "Pokémon battle" déjà vu.

I see...

Atop the dome, the Black and White Dragons who hadn't dared speak throughout the match looked at each other and slowly exhaled.

"Roar" (This guy is too truthful...)

Black Dragon Zekrom looked at the quick-changing Johan and couldn't help saying quietly.

White Dragon Reshiram raised its head high, full of pride, white wings lightly trembling.

Zekrom: I wasn't praising you!!

"But these tactics still have deficiencies. Let me tell you—"

"Deficiencies?" N immediately showed a humble learning attitude.

"You could totally add an 'Intimidate' ability Incineroar. One weather setter like Ninetales or Torkoal is enough. Support Talonflame, let Leafeon handle some damage output. Anyway, Volcarona doesn't particularly depend on Sunny Day. Volcarona receives Ninetales' baton for main damage output, let Zoroark and Incineroar rotate..."

"Mm! Mm!" N nodded repeatedly as if new world doors were opening.

"...I call this tactic 'Journey to the West'—cough, no, call it 'Two Tigers.'"

"Oh~~" N had an enlightened expression.

Zekrom: "..."

Why did it feel like N would go further astray on Teacher Johan's "path"?

Looking at Reshiram.

Zekrom: No, why are you still being proud?!

----------

Powerstones?

For 20 or 10 advance chapters: patreon.com/michaeltranslates
