# 784: Advancement to Eight Masters!

Entei?

The fifth Pokémon Spinel summoned was one that looked very similar to the legendary Pokémon Entei, but appeared more rugged and wild than Entei.

Its true name was Gouging Fire!

A Fire and Dragon-type Paradox Pokémon.

It resembled Entei, similar to the Walking Wake that looked like Suicune that Johan, Cynthia, and Lance had encountered at the Dragon Tamer family in Paldea region.

Besides Walking Wake that resembled Suicune and Gouging Fire that resembled Entei, there was also Raging Bolt that resembled Raikou—all ancient Paradox Pokémon.

Strictly speaking, you couldn't really say Walking Wake resembled Suicune, Gouging Fire resembled Entei, or Raging Bolt resembled Raikou.

Because they were ancient species, existing earlier than Raikou, Entei, and Suicune.

That Ho-Oh ultimately transformed the three Pokémon revived in the Brass Tower into Raikou, Entei, and Suicune was quite intriguing.

However, Gouging Fire's appearance still caused considerable commotion, with audiences thinking another legendary had entered.

When Johan saw Gouging Fire, he couldn't help raising an eyebrow.

Gouging Fire?

He wasn't very surprised to see Walking Wake at the Dragon Tamer family, but seeing Gouging Fire in Spinel's hands was somewhat shocking.

If he remembered correctly, Gouging Fire should be one of the Pokémon belonging to Lucius, one of the Explorers' founders and legendary adventurer.

They were called the "Six Heroes."

Among them were not only powerful beings like Gouging Fire that resembled Entei, but also legendary Pokémon like Galarian Articuno, and even exaggerated existences like Black Rayquaza.

They were called "Six Heroes" because they received Terapagos's power and jointly sealed the "Rakua Paradise" where "Rakua elements" were located.

Therefore, in the "Pokémon Horizons" plot, Liko and others needed to gather the power of all six "Six Heroes" Pokémon to open "Rakua Paradise."

The Explorers, who also aimed to find "Rakua elements," similarly targeted finding the "Six Heroes."

So... had the Explorers already begun capturing the "Six Heroes" and succeeded partially?

The Walking Wake at the Dragon Tamer family being influenced by "Rakua elements" wasn't entirely coincidental—was Walking Wake used as a test subject for Gouging Fire?

"Roar—!!"

Gouging Fire roared with violent fury.

Though Spinel's previously summoned Pokémon had all been somewhat affected by "Rakua elements" and gained strength enhancements, that restless, uncontrolled feeling was barely noticeable.

That's why the League couldn't detect it and Spinel could command and battle.

But after this Gouging Fire appeared, Johan clearly felt a wild, rampaging aura.

This was much more severely affected than the Walking Wake they'd seen at the Dragon Tamer family.

Was it because Spinel couldn't control Gouging Fire after capturing it, so he simply increased the dosage?

"Cha!!"

Tinkaton gripped its hammer handle with both hands, small nose wrinkling as it sensed considerable threat from Gouging Fire.

"Hammer Sister, be careful," Johan softly reminded, glancing at Spinel across from him with his iron-blue face showing some uncontrolled madness.

Some cleverness indeed, intelligence wasn't low either.

But his desire for control was too strong, calculating and planning everything. Once accidents caused things to deviate from expectations and control, he'd become crazy...

After briefly summarizing Spinel's psychology, Johan refocused on Gouging Fire.

Sunny Day was still active.

Its "Protosynthesis" ability was activated, with its already impressive physical defense receiving significant enhancement.

Fire and Dragon-type combination meant 2x weakness to Ground, Rock, and Dragon.

Dragon-type moves were ineffective against Fairy-type Tinkaton, but Fire-type moves... posed considerable threat to Tinkaton.

Especially Fire-type moves with Sunny Day boost.

"Roar!!!"

Gouging Fire roared again as surging flames enveloped its entire body, charging toward Tinkaton like a tank.

Flare Blitz!

This was a real tank.

The visual impact of the over three-meter-tall Gouging Fire charging was enough to make everyone understand this move's power.

"Tinkaton," Johan called lightly.

"Cha!"

The massive hammer spun flower-like twice in Tinkaton's hands before being heavily slammed into the ground.

Crack—

The ground immediately cracked as thick, solid earth layers rose like rock barriers.

Then the rock barriers became sharp stone spikes, piercing toward the charging Gouging Fire.

Stone Edge!

Bang! Bang! Bang—

Gouging Fire demonstrated "rampage" to its extreme, savagely smashing through all the stones Tinkaton hammered out.

At this point, Spinel seemed to have lost command over Gouging Fire, which rampaged around the field like an out-of-control tank, eyes seeing only Tinkaton.

"Cha!!"

Seeing Gouging Fire about to impact, with Stone Edge providing slight hindrance, Tinkaton suddenly hurled its hammer.

Thud!!

The hammer seemed to strike an incredibly thick metal wall, producing dull sounds while bouncing away.

However, the rampaging Gouging Fire finally stopped, flames fading as it shook its head continuously, seemingly dizzy from the impact.

"Roar..."

Tinkaton leaped high, catching the rebounding hammer.

At this moment, Gouging Fire had already recovered, looking at the airborne Tinkaton with red eyes, violent aura surging again as it erupted in scorching flames once more.

Outrage!

A move similar to Outrage, continuously attacking opponents madly before entering confusion.

"Roar!!"

Gouging Fire roared, lunging at midair Tinkaton.

In the air, Tinkaton couldn't dodge, only raising its hammer as a shield to block Gouging Fire's impact.

Bang!!

Under the terrifying impact, Tinkaton flew backward, heavily crashing to the ground.

Seeing this scene, the audience erupted in gasps.

Since Johan and "Gibeon's" battle began, this seemed... the first time "Gibeon's" Pokémon had sent Teacher Johan's Pokémon flying, the first time putting Teacher Johan at a disadvantage?

"Just... why does this feel like it has little to do with the trainer?"

"'Gibeon' didn't give commands either, and this 'Entei' doesn't seem to obey his commands..."

"Could it be freshly captured? Or captured but not completely tamed?"

Perceptive people existed—many immediately noticed this Gouging Fire didn't seem controlled by Spinel at all.

Moreover, it fought purely on beast instinct.

Powerful, ferocious.

Yet irrational, just mindlessly charging, charging, and more charging.

Should such battles really appear in "Master Tournament"?

A Master sending out incompletely tamed Pokémon—could such a trainer be called "Master"?

"Cha..."

Tinkaton grimaced as it got up from the ground.

Fire-type's 2x effectiveness, plus Gouging Fire's strength and Sunny Day boost—this hit was somewhat painful.

Fortunately, the hammer blocked most flame impact, so Tinkaton's body didn't suffer particularly severe damage.

After getting up, the little one's first action was checking its hammer.

Seeing only surface soot on the hammer without major damage, the little one visibly relaxed.

The hammer being the true body was confirmed again.

After all, even shiny Tinkaton only differed in hammer color.

Tinkaton's hammer, forged combining Sword and Shield methods and materials, couldn't be broken even by Eternatus, let alone mere Gouging Fire.

"Cha!!"

But the hammer was blackened—Tinkaton was angry. Consequences... were serious!

If this Gouging Fire fought under Spinel's command, it would indeed be troublesome.

Because Gouging Fire excelled at physical defense, specifically countering physical attackers like Tinkaton.

More importantly, Gouging Fire knew a Protect-like move called Burning Bulwark—not only defending like Protect but inflicting burn status on contact move users.

Already countering physical attackers while causing burns, plus Fire-type familiarity—too obviously countering Tinkaton.

But since Spinel couldn't command it and Gouging Fire was already in Outrage state... it was much easier to handle.

"Roar!!" Gouging Fire roared again, wreathed in rolling flames, charging at Tinkaton once more.

"Tinkaton!"

Johan's eyes sharpened as he waved solemnly:

"Gigaton Hammer!"

"Cha!"

The pink Tinkaton now showed heroic spirit, gripping the hammer handle and swinging.

Hum—

The enormous metal hammer erupted in pale pink light. As Tinkaton swung, the pale pink hammer phantom expanded visibly like Dynamax.

Except others' Dynamax enlarged everything, while Tinkaton's only enlarged the hammer.

In just a blink, Tinkaton's hammer seemed to grow dozens of times larger.

"Holy crap..."

"Is this Dynamax? Just the hammer Dynamaxing?"

"Didn't see Teacher Johan use a 'Dynamax Band'..."

"Cheating! Referee, someone's cheating here!"

"What? The opponent's also a cheater? Then never mind—the best way to deal with cheaters is sending another 'cheater.'"

The referee swallowed, dragging Aegislash in rapid retreat.

The next second, the rampaging Gouging Fire faced the incredibly massive hammer, dozens of times larger than itself.

It slammed down heavily.

Boom!!

The entire arena field erupted in massive impact as countless branches, rocks, and earth flew up.

Even spectators felt violent ground trembling.

Watching Teacher Johan's battles was truly... too thrilling!

Experiencing "4D" feelings—beyond visual and auditory impact, there was tactile sensation.

Spinel's clothes and hair fluttered in the impact's aftermath, like his current turbulent thoughts.

"Tinkaton, finishing Play Rough."

In the massive aftermath and dust, Tinkaton finally used "Hammer Sleep Technique," sending the still-struggling Gouging Fire to dreamland.

It was over...

Though Spinel still had one Pokémon left.

But seeing Gouging Fire knocked unconscious by Tinkaton, he knew... it was already over.

He hadn't expected to defeat Johan, just hadn't expected such a crushing loss.

Truly feeling the absolute power gap between himself and a Champion.

Johan shook his head slightly.

Weak.

Too weak.

Despite various cleverness, "Rakua element" enhancement, and these rarely-known Paradox Pokémon, Spinel's strength was too weak for this "Master Tournament."

Honestly, without his methods, Paradox Pokémon surprises, and extensive targeting, he shouldn't have appeared in the Master Tournament main stage.

Look at this Master Tournament's contestants—Lance, Leon, Cynthia, Steven, Pryce, Giovanni, Agatha, etc. Which wasn't someone Johan needed to treat with absolute seriousness?

Spinel was too far below this tier's strength level.

Though Johan disliked Spinel personally, having the tournament organizers arrange his battle was basically handing him the top eight?

Thinking this, Johan's mood improved considerably.

Quite considerate of him...

"I forfeit."

After internal struggle, Spinel ultimately chose to forfeit.

"6-0! Fastest Master Tournament main stage 'speedrun' record broken!"

"One-shots! One move kills, two move kills! At most three moves—Teacher Johan displayed crushing strength perfectly!"

"Witnessed history—Master Tournament main stage becoming baby cup live."

Though not very exciting...

But from Teacher Johan's perspective, it felt... quite satisfying.

Spinel clenched his fists, gritted his teeth, stared hard at Johan for a while, then turned and left.

At the last moment, he barely maintained rationality.

This "experiment" was enough, "conclusions" confirmed. Time to retreat—staying longer might prevent escape.

"Ladies and gentlemen, our 'Pokémon World Championships' first top-eight advancement is born—our Teacher Johan!"

Eight Masters!

Quite a prestigious title.

Unfortunately obtained from Spinel, lacking much sense of achievement.

Whatever—complaining about "easy" scheduling while having it was getting greedy.

Night fell.

Wyndon City, a remote warehouse.

Several Outstanding Inc. trucks departed in different directions under cover of darkness.

Hidden watchers sent people to follow the trucks.

Spinel stood by the warehouse's floor-to-ceiling window, snorting disdainfully, then walked out calmly with several capable subordinates.

Upon exiting:

"Hey, what a coincidence, Mr. 'Gibeon.'" A familiar voice came.

Hearing this voice, Spinel felt terrible, goosebumps rising.

Looking up, he saw Johan, Janine, several International Police officers, and Officer Jennys standing nearby in the darkness.

"Those..." Spinel glanced toward where trucks had departed.

Wait, weren't you afraid I'd taken any random truck or escaped another way?

Following Spinel's gaze, Johan smiled: "Oh, had dinner late tonight, so we're late. Didn't expect to meet coincidentally—what a coincidence, right?"

Joke.

Janine might be timid on the battlefield, but off it, she'd show you what ninja meant!

Playing shell games before a top assassin—you underestimated Janine too much.

Spinel: "..."

"Sigh—"

Spinel slowly exhaled, showing a calm smile: "Somewhat coincidental. But I wonder what Teacher Johan and Mr. Janine need so late?"

Johan waved dismissively: "Nothing much, just arresting you."

"Arresting me?" Spinel smiled mockingly: "Arrest warrant? What grounds? What crime have I committed?"

"Arrest warrant?"

Johan looked at Janine, then at the International Police and Officer Jennys behind him, smiling: "Don't have one."

Spinel relaxed slightly: "Without one, even League police..."

Before finishing, Johan somehow produced a small paper and pen from Janine, quickly scribbling a crooked word—"Arrest Warrant."

He showed it to the stunned Spinel.

"There, now we have one."

As Police Inspector, highest commanding authority in all Galar region, he could finally say:

My word is command!

"You..." Spinel pointed at Johan, mouth agape, momentarily speechless.

This was possible?

Johan waved.

International Police and League officers immediately stepped forward.

Janine's eyes showed dangerous light.

If Spinel and Outstanding Inc. people dared resist, she'd take them down first!

But Spinel was somewhat honest, not resisting, only shouting: "Even the League can't arrest us good citizens without evidence!"

Evidence!

Spinel's source of confidence.

"We don't need evid—" Johan's "arrogant" words stopped halfway, noticing Officer Jenny's strange looks, so he coughed lightly: "Evidence? We suspect you, so we arrest you. Evidence will come soon."

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar wearing a detective hat and holding a magnifying glass floated out of the warehouse looking professional.

When?

Seeing Gengar, Spinel paused.

But he quickly relaxed.

He believed he'd handled things cleanly—even if Gengar snuck into the warehouse, it couldn't find evidence of wrongdoing.

"Cooperating with investigation is every League citizen's duty," Spinel said, calmly accepting arrest.

He added: "However, I believe our Outstanding Inc. legal team will have us bailed out soon."

"That's nice," Johan smiled. "Bail isn't cheap."

"Advisor Johan?" Janine looked puzzledly at Johan.

Let them post bail—was Team Rocket supposed to act?

Johan gave a "stay calm" look, leading everyone to the vehicle.

In the vehicle, Spinel and Outstanding Inc. employees were very compliant.

"Teacher Johan, we... indeed lack evidence temporarily," Officer Jenny whispered.

"I'm also skilled at interrogation," Janine volunteered.

Johan smiled: "No need for such trouble."

They looked confused.

"Watch me."

After giving them a reassuring look:

"What?"

Johan suddenly spoke, meeting Spinel's confused gaze with an even more confused expression.

Spinel: "?"

"Can't you speak louder?" Johan irritably sat beside Spinel, leaning close with sudden realization: "You want to be a witness?"

Hearing this, Spinel paused, then sneered disdainfully.

Such tricks to fool children?

The Outstanding Inc. employees behind couldn't see Spinel's expression but all appeared unmoved.

Be a witness?

Mr. Spinel?

What a joke...

"So your name is Spinel~" Johan's voice echoed in the closed vehicle.

Hm?

Outstanding Inc. employees looked up in confusion.

Identity revealed already?

Spinel also stared at Johan in shock.

How did he know?

"You want to report Gibeon?" Johan crossed his legs, smiling as he "performed" alone.

"I—"

"Ka-kaah!" Gengar directly stuffed the magnifying glass handle into Spinel's mouth, glaring at him.

Watch properly! Don't interfere with my acting improvement!

"So it's called 'Explorers'..."

Explorers?

The Outstanding Inc. employees, actually Explorers members, exchanged glances.

How... how did it seem like Mr. Spinel was really saying things?

Otherwise, how would they know about Explorers?

"Oh~~ and Agate, Onyx, Coral, and others..."

Spinel: "??!"

Everyone behind: "!!"

"Researching something called 'Rakua elements'?"

!!

This time Spinel truly couldn't sit still, about to speak but paralyzed by Gengar's glare.

His eyes showed disbelief.

If Explorers, Agate and others being known by Johan could be explained by thorough League investigation, then knowing the secret "Rakua elements" research shocked him.

Even many Explorers members didn't know about "Rakua elements," except important leaders.

When "Rakua elements" came up, even if they didn't want to believe Mr. Spinel had "betrayed," they had to.

This was absolutely insider knowledge.

"Gibeon actually has a shiny Zygarde?"

"Mmph mmph!!" Spinel looked at Johan like seeing a ghost.

Impossible!

"Alright, I understand." Johan stood, patting Spinel's shoulder earnestly: "Just cooperate well, support the League, and you'll be out soon. The League won't wrong any good person. Don't worry."

After saying this, he stopped "performing."

Seeds of doubt were planted.

"Teacher Johan, you..." Officer Jenny spoke quietly as Johan returned.

Janine continued: "Making them fight internally, then interrogating subordinates?"

Both Janine and Officer Jenny were impressed.

Though both wondered how Teacher Johan knew so much information, they wisely didn't ask.

"No."

Johan casually waved, accepting coffee Gengar eagerly offered.

Learning acting still required watching Johan!

My only weakness now was acting.

"No?" Officer Jenny and Janine looked confused.

"Later, ask a few random questions, then release those small fry," Johan said.

"Re-release them?"

"They're not even executives—what could they know? Releasing them helps us pass 'information.'"

"Then..." Officer Jenny glanced at the slumped, blank-faced Spinel.

"Hold him forty-eight hours, then release too."

"Release him too?!"

"Oh right, confiscate all his Pokémon and Poké Balls, especially Gouging Fire's. Then... escort him to Paldea region, release him 'coincidentally' outside Levincia City. You can handle this, right?"

The International Police officers nodded despite confusion.

"This is to..." They didn't understand.

Johan didn't explain, just asked:

"Do you know what Spinel wants to do? Why participate in Master Tournament? What does Gibeon want? Why did Explorers and Outstanding Inc. expose themselves?"

They all shook their heads.

Don't know.

"There you go." Johan shrugged.

Janine asked: "So... Teacher Johan, do you know?"

"I don't know either."

Everyone: "..."

"But releasing him back, won't we find out? Let them fight internally first. If Gibeon has any competence, Spinel will suffer. Then... he'll truly become a whistleblower."

After fighting among themselves, many things would show their true colors, allowing them to follow the trail.

Everyone exchanged glances.

Janine finally understood why Boss Giovanni chose Teacher Johan as successor.

Not just because of strength.

But because of methods.

Reportedly, Chairman Geeta also received anonymous documents reporting Explorers and Outstanding Inc. Didn't matter if Spinel did it—just pin it on him.

Had to say, Johan hit the nail on the head—those documents were indeed sent by Spinel.

But that's another story.

Anyway, after his manipulation, even if Spinel remained trusted by Gibeon, once suspicion began, it couldn't stop.

The rest would be left to Geeta.

Regarding Explorers, he didn't know much—he'd already told Geeta everything he knew.

Next, he could finally focus on preparing for the upcoming Master Tournament.

While anticipating tomorrow's "main event" and regular feature—Lance VS Lorelei!

# 785: Eight Masters Revealed!

The next day.

The world's attention would be focused on Wyndon Stadium in Galar region for an extended period.

The massive stadium provided all contestants with a field for free expression, giving them sufficient operational space.

Johan sat in the contestant viewing area, holding little Jirachi, somewhat expectantly waiting for the upcoming matches to begin.

Watching others battle felt much more exciting than fighting himself.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar floated beside Johan, tongue rolling to sweep all the fruit from the table into its mouth, making little Jirachi, who was just reaching for grapes, puff up its small bun-like face.

[Johan~ Johan~]

Little Jirachi tugged Johan's clothes, pointing at the empty plate.

Johan smiled and pinched Jirachi's chubby cheeks, taking out another plate of berries and placing it on the table.

"Oowoo~"

Ninetales lazily sprawled on the sofa beside Johan, tail gently patting Jirachi's head before curling around a Pecha Berry and placing it before Johan.

Johan smiled and accepted the Pecha Berry. "Thank you, Ninetales."

Ninetales smiled gently, lying on the sofa with pale blue eyes quietly watching Johan.

Crunch crunch—

Victini sat on Johan's shoulder, swinging its little feet while munching small crackers, the "V" on its head flickering as it seemed to detect "victory fluctuations."

"Who do you think has better odds—Lance or Lorelei?" Johan bit into the Pecha Berry, finding it juicy, soft, sweet, and quite delicious.

Home-grown berries were indeed tasty.

The batch of berries grown on the island, with help from Calyrex's wooden sculpture, had already produced harvestable berry trees.

Berry trees differed from ordinary fruit trees.

While requiring higher soil and nutrition standards, they also fruited faster—multiple harvests per year.

"Lorelei!" Caitlin firmly expressed her view.

"Logically, Lance," Steven said calmly.

"Lorelei's chances aren't low either," Diantha added.

"Lance," Cynthia said after slight hesitation, thinking Lance had better winning odds.

However, both sides' supporters had valid reasoning.

Lance's raw strength was superior, plus he had "explosion" passive ability, but his weakness was obvious type disadvantage against Lorelei.

Moreover, Lorelei had reportedly learned techniques from Pryce, raising her strength another level.

Maintaining stable top-five "Ultra Ball Tier" ranking during points stage proved this sufficiently.

Everyone had different opinions.

Suddenly Flint realized something.

The person asking hadn't given his own view yet.

"What do you think, Teacher Johan?" Flint asked.

Everyone looked at him in unison.

Johan smiled slightly, peeling orange segments for Cynthia while stuffing one into little Jirachi's mouth:

"We'll know after they fight."

Flint pursed his lips, about to complain, when Johan continued:

"Though Lance's odds are slightly higher—after all, he's unlocked his 'final form.'"

"Final form?" Everyone was confused.

Cynthia covered her mouth, knowing what Johan meant, and nodded:

"It's starting."

On the field, Lance and Lorelei's battle had begun.

Lorelei opened with snow team Ninetales, while Lance's approach was more conventional—"Intimidate" red Gyarados.

But as they fought, everyone sensed Lance's difference.

"Rain Dance?"

Lance actually began competing for weather control with Lorelei.

"So everyone progresses quickly during Master Tournament stage..." Johan sighed.

Not only had Lance learned to combine Rain Dance, even Flint had learned Sunny Day.

Two famous "hotheads" were playing tactics now—though somewhat rough, they could sometimes produce surprising effects.

Like Lance competing with Lorelei for weather made Lorelei quite uncomfortable.

Rain Dance combined with Hurricane and Thunder.

Lance's Pokémon could indeed combine with rain.

Though Lorelei struggled with stolen weather, her Pokémon displayed extremely powerful Ice-type moves.

Without exaggeration, besides Pryce, Lorelei's Ice-type Pokémon could rank top three worldwide, possibly higher.

Both sides battled intensely.

Soon reaching the crucial stage.

"Lance has sent out his pride—Dragonite!"

The commentator was highly infectious: "But now snow rages! How will Lance and his Dragonite respond!!"

Current score was 3-4—Lance lost three Pokémon while Lorelei lost four.

If Lance's Dragonite fell here, Lorelei could even the score, but if Lorelei lost, the score would become 3-5, virtually deciding the outcome.

After all, not everyone could explode like Flint—with one weakened Infernape remaining, pulling off a one-versus-three comeback.

Lorelei was more "academic school," with excellent tactical reserves and literacy, but lacking in "explosion" aspects.

"Dragonite, Thunder Punch!"

"Lapras, Blizzard!"

Both sides completely invested in battle.

The next second, both Pokémon erupted in different brilliant lights.

"Lance chooses Terastallization!"

"While Lorelei simultaneously uses Gigantamax!!"

This was one of Master Tournament's most exciting stages—various enhancement methods appearing as both sides' Pokémon displayed abilities.

Tera Normal Dragonite VS Gigantamax Lapras!

Tera Normal removed Dragonite's greatest Ice weakness, while Lapras already excelled in HP—after Gigantamax, its stamina reached exaggerated levels.

"Dragonite, Extreme Speed!"

"Lapras, G-Max Resonance!"

[G-Max Resonance: Gigantamax Lapras's exclusive move—damages opponents while creating aurora veil, reducing incoming damage.]

"Leaving Kanto region, Lance with Terastallization and Lorelei with Gigantamax are obviously stronger."

Lance and Lorelei's Pokémon rarely had Mega Evolution options—Lance only had Gyarados, Lorelei only had Abomasnow, but Abomasnow's weaknesses were too obvious. Fine as weather setter, but somewhat wasteful as Mega main attacker.

But Gigantamax and Terastallization suited them better.

"Again Dragonite, Extreme Speed! Shatter the aurora veil!" Lance's cape fluttered, expression stern.

Lorelei gritted her teeth: "Lapras, G-Max Chi Strike!!"

"Fighting-type move?" Flint was bewildered.

Lapras had Fighting-type moves?

No way.

Whose Lapras learned Fighting moves?

"Heh, seems Lorelei also watched Lance's previous battle videos," Johan grinned mischievously.

Knowing Lance's Dragonite Terastallized Normal, she had to learn Fighting moves as backup.

"Lapras can learn Fighting moves?"

"Body Slam," Johan shrugged.

Lapras could only learn one Fighting move—Body Slam.

After Gigantamax, Fighting moves became G-Max Chi Strike, also boosting physical attack after use.

"Oh~~" Flint looked enlightened.

Others like Lucian, Steven, Wallace understood one principle:

Never casually "provoke" women, or they'll remember forever!

Rumble—

Massive dark red fists descended from swirling red clouds above, highly visually impactful.

"Dragonite, Extreme Speed!!"

Such huge fists were hard to dodge even with Dragonite's speed, so Lance chose the most direct, extreme approach... head-on collision!

If this were Johan, he'd wait until Gigantamax ended—Gigantamax and Terastallization had different duration limits.

Gigantamax Lapras with double HP...

But this approach indeed matched Lance's style.

Boom!!

Dust clouds rose across the entire field.

"Flying-type Elite Four playing Normal-type, Ice-type Elite Four playing Fighting-type..." Johan clicked his tongue.

Times were changing, hearts weren't what they used to be.

But... truly interesting!

This was real battle.

Rumble—

Amid massive roaring mixed with commentator's passionate shouts, audience cheers reached their peak.

When everything settled and the arena gradually calmed...

"Lapras, unable to battle!!"

The camera immediately focused on Lance, forehead slightly sweating, but when cameras arrived, he immediately swept his cape and showed a confident smile.

Lapras's defeat almost declared Lorelei's loss.

The final result was indeed as expected...

"Jynx, unable to battle! So the final winner is—from Johto region, Dragon Master Lance!!"

Whoosh—

Audiences immediately erupted in enthusiastic cheers, not just for Lance but also Lorelei.

Both presented a spectacular battle.

The final score was 4-6.

In Johan's view, this score actually represented Lorelei's success.

According to rumors, when Lance first challenged for Kanto League Champion, he beat Lorelei 3-6—though winning, it was pyrrhic victory preventing continuation of his championship path.

Now at 4-6, Lorelei's improvement exceeded Lance's.

"So let's congratulate the second 'Eight Masters' contestant, Dragon Master Lance!!"

"The third 'Eight Masters' advancement is born!!"

The commentator's voice remained infectious and passionate as audiences showed no aesthetic fatigue, continuously cheering.

This match perhaps had the highest attention since Master Tournament main stage began, even higher than Johan versus Spinel and Lance versus Lorelei.

Because the battling pair was...

Cynthia!

Diantha!

Two beautiful Champions from Sinnoh and Kalos regions, and they were close sisters and best friends—such battles were truly...

Too exciting!

Ultimately, Cynthia successfully defeated Diantha 2-6 to advance.

Honestly, this result was somewhat surprising for fellow Champions.

Even if Kalos region was famously laid-back and Diantha was actress before trainer...

Could only say Cynthia's strength exceeded her original counterpart considerably, while Diantha's difference wasn't particularly large, producing this score.

Of course, they weren't plastic sisters—Caitlin said nothing about Cynthia's 1-6 defeat against her.

They even went for ice cream together afterward... with Teacher Johan paying, naturally.

"About to battle are Mr. Pryce from Johto League and Mr. Steven from Hoenn region!!"

Pryce wore a gentle smile, slowly entering the arena in his wheelchair.

According to Agatha, Brandon, Drake and others, current Pryce's personality was much better than previous years and decades.

But becoming gentle didn't mean losing battle capability.

Just that after experiencing life's ups and downs, he'd grown indifferent to many things.

"Mr. Pryce, battling you is my honor," Steven wore a handsome, crisp suit, handsome face under light blue hair showing respectful smile as he bowed slightly with hand on chest.

Pryce and Steven—one specializing Ice-type, one excelling Steel-type.

At first glance, Steven seemed to have considerable type advantage.

Actually not.

As everyone knew, Water and Ice were inseparable—Pryce's Lapras, Dewgong, Cloyster and other Pokémon were actually Water and Ice-type combinations, not fearing Steel-type.

Conversely, though Steven excelled at Steel-type, his team included many Rock-types weak to Water and Ground-types weak to Ice.

So type-wise, Steven and Pryce were evenly matched.

But Steven had advantages—his invincible Mega Shiny Metagross!

Among Pryce's Pokémon, none could Mega Evolve.

Though League classified Mega Evolution, Z-Moves, Gigantamax, and Terastallization as unified "enhancement methods," in Johan's view... Mega Evolution surpassed the other three.

Only Mega Evolution could fully display bonds' power, turning impossibilities into possibilities.

New versus old, strong versus strong!

Johan barely ate any sunflower seeds this match, completely focused on the arena.

If Pryce were the Special Edition version with exaggerated strength, Steven, not yet at his peak, wouldn't be his opponent.

Of course, Johan's existence meant Steven determined his goals and direction earlier, allocating slightly more time from stone-digging to Pokémon training and battles, making him considerably stronger than his original counterpart.

Finally, Steven used tenacious resilience and deep bonds with Shiny Metagross to defeat Pryce's Delibird!

Final score—5-6!

This match was called the most spectacular battle to date.

Both sides' strength had reached Champion level in some sense.

At least... more Champion-like than certain regional Champions.

Sixteen to eight advancement required over a week even at one match per day.

Following Johan, Lance, Cynthia, Steven also successfully advanced.

This result was called the beginning of a "new era" by many!

Younger generation powerhouses were taking over the entire League!

Then the next day, in Agatha versus Brandon's battle, Granny Agatha won with eerie advantage over Brandon's three Legendary Titans, successfully advancing to "Eight Masters."

The older generation still had their strengths—"more cunning with age" perfectly described Granny Agatha.

This produced comments like:

"We old-timers aren't completely gone yet!"

"We'll give you—the League's future belongs to you, but we won't give it unless we choose to!"

Many comments supporting the older generation.

But another day later, in Giovanni versus Master Mustard's battle, the powerful Boss Giovanni surprisingly defeated Master Mustard!

This was someone who'd consecutively won "Master Tournament" championships!

Though he was old now...

But this Gym Leader Giovanni, appearing from nowhere, could even defeat legendary figures like Master Mustard?

Master Mustard didn't even reach the top eight?

So exaggerated?

This made the world recognize Giovanni's existence and learn of "Rainbow Rocket Corporation."

Had to admit, this promotional effect was much better than posting on job sites.

This "Rainbow Rocket Corporation" promotion whitewashed Team Rocket faster than even the League could stop.

Boss Giovanni's achievement made middle-aged people stand up between youth and elderly.

Wait for us!

New-old transition? Did you forget that between old and young, there's middle?!

Afterward, Geeta's difficult 5-6 victory over Champion Alder advancing to "Eight Masters" made "middle-aged people" feel their rise.

Young people wait—if the League changes hands, it should go to us!

For a time, various "confrontations" filled League websites and forums, incredibly lively.

The final match between Leon and Flint was also spectacular and blood-pumping.

Leon first used "Torrent" Inteleon to immediately defeat two of Flint's Pokémon, putting Flint at massive disadvantage.

Though Flint used Arcanine to defeat Inteleon, he was immediately swept again by Leon's Dragapult!

1-4!

With Leon leading by absolute advantage...

Flint began exploding.

Sending out Infernape in a manner that made even Johan gasp, directly sweeping three—consecutively defeating Leon's Rillaboom, Cinderace, and Rhyperior.

A Solar Beam Leon never expected.

Young Leon was nearly stunned.

But only nearly.

After recovering, Leon immediately activated his "Champion Time," sending his unreasonable Charizard to first defeat Flint's weather-setting Ninetales, then clash with Flint's "Blaze, Iron Fist" Infernape.

Finally, Leon won 4-6!

Flint's strength made Johan feel he was approaching Champion level...

Probably from following Johan through too many big scenes.

Though he lost, advancing from "Ultra Ball Tier" near 200th place to Master Tournament main stage top sixteen, Flint successfully... gained a name!

The fire-playing explosion-head was called... Flint! From Sinnoh!

Thus, the "Eight Masters" were officially determined!

Next came the intense eight-to-four competition!

# 786: Dragonite, You Can't Really Mega Evolve, Can You?!

After Leon defeated Flint, the Master Tournament's sixteen-to-eight competition came to a close.

The trainers who advanced to "Eight Masters" were: Johan, Lance, Cynthia, Steven, Agatha, Giovanni, Geeta, and Leon!

With the "Eight Masters" officially announced, audiences worldwide showed no decrease in enthusiasm—rather, it intensified further.

"Whether it's really a 'baby cup' or not, we'll know from Teacher Johan's second round!"

"What to do? I'm somewhat excited for Teacher Johan and Miss Cynthia's love-hate relationship, but also excited for the mentor-student battle with Mr. Steven..."

"Let's see how they're grouped first. What if Teacher Johan gets grouped with Elite Four Agatha, Gym Leader Giovanni, or Chairman Geeta?"

Looking at his phone, Johan made a "subway old man looking at phone" expression.

Pointing at those last few comments, he said to Gengar: "Gengar, if those 'prophecies' come true, have Porygon drag these guys out for me."

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar, wearing a detective hat and holding a magnifying glass and pipe, immediately nodded.

Mission guaranteed complete!

"Where'd the pipe come from?" Johan's eyes widened.

"Ka-ka-kaah..." Gengar's expression immediately froze.

"Hand it over. Kids can't smoke!"

Confiscating Gengar's pipe.

Gengar pouted and muttered a few words.

Kids?

I'm already several thousand years old...

But since Johan was the trainer, Gengar obediently handed over the pipe.

It didn't know how to smoke anyway.

"Let's go, Johan. Time for the draw," Lance called.

"Coming."

Johan pocketed his phone and joined the "main group," naturally taking Cynthia's hand.

Everyone was already used to this.

Initially, among their group, only Johan and Cynthia were a couple, subjecting everyone to constant PDA.

But after being fed so much dog food, they'd grown accustomed.

Steven and N were both considering when to start calling her "teacher's wife."

The group arrived at the arena and participated in the draw under organizer and staff arrangements.

Originally, Leon as Galar League Chairman should have hosted, but since Leon was also a contestant, the hosting duty fell to Professor Oak, the Pokémon world authority who'd just arrived in Galar region.

However... when Professor Oak and Elite Four Agatha met... everyone's expressions were somewhat subtle.

Fortunately, this was being broadcast worldwide, so Elite Four Agatha didn't cause trouble, proceeding with draws in advancement order.

Soon, the Eight Masters groupings were announced.

According to tournament rules, the eight-to-four stage called "Eight Masters Tournament" was divided into Groups A and B.

Only the final two who emerged victorious from Groups A and B respectively could meet in this "Pokémon World Championships" grand finale!

Based on draw results, Groups A and B were:

Group A: Johan, Geeta, Cynthia, Steven

Group B: Lance, Leon, Giovanni, Agatha

When grouping results emerged, the world immediately erupted in heated discussion.

"They really put together both 'love-hate relationship' and 'mentor-student battle'!"

"Someone drag the 'prophet' out and execute them!"

"Divine grouping!"

"Good heavens! At first glance... completely impossible to determine which two will ultimately advance!"

"Obviously—they're all the world's top trainers. It'd be weird if you could guess."

Both Group A and B looked absolutely powerful at first glance...

First, Group A had Teacher Johan whose strength was unquestionable—a contestant who could treat Master Tournament like baby cup.

If organizers hadn't established tournament rules banning legendary Pokémon, Teacher Johan probably could have won early.

You know, previous "Pokémon World Championships" had no rules prohibiting "first-tier legendaries" from competing.

This was completely added for Teacher Johan—quite prestigious.

Cynthia, as Sinnoh League's new Champion, had her "champion path" witnessed by many.

Last round defeating Kalos Champion Diantha with such a score despite both being Champions proved her strength unquestionable.

As for Steven... did handsome, rich, and invincible Steven need questioning?

Geeta was a long-established Champion and League Chairman who reached semifinals in the previous "Pokémon World Championships."

While Group A was formidable, Group B wasn't inferior.

Boss Giovanni's young achievements were unearthed, revealing he'd become Kanto League Conference Champion at a very young age.

He'd simply disappeared afterward, later becoming a Kanto Gym Leader...

But Giovanni displayed extremely strong power in this Master Tournament.

Additionally, based on his solo battle against Groudon during the "Ultra Necrozma Hoopa" incident alongside Teacher Johan... this was at least a terrifying existence capable of directly confronting "first-tier legendaries"!

Then there was Agatha.

Kanto League's enduring "face."

Without Champions appearing, Elite Four Agatha had been Kanto League's "face" for decades—exemplifying "more cunning with age," with strength rivaling Champions.

Uncertain rumors suggested Elite Four Agatha didn't become Kanto League Champion because... she was waiting for someone!

Group B also had Lance and Leon.

One was Johto League's future, once considered the future by both Kanto and Johto Leagues, and the earliest-established among younger generations.

The other was Galar League's future, called the "strongest Champion," undefeated in all Galar region competitions large and small—and this Master Tournament main stage was being held in Galar region!

With all these names together, who wouldn't be confused?

Causing heated discussion was obviously normal.

After grouping concluded came intra-group matchup draws.

This wouldn't happen on-site but would be announced online directly.

Galar region, Wyndon City.

A secluded café.

Gentle sunlight streamed in as Victini and little Jirachi played on swings in the yard, their delightful laughter involuntarily bringing smiles to anyone who heard.

Johan sat by floor-to-ceiling windows, gently sipping coffee.

"You also think we can advance into Paldea, right?"

Hearing this, Johan nearly spat out his coffee, wiping his mouth and quickly swallowing before looking at the square-faced, dignified middle-aged man in a black suit sitting across from him.

Boss Giovanni!

"Boss Giovanni, when did I ever think that?"

Giovanni casually picked up a small cookie and popped it in his mouth.

From some point on, Giovanni seemed increasingly fond of eating small cookies.

"Outstanding Inc.," Giovanni stated a name.

Johan's handling of Spinel and cooperation with Geeta couldn't escape his notice.

Johan rubbed his forehead.

Boss Giovanni, you're addicted to "eating" other companies, aren't you?

With Rose's disappearance, Leon had Macro Cosmos integrate with Galar League, allowing Rainbow Rocket to "feast" and grow considerably, capturing significant Galar market share.

Now seeing Outstanding Inc. showing similar signs...

Boss Giovanni, you're a business genius!

Why did you think about conquering the world before?

After pausing, Johan said: "I can only say... most likely..."

Giovanni nodded.

His observations reached the same conclusion.

So he'd already dispatched personnel to Paldea region for advance positioning.

"However..."

Johan gently rubbed his coffee cup rim, saying: "Paldea region differs from Galar—its situation is... more complex."

Naranja Academy's existence, its high cooperation with Paldea League, plus Outstanding Inc.'s presence—three parties entangled. Adding Giovanni with Team Rocket would undoubtedly complicate things further.

Giovanni waved dismissively, unconcerned.

"Not discussing that. You seem somewhat distracted?"

Johan smiled helplessly. Before he could speak, Giovanni continued: "Is it about your little girlfriend?"

Grouping was complete, but intra-group battles hadn't been drawn yet.

But... even if Johan and Cynthia didn't meet initially, as long as both won, they'd eventually encounter each other.

Facing little Cynthia, Johan didn't know about his current self...

"Think from her perspective—if you met, would she want to see you at full strength or holding back?"

Johan was stunned.

Recalling his and Cynthia's recent special training...

"Bystanders see clearly..." Johan murmured.

Seeing Johan's expression, Giovanni knew everything was fine. "I have more experience in this area."

"Hm?" Johan looked up. "Boss Giovanni, you've been in relationships?"

Giovanni's face immediately fell. He moved his mouth, finally saying: "How do you think Silver came about?"

"Uh, good point..."

Deadly—who could imagine legendary Team Rocket Boss Giovanni transforming into a "relationship expert"?

"Ka-kaah! Ka-kaah!"

Gengar suddenly rushed in holding a phone.

Groups A and B's battle lists were announced!

Gengar placed the phone on the table as Johan and Giovanni looked.

Group A: [Johan VS Geeta] [Cynthia VS Steven]

Group B: [Giovanni VS Agatha] [Lance VS Leon]

"Whoa."

Having figured things out, Johan's mood improved considerably. He wasn't too concerned about Group A arrangements—give it everything!

Perhaps this was his only chance at Master Tournament in this lifetime.

After all... after this tournament, after stepping down as Champion, he might retire.

So he first looked at Group B's matchups.

Seeing Boss Giovanni versus Granny Agatha, Johan couldn't help exclaiming "whoa."

Good heavens.

Giovanni VS Agatha?

Logically, the two had history—one as Kanto region's largest evil organization Team Rocket's boss, the other as Kanto League Investigation Department's commander. They'd definitely had dealings.

Whether they'd truly fought was unknown.

Now they could witness these two "legends" battle—truly...

Tsk tsk.

Master Tournament was indeed interesting.

Giovanni was quite calm.

"Agatha?"

When he was young, Agatha was already established. When they commanded subordinates, there were some conflicts.

But truly fighting... this seemed to be the first time.

However, Giovanni had absolute confidence in himself.

Even if his opponent was the famous Agatha, as long as he stood on earth, he was invincible!

Lance versus Leon was also interesting.

In the anime, Lance lost to Leon's invincible Gigantamax Charizard.

But logically... this world's Lance wasn't that foolish.

Though Leon's Pokémon configuration was indeed somewhat better, with Lance clearly disadvantaged in various type matchups.

But Pokémon battles weren't just about type advantages—otherwise Lance should have surrendered against Lorelei.

Ultimately, Lance suffered from "appearing early"—no wonder Dragon Champions were mockingly called "Dodo birds."

But Lance had also mastered decent tactics.

This battle would be extremely watchable!

"Geeta?"

Only then did Johan notice his opponent was actually Paldea League Chairman and Champion Geeta!

Speaking of which, after Cynthia, regional Champions basically no longer specialized in single types, having fewer weaknesses. Geeta was among them.

"Ka-kaah! Ka-kaah!"

Gengar pointed at the screen's "Cynthia VS Steven" battle poster, somehow producing a handkerchief and biting it with conflicted expression.

One side was "financial backer daddy," the other was an even bigger "financial backer daddy"—so conflicted about who to support...

"We're before them. If we can win, then worry about who we'll face," Johan said.

"Mega Evolution, Z-Moves, Gigantamax, Terastallization..."

In his room, Johan had Key Stone, Z-Ring, Dynamax Band, and Tera Orb placed before him.

He suddenly imagined Ash wearing all these items on his hands.

Shaking his head.

"Mega Evolution is still most comfortable, though Z-Moves are also considerable. As for Gigantamax and Terastallization..."

Johan prioritized the latter two enhancement methods lower.

But they weren't unusable.

After all, only Gengar and Tyranitar could Mega Evolve, while Z-Moves, though universal, were strongest with Ninetales' exclusive Z-Move, barely counting Gengar's "stolen" exclusive Z-Move.

Gigantamax and Terastallization were more versatile—anyone could use them.

Coach Urshifu also had G-Max form.

For Terastallization... looking at everything, the most compatible might still be Dragonite.

Tera Normal Dragonite.

"Draaa?"

Seeming to notice Johan's gaze, Dragonite, who'd been lying on the floor imagining soaring through skies, sat up and patted its chubby belly.

"Nothing~" Johan smiled and shook his head.

He hadn't changed Dragonite's Terastallization type yet—couldn't use Tera Normal Dragonite.

"Draaa~"

But Dragonite pointed at the Key Stone before Johan, gently flapping its small wings.

"Mega Evolution?"

Johan was stunned.

Something stirred in his heart.

He remembered once when Dragonite seemed to have some connection with the Key Stone...

But Mega Dragonite?

In Johan's memory, among confirmed Mega Evolutions, there was definitely no Dragonite.

To date, no Mega Stone belonging to Dragonite had been discovered.

Could it be... influenced by Rayquaza?

After all, Rayquaza could Mega Evolve, and Dragonite had obtained fragments of its orb, even learning Dragon Ascent.

Or maybe...

"This is a real world after all—this possibility isn't impossible..."

Johan grasped the Key Stone.

Injecting aura.

Hum—

"Draaa?" Dragonite flapped its small wings.

There seemed to be a real connection.

Wait!

"Dragonite, you can't really Mega Evolve, can you?!"

# 787: You're the Real Dragon of Truth, Aren't You? Rayquaza

"You're going back to Hoenn?"

Late at night.

Cynthia wore cute Jigglypuff pajamas, her golden hair cascading down as she lazily yawned while showing surprise in her eyes.

Suddenly saying he needed to return to Hoenn at this hour?

Moreover... the Master Tournament draw had already concluded, and the so-called "Eight Masters Battle" was about to begin.

If not for seeing Johan's serious expression, Cynthia would have reached out to feel his forehead.

Had something major happened?

Or some emergency situation?

Seeing little Cynthia's expression grow serious, Johan knew she'd misunderstood and smiled:

"It's nothing major, just a somewhat personal matter I want to find an answer to."

"I'll come with you."

Cynthia immediately turned to go change clothes.

Johan quickly grabbed her hand, saying: "It's really nothing major, just a hypothesis I need to verify, truly. I'm telling you so you won't worry, and to avoid those guys asking questions."

"Really?" Cynthia turned around.

"Of course."

"Can I... know what it is?" Cynthia asked somewhat hesitantly.

Johan had said it was a personal matter.

"How could there be anything you can't know." Johan pinched Cynthia's fair cheek, smiling: "It's just because it's uncertain that I didn't tell you directly."

After pausing, Johan said with slight hesitation: "I think Dragonite might be able to Mega Evolve."

"Dragonite?"

Cynthia was stunned.

With Mega Evolution's emergence, all Pokémon capable of Mega Evolution had basically been announced, including Rayquaza.

After all, Mega Evolution required Mega Stones...

That wasn't entirely correct.

Johan and his Gengar could Mega Evolve without relying on Mega Stones.

But in most cases, both Mega Stones and Key Stones were indispensable.

If Dragonite could Mega Evolve, why hadn't the League's many search teams and all the world's trainers found Dragonite's Mega Stone?

Like Lance—with Dragonite as his ace Pokémon, if it could Mega Evolve, why would he have the "Normal-type Elite Four" nickname?

"So I'm very uncertain too," Johan said.

Cynthia looked at him deeply.

From Cynthia's understanding of Johan, if he truly had no confidence, he definitely wouldn't choose this late bedtime hour to return to Hoenn.

He must have some leads to act this way.

Then Johan briefly described how he'd discovered the clues.

Actually, thinking back carefully.

From when Dragonite completely digested Rayquaza's orb fragments, from the giant rainbow meteorite's appearance, from Gengar's proficient Mega Evolution, from when Johan and Gengar could bond-evolve without Mega Stones—Dragonite had shown various reactions...

"So you're going back to Hoenn to find Rayquaza?" Cynthia understood Johan's intention from his explanation.

If Rayquaza had caused Dragonite's changes, enabling Dragonite to Mega Evolve...

That would make sense.

After all, Rayquaza had nearly beaten up the Hoenn duo over that giant rainbow meteorite.

"Going to try," Johan nodded.

What if?

Since so many Pokémon could Mega Evolve, why couldn't there be one more Dragonite?

Ash could make his Greninja bond-evolve...

"Will there be enough time?" Cynthia asked.

"Worst case... I'll ask Giratina or little Hoopa to open a portal," Johan smiled.

"Mm."

Cynthia smiled gently.

She'd forgotten Johan had this "skill."

"Then you... mm, go and come back quickly, stay safe."

"Don't worry."

Watching Johan's quickly departing figure, Cynthia's smile was gentle as she involuntarily imagined Lance's expression seeing Johan with Mega Evolved Dragonite...

That would be quite interesting.

The next day.

Hoenn region.

"Next meal, I'll definitely give you more than Dialga and Palkia!"

Johan called to Giratina, whose fierce head was poking through the massive rift opened in midair.

"Garruu~"

Giratina immediately showed satisfaction, giving Johan a "kind" smile before turning and disappearing into the "Distortion World."

It had been pulled away as labor before finishing yesterday's drama—time to hurry back and catch up!

"Phew—"

Johan exhaled lightly.

Giratina could only send him outside Hoenn, otherwise though the Hoenn duo wouldn't fight Giratina due to Johan's presence, any movement from them would be enough to startle Hoenn's people.

So to avoid unnecessary trouble, Johan didn't have Giratina open the "portal" directly within Hoenn region.

"Let's go, Dragonite, to Sootopolis City, Sky Pillar."

"Draaa~~ (ˊˋ)"

Dragonite carried Johan, happily heading toward Sky Pillar.

Hoenn, Sky Pillar.

The massive Rayquaza coiled around this pillar closest to the ozone layer, resting as it lowered its head, crimson eyes looking down at Johan.

[Master Tournament?]

Its expression became strange.

[You're not thinking of having me help you fight in the Master Tournament, are you?]

Johan spread his hands: "I'd like to, but they'd have to give you a chance to participate."

Rayquaza slightly raised its head, surging with powerful airflow, displaying its aura as "Dragon of the Sky."

Then immediately stared intensely at Johan.

[Three meals?]

"What?" Johan was confused.

[Three meals, and I'll solve those so-called organizers for you, including all your opponents!]

Johan: "..."

No way.

This realistic?

Reshiram stole your title, Rayquaza.

He waved dismissively: "Stop fooling around, let me finish talking."

Interrupting halfway was very rude, Rayquaza.

Then Johan explained about Dragonite and his hypothesis about Dragonite's Mega Evolution.

After listening, Rayquaza looked at the chubby Dragonite beside Johan.

The little one was playing with clouds from "Sky Pillar" like snowballs, rolling them round and shaping them, trying to make Rayquaza's form but only managing a line.

Dragonite felt this was wrong, coiling the line up, immediately creating... a lump.

Rayquaza: "..."

So annoying.

But considering helping Johan would owe him a meal, Rayquaza suppressed its irritation and took a deep breath.

[It can.]

"Can?" Johan's head shot up as Dragonite also looked over in surprise.

Really possible?

Rayquaza looked at Johan strangely, asking back:

[You never knew?]

"Should I know?"

[How do I—what's the human term—Mega Evolve? Do you know?]

"Of course," Johan said: "You have a special organ inside that, after being activated by that giant rainbow meteorite, lets you Mega Evolve through this organ."

[Wait, you know this much?]

Rayquaza slowly backed away, looking at Johan with strange, wary eyes.

But quickly realized that while this was secret to others, it wasn't confidential information between it and Johan.

So it coughed twice:

[Cough cough, not just Mega Evolution—my energy storage and using certain moves like Dragon Ascent all go through it.]

Dragon Ascent?

Johan looked at Dragonite.

His Dragonite had also learned Dragon Ascent from Rayquaza, though the "censored version."

Could it be...

Rayquaza nodded at Johan, indicating his thoughts were correct.

Did he think its shed scales and green orb fragments were free?

Did he think any random Dragon-type Pokémon could learn Dragon Ascent?

Even the "censored version."

[Does it really like flying?] Rayquaza asked again.

"Indeed."

Dragonite had to fly several circles daily to feel satisfied. Previously Gengar took it out for air, afraid its bad luck might encounter something unfortunate.

Now... as long as it didn't meet legendary Pokémon, at most a few tumbles wouldn't cause problems. Dragonite went wandering alone.

Rayquaza also constantly flew around the ozone layer or space...

Johan frowned.

"But if Dragonite can indeed Mega Evolve, I shouldn't have only noticed now..."

[Still missing something.]

"Missing something?"

Rayquaza pondered briefly as a broken light green orb flew from its body.

[Whatever, here you go. It's already broken anyway.]

Rayquaza's green orb!

For Dragonite?

Why didn't you bring this out earlier?

[And this.]

Rayquaza somehow produced a meteorite.

[The meteorite you asked me to find carrying "cosmic energy"—take a look.]

Cosmic energy?

Little Jirachi's power source!

This meteorite was obviously far inferior to the one Eternatus had summoned.

But... finding another meteorite carrying "cosmic energy" meant little Jirachi could maintain consciousness this way!

As long as it didn't make wishes or excessively consume its energy, the little one might truly stay conscious indefinitely.

"Thank you," Johan said sincerely.

Thanking not just for the meteorite, but also the half-broken green orb.

Rayquaza waved its claw.

[Let's get practical.]

Johan laughed helplessly.

Felt like Reshiram should really give up the "Dragon of Truth" title.

Then he called Gengar from the shadows, producing large quantities of Energy Cubes.

Rayquaza immediately nodded with satisfaction.

Understanding.

[Remember to call me when I can participate.]

After saying this, Rayquaza immediately roared, sweeping up Energy Cubes toward the ozone layer.

It preferred enjoying delicacies in the ozone layer.

Watching the hurried Rayquaza, Johan grinned.

"Such a rush?"

He hadn't even brought out his first-developed "secret seasoning" cuisine.

Oh well.

There'd be other opportunities.

Looking at Dragonite.

"Draaa~ Draaa~"

Dragonite looked eagerly at the half green orb in Johan's hand, flapping small wings and calling softly.

"Here, it's definitely yours anyway."

Johan handed the green orb fragment to Dragonite.

Dragonite didn't hesitate, directly throwing it in its mouth, teeth grinding against the orb with "crunching" sounds—obviously couldn't bite through.

This scene made Johan's eye twitch.

From some point on, his Pokémon's eating had become so simple and brutal.

Started with Gengar?

Or Tyranitar?

Gulp—

Dragonite was straightforward—if it wouldn't break, just swallow it whole.

Johan: "..."

Didn't that feel uncomfortable?

"Draaa~ (ˊˋ)"

After eating, Dragonite seemed very happy, small wings constantly flapping as its body faintly glowed light green, then...

Then nothing happened.

"How do you feel?" Johan asked.

"Draaa~" Dragonite gently patted its chubby belly, scratching its head.

Still hungry~

Johan: "..."

Why did Dragonite seem infected by Tyranitar too?

Let me try myself.

Johan took out his Key Stone, closing his eyes.

Aura dwells in the heart!

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite's wings instinctively trembled.

Soon, Johan opened his eyes with some excitement.

There really was a reaction!

Having performed Mega Evolution so many times with Gengar and Tyranitar, even bond-evolving with Gengar, Johan was extremely familiar with Mega Evolution and Key Stone reactions.

Just the reaction wasn't as strong as Gengar and Tyranitar's.

Rather... it was like Gengar during bond evolution without Mega Stones.

"With mine and Dragonite's 'shared hardships' relationship..."

"Draaa draaa~"

Dragonite flapped its wings and flew up.

Strong winds whipped around "Sky Pillar" as Johan's clothes fluttered, his expression suddenly becoming sharp.

"Dragonite—"

"Draaa!!"

Galar region, Wyndon City.

"Ladies and gentlemen, welcome to the 'Eight Masters Tournament' battle site!!"

The commentator's passionate voice echoed throughout.

"Today's battlers are Lance from Johto region and Leon from our Galar region!"

Boom!!

Leon was fighting at home plus being a popular contestant, obviously enjoying high support.

Lance, as the first famous among Johan's generation, also had considerable popularity.

Moreover, while others only had one region's support, Lance had both Kanto and Johto backing him!

Under countless expectations and attention, two handsome cape-wearing figures emerged from their respective contestant tunnels.

"Why do they love capes so much?" Flint couldn't help asking.

Cynthia didn't bother answering. Before Lucian could speak, a voice came from nearby.

"Because capes are their true bodies."

"Teacher Johan?"

Flint looked at the breathless Johan with surprise.

Didn't he go back to Hoenn?

"Back?" Cynthia, whose face showed little emotion, smiled slightly upon seeing Johan.

"Mm."

Johan sat directly beside Cynthia, making Caitlin's blonde hair bristle until Diantha's laughing comfort finally calmed her.

"Went smoothly?" Cynthia asked softly.

Johan grinned at her words.

"Heh."

He nodded.

Cynthia's eyes immediately sparkled with amazement she couldn't suppress.

Dragonite could really Mega Evolve?!

Following Johan's mischievous gaze to Lance on the field, Cynthia immediately understood his thoughts and couldn't help covering her mouth as her eyes curved with laughter.

Johan was still so mischievous.

Flint, Lucian and others exchanged glances.

What riddles were these two playing?

Why so happy?

Everyone looked confused while only this couple knew what they were discussing.

Johan was naturally thinking of giving Lance a little shock!

"This scene looks familiar," Johan couldn't help raising an eyebrow when focusing on the field battle.

Lance VS Leon.

Red Gyarados VS Charizard!

Too "familiar."

"Gyarados, Aqua Tail!" Lance swept his cape, roaring commandingly with full momentum.

"Charizard, dodge!"

Leon showed no weakness, calmly commanding with a wave.

"Don't think such attacks can hit us, Senpai Lance," Leon smiled.

Leon still respected Lance greatly.

When he left Galar to learn and battle with other regions' trainers under Rose's support, his first stop was Kanto region where Lance had helped the directionally-challenged him considerably.

Though calling him "senpai," appearance-wise... Leon obviously looked more mature than Lance.

Lance smiled: "Whether it hits doesn't affect our tactics!"

Hm?

Leon was confused, then quickly realized and hurriedly looked skyward.

He saw...

Though Charizard avoided red Gyarados's exaggerated Aqua Tail, the water carried upward... formed torrential rainfall.

"Rain Dance!!"

Splash splash—

Torrential rain poured down.

Rainy weather!

"Gyarados, Thunder!"

"Come back, Charizard!"

Leon's Poké Ball's red light timely recalled Charizard as massive lightning struck the ground, immediately blasting a charred crater.

"Tsk."

Johan clicked his tongue.

This was the Lance versus Leon battle he wanted to see...

Gyarados VS Charizard—Leon's Charizard was strong, his ace, but was Lance's Gyarados weak?

Even if Gyarados wasn't Lance's ace, its strength was definitely formidable.

Leon seemed at a disadvantage.

But he quickly sent out a Pokémon to turn the tide.

"You're up, Swampert!"

"Swift Swim" Swampert, Water and Ground-type, gained even more benefits in rain than Gyarados!

Leon's expression grew serious: "I have plenty of experience dealing with weather tactics!"

Raihan in the audience curled his lips.

Yes, you have experience—always picking on my weather. Have the guts to bully Teacher Johan!

"Come back, Gyarados!"

Lance also decisively recalled Gyarados, sending out...

Kingdra!

Also with "Swift Swim" ability.

"For these two to determine a winner really isn't simple..." Johan sighed softly.

Young Lance versus even younger Leon...

"Next match, it's our turn again..."
