# 788: Eight Masters Battle! Johan VS Geeta!

"So exciting..."

Johan glanced at the big screen displaying the score for Lance VS Leon.

5-5!

The entire battle had been in a heated state, and now both sides had fought down to their last Pokémon.

Lance's Dragonite and Leon's Charizard continuously clashed in the arena's sky.

"Charizard, show your true form!!"

Leon suddenly threw off his cape, showing off his muscular physique as the Dynamax Band on his wrist erupted in brilliant light.

"Now is our—Champion Time!"

Rumble—

As if with its own BGM, Leon's Charizard's body rapidly expanded at a visible rate.

An enormously massive Charizard immediately appeared on the field.

Gigantamax!

"Dragonite!"

Lance on the opposite side wasn't to be outdone, also activating his Dynamax Band.

Against Gigantamax Charizard, Terastallization would actually be decent, but Tera Normal's effect would be rather limited, so Lance also chose to respond with Gigantamax.

For a moment, two incredibly massive Pokémon appeared under Wyndon Stadium's towering dome.

This scene... was like two giant monsters battling in the Ultraman universe.

Deafening cheers erupted in the arena as even Johan and the other contestants couldn't help showing serious expressions, focusing their attention.

Lance fought this match very seriously, his choices weren't wrong, and he wasn't holding back.

Leon had also made sufficient preparations against Lance, doing considerable advance planning.

Compared to trainers specializing in one or few types, trainers like Leon, Johan, and Cynthia who covered most attributes were harder to target.

"Charizard, G-Max Wildfire!!" Leon shouted with a punch.

In terms of momentum and emotion, Lance was not only not weaker than Leon but even more intense.

"Dragonite, Max Geyser!!"

Boom!!

Terrifying flames and surging water collided in the arena, creating vast clouds of steam that obscured vision.

The impact's aftershocks made the arena's Gigantamax barrier continuously flicker.

"Final Dragonite, Max Lightning!!"

Rumble—

Thunder struck down.

"Dragonite's advantage is still in its wide coverage..." Johan crossed his arms, speaking seriously.

Lance's Dragonite and Leon's Charizard were actually very close in strength.

But Dragonite's advantage mainly lay in its ability to learn many moves of various types, with extremely exaggerated coverage.

Combined with Gigantamax's move enhancements.

Max Geyser brought rain, then thunder struck in the rain...

"Draaa!!!"

On the field, Dragonite, now returned to normal size and covered in injuries, flapped its wings and roared excitedly.

After brief silence—

Whoosh!!!

The massive arena completely erupted.

"The first contestant to advance to the semifinals is born!!"

The commentator's voice was hoarse but excited: "He is from Johto League, Champion Lance!!"

"Let's also give our biggest cheers to contestant Leon and both contestants for presenting us with this spectacular battle!"

Leon lost his "home field battle," but no audience member or Galar region spectator felt Leon's performance was inadequate or wrong.

On the contrary, precisely because Leon was excellent enough, he could present such a spectacular battle together with Lance.

For Leon, though his "undefeated legend" was broken... this might actually be a good thing.

After all, carrying the "undefeated" title created pressure for anyone—losing to a strong opponent like Lance could actually relieve that pressure.

Moreover, Leon had recently spent too much time handling large and small Galar League affairs due to Rose's disappearance.

The two walked to the field's center and shook hands.

Regardless of others' views, both had fought enjoyably and considered each other strong and excellent.

"Well fought, Lance-senpai," Leon said.

"Just lucky, just lucky," Lance replied modestly.

The two looked at each other with mutual appreciation.

"In that case..." Leon smiled: "Teacher Johan, I'll leave him to you, Lance-senpai."

Johan!

Lance smiled confidently: "Definitely!"

"Achoo—"

Johan in the audience sneezed, rubbing his nose and looking around suspiciously.

Felt like some "troublemaker" was plotting against him.

"Caught a cold?" Cynthia asked.

"No." Johan shook his head. "Just have a chill down my spine feeling."

There wasn't much time to congratulate Lance on successfully advancing to the Master Tournament semifinals, because after Lance and Leon's match ended, the next day would bring Teacher Johan versus Chairman Geeta's battle!

Another Champion VS Champion!

With Lance and Leon's exciting 6-5 battle, audience expectations for this match were undoubtedly maxed out.

The next day.

Galar Wyndon Stadium.

Leon, having finished his match, had reclaimed his identity as Galar League Chairman.

Today he sat in the arena as Galar League Champion and Chairman, commentating today's match alongside the previously passionate commentator.

Rarely, he wasn't lost or late.

"It's Teacher Johan's match!"

In the audience, Team Rocket trio working as vendors to earn side income appeared very excited.

How could they miss such a grand Master Tournament event?

"I wonder if the note we slipped under Teacher Johan's door this morning was seen..." James worried somewhat.

That was information about Chairman Geeta's Pokémon for this match that they'd worked hard to gather.

If Teacher Johan could get it, it would definitely provide considerable help.

"He'll definitely see it—Teacher Johan's observation is so sharp," Jessie said confidently.

"Then we've definitely made a great contribution!"

"Promotion! Raise! Meow!"

"Sonansu~! ∠(°ゝ°)"

And that little note they mentioned...

"What is all this scribbling? So crooked..."

In the contestant tunnel, Johan squinted hard but couldn't understand what was on the note.

"Whatever."

The trio's educational level needed improvement...

He should suggest Boss Giovanni open an "executive training class" sometime.

Team Rocket's elites were fine in battle, but their cultural abilities were questionable.

This wouldn't do.

Now they were developing toward a legitimate enterprise—executives, no, managers couldn't just know a few characters. They needed strengthening.

He'd recruit the trio then, training them as reserve executives.

As for the information about Geeta's Pokémon they provided...

Johan indicated he really didn't need it.

First, through recent matches, he had some understanding of Chairman Geeta's Pokémon and tactics.

Second... whether in anime or games, Chairman Geeta was famously a "quality person."

First, Pokémon like Glimmora were very "quality."

The game deliberately put Geeta's Glimmora last as the finale Pokémon—if placed first, who knows how many would have failed.

Not just Geeta.

Many trainers throughout Paldea region had quite "quality" tactics, especially in "Pokémon Horizons" where any veteran trainer with some age had completely "quality" tactics.

Highlighting one thing:

If I don't play "quality" on others, others will play "quality" on me, so let's all be "quality" together.

Johan had this feeling watching the original work, thinking the writers might be game players with some "quality" tendencies.

Coming to the Pokémon world and visiting Paldea region, Johan finally understood.

This had a great relationship with Naranja Academy, which had existed in Paldea region for over 800 years.

Academic schools taught tactics, which naturally had some...

Cough cough.

However, regarding "quality," Johan really wasn't afraid of anyone.

At this moment, the countdown on the arena's big screen ended as the host immediately shouted loudly:

"Ladies and gentlemen, welcome once again to the Eight Masters Tournament arena!"

"The contestants about to compete are—"

The entire venue immediately quieted as the host deliberately dragged out his voice.

"From Hoenn region—Teacher Johan!"

"And from Paldea region—Champion Geeta!!"

Boom!!

The entire venue's cheers immediately exploded.

Under spotlights, Johan in black clothing and Geeta adjusting her black gloves emerged from opposite contestant tunnels.

The host's voice nearly drowned under tens of thousands of spectators' cheers.

"Chairman Geeta and Teacher Johan..."

Gender-"tentative" Rika leaned against the railing, watching the two entering with blazing eyes.

"Rika, who do you think will win?" Poppy, brought to Galar region by Rika during Pokémon School break, stood on a brought stool asking with interest.

"Mm..." Rika pondered.

Poppy answered directly without hesitation: "I think it's Teacher Johan."

"Oh?" Rika raised an eyebrow, somewhat surprised.

Though Poppy was now Teacher Johan's student, as a girl under ten, she spoke very directly and simply—she said what she thought.

If another child said this it would be one thing, but Poppy was an Elite Four!

"Hehe..."

Poppy took a lollipop from little Jirachi nearby, immediately beaming with joy.

Rika: "..."

Looking skyward.

She suddenly felt that sending Poppy to Hoenn region, to Teacher Johan, might not... entirely be a good thing.

On another side of the audience.

"I heard Chairman Geeta is a poison expert," Lance, having secured a semifinals spot, said. He could relax somewhat now.

"Poison..."

Behind them, Janine appeared from wall corner shadows, startling everyone.

When had she hidden there?

Janine remained unmoved, only saying flatly: "Regarding poison use, Teacher Johan won't be inferior."

Among those present, except for silent Granny Agatha, Janine was the foremost poison user, naturally having some authority.

But thinking carefully... it was really true!

Wasn't Teacher Johan's start precisely with poison, or rather Gengar's poison?

That would be interesting.

On the field, Johan and Geeta took their positions.

Geeta smiled: "Teacher Johan, regardless, thank you for your help."

Others heard this confusedly—thanks for what?

But Johan and some informed people knew Geeta was thanking Teacher Johan for his help to Paldea League.

And not just once.

The four Treasures of Ruin, the Dragon Tamer family and Elite Four Hassel's matter, Outstanding Inc. and Spinel's affair, Rakua elements...

The help was too great.

Johan smiled slightly.

"Then Chairman Geeta should go easy on me."

Publicly asking an opponent to go easy!

Though tomorrow's headlines might become "Shocking! Famous Teacher Johan Publicly Asks Opponent to Go Easy in Master Tournament," even the live audience didn't take it seriously, just smiling knowingly.

Just friendly banter, very normal.

Little did they know... Johan was seriously asking Geeta to go easy—they were so familiar, couldn't they avoid such tiring fights?

Geeta naturally didn't take it seriously either, only saying: "Just don't give me a clean sweep, Teacher Johan."

Spinel in prison: &%¥#%!

This was normal opening pleasantries.

Seeing the timing was right, the referee immediately signaled:

"Please prepare, both contestants!"

Time to battle!

Seeing both become serious, the referee nodded.

"Now, please summon your Pokémon!"

Geeta clicked the button on her Poké Ball, which quickly returned to normal size as she looked at Johan with serious, stern eyes.

Throwing it out.

"Go!" Geeta threw out an Ultra Ball, voice solemn: "Glimmora!"

"Fuuu—"

Like a flower bud yet resembling an alien UFO, Glimmora appeared floating on the field, its peculiar golden eyes seemingly emanating chilling light.

Simultaneously, Johan's Poké Ball was already thrown to the field's center.

"I'm counting on you, Indeedee!"

The gentle-mannered, warmly smiling Indeedee sister clasped her hands to her chest, smiling as she bowed to Geeta and Glimmora.

"Orangu~"

Sorry...

Apologizing in advance since there might not be a chance later.

When viewers saw Johan's first Pokémon was actually Indeedee, live stream audiences couldn't help trembling.

It's her!

The "Smiling Demon" Indeedee stationed at Hoenn region's "sixth Battle Frontier facility" café!

"Oh my god! Indeedee! All the bad memories came flooding back."

"Looks like the gentleman above also challenged the café? Brave!"

For those who'd witnessed Indeedee sister's strength, everyone knew her power.

But for those who'd never seen Indeedee sister or didn't even know Teacher Johan had an Indeedee, they expressed confusion with continuous question marks.

"Indeedee? Is this Teacher Johan's newly caught baby?"

"Looks so gentle—can it really battle?"

"Aren't Indeedee best at caring for people? I've seen Nurse Joy's Indeedee—is having it battle appropriate?"

Those "in the know" couldn't help sneering at these "ignorant" comments.

"Can it battle? Please take back that question word."

"Ridiculous—is the 'Smiling Demon' title joking with you?"

"Nothing else to say, but you could have an Elite Four's Pokémon fight Indeedee sister one-on-one without commands, and who wins would be hard to say."

Hearing these comments, the "ignorant" ones were all surprised.

"Is, is it this strong?"

"This strong? Only stronger than you imagine!"

Stronger?

Exactly.

Though Indeedee sister hadn't traveled with Johan, her training tasks were never neglected.

Not traveling with Johan was partly because she had to watch the café, island, and island Pokémon, and partly because there was someone at the café who was her best learning target.

Mewtwo!

She was always ready!

Though Johan didn't bring Indeedee sister, that didn't mean he didn't care about her—he called home almost daily to chat and inquire about conditions, understanding Indeedee sister's situation.

Of course, most importantly, to check if Mewtwo had blown up their home.

Regarding battle attitude... Indeedee sister wouldn't lose to any of Johan's Pokémon!

"Fuuu?"

Glimmora turned to Geeta.

So gentle—do we really have to fight such an opponent?

Even Glimmora was "conquered" by Indeedee sister's gentle appearance.

But Geeta remained serious, saying: "Be careful, don't be fooled by its appearance. I've researched Teacher Johan's Indeedee somewhat."

"Fuuu."

Hearing this, Glimmora nodded.

Since Geeta said so, she should be serious.

Seeing both had summoned Pokémon, the referee nodded slightly then immediately dropped his flags.

"Battle begin!"

After speaking, he ran backward with Aegislash.

As the referee's voice fell, Geeta's eyes sharpened.

Her opening with ace Glimmora showed absolute recognition of Teacher Johan's strength, so she absolutely wouldn't be careless seeing Indeedee.

"Glimmora, Stealth Rock!"

"Fuuu—"

Glimmora's flower-like body immediately spread as countless purple dust scattered down. Sharp, angular rocks of various sizes immediately appeared from thin air on the field.

Some on the ground, some floating in air—any Pokémon carelessly touching or hitting them would inevitably suffer considerable damage.

These rocks immediately became invisible.

Entry hazard tactics!

Johan was too familiar with this tactic.

Steven and Lance were also familiar.

Back then, Johan had commanded Steven's Skarmory and Lance's newly caught Aerodactyl in a match with not only entry hazard tactics but also Iron Defense + Body Slam tactics, leaving both with very deep memories.

Opposite them, from Indeedee sister's feet immediately spread a field filled with peculiar power, instantly covering the battlefield.

Ability: Psychic Surge!

Psychic Terrain!

Johan smiled and raised his hand.

The best way to deal with entry hazard tactics was... aggressive assault!

"Indeedee, Expanding Force!"

Hum—

Geeta's Glimmora suddenly felt a chill behind it, then psychic power was piled on it from nowhere.

The next second—

Boom!!

Massive rumbling immediately exploded around Glimmora as terrifying psychic pressure caused Glimmora and the ground beneath it to directly collapse.

At this moment, the entire venue fell silent.

This... was this caused by the same Indeedee who'd been smiling so gently just now?

# 789: Go Ahead and Roar, Keep Roaring

Boom!!

The collapsing ground and exaggerated commotion caused the entire venue to fall silent.

Some who previously didn't believe Indeedee was the "Smiling Demon" couldn't help but swallow hard, hating themselves for their shallow understanding and wanting to slap themselves twice.

This psychic power's explosive force...

If they went up there, they'd be instantly reduced to dust, right?

"Hoenn Battle Frontier's Sixth Facility"?

Not going there even if killed!

This was also why Johan's café had fewer and fewer challengers nowadays.

Challenge?

Wasn't that purely delivering food to Indeedee?

However, Sister Indeedee's explosive power was indeed very strong. Under the enhancement of Psychic Terrain, Expanding Force also burst forth with tremendous power.

Moreover, after training and coaching from Mewtwo, Sister Indeedee's original weakness of insufficient control over her powerful psychic abilities had been greatly compensated, completely unleashing her own power.

But even so, her opponent was ultimately a Champion's ace Pokémon!

"Glow!"

From within the crater, Glimmora suddenly floated up, the air currents it stirred scattering the dust. Its flower-like body fully expanded, displaying a bone-chilling aura.

Even with a double weakness to Psychic-type moves.

Glimmora couldn't possibly be one-shot by Sister Indeedee.

"Glimmora, Toxic Spikes!"

Nemona was also surprised by Sister Indeedee's psychic explosive power.

But Glimmora wasn't a Pokémon known for frontal assault, and frontal combat wasn't Nemona's tactical arrangement or style either.

So even after being combo'd by Sister Indeedee, Glimmora still followed Nemona's instructions, fluttering the petals behind it and scattering starlike purple dust that transformed into particles falling down.

These particles immediately became toxic spikes covered in sharp barbs, spread across the entire field. Any opposing Pokémon that stepped on them would suffer poisoning from the toxic spikes.

Hazard-setting tactics, complete!

Boom!!

However, just as Glimmora finished scattering the Toxic Spikes, another powerful psychic force immediately pressed down on it.

The already collapsed crater, along with Glimmora's fall, collapsed again, erupting with considerable rumbling.

"So violent..."

"Who cares about your fancy tricks and gibberish, eat my Expanding Force!"

"Although President Nemona's Glimmora is very strong, possibly stronger than Teacher Johan's Indeedee, if it keeps taking hits like this, won't President Nemona's ace be defeated right after setting up tactics?"

Nemona frowned.

Just as some audience members said.

The psychic power unleashed by Indeedee was indeed too powerful. Even with Glimmora's obvious strength advantage, continuing like this...

It wouldn't be sustainable.

So.

Although in Nemona's original plan, Glimmora needed to scatter Toxic Spikes twice, or endure one attack from the opponent to trigger its "Toxic Debris" ability and scatter a second layer of Toxic Spikes, this plan had to be temporarily shelved.

"Toxic Debris" was an exclusive ability of Glimmora's species. When hit by physical attacks, it would scatter Toxic Spikes, very practical when combined with hazard-setting tactics.

Among Johan's Pokémon, Tinkaton, Dragonite, Urshifu, Ceruledge, and Tyranitar were all relatively typical Pokémon skilled in physical attacks.

Among them, only Steel-type Tinkaton and Water-type Urshifu posed relatively greater threats to Glimmora, but enduring one attack was completely acceptable.

As for Glimmora having a quadruple weakness to Ground-type, if Tyranitar came with an Earthquake to one-shot Glimmora, with Glimmora's own levitation ability, it could minimize damage from Earthquake, so it was also bearable.

Special attackers like Ice Ninetales and Serperior would have difficulty gaining much type advantage against Glimmora, also within acceptable range.

Especially since Nemona had learned that Teacher Johan's Ice Ninetales, while powerful, often focused on defense during battle with offense as secondary, allowing her to make judgments based on circumstances.

But she never expected.

Teacher Johan's first Pokémon would actually be an Indeedee that had never appeared before!

Did Teacher Johan even have an Indeedee?

Had it appeared in previous matches?

Nemona didn't know.

Just like Johan himself said:

Research me?

What's there to research? Baby Cup battle videos?

With Indeedee's appearance, Psychic Terrain combined with Expanding Force, plus Indeedee's exaggerated and powerful psychic abilities, the fact that Glimmora could withstand two Expanding Force attacks and still maintain some condition was already quite excellent.

No matter how much stronger it was, it couldn't withstand such punishment.

"President Nemona's Glimmora can't scatter a second layer of Toxic Spikes."

From the contestant viewing area, Steven couldn't help saying.

"More accurately, it can't afford to scatter more." N also rarely offered his opinion.

Flint looked at Steven, then at N.

These two students of Teacher Johan...

They understood these tactics so well?

Seeming to notice Flint's gaze, Steven smiled: "Teacher mentioned it to me once before - there's a fundamental difference between one layer of Toxic Spikes and two layers."

One layer of Toxic Spikes meant opposing Pokémon would just be poisoned when they entered.

But if there were two layers of Toxic Spikes, opposing Pokémon would be badly poisoned when they entered!

Although Steven wasn't afraid of Toxic Spikes tactics, it didn't prevent him from understanding and learning these strategies.

Flint gave a thumbs up.

All he could say was...

Worthy of being Teacher Johan's student.

"Scattering another layer isn't impossible, but if that happens, President Nemona's Glimmora's condition would make it difficult to return to battle later." N said again.

Everyone agreed with his words.

Indeed.

Taking another Expanding Force hit, Glimmora might still not lose its ability to battle, but...

If the "hazards" were cleared afterward, wouldn't this tactic become useless?

Just as N finished speaking, Nemona's voice came from the field.

"Come back, Glimmora."

She indeed took out a Premier Ball and recalled Glimmora.

The sudden appearance of Indeedee had caught her off guard.

Moreover.

It was well known that even in battle, Indeedee was more suited to serve as a support role in doubles, and Nemona was familiar with moves like Follow Me and Helping Hand.

But such an aggressive, violent Indeedee was something Nemona was seeing for the first time.

"Love~"

Sister Indeedee bowed slightly toward the returning Glimmora, expressing apology once again.

"She's really too polite and gentle, I'm crying."

"Gentle is gentle, but she doesn't hold back at all when attacking."

"However, President Nemona's tactics could be considered successful, right?"

Yes.

Although it wasn't two layers of Toxic Spikes.

But Stealth Rock and Toxic Spikes were enough to give Teacher Johan's next switching Pokémon quite a hard time.

Nemona's black gloves complemented the black Premier Ball as she immediately summoned her second Pokémon.

"President Nemona's second Pokémon is—the pseudo-legendary Pokémon, Dragapult!!" the commentator shouted with some fervor.

Their Galar region's pseudo-legendary Pokémon, the "Little Hades Dragon" Dragapult!

"Pult—"

Dragapult's body seemed to exist between reality and illusion, with the front half visible while the back half appeared to be invisible.

Ghost-type Dragapult had a type advantage against Indeedee.

The battle continued!

"Dragapult, Dragon Dance!" Nemona called out in a low voice.

"Pult!!"

Floating in mid-air, Dragapult began dancing its body, rippling with a peculiar rhythm as its momentum rapidly climbed.

Johan chuckled, "Indeedee, Disarming Voice!"

"Love—"

Sister Indeedee immediately emitted a gentle but highly penetrating sound, sweeping toward Dragapult.

[Disarming Voice: When the opponent's abilities are enhanced, attack the opponent and inflict 'Confusion' status!]

But Nemona clearly anticipated such a move and immediately waved her hand, shouting:

"Dragapult, Clangorous Soul!"

"Pult!!"

Flying through the sky, Dragapult continued dancing its body to build momentum while its internal membranes vibrated, producing inspiring drum sounds.

The drum sounds collided with Indeedee's gentle voice in terms of sound waves on the field.

Sound waves clashing with sound waves immediately exploded in the arena.

Dragon Dance could be used together with Clangorous Soul?

The audience exclaimed in surprise.

Even Lance, called the "Dragon Master," couldn't help showing surprise.

From this move alone, it wasn't hard to see that President Nemona had considerable experience in training and raising Dragon-type Pokémon.

Johan was also somewhat surprised.

It seemed President Nemona had been communicating with Dragon Tamer families quite a bit...

Boom!!

Sound waves collided with sound waves.

"Dragapult!"

"Pult!!"

From the dust kicked up by the sound wave collision, Dragapult, having completed its Dragon Dance, rushed through the sound waves at extreme speed, charging straight toward Sister Indeedee.

However.

Not only did Dragapult take action, Sister Indeedee did the same.

When Dragapult was about to reach Sister Indeedee, her body also emanated an aura wave similar to Dragapult's earlier momentum.

Copycat!

Through self-suggestion, gaining the same ability enhancement as the opponent.

"Dragapult, Dragon Tail!"

Nemona shouted again.

Dragapult's invisible tail appeared for the first time, wrapped in thick blue-purple Dragon-type energy.

Encased in blue-purple Dragon-type energy, the thick tail swatted toward Indeedee.

But just as Dragon Tail was about to contact Indeedee, she transformed into a white light and automatically returned to the Poké Ball in Johan's hand.

Baton Pass!

Another key component of hazard-setting tactics: forcibly making the opponent switch Pokémon.

So when seeing Dragapult, and Nemona unhesitatingly having it use Dragon Dance to enhance speed and improve agility, Johan had already anticipated this move.

Setting aside other tactics.

Johan really understood hazard-setting tactics too well; he had used them plenty before.

So.

Rather than wait for Dragapult's Dragon Tail to force a switch, it was better to be proactive and still gain the same enhancement as Dragapult's Dragon Dance.

Seeing this, Nemona's eyes narrowed.

She felt... Teacher Johan seemed to really understand hazard-setting tactics, or more accurately, seemed very familiar with her personal tactical style and combat habits.

Not only Nemona herself felt this way, but also some discerning audience members with decent strength.

"Teacher Johan really understands these 'stall' tactics too well!"

"Otherwise, why do you think Teacher Johan has the title 'Master of Stall'? Is that title for nothing?"

"But regardless, Toxic Spikes and Stealth Rock are both set. Even the Pokémon coming in with Baton Pass will suffer from these two traps."

On the field.

Johan said to Sister Indeedee: "Sister, you've worked hard. Rest for a bit."

The Poké Ball gently swayed.

Being able to participate in battle, Sister Indeedee was already very satisfied. Switching according to tactics and strategy was fine with her, of course she wouldn't have any objections.

Feeling Sister Indeedee's joy and happiness, Johan smiled slightly.

Going back to Hoenn and bringing Sister Indeedee to participate in the competition, letting her have some fun, was obviously the absolutely correct choice.

After putting away Sister Indeedee's Poké Ball, Johan quickly took out a second one.

He threw it out.

"Go, Tinkaton!"

Rainbow light flashed.

Tinkaton, carrying her hammer, appeared on the field.

More accurately, standing on her hammer.

Ding ding—

Upon entering, the spike-like Toxic Spikes under her hammer immediately made harsh, teeth-grinding scraping sounds.

"Cha?"

Tinkaton jumped down from her hammer, looking down at her feet to find all those Toxic Spikes had been flattened.

She scratched her head.

What the heck?

As a Steel-type Pokémon, Tinkaton wasn't afraid of these Toxic Spikes on the ground.

Moreover.

The Stealth Rocks scattered around also didn't deal very noticeable damage to Tinkaton due to her double resistance to Rock-type.

Of course.

If Tinkaton wanted to move flexibly and quickly, the Stealth Rocks floating in mid-air and these Toxic Spikes on the ground would still have considerable impact on her.

However, most importantly...

Dragapult's Dragon Tail couldn't deal much damage to Tinkaton as a Fairy-type Pokémon, so the forced switching effect attached to Dragon Tail naturally couldn't work either.

Additionally.

Tinkaton inherited Sister Indeedee's baton, immediately gaining Dragon Dance's enhancement upon entry, with both physical attack and speed boosted considerably!

Perfect counter, basically.

But Dragapult didn't only have Dragon-type moves.

"Dragapult, Will-O-Wisp!"

Puff puff puff—

One by one, ghostly blue flames formed around Dragapult, taking advantage of the Pokémon switching gap to launch the first attack.

"Cha!"

Bang—

"Cha!"

Ow!

Tinkaton instinctively tried to dodge but directly smacked her head into an invisible rock nearby. As the rock crumbled, Tinkaton quickly covered her head.

It hurt!

Johan's eye twitched as he shouted: "Tinkaton, use your hammer!"

"Cha!"

Got it!

Tinkaton quickly reacted, covering her head with one hand while gripping her hammer with the other, directly starting to spin like a windmill on the spot!

The huge, hard hammer continuously rang with "bang bang" sounds as those invisible rocks immediately crumbled to powder, forcibly creating a Stealth Rock-free zone around Tinkaton's body.

Even.

All the Will-O-Wisps shot by Dragapult were indiscriminately swept into scattered sparks by Tinkaton.

Witnessing this scene, the audience moved their lips and finally sighed:

"Indeedee, Tinkaton... are all the 'girls' in Teacher Johan's house like this?"

However, some people felt that although Tinkaton was violent and had type advantages, her body was surrounded by poisonous Toxic Spikes, which weren't as easily destroyed as Stealth Rock.

While Tinkaton was a Steel-type Pokémon, only her hammer was Steel-type. That pink body wasn't Steel-type and could still be poisoned.

This was quite different from games that simply dealt with types.

Moreover, Dragapult could not only fly but was extremely agile. It wouldn't be easy for ground-bound Tinkaton to hit airborne Dragapult.

But Tinkaton quickly demonstrated... what it meant to be one with the hammer.

"Cha!"

Tinkaton suddenly hurled her hammer at airborne Dragapult.

Whoosh—

The incredibly heavy hammer flew toward the sky at surprising speed.

"Dodge quickly, Dragapult!" Nemona waved her hand.

Dragapult was indeed very agile, moving in an extremely nimble way to precisely and skillfully avoid the incoming hammer.

But when Dragapult looked down to find Tinkaton after dodging...

She blinked.

Where was she?

"Above you!" Nemona's voice came again.

Above?

Wasn't that just the hammer that flew by?

"Cha!"

Tinkaton's voice indeed came from above.

Looking back.

She saw the hammer had already flown to the dome barrier, with Tinkaton holding onto it and flying up too. Her feet were even standing upside down on the upper barrier, waving cheerfully at the incredulous Dragapult.

The next second.

Tinkaton, practically hanging upside down from the barrier, suddenly kicked off with both feet. Combined with gravity's effect, she immediately swung her hammer down at Dragapult at extreme speed.

Play Rough!

Boom!!

Dragapult fell to the ground like a cannonball, causing the ground to shake continuously.

Tinkaton's hammer strike was even more violent than Sister Indeedee's psychic power.

Moreover, while Sister Indeedee was weaker than Glimmora, Tinkaton's strength... was superior to Dragapult's.

The live stream chat immediately exploded.

"Holy! Tinkaton nearly one-shot President Nemona's Dragapult with one hammer?!"

"It would be weird if she didn't nearly one-shot it, right?"

"Have you ever heard of a hammer technique that descends from heaven?"

"Want to learn?"

Dragapult wasn't directly one-shot thanks to its agile form avoiding the most direct impact. Otherwise, with enhanced physical attack, Tinkaton's hammer would definitely be too much for Dragapult to handle.

"U-turn!"

Nemona immediately shouted, no longer having Dragapult clash head-on with Tinkaton.

"Pult—"

Dragapult immediately became a pale green flash, striking just-landed Tinkaton before returning to Nemona's Premier Ball.

Another switch!

"Please, Altaria!"

Nemona's switch was extremely fast this time, immediately summoning her third Pokémon, the massive Ice-type Altaria.

"Cha?"

Tinkaton hadn't even fully stabilized when she heard...

"Altaria, Roar!"

"Roar—"

"Cha?"

Tinkaton immediately became a white light, flying back to Johan's Poké Ball with a confused expression.

After a brief moment of surprise, Johan quickly put Tinkaton's Poké Ball back on his belt.

"Then let's go, Urshifu!"

"Garruu!!"

Wide-eyed Urshifu appeared on the field. The poisonous Toxic Spikes underfoot and surrounding Stealth Rock immediately dealt considerable damage, causing his eyes to bulge like copper bells.

"Here we go, hazard-setting tactics are finally starting to take effect!"

"Besides Dragapult, President Nemona was actually hiding an Altaria that knows Roar!"

"Good gracious, it seems the 'stall master' Teacher Johan has encountered his first opponent who can match him in 'stall' tactics."

Nemona continued:

"Altaria, Roar again!"

As long as Roar or Dragon Tail worked once, hazard-setting tactics could operate.

At the same time, Johan spoke at rapid speed.

"Sucker Punch, Wicked Blow!"

"Roar—cough cough cough—"

Altaria was just about to roar when it saw a dark shadow at extreme speed appear before it, a strong, powerful palm firmly grasping its throat.

"Garruu..."

Urshifu slowly raised his head, facing Altaria's shocked gaze and revealing a "menacing" smile.

Go ahead and roar, keep roaring...

Heh, heh heh...

# 790: The Abandoned Hazard-Setting Tactics!

Wicked Blow.

A Dark-type move specifically targeting sound-based attacks.

Any Pokémon hit by this move would experience hellish pain in their throat, unable to use any sound-based moves for a period of time, such as Roar, Hyper Voice, or Sing.

Nemona's Pokémon lineup consisted of Glimmora setting up hazards, combined with Dragapult's high mobility using Dragon Tail to force opponent Pokémon switches.

When Dragapult's Dragon Tail couldn't take effect, there was also Altaria, a typical tank-type Pokémon that could delay the opponent's poisoning process and wear down their stamina.

This was a very typical hazard-setting tactics approach.

However, few people knew that Altaria could actually master the Roar move, able to force switches on Pokémon that Dragapult couldn't force to switch.

Even more so.

According to Johan's understanding of these tactics, among Nemona's remaining Pokémon, there were probably more than just Altaria that had mastered Roar.

But now...

Urshifu tightly gripped Altaria's throat, preventing it from making any sound and unable to use Roar for a certain period.

But similarly, Urshifu had also been poisoned by stepping on the Toxic Spikes on the ground and injured by hitting the seemingly omnipresent Stealth Rock.

Regardless of the current situation, Nemona's hazard-setting tactics had taken effect.

"Altaria, Rapid Spin, shake it off!" Seeing Urshifu suddenly appear in front of Altaria, Nemona was also somewhat surprised.

So fast?

Then she realized.

The Psychic Terrain created by Indeedee had already disappeared, so priority moves could finally take effect. Urshifu had used Sucker Punch to reach Altaria so quickly.

However, Altaria was inherently a slow Pokémon and definitely couldn't compare to Urshifu in speed.

But Altaria's size, its exaggerated physical defense, and stamina made it a typical tank-type Pokémon that wasn't easy to deal with.

Therefore, after Nemona's command, Altaria immediately exerted all its strength, twisting its body and trying to shake off Urshifu through spinning.

"Garruu!"

Feeling the terrifying force from Altaria's body, Urshifu immediately raised an eyebrow in surprise.

Truth be told, this Altaria's strength was indeed quite powerful. Urshifu's arm muscles completely bulged with clear definition, his feet slowly grinding against the ground, carving out a groove.

This strength...

Even Johan was secretly amazed.

He knew very well how strong his bear coach was. Nemona's Altaria could actually drag Urshifu and almost spin him around?

Not only was Johan surprised, but Nemona on the opposite side was even more surprised.

Her Altaria's massive size required terrifying strength to support, and she had indeed trained Altaria in the direction of power...

"Urshifu, throw it up!"

Altaria needed all four limbs on the ground to fully exert its strength; once off the ground, it would be difficult to perform.

"Garruu!!"

Urshifu roared explosively, both arms firmly gripping Altaria's body, his whole body suddenly exerting force, actually managing to forcibly lift Altaria up!

The entire audience stared blankly at this scene.

Altaria varied in size - normal ones might be around two meters tall, but Altaria was a horizontally-oriented Pokémon. A two-meter height was already quite exaggerated, and the weight from that thick, solid ice was immeasurable.

Nemona's Altaria was already over three meters tall, and its size and weight were even more...

It gave the impression that Urshifu had forcibly lifted up half a mountain!

"Hoo hoo hoo~~" Master Mustard, with his hands in his pockets, laughed happily. "Unity of waist and horse! This is the most standard unity of waist and horse!"

Seeing Urshifu's current strength and fighting techniques, Master Mustard was very pleased and especially grateful that he had helped Kubfu find Johan back then.

Even more exaggerated, Urshifu roared again and actually threw Altaria up.

"Re-really threw it up?"

"That, that Altaria's weight must be..."

"Indeedee, Tinkaton, plus Urshifu... Where is Teacher Johan's 'stall' team? This is completely a 'violence' team!"

The audience was amazed.

Nemona's expression froze for a moment, but she immediately snapped back to reality.

She knew that once off the ground, Altaria would become a sitting duck for Urshifu.

"Altaria, Iron Defense!"

Hum—

Altaria's massive icy body immediately took on a metallic luster.

Its already exaggerated physical defense became even more ridiculous with Iron Defense's enhancement.

As long as it could block Urshifu's impact this time, being thrown into the air would actually become an advantage rather than a disadvantage.

"Urshifu!" Johan's eyes gleamed with a faint blue light.

"Garruu!"

Urshifu assumed the stance for Focus Punch, his body emanating an air current that scattered the surrounding dust. Faint blue streams flowed around him as he stared intently at Altaria thrown into mid-air.

Tuck abdomen, bend knees, clench fist, focus mind.

Bang!!

The ground exploded.

Urshifu shot skyward, wrapped in pale blue light, and delivered a fierce punch to Altaria's massive belly.

Bang—

For an instant, time seemed frozen.

Altaria blinked.

Hm?

Why didn't it feel anything?

Not only did Altaria feel this way, but the audience felt the same.

But the next second.

Boom!!

Altaria's back suddenly erupted with a terrifying explosion, the blast wave blooming like a flower and shooting straight to the clouds.

Altaria, whose eyes had been filled with confusion the previous second, now only had whites showing in the next second.

Thud—

Urshifu landed.

"Garruu..."

A puff of white vapor slowly exhaled from his slightly open mouth.

Bang!!

Altaria crashed heavily to the ground.

"Al-Altaria is unable to battle!" The referee, watching the massive Altaria fall, felt the ground shake beneath his feet and shook along with it as he announced the result.

It... it was one-shot?

The entire audience had almost identical expressions.

Such a large Altaria, with such strong defense, and it had used Iron Defense. Even with the double weakness to Fighting-type, wasn't this one-shot a bit too exaggerated?

"Aura?"

As a Champion, Nemona had vision beyond ordinary trainers and quickly replayed the scene in her mind.

Altaria was indeed skilled in physical defense, but aura...

That was special attack!

Combined with Urshifu's Focus Punch technique...

Nemona could only barely understand it. To truly comprehend it required professional fighters like Master Mustard and Bruno.

"Using aura as force, making aura the striking power of Focus Punch..." Bruno crossed his arms with a serious expression.

Master Mustard smiled even more happily.

Not because he saw Urshifu one-shot Altaria, but because he saw... beyond the techniques of Focus Punch and Rapid Strike, Urshifu had achieved a new breakthrough!

"Garruu..."

Urshifu frowned slightly.

Poison was rapidly spreading through his body.

Nemona's Glimmora's poison was no ordinary toxin. As a Champion's ace Pokémon specializing in poison, even if it wasn't badly poisoned, the spreading toxin wasn't something ordinary Pokémon could endure.

"Thank you for your hard work, Altaria." Nemona recalled Altaria.

In their battle so far, this was the first Pokémon to lose its ability to fight.

Looking at Urshifu, who was already glowing with a purple aura, Nemona hesitated briefly before throwing out her fourth Poké Ball.

"Please, Chesnaught!"

"President Nemona's fourth Pokémon is Chesnaught!!" the commentator announced.

The final evolution of the Kalos region's Grass-type starter, the Grass and Fighting-type Spiny Armor Pokémon.

According to Pokédex descriptions, Chesnaught had a gentle nature and didn't like fighting, but if angered, it could unleash enough power to overturn a fifty-ton tank. It would use its sturdy, spike-covered shell to protect its companions.

Another typical tank-type Pokémon.

"Chesnaught, Leech Seed!"

Nemona had understood Urshifu's exaggerated strength and fighting techniques, so her tactical approach and focus had changed accordingly.

Before continuing with hazard-setting tactics, she needed to ensure her Pokémon had sufficient survivability.

Fortunately, while Chesnaught's physical defense wasn't as strong as Altaria's and didn't look as sturdy, its agility was incomparable to Altaria's, and its defensive capabilities were actually no worse than Altaria's.

The spikes on Chesnaught's shell flew out one by one, transforming into seeds that rapidly shot toward Urshifu's position.

However, just as the Leech Seeds were about to contact Urshifu, a red beam shot over and Urshifu disappeared from the field.

"Teacher Johan has chosen to switch Pokémon!!"

Hazard-setting tactics were meant to force opponent Pokémon switches, and Teacher Johan was actively doing this again?

To avoid Leech Seed?

But... the newly switched Pokémon would also be affected by Leech Seed, right?

Facing numerous questions, Johan quickly provided an answer.

A Luxury Ball was thrown into the air.

"Urshifu, you did excellently. Leave the rest to your teammates. Go! Dragonite!"

Although Urshifu still had fighting capability, Glimmora's poison was indeed strong enough. Without reaching an emergency situation, there was no need for Urshifu to continue fighting while enduring poison erosion.

Red light flashed.

"Draaa~~ (ˊˋ)"

The "robust" and sturdy Dragonite appeared in the sky above the battlefield.

Toxic Spikes and such had no effect whatsoever on Dragonite appearing directly in mid-air.

As for Stealth Rock...

It could only be said that the threat was far less than the poison carried by Toxic Spikes. Dragonite could shatter several rocks just by bumping them with its big belly.

"Teacher Johan's fourth Pokémon is the pseudo-legendary Pokémon—Dragonite!!"

With Nemona's Ice-type Altaria having lost its ability to fight, the existence that posed the greatest "threat" to Dragonite was gone.

Seeing Dragonite, Nemona frowned.

Her hazard-setting tactics were most afraid of encountering Teacher Johan's Dragonite and Gengar.

Since she didn't want to see it, then...

"Chesnaught, Roar!"

"Ches!!"

Chesnaught's voice was steady and deep, roaring with all its might at Dragonite that had just appeared in the sky.

Chesnaught could also learn Roar?

This was something many people didn't know.

But the agile Dragapult's Dragon Tail, the heavy tank Altaria's Roar, the comprehensive tank Chesnaught's Roar, plus Glimmora's hazard-setting, truly constructed Nemona's complete "stall" hazard tactics.

The audience had to admit that in terms of "stall," if Nemona ranked second, probably only Teacher Johan could claim first place.

"Dragonite, Clangorous Soul!" Johan smiled slightly and commanded.

"Draaa~ (ˊˋ)"

Dragonite's small wings vibrated as its thick arms beat against its round, soft, scale-covered belly, producing deafening drum sounds.

The sound waves from the drumming collided with the sound waves from Chesnaught's roar in mid-air.

"Eh? This scene... looks familiar."

"Earlier, President Nemona's Dragapult used Clangorous Soul against Indeedee's Disarming Voice. Now Teacher Johan's Dragonite uses Clangorous Soul to counter Chesnaught's Roar..."

"Good grief, using the opponent's methods against them?"

Rumble rumble—

Sound waves collided with sound waves in mid-air.

"Following the 'script,' shouldn't Dragonite be charging down now?"

Nemona squinted, watching together with Chesnaught as the expanding smoke in the sky, waiting for Dragonite's appearance.

Although Roar didn't take effect, facing Dragonite... Chesnaught wasn't particularly afraid, even though Flying-type had a quadruple advantage over Grass and Fighting-type Chesnaught.

With its Spiky Shield defense, it had this confidence.

Even if Dragonite came down, it had a chance to make Dragonite contact the Toxic Spikes.

Soon, Nemona and Chesnaught got what they were waiting for.

But what came down wasn't Dragonite, but two arc-shaped bright white air blades.

Air Slash?!

Nemona's expression froze.

Shouldn't it take advantage of the excitement from Clangorous Soul and charge down with Dragon Rush?

"Chesnaught, Seed Bomb!" Despite her confusion, Nemona still showed excellent reaction speed.

"Ches!!"

The spikes on its shell shot out like cannonballs, meeting the falling Air Slash.

Boom boom boom—

"Dragonite, Hurricane!"

"Draaa—"

Wings flapped.

A terrifying storm immediately swept over.

It could block one or two air blades but couldn't stop that massive storm.

For a moment, the terrifying storm raged within the arena barriers, and the audience's vision was completely obscured by the dust kicked up by the storm.

"Chesnaught, Pain Split!"

"Ches!"

In the massive storm, Chesnaught, enduring the storm's ravages, emanated an eerie black light that quickly swept through mid-air and enveloped Dragonite.

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite let out a pained cry, immediately feeling nearly half its stamina drained.

But fortunately, Chesnaught finally fell in the center of the raging storm.

When the storm dispersed.

"Chesnaught is unable to battle!!" the referee immediately announced.

With a quadruple weakness to Flying-type and such a powerful Hurricane, Chesnaught's fate was predictable.

But Chesnaught's Pain Split at the last moment also made Dragonite suffer considerably.

Not a loss!

Nemona took a deep breath.

With Altaria and Chesnaught consecutively losing their ability to fight, it almost announced that... her hazard-setting tactics had been more than half破解了.

She had a feeling... as if Teacher Johan could see through her every move.

Urshifu's Sucker Punch approach to Altaria, Wicked Blow forcibly interrupting Altaria's Roar.

Dragonite's Clangorous Soul dispersing Chesnaught's Roar. Accustomed to starting with Dragon Rush, today it opened with Air Slash and Hurricane, two special attack moves, giving Chesnaught no chance for Spiky Shield defense.

Only Chesnaught's final tenacity with Pain Split consumed considerable stamina from Dragonite.

But... ultimately, Nemona had fallen into an obvious disadvantage.

The score: 2-0!

Time to change tactics...

Looking at Dragonite in the sky, Nemona slowly exhaled the air from her lungs, took out the Premier Ball she had used before, and threw it out.

"Dragapult!"

"Pult—"

Nemona's Dragapult entered the field again!

Only Dragapult could better handle Dragonite's aerial agility!

"Draaa?"

Dragonite, who had been rubbing its belly with a pained expression, immediately perked up upon seeing Dragapult enter.

A dragon?

Seeing this scene, Johan, who had been preparing to switch Dragonite out, chuckled and put Dragonite's Poké Ball back.

Upon seeing Dragon-type Pokémon, Dragonite probably wouldn't obediently return.

At the same time, Johan's heart was getting excited.

Whether he could meet Lance later was uncertain, but the "surprise" reserved for Lance could very well be revealed to him now.

"Dragapult, Dragon Darts!"

After abandoning hazard-setting tactics, Nemona's entire momentum and state changed extremely quickly.

As soon as Dragapult entered, she unhesitatingly issued an aggressive command.

Swoosh swoosh—

From the two holes in Dragapult's triangular jet-like head, two arrows wrapped in blue-purple Dragon-type aura shot out at extreme speed.

"Dragonite, Dragon Claw!"

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite's expression became serious as it swung both claws. Despite its chubby appearance, it displayed agility completely inconsistent with its body.

Facing the Dragon Darts shot by Dragapult, it immediately swung two claws and deflected both arrows.

These two "arrows" were clearly the initial evolution of Dragapult's species, Dreepy.

After being swatted away by Dragonite, they bared their teeth and called out a couple of times at Dragonite before vanishing invisibly.

Seeing this scene, a thought suddenly occurred to Johan.

Logically speaking... Dragapult and Dragon Darts amounted to three Pokémon entering at once, right?

Although the relationship between Dragapult and Dreepy was recognized as special, but... this was still three Pokémon.

Then couldn't his side also have "external assistance"?

Reasonably speaking, there was nothing wrong with the Meltan on Tinkaton's hammer, or the Spectrier in Gengar's shadow...

"Dragapult, Dragon Pulse!"

His scattered thoughts immediately refocused. Johan, who could multitask, immediately waved his hand and shouted sternly:

"Dragonite, Dragon Rush!"

# 791: Mega Dragonite!!

"Pult—"

"Draaa!!"

After hazard-setting tactics could no longer take effect, Nemona unhesitatingly chose to engage in direct confrontation with Johan.

It had to be said.

Even setting aside tactical aspects, the ability Nemona displayed was absolutely worthy of the title "Champion."

Even...

From a certain perspective, Nemona performed with more experience and capability than young Champions like Lance and Leon, and showed more courage than veteran Champions like Alder.

Boom!!

Dragonite, wrapped in a blue-purple dragon aura, tore through Dragapult's Dragon Pulse and charged straight toward Dragapult.

"Dragapult, Phantom Force!"

Dragapult flicked its tail.

Originally only Dragapult's tail was invisible, but in an instant it was like donning Harry Potter's invisibility cloak, completely disappearing from Dragonite's sight.

"Draaa?"

Dragonite's form immediately stalled, hovering in mid-air as it looked around searching for Dragapult's figure.

"Dragon Darts!"

Swoosh swoosh—

Two dragon arrows wrapped in blue-purple Dragon-type energy shot out from an unseen corner, rapidly approaching Dragonite.

Bang! Bang!

Dragonite's reaction speed was also quick. Although the Dragon Darts came from behind, it still quickly turned around and swatted out Dragon Claws, batting away both Dreepy.

After being swatted away again, the two Dreepy showed no hesitation and immediately vanished invisibly.

On the field, it seemed only Dragonite remained as a single Pokémon.

"Gone?"

"Phantom Force can maintain for such a long time? And can use moves while maintaining invisibility?"

"Isn't this a bit unfair? You can't see the opponent, but they can hit you!"

"However, this does fit President Nemona's identity as the 'second master of stall.'"

This was indeed a bit unfair.

But... that was only for others.

Regarding the move Phantom Force, not to mention Johan, even Dragonite was very familiar with it. Wasn't it one of the signature techniques of their purple, chubby teammate back home?

However, the Phantom Force used by Dragapult was different from Gengar's Phantom Force.

Gengar directly tore through space and drilled into the "Distortion World" where "electronic pets" resided, while Dragapult relied on its own innate invisibility talent.

This was actually much easier to deal with than Gengar's method of disappearing by tearing through space.

After all, Gengar's disappearance and appearance only had tiny spatial fluctuations when leaving and returning, while Dragapult's physical form was always on the field.

But precisely because of this, Dragapult could use Dragon Darts while maintaining Phantom Force.

And as long as Dragapult was on the field, for trainers with aura power, psychic abilities, and such capabilities, finding it wasn't difficult.

This could be considered gains and losses balanced.

However, for dealing with Dragapult in this state, Johan had a more straightforward, simple, and effective method...

"Dragonite, Blizzard!"

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite flapped its wings, and a cold current immediately swept over, creating a terrifying blizzard.

"Dragapult!" Nemona immediately signaled for Dragapult to avoid it first. After all, without Hail's enhancement, Blizzard had low accuracy to begin with.

But... whether by coincidence or because of Dragonite's good luck, just as Dragapult moved to one side, the blizzard happened to sweep toward it.

It looked as if Dragapult had run right into it.

The audience couldn't see this, or they would definitely complain.

However, soon the audience could see Dragapult's figure.

Under the blizzard, the invisible Dragapult not only revealed its form due to the snowflakes sticking to it but also took the impact of the blizzard.

"Draaa!"

Dragonite grinned.

Wasn't it found now?

"Dragonite—"

Johan's expression became stern as he waved his hand and shouted:

"Dragon Rush!"

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite once again summoned its fierce dragon aura, tearing through the air and roaring toward Dragapult.

Seeing this was unavoidable, Nemona's expression also hardened as she shouted: "Dragapult, Dragon Rush!"

Dragapult's body immediately surged with blue-purple Dragon-type energy, dispersing the surrounding ice and snow while flicking its tail and charging fearlessly toward Dragonite.

Dragon Rush VS Dragon Rush!

Boom!!!

The terrifying impact shockwave spread out, continuously battering the arena barriers.

Under the audience's gaze, the commentator's passionate voice echoed through the boiling arena.

"Dragapult is unable to battle!!"

Whoosh—

The entire venue erupted again.

"Draaa!!"

Hovering in mid-air, Dragonite had quite a few scales shattered, but it excitedly hovered in the arena airspace, flapping wings and waving arms as it roared with excitement.

Exhilarating!

Nemona had lost her third Pokémon.

Field change!

Rumble rumble—

Nemona recalled Dragapult, rubbing the Premier Ball while feeling considerable pressure.

Although Johan's Urshifu probably wouldn't return due to poisoning, and Dragonite had also consumed considerable stamina due to Chesnaught's Pain Split plus the collision with Dragapult, but...

Ultimately, Teacher Johan hadn't lost a single Pokémon yet.

The current score was 3-0.

She had to do something.

Looking at Dragonite still excited in mid-air, Nemona slowly narrowed her eyes.

Soon, the field change was complete.

At the referee's signal, Nemona took out her fifth Premier Ball and threw it out without hesitation.

"The decisive moment, Kingambit!"

Bang!!

The heavy body landed on the newly replaced sandy field, kicking up large amounts of dust.

Gleaming sharp blades collided and rubbed against golden sand particles, producing dense, crisp sounds.

Kingambit!

The evolution of Bisharp, a Dark and Steel-type Big Blade Pokémon.

"President Nemona's fifth Pokémon is—Kingambit!" the commentator immediately announced.

"Gam!"

Kingambit patted its heavy armor and stood up with great presence, displaying a weathered yet commanding and imposing appearance.

With a low roar, Kingambit's body immediately surged with a powerful aura.

Moreover, this aura continued climbing, and that domineering invincible stance was as sharp and unmatched as the giant blade on its head.

"This..." the commentator was momentarily speechless.

Although Kingambit was registered in the Galar region Pokédex, because Kingambit's evolution requirements were extremely high and it was very rare, many people had limited understanding of this Pokémon.

Leon immediately explained from the side:

"This is Kingambit's ability 'Supreme Overlord.' Each time a Pokémon loses its fighting ability, it gains massive benefits when entering battle."

Leon had helped the commentator out more than once today, but his expression had never been as grave as now.

Not only because of the considerable boost Kingambit's "Supreme Overlord" ability brought it.

But more because... the aura Kingambit displayed.

Combined with the "Supreme Overlord" ability's enhancement, Kingambit was almost reaching legendary levels, wasn't it?

If—

"Dragonite, Focus Blast!"

Leon could sense the threat Kingambit posed, and Johan naturally could too.

So he rarely chose to strike first!

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite brought its hands together, assuming a "Kamehameha" stance as terrifying energy gathered between its palms.

This scene rather resembled a beam struggle setup.

Unfortunately, the long-range moves Kingambit could master were... rather limited.

"President Nemona's Kingambit is powerful, but its greatest weakness is Fighting-type moves."

Leon seemed to take over the commentator's job.

"And Dragonite's greatest advantage as a pseudo-legendary Pokémon is the moves it can master—its attack coverage is very broad!"

There was another thing Leon didn't say.

That was, relying on Dragonite's aerial advantage plus Kingambit's quadruple weakness to Fighting-type, even though Nemona's Kingambit was very strong, it wasn't impossible to defeat.

Actually, the audience understood this point without Leon saying it.

Quadruple weakness to Fighting-type.

This weakness was too obvious.

"Draaa—"

Dragonite brought its hands together, and the energy ball formed from surging Fighting-type energy immediately shot toward Kingambit on the ground.

Just then, Nemona took out a Tera Orb and threw it toward Kingambit.

"Show your general's bearing, Kingambit!"

Crack crack crack—

The Tera Orb's power enhanced Kingambit, immediately transforming into crystal covering its entire body.

When Dragonite's Focus Blast descended, the crystals on Kingambit shattered and scattered, completing Terastallization!

Boom! Boom!

Two explosion sounds erupted.

The commentator opened his mouth wide and stammered: "King-Kingambit split Dragonite's Focus Blast in half?!"

The reason there were two explosions was precisely because the giant blade atop Kingambit's head had actually cleaved Dragonite's Focus Blast in two!

This exaggerated scene made everyone's eyes widen.

However... what was most shocking wasn't just Kingambit splitting the Focus Blast, but also because...

"Tera Flying!" Leon said gravely.

Kingambit's Tera type was actually Flying-type!

Double resistance to Fighting!

"Gam!"

Kingambit, covered in transparent crystals all over its body, raised its head to stare directly at Dragonite in mid-air, its eyes full of cold light.

"Draaa?"

Dragonite looked at Kingambit's gaze and scratched its belly.

Acting tough now?

Seeing this scene, Johan's reaction differed from all the audience and top trainers like Leon.

He chuckled with a "heh."

Worthy of being "Master of Stall President Nemona."

Glimmora's hazard-setting, Dragapult's Dragon Tail and invisible Dragon Darts, Altaria's Roar, Chesnaught's Roar, Spiky Shield, and Pain Split, plus Tera Flying Kingambit...

If Nemona wasn't the master of "stall," who was?

However, Johan had actually anticipated this move from Nemona.

The reason he still had Dragonite use the unfamiliar Focus Blast was precisely to force out Nemona's Terastallization.

Dragonite didn't only know Fighting-type moves.

Compared to the unfamiliar Focus Blast, Ice and Electric-type moves were all Dragonite's specialties.

But combined with Terastallization and the "Supreme Overlord" ability, Kingambit after completing Terastallization had now reached legendary level strength!

"Worthy of someone who's been Champion for nearly ten years..." Johan murmured.

Unlike Leon and Lance's rising stars, and unlike Alder and Master Mustard's heroic twilight, Nemona was the only Champion among all Eight Masters participants who was at her absolute peak!

Moreover, Nemona was also the best user of Terastallization among all contestants.

As everyone knew, whether Mega Evolution, Z-Moves, Dynamax, Terastallization, or other enhancement methods, the benefits each brought were related to the user and Pokémon.

Obviously, Nemona's Terastallization not only changed Kingambit's type but also granted Kingambit tremendous power increases.

Legendary!

This wasn't the first Pokémon in this Master Tournament to appear at or "temporarily" boost to legendary levels.

While everyone was shocked by Kingambit splitting the Focus Blast and by Tera Flying, Nemona wouldn't forget this was still a battle—her battle with Teacher Johan!

As for legendary level... from what she knew, Teacher Johan had more than one Pokémon that had reached legendary levels: Gengar, Ninetales, and even... including this very Dragonite before her!

The pressure was always on her side.

So...

"Kingambit, Kowtow Cleave!"

"Gam!!"

The crystal-covered Kingambit roared lowly, and the large golden blade atop its head actually shot directly toward Dragonite in the sky at incredible speed!

Damn, is this Ultraman Seven?

"Dodge, Dragonite!"

"Draaa~!!"

Dragonite flapped its wings, its chubby-looking body moving with incredible agility.

But Kingambit's blade was fast and precise. Dragonite barely managed to dodge, still getting scales torn on its arm.

In the games, this was a move that never missed.

"Behind you!" Johan's pupils contracted as he urgently called: "Dragonite, Extreme Speed!"

"Draaa—"

Dragonite's body glowed white.

Swoosh—

A blade flash disappeared instantly.

Ding!

Kingambit caught the returning large golden blade and placed it back as its helmet.

"Draaa?"

As the white light faded, Dragonite turned its head to look at its other arm, where several scales mixed with traces of blood scattered down.

A boomerang blade too?

"Tsk," Johan clicked his tongue. "For an opponent like this, I should have sent Ceruledge..."

Ceruledge was most interested in weapons and such.

But obviously now, Dragonite was the most interested.

"Draaa!!"

A violent aura surged from Dragonite's body.

That dark red airflow signified the precursor to using Outrage.

Johan smiled.

In the contestant seating area, Cynthia, who had been sitting, glanced at Lance and stood up with a smile.

"Eh? Big sister, why are you standing up?" Flint couldn't help asking.

Cynthia gave him a sideways look. "We'll all be standing up soon anyway."

"Eh?" Flint looked confused.

Everyone else also cast puzzled glances.

Why stand up?

"Wait!" The clever Lucian seemed to sense something from Cynthia's words and quickly stood up, saying with disbelief: "Big sister, don't tell me..."

"Here it comes," Cynthia said softly.

On the field, Johan loosened his collar.

Now.

This is our Champion Time!

"Dragonite—"

Johan suddenly shouted.

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite, sharing Johan's intentions, naturally understood what Johan meant. Its eyes suddenly brightened as its wing-flapping frequency involuntarily increased.

Feeling Dragonite's excitement, Johan's face was also full of smiles.

"Mega Evolution!!"

"Draaa!!!"

Hum—

Dragonite's body immediately emitted emerald green fluorescence, followed by seven-colored rainbow light suddenly rising, then evolution light appeared, completely enveloping Dragonite!

Lance: "?!!!!"

Lance in the contestant seating area immediately shot up from his position, staring with incredulous eyes as he gripped the railing.

If this weren't during a match, he probably would have jumped down already.

Reacting the same way as Lance were Flint and others who had been confused earlier, all showing rather stunned expressions.

And those ordinary audience members needn't be mentioned—disbelief was written directly on their faces.

Live stream comments and forum discussions exploded even more directly.

"Dragonite? Mega Evolution? Did we hear wrong? Is this a joke or..."

"Didn't the League already announce all Pokémon capable of Mega Evolution?"

"This is... did the League miss one? Or is this a newly discovered Mega Evolution? Or something like Mega Evolution but not actually Mega Evolution?"

"Bad! Dragonite's about to take off, hurry up and catch one!"

"Laughable, you think Dragonite is cabbage? Catch one just like that?"

"But this way, won't Lance be taking off?"

Whether Lance would take off was unknown, but Dragonite was definitely about to take off.

"Draaa—"

Wings wrapped in snow-white feathers pierced through the evolution white light. The seven-colored symbol of Mega Evolution flashed briefly, and Dragonite, whose appearance had changed, appeared in everyone's sight.

The overall shape hadn't changed much, and the small wings on its back still existed, but atop its head, a pair of massive snow-white wings flapped, accompanied by gusts of strong wind.

Its tail became more slender, and in the middle section near the tail's end, there was also a crystal orb like those at the end of Dragonair's tail, emanating peculiar light.

Mega Dragonite!

At first glance, it looked somewhat like... a combined form of Dragonite and Dragonair.

Those wings atop its head, that crystal orb at its tail that could control weather and airflow.

Johan had actually complained about these when he first saw Mega Dragonite.

Regardless of what Mega Evolved Dragonite looked like, one thing was certain: its strength had gained tremendous improvement!

This point could be seen from the reaction of the "Lance Support Group" from Blackthorn City's Dragon Clan who had originally come to support Lance in the audience.

If this weren't during a match, this group of Dragon Clan members would probably have jumped down just like Lance.

"Mega Dragonite?"

Nemona also looked with amazement at Dragonite that had completed Mega Evolution.

As League Chairman and Champion, even she didn't know that Dragonite, this pseudo-legendary Pokémon, could actually Mega Evolve.

Originally, because Dragonite couldn't Mega Evolve, it had been somewhat outpaced by other pseudo-legendary Pokémon like Tyranitar, Metagross, Salamence, and Garchomp, but now...

"Mega Dragonite." Leon had also stood up from the commentary booth, thinking of Lance's Dragonite while feeling the changes in Teacher Johan's Dragonite.

"Legendary..."

Johan waved his hand broadly and shouted:

"Dragonite, Thunder!"

Rumble—

----------
