# 792: Calm Down, Calm Down Lance!

Rumble rumble—

A tremendous thunder crashed down, and the already Terastallized Kingambit suffered a heavy blow from the golden lightning.

The appearance of Mega Dragonite had shocked everyone's jaws.

No one expected that after the League had announced almost all Pokémon capable of Mega Evolution, Dragonite's Mega Evolution would still appear!

Moreover, it seemed to be a Mega Evolution that didn't rely on Mega Stones.

Scattered golden arcs of electricity danced on the arena barriers like blooming fireworks—beautiful but dangerous.

"Kingambit, this isn't over yet, Stone Edge!"

Nemona's face flushed slightly red.

Even she felt the tremendous threat from Mega Dragonite, but as a Champion, she wouldn't choose to give up at this moment, nor was it time to give up yet.

Even against Mega Dragonite, the current Kingambit still had a chance of victory!

"Gam!!"

From the charred crater with jumping arcs of electricity, Kingambit raised its arms with a clang, striking the ground. Sharp rocks appeared from thin air, piercing toward Mega Dragonite in the sky like spears.

"Dragonite! Extreme Speed!"

Johan called out softly.

"Draaa—"

The massive wings atop Mega Dragonite's head flapped gently.

After Mega Evolution, Dragonite finally gained wings that matched its body size.

Although... this appearance did make Johan feel somewhat strange.

But with larger wings and having completed Mega Evolution, Dragonite displayed even faster speed.

It sped through the air, and facing the dense barrage of sharp rocks like a forest of spears, it actually managed to avoid Kingambit's Stone Edge through sheer speed.

Ultimately, Mega Dragonite's aerial advantage was its greatest advantage against Kingambit. Before Kingambit's attacks could reach Dragonite, Dragonite always had some reaction time—the only difference was how long that time was.

Before Mega Evolution, Dragonite might have been half a beat slow, but after Mega Evolution, Dragonite regained the advantage.

The Dragonite that had avoided Kingambit didn't continue staying in mid-air but took the opportunity, wrapped in white light, to charge toward Kingambit!

Dragonite wasn't one to like hiding—it preferred attacking, especially after Mega Evolution when it had gained tremendous power. Dragonite wanted to test its current strength!

Otherwise, Dragonite wouldn't be so fond of Dragon Rush.

"Dragonite, Thunder Punch!"

Lightning gathered around Dragonite's body once again.

Seeing Johan and Dragonite's intentions, Nemona and Kingambit naturally showed no sign of backing down.

A frontal clash?

This was exactly what Kingambit excelled at!

"Kingambit, Kowtow Cleave!"

Bang—

Kingambit suddenly knelt on the ground, the massive golden blade atop its head glowing with intense metallic luster as it rose to meet the descending Dragonite.

Boom!!!

The next instant, lightning and silver light intertwined. With Kingambit at the center, the entire ground rippled in waves, spreading wildly in all directions, leaving the field in complete chaos.

"Kingambit, Sucker Punch!"

The collision was far from over. Nemona knew she couldn't give Dragonite another chance to take flight, or Kingambit would have little opportunity to counterattack.

But Johan and Dragonite had no intention of "kiting" again.

We've already Mega Evolved, and this is Dragonite's first Mega Evolution on stage—if we're still dodging at this point, wouldn't our Mega Evolution be wasted?

Let's see Dragonite's upper limit then!

"Dragonite!" Johan's gaze was piercing as his aura power rippled in waves.

He waved his hand and shouted sternly: "Out-rage!"

"Draaa!!"

In the swirling dust clouds, Dragonite's body immediately surged with terrifying dark red airflow as violent aura spread wildly.

All the dust immediately scattered explosively as if at the center of a blast.

The tremendously violent Mega Dragonite displayed the tyrannical aura and proud dominance of a great dragon to the fullest.

But within the violence, Dragonite retained the necessary rationality.

Therefore, when Kingambit's fist wrapped in dense black aura swung toward it, Dragonite raised its hand.

Bang!!

"Gam?"

Kingambit looked in amazement at its fist caught by Dragonite.

Mega Dragonite after Mega Evolution had reached genuine legendary levels.

And when Mega Dragonite activated "Outrage state"...

"Draaa—"

Dragonite grinned, revealing a fierce smile.

The next second, Kingambit felt the world spinning around it.

Boom boom boom!!

Continuous thunderous roars, accompanied by ground tremors and flickering arena barriers, spread throughout the venue.

Crack—

When the final Terastallization crystals shattered, it announced the end of this battle.

At the same time, it basically announced the end of this round of battle between Nemona and Johan.

Because after losing Kingambit, and more importantly losing Terastallization as an enhancement method, even though Nemona still had two Pokémon left, it would be difficult to turn the tide.

Even though Johan's Urshifu could no longer battle, even though Dragonite, which had defeated Kingambit, was nearly exhausted after canceling Mega Evolution.

Because Nemona only had one Espathra left, plus her ace Glimmora in poor condition.

Setting aside Glimmora for now, the Psychic-type Espathra couldn't possibly defeat Johan's Tyranitar in this desert field.

And Glimmora... the rumored two strongest Pokémon of Johan's team, Gengar and Ninetales, hadn't even appeared yet.

However, Nemona didn't announce surrender, but the final result... didn't deviate much from expectations.

Espathra couldn't create much of a stir against Tyranitar, and Glimmora... although its poison made Tyranitar suffer somewhat, with its quadruple weakness to Ground-type and already poor condition, it ultimately only managed a draw with Tyranitar.

After all, although Tyranitar was a Rock and Dark-type Pokémon, its Ground-type moves hadn't weakened much after evolution and losing the Ground-type. The Earth Plate and countless diamonds had provided Tyranitar with sufficient nourishment.

Plus the intimidation of Precipice Blades... being able to draw with Nemona's ace Glimmora, even a Glimmora with less than half stamina, Tyranitar's strength was worthy of praise from Johan and everyone else.

In the end, Johan VS Nemona ended with a score of 6-1!

At least she wasn't swept...

When the referee raised the flag and announced the final result, Nemona secretly sighed in relief.

She had already clearly felt the strength gap between herself and Johan.

Not only because she was beaten 6-1, but also because she hadn't even forced Johan to send out either Gengar or Ninetales!

Anyone who knew Johan understood that Gengar was the ace among Teacher Johan's aces.

As League Chairman and Champion, Nemona knew even more clearly that Johan's Gengar had already reached legendary levels in its normal state, and not just ordinary legendary—she didn't dare imagine how strong Gengar would be after Mega Evolution.

In any case... not being swept was already not a loss!

"Congratulations, Teacher Johan."

"Thank you."

After the match, the two shook hands at center field, both wearing smiles to varying degrees.

"Mega Dragonite is truly amazing," Nemona recalled the moment she first saw Dragonite Mega Evolve.

Setting everything else aside, Dragonite's Mega Evolution would definitely change, to some extent, the current "ecological" order of families mainly focused on Dragon-type Pokémon across regions in the Pokémon world.

"An unexpected discovery," Johan said vaguely.

He wasn't sure whether others could replicate Dragonite's Mega Evolution.

Nemona smiled: "Remember to tell others the same thing."

As she spoke, her gaze moved past Johan to the contestant tunnel behind him.

The contestant tunnel, which should have only had League staff, was now crowded with people.

Those reporters seemed to have "broken through" the organizers' blockade.

If it were just these reporters, it would be fine... but Johan saw Lance, saw Clair, and saw those members of Lance's "support group"—the Dragon Tamers from the Dragon Clan.

Under Nemona's somewhat teasing smile, a drop of cold sweat appeared on Johan's forehead.

This situation... seemed a bit overwhelming.

"Calm down, calm down Lance!"

"I'm very calm! You're the one who needs to calm down, Johan!"

With his cheek pressed against the sofa, Lance struggled to speak while Teacher Johan held him in a very standard restraint position, controlling his arm.

Nearby, Clair's face was cold and even radiated "killing intent" as she gripped her whip tightly, but... couldn't get past Gengar even one step.

The others around looked at Lance being pinned to the sofa by Teacher Johan, unable to move, then at Teacher Johan telling Lance to calm down but not letting go, and broke out in cold sweats.

Is this the strength of a "Fighting Master"?

Lance, who came from a Police background, was taken down by Teacher Johan in one move...

From now on, if Teacher Johan ever claims he doesn't know how to fight, nobody should believe him!

"Johan." Cynthia looked at Johan and Lance's situation, wanting to laugh but feeling it wasn't quite appropriate, so she could only tug at Johan, signaling him not to be so tense.

"Teacher."

"Teacher."

Steven and N also hurriedly stepped forward to "mediate."

"Have them calm him down, and I'll let go," Johan said clearly while restraining Lance and setting conditions.

Everyone: "..."

"Cough, well, Teacher Johan, I think Lance is probably quite calm now."

As the host, Leon had to speak up.

If he didn't speak up soon, there would be trouble!

Tomorrow's newspaper headlines might not even give Mega Dragonite first place. News like "Shocking! Teacher Johan Beats Up Champion Dragon Master Lance in Rest Room!" might be more exciting than Mega Dragonite.

Johan looked at the "obedient" Lance, then at Leon, Cynthia, and others, and exhaled lightly.

"Alright then."

He released Lance.

The next second, Lance immediately flipped over and grabbed Johan's clothes, showing no anger about being pinned down earlier, just asking urgently:

"Johan, does Dragonite really have Mega Evolution?"

Lance wasn't being overly excited without reason.

Others, even those who also specialized in Dragon-type Pokémon like Clair, old man Drake, or other trainers, had at most one Dragonite.

Even most trainers in the Pokémon world who were enthusiastic about Dragon-type Pokémon didn't have a Dragonite, but Lance... had three!

Three Dragonite!

Plus three Dragonair!

And numerous Dratini...

Others might not know why Lance trained so many Dragonite, Dragonair, and Dratini, but Johan was one of the few who guessed Lance's thinking.

He wanted to cultivate his own "Dragon Legion"!

Just like Elite Four Agatha's "Ghost Legion."

The appearance of Mega Dragonite would undoubtedly make Lance's "Dragon Legion" even stronger, and naturally boost his own strength to another level.

It could even elevate the overall strength of the entire Dragon Clan of Blackthorn City!

That's why Johan had to pin Lance down and make him calm down—this concerned not just him, but their entire Dragon Clan.

"Cough."

Johan struggled to break free from Lance and coughed lightly: "I don't really know how to explain the specifics, but... as you can see."

As I can see?

Dragonite could indeed Mega Evolve.

But...

"Why have I never seen Dragonite's Mega Stone?" Lance frowned.

"Actually, I haven't seen one either," Johan spread his hands.

Then he briefly recounted what had happened with Dragonite and what Rayquaza had said.

After hearing this, everyone fell silent.

Dragonite's Mega Evolution was related to Rayquaza?

How could others replicate that?

Seeing Lance's helpless expression, Johan thought for a moment and said:

"However, it's not necessarily true that Dragonite's Mega Stone doesn't exist—we just haven't found it yet."

Haven't found it?

The League had practically turned the Hoenn and Kalos regions upside down.

Actually, Johan's thought was... perhaps Dragonite's Mega Stone existed in a parallel universe of another Pokémon world?

Even in that parallel universe, the Pokémon capable of Mega Evolution might be completely different from those in his current world.

But the Pokémon themselves were the same.

So Dragonite could Mega Evolve.

Strictly speaking, Johan and Dragonite's Mega Evolution wasn't the same kind of bond evolution as his and Gengar's, but rather borrowed Rayquaza's power.

Lance shook his head.

He understood that replicating what happened with Johan and Dragonite would be very difficult.

But Lance quickly regained his spirits.

Since Johan had proven that Dragonite could Mega Evolve, then... even without relying on Mega Stones, he and his Dragonite could achieve it someday!

At least now there was a clear direction!

Lance's reaction didn't represent just him alone.

In a short time, the news that Dragonite, a pseudo-legendary Pokémon, could Mega Evolve swept across the internet.

Even the news of Johan beating Champion Nemona with a 6-1 record was overshadowed.

However... the two pieces of news could actually be combined into one, so there wasn't much difference.

But this was still during the Master Tournament, so even though Mega Dragonite's appearance was shocking, it was still just one Pokémon.

So after "causing a commotion" for a while, everyone quickly refocused their attention on the upcoming matches.

After Johan became the second to advance to the semifinals, the Eight Masters Tournament continued.

Next up was the clash between Cynthia and Steven!

One was his girlfriend, the other his most accomplished student.

On his way to watch the match, Johan was intercepted by reporters.

"Teacher Johan, who do you think will win between Ms. Cynthia and Mr. Steven?"

To this "death" question, Johan answered very decisively with a slight smile:

"Of course, the victor will be the one who wins."

Reporter: "?"

# 793: The Four Finalists Emerge!

"Ladies and gentlemen, welcome once again to the Master Tournament!"

The familiar commentator's voice was still passionate. "Today's battle is between Champion Cynthia from the Sinnoh League and the young genius trainer Steven Stone from the Hoenn League!"

Cynthia VS Steven Stone!

What was meant to come would eventually come.

There were only eight people in the Eight Masters, with nearly half being "family," so encounters were almost inevitable. Johan had already "given up" on this.

That said, the showdown between Cynthia and Steven...

He couldn't judge who would win either.

The strength gap between the two wasn't large, and both their tactical levels and command abilities were basically on the same level.

Moreover, both had been influenced by Johan, making them certainly much stronger than their counterparts in the original work at the same period. They might even match their peak states from the original work.

If he had to say anything...

Perhaps Steven had a slight disadvantage in terms of his Pokémon's types.

Like Johan, Alder, Nemona, and Leon, Cynthia was a trainer who didn't specialize in a single type, so when facing various opponents who specialized in one type, she could more or less gain certain type advantages.

Steven, like Lance, was enthusiastic about Steel and Rock types, so most of his Pokémon were of these two attributes. When facing some specifically targeted Pokémon, he would inevitably fall into certain disadvantages.

But Steven had his invincible Shiny Metagross.

However, Cynthia also had her invincible Garchomp.

Ultimately, the outcome between the two might really depend on their respective states at this moment.

Perhaps one person would win this match, but if they fought again, it might be the other person who would win.

In short, it was hard to say...

"Now, please welcome both contestants!"

Cheers shook the stadium, with screams and shouts rising and falling.

Both Cynthia and Steven had incredibly exaggerated fan bases.

The heroic Cynthia still wore her signature black outfit, while Steven wore his rather fitting tight suit.

Now came the moment for both contestants to "talk tough."

"Cynthia, I must advance and then battle Teacher to test myself," Steven declared seriously, voicing his desire for victory with a grave expression.

Cynthia put one hand on her hip, staring directly at Steven, and said likewise:

"Sorry, but I also must battle that guy!"

Their conversation was naturally heard by all the audience.

The director was quite good at this—a camera Rotom immediately flew to the contestant viewing area, focusing on Johan who had just picked up a grape.

Behind him, Gengar, noticing the camera, immediately floated out to face the filming Rotom, sticking out its large tongue and making Victini's signature "V" scissors gesture.

The big screen showed Johan frozen mid-motion.

"I've seen two men fighting over one woman, even two men fighting over one man, but one man and one woman fighting over one man is rare... Oh, it's Teacher Johan, never mind then."

"Teacher-student battle VS domestic status battle!"

"This time I stand... sorry, this time I can't take a side."

"Can't take a side? This time I stand with Teacher Johan!"

"How can there be such a cute Pokémon as Gengar!"

Amid the audience's teasing, the referee raised the flag, signaling both sides to prepare.

Cynthia and Steven threw out their Poké Balls simultaneously.

"Let's go, Skarmory!"

"Please, Togekiss!"

"Battle begin!"

After confirming both were ready, the referee dropped the flag and officially announced the start of the match.

"Skarmory, Stealth Rock!"

"Togekiss, Tailwind!"

Whoosh—

Sitting in the audience, Johan exhaled lightly.

It had begun...

His expression became serious.

The battle between Cynthia and Steven was truly worth watching.

Moreover, he could clearly see both their battle intentions.

Skarmory's hazard-setting tactics, while Togekiss would gain initiative advantage with Tailwind, then follow up with moves like Thunder Wave and Air Slash, while also creating more priority opportunities for subsequently entering Pokémon.

Everyone's attention gradually shifted from "teasing" about Johan to the battle itself.

Amid the audience's cheers, the battle immediately entered a heated phase.

Togekiss's consecutive Air Slash attacks landed on Skarmory, creating "clanging" sounds accompanied by numerous sparks.

In terms of initiative, it was definitely in Cynthia and Togekiss's hands, but Skarmory could withstand Togekiss's continuous bombardment.

"Togekiss, Tailwind!"

After setting up Tailwind again and abandoning further Thunder Wave probing, Cynthia was the first to switch Pokémon.

"Spiritomb!"

Cynthia's second Pokémon was Spiritomb!

In Johan's view, the most crucial aspect of Cynthia and Steven's battle wasn't actually Garchomp, because Steven's Shiny Metagross could basically "cancel out" Cynthia's Garchomp.

The real key lay with Spiritomb and Lucario.

Spiritomb feared no Steel or Rock-type Pokémon and had tremendous advantage over Pokémon skilled in physical attacks and close combat.

And Lucario needn't be mentioned.

It could be considered one of Cynthia's important trump cards in this match.

Not only Johan knew this—Steven, Lance, and the other contestants all thought the same.

How to maximize Lucario's potential would be key to Cynthia's victory.

Steven's trump card...

Lay with his Claydol.

Sandstorm tactics and Trick Room tactics—how to choose would determine Steven's subsequent battle results against Cynthia.

Spiritomb's appearance had immediate effect. After a fierce battle, it successfully defeated Steven's Skarmory, helping Cynthia take the first lead!

But Skarmory had actually completed its "mission"—the hazards and Stealth Rock would cause all of Cynthia's subsequently entering Pokémon to lose some stamina first.

Afterward, Steven sent out his Mawile!

As a Pokémon that, like Tinkaton, seemed harmless, Mawile showed its terrifying side—nearly chomping through Spiritomb's Odd Keystone.

Finally, Mawile used Play Rough to successfully defeat Spiritomb, tying the score.

Moreover, after defeating Spiritomb, Steven recalled Mawile.

Because its "Intimidate" ability would surely play a significant role in this match.

So after each losing one Pokémon and showing two Pokémon, Cynthia and Steven were essentially back at the same starting line, simultaneously summoning Pokémon again.

Steven's third Pokémon was...

"Aggron!!! Steven's third Pokémon is the 'Iron Armor Pokémon' Aggron!!" the commentator exclaimed excitedly.

"Aggr—!!!"

The massive Aggron roared with raised arms, displaying its presence to the fullest.

What Cynthia summoned was still... Togekiss!

Continuing with Tailwind!

This was partly to maintain Tailwind and partly to see what Steven's third Pokémon was for better response.

More importantly...

"Togekiss, Defog!"

Togekiss cleared away the "hazards" set by Skarmory but inevitably took a hit from Aggron, nearly losing its ability to battle.

To preserve this "Tailwind user" and better deal with Aggron, after renewing Tailwind, Cynthia recalled Togekiss again, substituting with—

"Lucario!! Champion Cynthia's third Pokémon is the 'Aura Pokémon' Lucario!!"

White light flashed as Lucario slowly opened its red eyes, its gaze serious and sharp. Bringing its palms together, its aura power burst forth.

Cynthia raised her arm: "Lucario, use Aura Sphere!"

Boom!!

Aggron had a quadruple weakness to Fighting-type. It wouldn't be wrong to say Lucario had been waiting for Steven's Aggron.

If Lucario was second only to Garchomp among Cynthia's Pokémon, then Aggron was second only to Shiny Metagross among Steven's Pokémon!

Of course, despite Aggron's quadruple weakness to Fighting-type, it couldn't be one-shot by Lucario, so Steven immediately switched Pokémon.

"Claydol, Trick Room!"

Space activated!

Steven's Pokémon were mostly heavy assault types, so from a certain perspective, Trick Room suited Steven's team better than Sandstorm.

Moreover, Steven deliberately waited until after Spiritomb's defeat to send out Claydol, avoiding the greatest threat.

Additionally, Psychic-type's advantage over Fighting-type Lucario was equally effective.

At this point, one had to admire Cynthia's early decision to have Togekiss pay a certain price to clear the "hazards" set by Skarmory.

Otherwise, each of Cynthia's switches now would be handing victory to Steven.

"Gastrodon, Muddy Water!"

Cynthia's fourth Pokémon—Water and Ground-type Gastrodon!

"Cradily, Energy Ball!"

Steven also switched, his fourth Pokémon—Rock and Grass-type Cradily with the "Storm Drain" ability!

Gastrodon had a quadruple weakness to Grass-type!

This was why Johan said Cynthia might have some type advantages, but they weren't large and insufficient to determine this match's outcome.

Steven's defensive synergy... was actually quite good.

"Gastrodon is unable to battle!"

Cynthia, who had first defeated one of Steven's Pokémon, consecutively lost both Spiritomb and Gastrodon!

However, this way, Steven had already used five Pokémon. Including his yet-to-appear Shiny Metagross, his team configuration was basically fully revealed.

Looking at Cradily rooted on the field, continuously absorbing water flow, trying to recover stamina with sunlight, and still enhanced by Trick Room, Cynthia recalled her defeated Gastrodon and narrowed her eyes.

"Soar through the sky, Garchomp!!"

Cynthia's ace Garchomp!

It was entering at this moment?!

Deafening cheers erupted in the venue, with Gengar even floating outside the window, cupping its hands like a megaphone and shouting:

"Ka-kaah!"

Shark girl, go for it!!

Shark girl?

Johan's expression was strange.

"Garchomp, tear everything apart, Dragon Rush!!"

"Here it comes!" Lucian immediately felt his back tense, that familiar feeling arrived. "Big sister's unreasonable move..."

"Gar-Garchomp forcibly tore through Trick Room?!" The commentator's voice filled with disbelief spread throughout the venue.

This was the "experience" Lucian had given Cynthia and Garchomp.

Who said Trick Room definitely needed to be used again to close?

Just tear it apart!

The forcibly shattered Trick Room caught Cradily off guard. Dragon Rush plus close-range Brick Break...

"Cradily is unable to battle!!"

Score: 2-2!

"Mawile!"

Facing Garchomp, Steven's trump card was Mawile.

"Intimidate" lowered Garchomp's physical attack ability, Fairy-type against Dragon-type.

If Cynthia had Garchomp Mega Evolve now, Steven would definitely have Mawile Mega Evolve without hesitation!

Having the "Huge Power" ability wasn't exclusive to the "Dragon Slayer" Azumarill.

But Cynthia's rationality made Johan feel "afraid."

After dealing with Cradily and Trick Room, Cynthia actually recalled Garchomp.

In its place was Cynthia's final Pokémon.

"Milotic!"

Another Water-type Pokémon!

Among Steven's remaining Pokémon, both Claydol and Aggron feared Water-type, and the originally optimal Cradily had been caught off guard by Garchomp.

"Milotic, Scald!"

The fierce battle continued between both sides!

Cynthia VS Steven Stone—their battle was so spectacular that the audience barely dared blink, afraid of missing any detail that might occur at any moment.

It was precisely because of their nearly evenly matched strength that the battle became so spectacular.

As time slowly progressed, not only did the battle situation not become clear, but it became increasingly unpredictable.

Especially when the score became 5-5.

"Garchomp—"

"Metagross—"

Cynthia took out the lipstick Johan had given her, gently applying it to her lips as her Key Stone burst with brilliant light.

Steven removed his tie pin, elegantly kissing it, and the Key Stone embedded within also radiated brilliant light.

The director split the screen, showing Cynthia and Steven's current actions on either side of the screen.

Finally, both shouted almost simultaneously:

"Mega Evolution!!"

"Chomp—!!"

"Meta—!!"

Mega Garchomp VS Mega Metagross finally appeared under everyone's anticipation.

The invincible Garchomp and the invincible Metagross...

Who was truly more invincible?

"Metagross, Ice Punch!"

"Garchomp, Earthquake!!"

Legendary VS Legendary!

Both trainers' ace Pokémon had reached legendary levels after Mega Evolution!

This made Johan complain involuntarily: "Are legendaries everywhere now?"

But thinking about it, if even top geniuses like Cynthia and Steven in the Pokémon world couldn't reach legendary levels with their ace Pokémon after Mega Evolution, that would be rather unreasonable.

"Garchomp, hold on, Stomping Tantrum!"

"Metagross!"

Rumble rumble—

The entire field was completely destroyed, and even the arena barriers showed faint signs of cracking.

The originally boiling stadium was now silent as everyone craned their necks, staring intently at the field completely obscured by dust.

The result?

What was the final result?

As the dust gradually cleared, everyone saw...

Garchomp, having shed its Mega Evolution, and Shiny Metagross, still maintaining Mega Evolution, stood on either side of the field.

"This..."

"Did Ms. Cynthia lose?"

"Garchomp has canceled its Mega Evolution, so it should be... over, right?"

However, the next second.

"Meta..."

Mega Shiny Metagross whimpered as the seven-colored Mega Evolution energy peeled away like silk, its body swaying slightly before falling on the field.

"Chomp..."

Garchomp's body also swayed, half-kneeling on the ground with large drops of sweat pouring down, but struggling to maintain its clarity.

"Mega-Metagross is unable to battle!" the referee announced the result in disbelief.

"So the winner is, from the Sinnoh region—Champion Cynthia!"

"Chomp—"

Hearing this, Garchomp's body swayed again and fell to the ground.

Though it ultimately lost its ability to battle...

It had persisted to the end!

"The third contestant to advance to the semifinals has been born!!!"

The commentator shouted: "She is the Champion from the Sinnoh League, Cynthia!!"

"Holy crap! Big sister is awesome! Garchomp is awesome!" Flint jumped three meters high, shouting loudly.

"Whew—"

Cynthia exhaled deeply, looking toward the audience seats with relief and meeting Johan's gaze.

She smiled elegantly.

Johan, I'm coming...

The contestants shook hands.

Steven, recovering from defeat, shook hands with Cynthia while sighing helplessly: "At the crucial moment, canceling Mega Evolution to give Garchomp faster speed and thus gain initiative... this was indeed something I couldn't predict."

Why was it the de-Mega Evolved Garchomp that remained standing?

Steven provided the answer.

Admittedly, Mega Evolution allowed Pokémon to gain stronger power, speed, and defense, but...

With this temporary enhancement, Pokémon were ultimately more familiar with their original bodies.

Moreover... Garchomp's Mega Evolution had a certain "peculiarity."

Cynthia shook her head: "If we battled again, I wouldn't have any confidence."

"Same here."

The two were essentially even.

It could only be said... in this match, it was Cynthia and Garchomp who stood to the end.

"Then..." Steven took a deep breath, quickly shaking off his dejection with a smile: "Take my portion with you and have a good battle with Teacher!"

"Yes!"

Cynthia nodded seriously.

Then she winked playfully:

"Next time, I'll send you Johan's location, you can challenge him."

Steven's eyes lit up.

That was possible?

That sounded great!

"Good!"

"Achoo—"

Johan sneezed, accepting the towel Ninetales handed him.

"Ninetales, thank you."

Looking at the two whispering on the field, Johan sighed somewhat wistfully: "Canceling Mega Evolution for the final strike's priority... Cynthia is amazing..."

One day later.

The final match of the Master Tournament's round of eight.

Giovanni VS Agatha!

Finally, Giovanni claimed victory!

Poison-type was still severely countered by Ground-type.

The Master Tournament's final four were officially decided.

Lance, Giovanni, Johan, and Cynthia!

# 794: Semifinal! Johan VS Cynthia!

"If you ask me, Teacher Johan is simply invincible—his ace Gengar hasn't even appeared yet!"

"But isn't Ms. Cynthia's Garchomp fierce? That Dragon Rush move... hiss—it could even forcibly tear through Trick Room."

"I think Mr. Giovanni is the one who's truly hiding his depths. We at least know Teacher Johan's ace is Gengar, but who knows what Mr. Giovanni's ace is?"

"Lance! Champion Lance's Dragonite is equally invincible, and moreover... it might even be able to Mega Evolve!"

When the battle between Agatha and Boss Giovanni ended and the semifinal roster was officially announced, as the audience dispersed under the night sky, they were already looking forward to the upcoming semifinal showdowns.

Everyone had their own favorites to support.

Even Boss Giovanni, who hadn't been very famous before the tournament began, had reached the semifinals, surpassing so many Champions and Elite Four members with his Gym Leader status, already gaining a large fan base and attention.

Moreover, as a mature man with his stern, unsmiling appearance and steady, resolute battle style, Boss Giovanni had won considerable admiration from many young women.

Mature men always had their allure.

Matori was in danger!

With the semifinals decided, following the tournament's A and B group format, the semifinal battles didn't require a draw.

Johan VS Cynthia!

Lance VS Giovanni!

The battle lineup was clear.

Naturally, the audience was full of anticipation for the battles between the semifinalists.

Actually, Johan was quite looking forward to the showdown between Lance and Boss Giovanni, like the confrontation between Lance and Giovanni in the special manga.

But just thinking about having to battle Cynthia made Johan's mood somewhat less cheerful.

Though it wasn't their first time battling each other.

"But fighting Steven would also be a headache..."

While muttering and holding ice cream, Johan knocked on Cynthia's door.

Knock knock—

The door opened.

"Rio—"

"Thank you, Lucario." Johan smiled.

From the living room came Caitlin's voice.

"Hmm, I admit Johan's strength isn't weak, but with proper tactical arrangements, there's still a decent chance. For example, having Gastrodon face Ceruledge, having Glaceon face Dragonite..."

"Cough."

Johan coughed lightly as he approached.

Caitlin was giving Cynthia strategic advice, and Diantha was also in the living room.

"What are you coughing for!" Caitlin glared at him. "Even if you cough louder, I'll still say what I need to say!"

Johan shook his head: "Go ahead and say it. Those so-called strategies and tactics of yours are just interfering with Cynthia."

As he spoke, he handed ice cream to Cynthia, Diantha, and the blonde tsundere Caitlin one by one.

"Cynthia understands me much better than you do."

"Hmph!"

Caitlin was angry, but she wouldn't turn down ice cream, accepting it before curling up in a corner of the sofa.

But she had to admit that what Johan said... was the truth.

Diantha smiled:

"Do you need us to step out for a bit?"

Johan chuckled, his gaze falling on Cynthia, or rather on the shadow behind her...

"Alright, stop hiding. Come out."

"Ka-kaah..."

The purple chubby Gengar quietly floated out from Cynthia's shadow.

Johan smiled.

You little fatty.

They're discussing "tactics" and you're here eavesdropping?

Cynthia patted Gengar's head and took a small sip of ice cream before saying softly:

"Gengar didn't say anything."

Hm?

Johan was stunned.

Then he realized, his eyes widening.

Good grief! So you weren't here as a "spy" but as a "traitor"!

We've got an "inside ghost" in the family!

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar had already floated to Johan's side. Seeing Johan's realization, it immediately wrapped its tongue around the ice cream ball in Johan's hand, swallowing it whole, then dove into the shadows in a diving pose.

Gurgle gurgle—

Looking at the empty cone shell, Johan's mouth twitched slightly.

Johan: "..."

He looked pitifully at Cynthia.

Cynthia's eyes curved with laughter. She moved aside to make room, and Johan sat down beside her, then... took a big bite out of Cynthia's ice cream ball.

Looking at her ice cream now missing nearly half, Cynthia's cheeks immediately puffed up.

But seeing Johan going "hiss hiss" from eating too much cold ice cream at once, she couldn't help but laugh.

"Cough, well, we'll be going first!"

Realizing they were being force-fed "dog food," Diantha immediately pulled the reluctant Caitlin and quickly left the room.

Cynthia's cheeks flushed slightly as she gave Johan an annoyed look.

The two sat quietly for a while.

Johan rubbed his frozen cheek. To keep the ice cream from melting, he had deliberately had Ninetales release cold air. If he had known...

"Johan." Cynthia curled up on the sofa, arms wrapped around her knees, speaking softly.

"Mm?"

"Give it your all."

Johan was startled.

For a trainer like Cynthia, whether she won or lost wasn't actually the most important thing. The most important was giving her all—that was what showed respect!

From her understanding of Johan, Cynthia knew very well that in his previous battles, whether against N, Spinel, or even Nemona, he hadn't given his all.

But...

Cynthia wanted to battle Johan at full strength, not someone holding back or going easy.

Johan raised his hand, hesitating briefly before placing it on Cynthia's head, gently stroking her smooth golden hair.

It seemed Cynthia really wanted to battle him seriously... Johan sighed inwardly.

In that case...

"Can I use legendary Pokémon?" Johan asked out of nowhere.

Cynthia: "?"

She turned her head, a big question mark appearing above it.

Johan rubbed his nose: "Well, although the organizers rule against using 'first-tier legendary' Pokémon, Mewtwo isn't registered in any Pokédex yet."

Cynthia: "?"

Mewtwo?

"Chomp—"

Nearby Garchomp couldn't listen anymore and decided to beat this guy up!

Did he really think having legendary Pokémon meant they'd be afraid?

Bring it on!

At worst... they'd report him after the battle!

Johan grinned and pulled Cynthia into his embrace.

Clearly, he was joking.

"I'll knock your teeth out the day after tomorrow," Cynthia "fiercely" muttered.

"Heh, you can't bear to."

"I'll have Garchomp do it."

Johan: Σ(дlll)

"Chomp!! (▼皿▼)"

After a moment of silence.

"Give it our all, one battle," Johan said softly.

"Yes!"

Leaning in Johan's arms, Cynthia's eyes were full of seriousness.

The sun was shining brightly.

The Wyndon Stadium was bustling with people, and Team Rocket's trio had earned considerable funds from this massive tournament.

"Teacher Johan and Ms. Cynthia's merchandise!"

"Give me one of Teacher Johan's!"

"I want ten of Ms. Cynthia's!"

"Do you have joint merchandise of Teacher Johan and Ms. Cynthia?"

"Of course!" Jessie immediately produced new merchandise.

Soon, the trio's merchandise stall was surrounded by people.

"Official licensing" was exclusive to them alone.

"Eh? You're... not selling Rage Candy Bars anymore?" Elite Four Bruno looked at the merchandise stall somewhat regretfully.

"Ah, that, this, cough cough cough... merchandise is good too, haha..." the trio was sweating profusely.

"Never mind, never mind!" Bruno waved his hand and returned to the group of contestants watching the battle.

"Teacher Johan is amazing, but I still firmly... Big sister is invincible!" Flint dared to ignore little Jirachi's "threats" and firmly stood on Cynthia's side.

This time it wasn't just him—even Lucian firmly supported Cynthia.

No choice.

Sinnoh Champion—as Elite Four of the Sinnoh League, their support was unquestionable.

However, because of the relationship between Teacher Johan and Cynthia, they believed that no matter who they supported, they wouldn't face "retaliation" from Teacher Johan.

"Though I have to admit Cynthia is amazing, I still want to battle Teacher Johan more."

Lance, one of the other semifinalists, said.

He had battled Cynthia many times, both in the past and recently, but his battles with Johan...

No choice—he couldn't sign up for League Conferences, couldn't participate in the "baby cups" that Teacher Johan participated in.

"Then you need to advance first by defeating Mr. Giovanni, Lance," N said with a gentle smile.

"Of course..."

Before he could finish, Lorelei interrupted: "Him? With those Flying-type Pokémon?"

Hahaha—

Everyone laughed knowingly.

"What's so funny?"

A voice suddenly came.

"Professor Oak."

The newcomer was none other than the Pokémon world's authority, Professor Oak.

Beside him were two young people who looked only about ten years old.

"Hello everyone." Professor Oak smiled and greeted everyone.

"Those two..." Lance seemed to recognize the two young people, and after a moment of surprise said: "Aren't they this year's Indigo Conference champion and runner-up?"

Champion and runner-up?

Indigo League?

Everyone was startled.

"Haha—" Professor Oak laughed while stepping aside: "That's right, it's them. Red, Green."

Green pouted, not fond of his runner-up status.

This time he lost to Red, but it didn't matter...

His goal was Champion!

Kanto League Champion!

He might become Kanto League Champion before Red.

Red was quite polite, calling everyone "senior."

They had also come to watch Johan and Cynthia's battle.

Johan had held high expectations for these two from very early on.

Though they were still young, they had already shown their potential.

In just over half a year, they had grown from novice trainers who had just started their journey to conference champion and runner-up.

League Champion might not be their limit!

"It's about to start!"

Green put away his disdain and looked at the huge screen hanging outside the massive stadium, seeing the host about to appear, and immediately said.

Hearing this, everyone temporarily put aside their joking and quickly entered the stadium.

Enthusiastic cheers shook the earth, and when the voices of tens of thousands of spectators came together, even the Wyndon Stadium trembled.

Bang! Bang! Bang—

Brilliant fireworks bloomed in the sky as the commentator walked onto the stage under everyone's gaze.

"Everyone!!"

"Welcome to the stage of our Master Tournament semifinals!!"

The commentator's neck veins bulged—even with a microphone, he had to use all his strength to avoid being drowned out by the audience's cheers.

"The two sides about to battle are—"

The big screen began showing exciting footage, some handsome, some heroic.

"From the Hoenn League, Champion Teacher Johan!!"

"And from the Sinnoh League, Champion Ms. Cynthia!!"

Wave-like cheers immediately connected, echoing through the circular stadium in increasingly intense waves.

"Now! Let's give our most enthusiastic cheers to welcome both contestants!!!"

In the contestant tunnel.

"Phew—"

Cynthia, wearing an elegant and noble black coat, breathed out lightly. Hearing the commentator's voice, she immediately stepped toward the exit.

Time to battle Johan?

As she walked, an elegant, gentle curve appeared on Cynthia's lips.

Though the probability of defeat was high...

She needed to fight to know her shortcomings.

Facing difficulties, as a Champion, she couldn't retreat even slightly, especially for a battle she had long anticipated!

When she walked out of the contestant tunnel, Cynthia's aura had returned to that of the heroic Sinnoh "Queen."

She swept back her golden hair, revealing her fair, delicate, yet determined face, lightly placing one hand on her waist, radiating confident presence as she entered the arena.

"Ms. Cynthia!!"

"It's Cynthia! Lady Cynthia!!"

"Go Ms. Cynthia! Beat Teacher Johan and show him who's boss at home!"

Amid incredibly enthusiastic cheers, the one who appeared was—

Sinnoh League Champion, Cynthia!

On the other side.

"We're all familiar with each other, so..."

Johan was giving final instructions to his Pokémon, looking around at them seriously:

"You don't want to be called 'sandbaggers' when we eat together in the future, do you?"

He clapped his hands: "Get your spirits up. Precisely because our opponents are little Cynthia and her Pokémon, we must show the greatest respect!"

Giving their all was respect!

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar, as the big brother, was the first to raise a fist and speak up, while hurriedly erasing the Cynthia support stickers and such from his body.

Johan glanced at this little fatty.

Hmph!

If there really was an "inside ghost," Gengar was definitely the most suspicious.

But Johan understood.

After all...

If he weren't a contestant, he might become the "inside ghost" himself.

But at this moment, regardless...

They should show their opponents the greatest respect!

"Let's go!"

Johan swept his black cloak, and the Pokémon before him, except Gengar, immediately turned into red lights and returned to the Poké Balls at his waist.

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar took the lead, leading Johan out of the contestant tunnel in a Gengar-style "terrifying entrance ceremony"!

"Teacher Johan! Time to show your boyfriend power!"

"The throne! Teacher Johan, go for it!"

"Ahh—Gengar is so cute!"

At the referee's signal, both approached the center of the field and shook hands.

"The loser..." Cynthia's lips moved.

Johan immediately continued: "Gives the winner leg massages for a month!"

Cynthia was startled.

Was this even punishment for Johan?

But after a brief daze, she smiled beautifully: "Okay."

The referee now had a pained expression.

He had to admit this was the most "ruthless" trash talk he'd heard in his refereeing career—so "ruthless" he felt like he'd eaten a ton of dog food.

Are you two here to compete or show off?

"Damn! Teacher Johan is still the master of showing off! Win or lose, it's all benefits!"

"Damn it, I suddenly have a bad feeling that this is just the beginning..."

"Day 133 of envying Teacher Johan..."

Fortunately, not just the referee, but the live audience and viewers were all fed quite a bit.

After brief communication, at the audience's strong request, Johan and Cynthia returned to their respective halves of the field.

They took their positions.

Swoosh swoosh—

Red and blue flags waved as the referee signaled both contestants to prepare.

At the same time, Cynthia and Johan exchanged glances, both their expressions becoming serious and determined.

"I've anticipated this battle for a long time..." Cynthia murmured softly, grasping her Poké Ball.

Johan also held his Poké Ball, saying quietly: "Then let's battle to our hearts' content!"

"Please, both contestants, summon your Pokémon!"

Two Poké Balls simultaneously landed in the field.

"Please, Milotic!"

"Let's go, Coach Bear!"

Rainbow light flickered and danced.

An elegant, graceful figure leaped into the field with a gentle cry.

Milotic, called the "most beautiful Pokémon"!

On the other side, with mane flowing and imposing presence like breaking bamboo, already in stance, was Coach Bear—Urshifu!

Teacher Johan VS Cynthia!

The battle between the two had officially begun.

Swoosh—

The referee's flag fell.

"Battle begin!!"

# 795: How "Ruthless" Teacher Johan Is!

When Johan first met Cynthia, it was all because of an insecure Feebas.

And that former Feebas had now evolved into the most beautiful Pokémon in the Pokémon world—Milotic!

As half a "matchmaker" between Johan and Cynthia, Milotic actually didn't really want to battle Johan.

But as Cynthia's Pokémon, since Cynthia wanted to battle Johan with everything she had, it would naturally help Cynthia achieve her wish.

So after looking at Johan and Urshifu with slight apology, Milotic's expression became extremely serious.

Milotic was a pure Water-type Pokémon, so its resistances weren't bad—at least at an above-average level.

Furthermore, in terms of base stats alone, Milotic was a typical special defense-oriented Pokémon, belonging to the "special tank" category.

But because of its "Marvel Scale" ability, it could also become quite an excellent "physical tank." Combined, it was an extremely outstanding "water shield" with tremendous staying power.

[Marvel Scale: When afflicted with a status condition, the Pokémon's physical defense is greatly increased!]

"Garruu!"

Urshifu assumed its stance, eyes determined.

Everyone was so familiar with each other that it didn't really want to battle Cynthia either, but—

This concerned Johan's future position in the family hierarchy, so it had to fight!

Sorry!

"Battle begin!"

The referee's words fell.

Hiss—

Before either side had made any moves, a small flame appeared on Milotic's body, with some of its scales being burned to a fiery red color.

However, the flame was quickly suppressed by the water flow surging from Milotic's body.

But the status—

Milotic had entered the "Burn" status!

Its "Marvel Scale" ability triggered, greatly increasing its physical defense!

"It seems Cynthia's Milotic is carrying a 'Flame Orb,'" Leon, continuing as half a commentator, immediately explained the reason for Milotic's change.

Flame Orb!

The holder would be burned by the orb's heat, entering the "Burn" status.

Although the "Burn" status would lower a Pokémon's physical attack, Milotic wasn't a Pokémon that specialized in physical attacks anyway, so the impact wasn't significant.

As for the damage from the "Flame Orb"... the water flow circling Milotic's body was Aqua Ring, and combined with Milotic's water flow suppression, it could be completely ignored.

This method of using Aqua Ring plus its own water flow to suppress the "Flame Orb's" burn was actually a suggestion Johan had made when he and Cynthia were training together last time.

He never expected it would be used against him in battle now...

Johan's mouth twitched slightly.

However, Milotic's "Flame Orb" activation was only momentary, and their battle officially began!

"Milotic, Scald!" Cynthia placed one hand lightly on her slender waist and launched the first attack without hesitation.

"Oowu—"

Milotic opened its mouth slightly, and a stream of scalding water immediately surged out, accompanied by rising hot steam.

Scald was the only Water-type move that could inflict the Burn status on Pokémon. For Urshifu, a physical attack-oriented Pokémon, the "Burn" status posed an enormous threat.

"Urshifu!" Johan shouted. "Use Sucker Punch to dodge!"

"Garruu!"

Urshifu's eyes focused, dark light flickering around its body as it began running at high speed across the field.

"Cover the area!"

"Oowu—"

Milotic turned its direction and continued spitting scalding water, chasing after Urshifu.

Although it couldn't hit Urshifu directly, most of the field had already been covered by the scalding water flow.

This water flow endurance...

But while using Sucker Punch to run, Urshifu was also constantly getting closer to Milotic.

"Urshifu, Wicked Blow!"

"Garruu!"

Bathed in dark light, Urshifu roared explosively and charged toward Milotic at extreme speed with a clenched fist.

"Milotic, wrap yourself in water flow!" Cynthia immediately called out.

"Oowu~"

The scalding water wrapped around itself. With the protection of Aqua Ring, plus already carrying the "Flame Orb," Milotic wasn't worried about its own hot water burning itself.

Protect wouldn't work against Urshifu, but the water flow...

Seeing this scene, Johan could only sigh that it was truly worthy of being a "water shield."

For Urshifu to hit Milotic, it would have to pass through that ring of hot water, which risked being burned.

But if it didn't pass through the hot water, relying solely on Urshifu's special attack ability obviously wouldn't cause much substantial damage to Milotic.

So...

"Urshifu!" Johan took a deep breath and said sternly: "Continue!"

Urshifu understood Johan's implication. While approaching, it took a deep breath and closed its eyes.

Gurgle gurgle—

Water flow also surged from Urshifu's body, wrapping around its arms.

Aqua Jet!

Bang!!!

A tremendously powerful punch passed through Milotic's scalding water flow. Because of the barrier from its own water flow, Urshifu wasn't immediately burned.

Got you!

The next moment.

"Close Combat!!"

"Garruu!!"

Urshifu's eyes snapped open, and its fists rained down like a violent storm. Milotic was immediately sent flying out of its own condensed water flow, with Urshifu following close behind.

"Watch out!" Johan used his aura power to send a warning directly into Urshifu's mind.

Although Milotic was flying backward, its agile body allowed it to quickly regain balance.

"Water Pulse!" Cynthia immediately commanded.

"Oowu!!"

Its mouth bulged, instantly spitting out an extremely turbulent water column—not only to attack Urshifu but also to create distance from it.

As a "water shield," Milotic's specialty wasn't just dual defenses; its recovery ability was equally outstanding.

Aqua Ring, Life Dew, Recover, and other moves.

As long as it could maintain sufficient distance from Urshifu, it could immediately restore its stamina.

When it came to staying power, it was absolutely serious!

Johan, who understood this point equally well, naturally knew Milotic's characteristics. Therefore, once Urshifu launched a fierce attack, it absolutely couldn't stop. Otherwise, no matter how strong Urshifu's abilities were, as long as it couldn't defeat Milotic in one go, the result would be the same...

Even if Urshifu wasn't afraid of prolonged battles either, delays could lead to complications—

"Urshifu, Detect!"

At the most crucial moment, Urshifu's mind became unified. Facing the Water Pulse that Milotic spat out, confronting that continuous turbulent water flow, it threw another punch without hesitation.

One of the mysteries of the Rapid Strike Style was breaking through water!

Crack crack crack—

Punch after punch was thrown in a dazzling display. The Water Pulse instantly scattered like fireworks, and Urshifu's pursuit of Milotic not only didn't slow down but became even faster!

Cynthia frowned slightly, feeling the strong pressure from Urshifu's continuous attacks.

She clenched her fist slightly.

"Milotic, wrap yourself in Scald again!"

If she could make Urshifu enter the "Burn" status, even at the cost of significant stamina, it would be acceptable!

The Water Pulse stopped, and the landing Milotic spat out Scald again. Urshifu instantly appeared before it, eyes half-closed, stance ready.

Quite the... Domain Expansion appearance.

"Urshifu," Johan called out. "Use Wicked Blow!"

"Garruu!!"

Some water flow appeared on Urshifu's fist again—not the water from Aqua Jet, but the water remaining on its body from Milotic's Water Pulse, as well as the water vapor floating in the air after the water splashes scattered!

Water Breaking! Strike Breaking!

The stationary Urshifu displayed fighting techniques to their peak, stepping forward with its lower body, concentrating all power into its fist.

Boom!!

In an instant.

The hot water wrapping Milotic exploded apart. This time Milotic didn't fly backward but was heavily slammed into the ground.

Critical hit!

"Oowu..." The fallen Milotic struggled to look at the majestic Urshifu once, then spirals appeared in its eyes as it collapsed.

"Milotic is unable to battle!" The referee waved the flag and announced the result.

So fast!

Cheers erupted in the stadium.

The first match between Teacher Johan and Miss Cynthia had no lovey-dovey moments—only both sides using their full arsenal without reservation.

Fast! Exciting!

"Garruu..." Urshifu lowered its fists, looking apologetically at the fallen Milotic.

Milotic's defense had been excellent, and Scald had once made it feel very troubled, but—

"Gururu~" Master Mustard, hands in pockets, nodded approvingly. "The combination of Rapid Strike and Single Strike mysteries is becoming increasingly smooth, gururu~"

Cynthia recalled Milotic and pressed her lips together.

Milotic had done very well. After all, it was a Pokémon built for defensive counterattacks. If it could have withstood Urshifu's continuous fierce assault, the result would have been different.

But Johan's Urshifu, after combining the mysteries of Single Strike and Rapid Strike styles, had explosive power that was too strong. Once the defense failed, it would become the current situation.

After thinking briefly, she took out her second Poké Ball.

"Please, Spiritomb!"

White light flickered, and an ancient keystone fell onto the field. A ghostly face covered in emerald green light points emerged with a cold aura.

"Sii, siipaa—"

Upon seeing Johan, Spiritomb immediately blushed, its misty ghostly face curling up with pleasing eyes.

It had to... to face Johan?

Such a difficult opponent...

But feeling Cynthia's gaze from behind, Spiritomb's body trembled slightly. Its curled ghostly face spread out, and an aura filled with coldness and chill immediately swept over.

No!

Serious battle!

This was both Cynthia's wish and respect for Johan!

The corners of Johan's mouth curved slightly.

We have our "undercover agents" too.

Taking out a Poké Ball.

"Teacher Johan takes the first lead, but he recalls Urshifu and substitutes—Alolan Ninetales!"

Ice Ninetales!

Whether Milotic or Spiritomb, both were very typical "shield" class Pokémon with strong targeting against opponents who specialized in physical attacks.

And Spiritomb, as a Dark and Ghost-type Pokémon, had even more obvious advantages against Fighting-type Urshifu—whether it was Ghost-type immunity to Fighting moves or the counterforce from its mastered move "Foul Play."

This was an important reason why Johan recalled Urshifu.

There was another reason...

Cough cough.

Although Urshifu had broken through Milotic's Scald, Coach Bear's paws were already red from the heat. If it didn't go rest soon, they might end up enjoying a "roasted bear paw" meal together.

Furthermore.

Spiritomb was a Pokémon with quite excellent type combinations—not only immune to Normal, Fighting, and Psychic types, but only weak to Fairy-type.

Considering everything comprehensively, Ice Ninetales was obviously the best choice.

Since he had promised Cynthia not to hold back, Johan would stand on the side of the most correct choice.

"Oowoo~~"

As soon as Ice Ninetales appeared, the temperature in the huge stadium dropped sharply, and large snowflakes began falling.

As one of Johan's ace Pokémon, Ice Ninetales' appearance also caused quite a stir.

"Ice Ninetales?! This is the first time Teacher Johan has sent out Ice Ninetales since participating in the Master Tournament!"

"Good grief! Light touches against Chairman Nemona, heavy punches against his girlfriend, is that it?"

"Tsk tsk, Teacher Johan has prepared to go home and kneel on the washboard tonight!"

Seeing Ice Ninetales, Spiritomb's ghostly face couldn't help but curl up again.

"Siipaa... (//▽//)"

"Spiritomb, Confuse Ray!" Cynthia struck first.

Since Johan had switched Pokémon, the initiative remained in her hands.

"Siipaa—"

The alerted Spiritomb immediately put away its little thoughts. From its ghostly face floating with irregular emerald green spots, several light points glowing with pale green fluorescence immediately drifted out, floating toward Ice Ninetales from all directions.

"Ninetales!"

"Oowoo!"

Crack crack crack—

Layer upon layer of ice crystals quickly built up around Ice Ninetales. Spiritomb's Confuse Ray hit the thick ice walls and immediately disappeared.

When it came to "shields," our Ice Ninetales was also the "strongest shield"!

"Watch behind you!" Johan suddenly shouted.

Ice Ninetales seemed to have sensed it already and immediately leaped forward gracefully, avoiding the black shadow-like tentacles that emerged from its shadow behind it.

Night Shade!

Just as Johan understood Cynthia's Pokémon well, Cynthia naturally also understood Johan's Pokémon well.

She knew exactly what Ice Ninetales was like and had already prepared some strategies for dealing with it before the match began.

It was just unfortunate that Johan and Ice Ninetales seemed to have predicted it.

She couldn't give Spiritomb more opportunities to "probe." Otherwise, whether it was Pain Split, Destiny Bond, or other moves, they would all make her and Ice Ninetales feel uncomfortable.

So...

"Ninetales!" Johan said sternly: "Moonblast!"

"Oowoo!!"

Ice Ninetales, having avoided Night Shade, moved like a spirit in the wind and snow, its nine tails spreading out as the power of moonlight descended from high above under its guidance.

At the same time.

On Ice Ninetales' body, one could faintly see a palm-sized pink stone plate, barely visible, emanating a soft glow.

Pixie Plate!

At this moment, the aura emanating from Ice Ninetales could truly be called "Pressure." All the audience felt their breathing freeze, as if the entire moon was about to fall!

Leon in the commentary booth gripped the microphone tightly and spoke in a deep voice:

"Legendary!"

Two words represented everything.

Ice Ninetales' strength had already reached legendary levels in its normal state!

The moonlight fell on Spiritomb.

Boom!!

It was like a meteorite crashing down. The ground where Spiritomb was located immediately collapsed, churning up rolling waves of smoke.

"Oowoo—"

Ice Ninetales' pale blue eyes quietly watched the scattering dust and snow.

When it dispersed.

"Siipaa..." Spiritomb's eerie ghostly face retreated into the keystone and fell silent.

"Spi-Spiritomb is unable to battle!" The referee swallowed and announced the result again.

Whoaa—

The entire venue immediately erupted in an uproar.

"It-it one-shot it?!"

"Holy crap! Is this Teacher Johan's true strength? A legendary-level Ice Ninetales?"

"What-what item was it carrying? That explosive power is too terrifying..."

"Hiss—I really admire Teacher Johan's courage. This time, kneeling on the washboard won't solve it when he goes home, right?"

Many people admired Teacher Johan's "courage."

Having lost two Pokémon, Cynthia remained relatively calm, showing little emotional change on her face.

In fact, seeing Johan truly battling without reservation, she felt somewhat gratified.

Finally showing some real skill.

At the same time, Cynthia was also somewhat emotional.

When she first met Johan, he didn't even have much desire to become a trainer. Now...

He had clearly become one of this world's top trainers. Even she could feel the strength gap between herself and Johan.

However—

If she was expected to give up her fighting spirit just from this, that was impossible!

Cynthia threw out her third Poké Ball.

"Miss Cynthia's third Pokémon is—Lucario!!"

"Luca!"

The majestic Lucario appeared on the field with flowing mane, pale blue aura power surging around its body like a tangible substance.

Ice Ninetales had a quadruple weakness to Steel-type. For dealing with Ice Ninetales, Lucario was undoubtedly the best choice.

Moreover, Lucario's nearly tangible aura power would undoubtedly allow it to better lock onto Ice Ninetales.

While Cynthia's other Pokémon might have various emotions when facing Johan, Lucario wouldn't.

It wasn't that its relationship with Johan was bad, but as a fighter, Lucario clearly understood what it was doing now and what it needed to do.

Cynthia had already lost two Pokémon. It had to make a breakthrough!

"Oowoo!"

Ice Ninetales suddenly let out a low cry, a muffled sound coming from its body as black mist accompanied by eerie laughter rose, its momentum visibly decreasing.

Hm?

Seeing this scene, Johan was stunned.

Spiritomb's parting gift?

He could tell what the black mist rising from Ice Ninetales was, but he hadn't expected when Spiritomb had given Ice Ninetales this parting gift.

No wonder Cynthia hadn't reacted much when seeing Spiritomb get one-shotted by Ice Ninetales...

[Memento: At the cost of losing the ability to battle, greatly reduces the opponent's special attack and physical attack abilities.]

This was why Johan said Ghost-type Pokémon like Spiritomb would be very troublesome if not dealt with quickly.

Cynthia immediately raised her hand.

"Lucario, Extreme Speed, Meteor Mash!"

# 796: Advantage Is Mine!

"Luca!"

Lucario didn't hesitate for a moment, seizing the gap when Ice Ninetales was affected by Spiritomb's final Memento, and rushed toward Ice Ninetales with Extreme Speed.

Its clenched fist was covered with a heavy metallic luster.

Even if Ice Ninetales' ice walls and Aurora Veil blocked its path, it would break through with overwhelming momentum.

Legendary?

Not unbeatable!

Lucario's gaze was stern, its expression focused. Looking at Ice Ninetales, its aura power had already been pushed to the limit.

Time seemed slow, but it was actually only an instant.

When Lucario swept through all of Ice Ninetales' ice walls and Aurora Veil and rushed to its front, Ice Ninetales was nowhere to be seen.

Johan now held two Poké Balls in his hand.

"Teacher Johan switches Pokémon again!"

Having the quadruply Steel-weak Ice Ninetales battle Lucario was obviously not a wise choice, especially when weakened by Spiritomb's Memento. Even with superior strength, but...

Lucario's current condition was exceptionally good.

In Johan's mind, he recalled the scene from when he and Cynthia were training together recently, when he practiced aura power with Lucario.

Honestly, as a half-baked user, Johan had indeed learned quite a few techniques for using aura power from Lucario, filling in his shortcomings in aura power application.

Johan's aura power wasn't weak either, but his use of aura power, aside from sensing Pokémon, only had one method: infusion! Infusion! Infusion!

But correspondingly, Lucario, as the most typical aura Pokémon, had also made noticeable progress in aura power through training with Johan.

At the time, Cynthia had jokingly said that Johan paired with Lucario would make a complete "Aura Guardian."

"Since it's Lucario, I'm counting on you!"

Johan threw out a Poké Ball.

"Teacher Johan's substitute Pokémon is—Ceruledge!"

Ghost and Fire-type Ceruledge—Ghost-type could immune to Fighting-type moves, and Fire-type had an advantage over Steel-type.

"Wow, today's Teacher Johan is surprisingly 'rational,'" Lucian sighed.

Given Teacher Johan's usual nature, even if Ice Ninetales was countered by Lucario and had its dual attacks reduced, he wouldn't have switched Pokémon so quickly.

After all.

All trainers were more or less enthusiastic about "reverse type matchups," especially when Ice Ninetales had the advantage in strength.

"I'm even worried for Teacher Johan," Flint said, stroking his chin with a "wise" expression.

He didn't dare imagine how Teacher Johan and big sister would get along after this battle ended...

Just thinking about it was brutal.

While Lucian and the others were sighing, Cynthia had already launched another attack without hesitation.

Ceruledge did have a type advantage, but the type matchup wasn't as significant as between Lucario and Ice Ninetales.

More importantly.

She had to seize the gap when switching Pokémon.

"Lucario, Bone Rush!" Cynthia raised her hand and pointed.

"Luca!"

Lucario brought its palms together and pulled them apart, and a azure bone club immediately appeared in its hand. Wielding the bone club, it slammed toward the newly entered Ceruledge.

"Goka!"

Ceruledge's aura sensed Lucario's approach and raised both swords to block, but suddenly found that its aura sensing... became blurred all at once.

Lucario's aura was interfering with Ceruledge!

Bang!!

A bone club strike landed, hitting Ceruledge and sending it flying.

"Luca!"

Lucario's eyes blazed as it directly pursued the sent-flying Ceruledge, swinging its bone club.

"Ceruledge!" Johan immediately shouted: "Will-O-Wisp!"

The flying Ceruledge struggled to control its body, releasing several ghostly blue flames.

Bang! Bang! Bang—

Lucario swung its bone club, and all the Will-O-Wisps were smashed into scattering sparks before they could reach it.

But the slight delay in Lucario's pursuit gave Ceruledge a gap to control its body. Its sharp blade stabbed directly into the ground, carving a charred furrow while barely managing to control its form.

Crack—

Cracks appeared on its armor.

Wisps of ghostly blue firelight leaked out.

"Lucario, Earthquake!"

Seeing that the pursuit had failed, Cynthia immediately changed tactics.

"Luca!"

The alerted Lucario slammed its bone club hard into the ground.

Rumble rumble—

The entire ground immediately shook with Lucario at the center, and earth-colored Ground-type energy formed waves of earth that crashed toward Ceruledge.

Besides being Fighting and Steel-type, Lucario, like Dragonite, could master a vast array of moves with broad coverage.

"Shadow Sneak, Ceruledge!"

Swoosh—

A sword slashed through the void, opening a gap, and Ceruledge disappeared from Lucario's sight in the blink of an eye.

Earthquake missed.

However, neither Cynthia nor Lucario became discouraged.

"Lucario, sense where it will appear!"

"Luca!"

Lucario closed its eyes.

Aura!

Suddenly.

Lucario's eyes snapped open.

Found it!

The bone club in its hand immediately transformed into a mass of azure energy that gathered in its fist, then it suddenly turned and threw a punch.

Just as it swung, a void crack appeared behind it, and twin swords wrapped in ghostly blue flames slashed out.

Clang!!

The collision of fist and twin swords was like metal clashing with metal.

Bang—

From the tremendous force collision, both Pokémon flew backward almost simultaneously, sliding for some distance before each stabilizing their forms.

"Luca!" The bone club reappeared in Lucario's hand as it looked at Ceruledge and swung it lightly.

Recalling when Ceruledge was still Charcadet, following behind it and Tinkaton to learn, now... it had also grown to this extent.

"Goka!"

Flames constantly surged from Ceruledge's body, its burning eyes staring intently at Lucario.

Its every movement was completely visible under Lucario's aura perception, while its own aura was heavily suppressed by Lucario's.

Really uncomfortable...

"Lucario, Dark Pulse!" Cynthia suddenly called out loudly.

"Luca!"

Lucario brought its palms together again, with rich black Dark-type energy surging from its palms, as if it would devour everything.

Just as Lucario had condensed Dark Pulse, it suddenly froze, because it discovered that its opponent had also aimed a golden cannon barrel at it.

Within it, crimson firelight flickered as energy accumulated.

"Teacher Johan's Ceruledge has turned into Armarouge again!!" the commentator shouted.

By now, everyone had accepted the fact that Teacher Johan's Ceruledge could transform into Armarouge.

Many people wanted to replicate it, but found...

It was impossible.

"Armarouge, Flamethrower!!" Johan shouted loudly.

Hum—

"Goka!"

From Armarouge's golden cannon barrel, the accumulated flames instantly burst forth.

At the same time, Lucario's Dark Pulse also unleashed freely.

Two forces collided head-on in the field.

Since close-combat swordsmanship couldn't deal substantial damage, then... rely on long-range!

Boom!!

Dark Pulse and flames erupted in thunder.

Obviously.

Flamethrower, released as a same-type move, was more powerful than Lucario's Dark Pulse.

But after the clash with Dark Pulse, the remaining flames from Flamethrower weren't enough to cause much substantial damage to Lucario.

"Aura Sphere!" Cynthia said sternly.

"Luca!"

Rich aura power gathered again in Lucario's palms.

"Armarouge!" Johan also spoke at this moment, "Aura Sphere!"

"Goka—!!"

Armarouge's arms joined together again, air waves rippled from the golden cannon barrel, and an azure cannonball shot out, piercing the entire field!

Rumble rumble—

"Aura versus aura?"

"I didn't expect Teacher Johan's Armarouge to have such powerful aura power?"

Dust clouds rose on the field.

"Lucario!" Cynthia's eyes sharpened, "Extreme Speed, Bone Rush!"

In the dust.

Lucario, wrapped in white light, rapidly charged at Armarouge.

Whether Ceruledge or Armarouge, their characteristics were very distinct—one specialized in close combat, the other in long-range.

So, using long-range against Ceruledge and close combat against Armarouge definitely wouldn't be wrong.

Moreover.

Whether Ceruledge or Armarouge, their ability was Weak Armor. Although taking attacks would increase speed, defense would inevitably decrease.

They had to take down the opponent before it could maximize its speed and use speed to kite Lucario!

"Ceruledge, Bitter Blade!"

Clang!!!

In the dust, the collision of bone club and sharp sword accompanied by sparkling sparks, the resulting shockwave immediately dispersed the surrounding dust.

Armarouge had transformed back into Ceruledge!

"Close Combat!"

Lucario combined Close Combat with Bone Rush and swung out.

"Ceruledge, we'll use Close Combat too!"

If Lucario could combine Bone Rush with Close Combat, Ceruledge could naturally combine Close Combat with Bitter Blade!

The next second...

Clang! Clang! Clang! Clang...

Two figures flashed continuously across the field, bone club and blade constantly colliding, instantly engaging in no fewer than dozens of collisions, dazzling the audience whose naked eyes couldn't capture the action.

Worthy of being Cynthia's Lucario, second only to Garchomp, this was already very close to legendary level.

In the seemingly evenly matched collision, Ceruledge was actually at a disadvantage.

Moreover...

Cynthia and Lucario had obviously grasped Ceruledge's "Weak Armor" characteristic.

Taking attacks would greatly increase speed, but defense would continuously decrease.

But Ceruledge had advantages too—its endurance in prolonged battles was much stronger than Lucario's.

Whether Bitter Blade or "Life Fire," both could continuously provide stamina to Ceruledge.

Suddenly.

"Above!" Johan's expression changed slightly.

"Goka!"

Ceruledge immediately raised its blades.

Bang!!

The bone club hammered down heavily.

But it was blocked.

Ceruledge's aura power was suppressed by Lucario, but with Johan added, it was different...

"Now!" Cynthia shouted loudly: "Earthquake, Lucario!"

The bone club immediately transmitted vibrations.

If this hit connected solidly...

"Ceruledge!"

Johan roared: "Armor Cannon!"

Ceruledge? Armor Cannon?

Everyone who heard this was stunned, but when everyone focused their eyes, they discovered that the other arm of Ceruledge holding a blade had actually transformed into a thick golden cannon barrel!

"Holy crap! Cheating, right?"

"Ceruledge and Armarouge? Isn't this two-on-one?"

"Others have sword and blade unity, but you have sword and cannon unity?!"

Since it would be caught for defensive weaknesses and continuously attacked, then why not... be more decisive!

Defense?

What is defense?

Whether as a swordsman or as a gunner, defense was never what it excelled at, never what it should do!

Hiss—

The ghostly blue flames wrapped around the blade instantly extinguished, and all the flames on Ceruledge's body disappeared in an instant.

But the golden cannon barrel it held up now became scorching hot and red-hot.

In Lucario's eyes, along with amazement, the fiery red color became increasingly obvious and bright.

The scorching high temperature rose.

In an instant.

Rumble rumble—

The ground shook violently, terrifying earthquake aftershocks scattered in all directions, and rolling dust and smoke thundered upward.

But at the same time.

Bang!!

The blazing fire pillar erupted.

The heat wave instantly dispersed the dust and smoke.

When everyone saw the devastated scene on the field, they all instinctively gasped.

The referee ran out from the rising dust and heat waves. Seeing this scene, he was also stunned for a moment, but his professional training made him immediately wave the flag.

"Lu-Lucario and Ceruledge are both unable to battle!"

Whoaa—

The entire venue erupted.

Actually...

Both lost the ability to battle simultaneously?!

"Gulp—for a moment, I can't tell if it's Teacher Johan who's 'ruthless' or if both of them are 'ruthless'..."

"Say they're lovers? Who would believe that?"

"But... this way, the score between Miss Cynthia and Teacher Johan is 3:1!"

"Damn! This wouldn't be Teacher Johan's 'gentleness,' would it? I really..."

Gentleness?

If Johan heard this, he would definitely sneer.

Buddy, didn't you see I'm already sweating profusely?

"One side has lost three Pokémon, entering halftime!"

The referee immediately waved the flag to announce a brief break and field change.

Cynthia stood at the sideline with her hands on her hips, face cold, silently thinking about the upcoming battle situation.

She had already lost three Pokémon and had three left, but had only taken out Johan's Ceruledge. Johan still had five left...

In terms of pure numbers, the advantage and disadvantage were clear at a glance.

Cynthia was also at a very obvious disadvantage.

But...

That didn't mean there was no way to fight.

"Miss Cynthia's expression looks so cold..."

"I feel like Teacher Johan is done for."

"For a moment, I can't tell if it's battle calmness or 'calmness' toward Teacher Johan."

At the same time.

"You worked hard, Ceruledge. You did very well, rest for a bit."

After solemnly putting away the Poké Ball, Johan murmured softly:

"She didn't choose Lucario for Mega Evolution?"

He had initially guessed that Cynthia might use Mega Evolution on Lucario to catch him off guard.

After all, from Lucario's previous condition...

If it Mega Evolved, Lucario should also be able to cross the legendary threshold and would definitely cause Johan considerable trouble.

But Cynthia didn't choose Lucario, so Johan's pressure was undoubtedly greater.

Garchomp...

That shark lady was probably the one with the highest "hatred value" toward him among all of Cynthia's Pokémon, right?

Definitely going for the kill!

Headache.

Soon, the field change was completed.

"Please, both contestants, get ready!"

Since both players' Pokémon had been defeated, they would summon Pokémon together after halftime.

Johan and Cynthia looked at each other.

They threw out Poké Balls simultaneously.

"Go, Togekiss!"

"It's your turn, Dragonite!"

Two red lights flashed, and two flying Pokémon immediately appeared in the arena's airspace.

"Togepi~" The snow-white Togekiss with red and blue triangular markings like a glider flew through the air, feeling the airflow in the arena.

"Draaa~(ˊˋ)"

Dragonite flapped its small wings, blowing wind that Togekiss liked toward it.

"Togepi~(ˊˋ)"

For a moment, the two little ones—one blowing wind on the field, the other riding the "tailwind" going "woo woo woo."

If Togekiss could talk, it would definitely sing "Little Windmill."

Seeing the two Pokémon playing so happily on the field, whether the live audience or those watching the broadcast were dumbfounded.

No...

In the first half, Teacher Johan and Miss Cynthia were fighting so fiercely, looking like they wanted to "crush" each other.

How did the second half...

How did the art style change so quickly?

Dragonite and Togekiss started playing "airplane" together?

However...

Anyone who wasn't blind could see that Teacher Johan's Dragonite and Miss Cynthia's Togekiss had a really good relationship.

How could it not be good?

Among Johan's Pokémon, there was only Dragonite as a Flying-type Pokémon, and among all of Cynthia's Pokémon, there was also only Togekiss.

Just two Flying-type Pokémon playing together during training breaks—how could their relationship not be good?

The battling duo also watched this scene.

A gentle smile appeared on Cynthia's cold face.

Johan's originally somewhat tense expression also relaxed considerably when he saw the smile on Cynthia's face.

Still gotta be Dragonite!

Still gotta be our Old Jing family's "Emperor of Luck"—just came on stage and already relieved the biggest "pressure."

Now they could fight properly again.

Speaking of which...

Cynthia's Togekiss had the "Serene Grace" ability and was a typical Pokémon that relied on luck to "make a living."

And our Old Jing family's Dragonite was also a Pokémon that relied on "Heavenly Father" for meals. Last time when battling Chairman Nemona, it didn't use much of its usually accumulated "luck."

Then let's have a proper, dignified showdown between "luck users"!

Johan grinned and chuckled softly.

Heh.

Advantage is mine!
