# 797: Invincible Garchomp, Enter!

Seeing the two Pokémon playing so happily, even the referee subconsciously forgot to announce the start of the battle.

Only when reminded by the commentator did he snap back to reality, realizing that it was precisely because he hadn't announced the start of the battle that the two Pokémon could play so happily.

"Ahem!" The referee coughed a few times to ease the embarrassment while hurriedly announcing:

"Halftime is over, battle begin!"

Togekiss VS Dragonite!

Hearing the referee's voice, Togekiss and Dragonite immediately ended their play, putting away the smiles on their faces and becoming serious.

Playing around in normal times was fine, but now they were on the battlefield, in battle!

This concerned their respective trainers' future "family status"!

"In terms of type matchups, Togekiss has a big advantage. It seems big sister judged that Teacher Johan would substitute Dragonite during halftime," Lucian said thoughtfully.

"Big sister is amazing!" Flint had already become Cynthia's loyal supporter.

"However, in terms of strength, Teacher's Dragonite obviously has more advantage than Cynthia's Togekiss," Steven analyzed rationally.

No one disagreed with this point.

Cynthia's Togekiss was clearly trained later than Milotic and the others. Although it had now grown to a level where it could hold its own on the Master Tournament stage, but—

The opponent was Johan after all, this person currently known to be the closest to "legendary" status!

"But if big sister's Togekiss gets into a favorable situation, Dragonite might even be controlled to death," Lucian had seen that kind of Togekiss—six consecutive Air Slash hits, even gods would have to kneel.

"Teacher's Dragonite... for some reason, also has exceptionally good luck," Steven said. He had also witnessed Teacher Johan's Dragonite's luck explosion moments.

Not just him—quite a few people present had seen it.

But...

Lucian and Steven looked at each other.

Had it become a "luck showdown"?

While they were discussing, the battle on the field had already begun.

"Togekiss, Tailwind!"

"Togepi~!"

Togekiss flapped its wings, and an airflow appeared behind it. Riding the wind made it faster and more agile, while Dragonite, facing the headwind, naturally suffered obstruction from the airflow.

"Dragonite, Thunder Wave!"

Johan waved his hand and called out.

"Draaa~!"

Fluffy golden electric currents formed between the two antennae on Dragonite's head, flickering with arcs as they flew toward Togekiss.

"Cut through it, Air Slash!" Cynthia shouted.

Hum—hum—

As Togekiss flapped its wings, two crescent-shaped pale blue blades headed toward the Thunder Wave and Dragonite.

Bang!!

The fluffy golden electric current was directly split down the middle, scattering into golden arcs in all directions.

The other Air Slash flew toward Dragonite.

"Dodge with Extreme Speed!"

White light enveloped Dragonite as it flapped its wings, avoiding Togekiss's Air Slash at extreme speed.

"Heh," Johan chuckled. "Dragonite, Ice Punch!"

Extreme Speed helped Dragonite avoid Togekiss while frost condensed on its fist.

"Togekiss, Sweet Kiss!" Cynthia's expression changed slightly.

"Togepi~ゝ)ノ"

Pink hearts were visible to the naked eye, flying toward Dragonite.

Sweet Kiss with 75% accuracy could hit our Dragonite?

A mere 75% accuracy rate—wasn't that the same as having no accuracy?

Of course, symbolic dodging was still necessary.

Johan immediately said: "Dodge, Dragonite, Ice Punch!"

Pop~

The pink balloon-like heart exploded in front of Dragonite, and Fairy-type energy wrapped in affection made Dragonite's movements suddenly stall.

Johan: "..."

Dragonite's "European luck" didn't work?

Could it be that the "European" encountered the "Emperor of Europe," and the "European luck" was suppressed?

In the audience seats.

Lucian and Steven looked at each other again, both their expressions somewhat strange.

Flint laughed heartily and said: "Facts prove that when two Pokémon both have very good luck, the trainer's luck becomes decisive!"

Only Flint dared to speak such blunt truths.

Johan on the field gritted his teeth, "Dragonite!"

Mere confusion couldn't completely restrict Dragonite. Johan, who started with Pokémon psychology, was confident about this.

The usual psychological training wasn't for nothing.

At least now when he battled, very few trainers dared to have their Pokémon use Hypnosis on his Pokémon.

But the brief stall was enough for Togekiss to create distance.

"Togekiss, once more, Air Slash!"

"Togepi~!"

While rapidly retreating, Togekiss flapped its wings and again slashed out two crescent-shaped Air Slashes.

"Draaa!"

Dragonite shook its head to clear up.

"Dragonite, Dragon Pulse!" Johan's voice came.

Hum—

Blue-purple energy condensed and scattered, dispersing the two Air Slashes.

Johan seemed somewhat unconvinced and called out again, "Dragonite, Air Slash!"

"Draaa!!"

Dragonite flapped its wings and also slashed out two pale blue air crescents toward Togekiss.

Cynthia half-closed her eyes, "Togekiss, Air Slash!"

The same two air crescents were slashed out by Togekiss.

But—

When everyone thought these Air Slashes would collide and dissipate—

The four Air Slashes actually brushed past each other!

Cynthia and Togekiss actually chose to trade Air Slash blows with Dragonite, trading luck?!

Bang bang—

Bang bang—

Four Air Slashes respectively hit Togekiss and Dragonite.

Togekiss cried out in pain, while Dragonite...

Johan: "..."

Could it really... be my problem?

Johan seemed to recall scenes of his own Air Slash missing seven times in a row...

No!

It must be the field's problem!

This field today, at a glance... at a glance it wasn't maintained very well!

And the weather!

Today's weather was really terrible, not a cloud in the sky, the sun so bright...

Cynthia smiled, "Togekiss, continue, Air Slash!"

Crescent-shaped air slashes came at tricky angles toward Dragonite.

Bang—bang—

"Togekiss, again!"

Just then.

Johan suddenly shouted loudly: "Dragonite!!"

"Draaa!"

Whether it was an illusion or not, Johan felt that Dragonite's glance toward him carried some "resentment."

"...Outrage!"

The best way to counter this kind of psychological influence was to use a more powerful and unreasonable emotion to "crush" it!

"Although Dragonite was affected by Air Slash, Teacher Johan's Dragonite obviously 'breaks free' from Air Slash's influence much faster than ordinary Pokémon," commentator Leon said.

Ordinary Pokémon might be controlled until the battle ended.

"Draaa!!"

Dark red airflow quickly rose around Dragonite, releasing violent aura wantonly.

Johan's expression was stern, not because of Dragonite's Air Slash missing, but because—

Cough.

"Dragonite, Ice Punch!"

"Draaa!!"

Taking advantage of Togekiss using Air Slash, when old strength was spent and new strength hadn't been born, it directly broke through the Air Slash and appeared before Togekiss in the blink of an eye.

The massive fist condensed with frost struck down mercilessly.

Bang!!

Togekiss was directly smashed from the air to the ground by Dragonite.

Rumble rumble—

The newly replaced field immediately cracked with numerous fissures.

When the thick smoke dispersed, Togekiss had already fallen unconscious.

The referee waved the flag, "Togekiss is unable to battle!"

The stadium shook continuously.

"Just like that, one-shot?"

"I can make countless mistakes, but you can only make one mistake!"

"No, Miss Cynthia and Togekiss didn't make any mistakes at all, right? It's clearly Teacher Johan's Dragonite being unreasonable..."

"Did he win? It seems Teacher Johan won, but actually lost quite thoroughly!"

Did he win?

Johan decided to let Dragonite "calm down" first, and let himself calm down too.

This was the first time he and Dragonite had lost to an opponent in terms of "luck" in battle...

Even if this opponent was Cynthia.

"Teacher Johan recalls Dragonite!" the commentator shouted. "He'll probably wait until Miss Cynthia's Pokémon enters before completing the substitution!"

The word "again" was used quite appropriately.

Now, Cynthia had already lost four Pokémon, with only two remaining—one of which had to be Garchomp, and the other...

Cynthia took a deep breath.

Honestly.

She felt considerable pressure.

She also clearly felt the "oppression" brought by Johan's strength.

Johan going all out... had the strength gap between them really become this large?

But thinking again.

Wasn't this exactly what she wanted to see?

So...

Give it everything, no regrets!

"Please, Gastrodon!" Cynthia threw her fifth Poké Ball, also summoning what was strictly speaking her last suspenseful Pokémon.

Bang—

The seemingly small but heavy Gastrodon landed on the field.

"Gastrodon, Stockpile!"

As soon as Gastrodon appeared, Cynthia immediately commanded, putting pressure on Johan who was switching Pokémon.

"Oowu!"

A mysterious force appeared out of thin air, then was swallowed by Gastrodon, and its physical and special defense abilities were enhanced accordingly.

Fortunately, Johan wasn't Spinel—he wouldn't hesitate much when switching Pokémon.

The combination of Water and Ground types allowed Gastrodon to avoid disadvantages when facing most type Pokémon, except when facing Grass-types...

Quadruple weakness to Grass was Gastrodon's biggest weakness.

So there wasn't much to hesitate about. Johan threw a Poké Ball and summoned Serperior!

For this battle with Cynthia, considering that Cynthia had Gastrodon, Milotic, and Ground-type Garchomp, Johan brought Serperior.

Not Glaceon, which was somewhat surprising.

But thinking carefully...

Pure Ice-type Glaceon had many weaknesses—Fighting, Rock, Steel, Fire... corresponding exactly to Urshifu, Tyranitar, Tinkaton, and Ceruledge.

Among Johan's Pokémon, only Dragonite and Serperior seemed to fear Ice-types...

Too many weaknesses, limited targeting—this was why Cynthia didn't bring Glaceon this time.

If battling Lance, then without hesitation, Glaceon would definitely be second only to Garchomp, with priority even higher than Lucario!

"Sni!"

Serperior's expression was serious.

This was a stadium, not a forest—its abilities couldn't be fully utilized.

"Serperior, Leaf Storm!"

As soon as Serperior landed, Johan immediately commanded.

"Sni!"

Emerald green leaves appeared out of thin air. Serperior's tail swayed, immediately stirring up numerous leaves like a braid dozens of times thicker, lashing toward Gastrodon.

"Gastrodon!" Cynthia's eyes flickered, seeming to guess who Johan would send to deal with her Gastrodon, so she calmly commanded: "Blizzard!"

"Oowu—"

Gastrodon's seemingly soft body bulged, and a fierce blizzard immediately surged forth.

The leaf tornado stirred up by Serperior was affected by the howling wind and snow before even touching Gastrodon—what froze froze, what scattered scattered.

Hm?

The commentator shouted in surprise: "Miss Cynthia's Gastrodon displayed quite powerful Ice-type move power!"

This power... even ordinary Ice-type Pokémon might not match Cynthia's Gastrodon.

Was Gastrodon carrying "Never-Melt Ice"?

And not ordinary "Never-Melt Ice" either...

Johan looked toward Cynthia. Cynthia's gaze also met his, with some cunning in her eyes.

On the icy Mount Coronet, when Cynthia was training there, it wasn't purely just for Garchomp's training!

"Serperior, Grassy Terrain!"

Johan changed strategy.

Setting aside the "Forest's Blessing" that Serperior possessed, having just evolved recently, its strength was only at the level of ordinary Champion main Pokémon.

But Cynthia's Gastrodon was one of the Pokémon Cynthia had captured earliest, and in terms of raw strength, it was somewhat stronger than Serperior.

"Sni!"

Serperior raised its head, and the vine-like patterns on its body seemed to come alive, constantly winding. With its position as the center, a large patch of grass quickly grew, sprouting and growing.

Even—

Small saplings were actually growing on the field, growing rapidly at a visible rate.

"Is this fucking Grassy Terrain?"

"Tell me this is Forest Terrain and I'd believe it!"

"Serperior will be rated first place for this year's 'Arbor Day'!"

Seeing the ordinary field about to transform into a forest field, the audience exclaimed in amazement.

Cynthia's eyes also showed surprise.

During the recent training, because they were in the forest, although Serperior had shown extremely strong power, perhaps because they were in the forest, Serperior's performance in this aspect wasn't obvious.

But now...

Even Johan had to sigh that Serperior had indeed absorbed considerable benefits from the "Divine Tree"...

No forest?

Then create a forest yourself!

Of course, this also consumed considerable stamina for Serperior.

"Gastrodon, Amnesia!"

While Serperior was using Grassy Terrain, Cynthia also had Gastrodon use Amnesia again, greatly boosting its special defense.

Gastrodon's eyes flashed with bewilderment, as if forgetting something, trading this for increased special defense.

"Serperior!" Johan took a slight breath, "Again, Leaf Storm!"

"Sni!!"

Serperior stirred up leaves again—leaves that appeared out of thin air, as well as leaves that fell from surrounding plants.

Bigger, stronger, more concentrated Leaf Storm formed again!

"Protect!"

A circle of emerald green energy barrier immediately appeared around Gastrodon.

Crack—

The barrier shattered, and the swirling leaves scattered accordingly.

Protect could block once, but not twice.

"The final Leaf Storm!"

Having used Leaf Storm twice already, with the "Contrary" ability's enhancement, Serperior's special attack had gained a 4-level boost. Such explosive power—

"Gastrodon, Mirror Coat!"

However, when the Protect barrier shattered, it wasn't just Johan and Serperior who moved, but also Cynthia and Gastrodon.

Hum—

As Gastrodon activated its power, a transparent barrier appeared around its body.

[Mirror Coat: Reflects received special attack moves back at the opponent with double the power!]

Because Mirror Coat was very slow to execute, to use this move, one had to predict what move the opponent would use.

Other Pokémon might be hard to predict, but Serperior—

Leaf Storm was quite easy to guess.

Moreover.

Cynthia had Gastrodon use Stockpile and Amnesia successively to boost Gastrodon's special defense, so it could withstand this Leaf Storm impact and then reflect it all back to Serperior!

Now Serperior's Leaf Storm was falling, Gastrodon's Mirror Coat was formed—the timing was perfect.

Cynthia was truly worthy of being Cynthia, her timing was excellent... Johan subconsciously thought this.

But—

Johan's eyes suddenly sharpened as he roared: "Serperior, don't hold back, maximum power!"

If Gastrodon couldn't withstand Serperior's attack, then there would be no reflection.

"Sni!!"

Serperior understood Johan's meaning and hissed lowly.

Rustle—rustle—

All the grass and trees on the field, all the plants gently swayed.

The vine-like patterns on Serperior's body completely "came alive," and the grass around it grew rapidly.

"Gastrodon, Recover!!" Seeing this, Cynthia immediately called out.

Hold on!

The audience's vision was completely obscured, only seeing numerous leaves filling the entire field.

They only heard a "boom," a emerald green glow shot skyward, shaking up airwaves that scattered in all directions before finally returning to calm.

Leaves fell in spirals like butterflies.

Among the leaves, Gastrodon was already lying there.

"Gastrodon is unable to battle!" The referee immediately announced the result.

Couldn't hold on?!

Gastrodon with three levels of special defense boost, plus Recover for healing, ultimately couldn't withstand Serperior's Leaf Storm wave?

"Is this the power of the 'forest'..." Cynthia murmured softly.

Really...

Recalling Gastrodon, she exhaled lightly and regained her calm.

Quadruple weakness to Grass, unable to hold on—this was within acceptable range.

Cynthia's slender fingers grasped her final Poké Ball.

Next.

Was her final ace!

She had been pushed to the brink by opponents multiple times, and it was always she and Garchomp who turned the tide against adversity.

She absolutely trusted Garchomp, just as Garchomp absolutely trusted her.

So.

As long as Garchomp was still there, her confidence remained!

Johan stared at Cynthia, clearly feeling the change in Cynthia's aura—that confidence in herself and absolute trust in her Pokémon... the aura of bonds!

"It's time to enter, Garchomp!"

The Poké Ball opened, and Garchomp charged skyward with great momentum, then dove down, heavily crashing onto the field, spreading its menacing scythe-like fins and roaring:

"Chomp!!!"

Its sharp claws pointed straight at Johan, face full of ferocity.

It's you!

Bullying our little Cynthia, eh?

Invincible Garchomp, enter!

Johan broke out in a cold sweat.

Garchomp had finally found its chance...

# 798: The Legendary "Gentleman," Returns to the Arena!

"Miss Cynthia's final Pokémon is—her ace Pokémon, Garchomp!!"

The ace's entrance brought cheers from the entire venue.

"Garchomp!!"

"Vicious Garchomp, tear Teacher Johan to shreds!!"

"Now it's—Miss Cynthia's Champion Time!"

Cynthia's ace Garchomp was well known, and many people had fallen for Cynthia because of Garchomp's existence.

Those who had known Cynthia early and become fans knew very well that Cynthia had more than once faced powerful opponents and forced her way through with Garchomp alone.

It wasn't an exaggeration to say.

Cynthia commanding Garchomp in battle and Cynthia commanding other Pokémon in battle were two different levels!

The bond between Cynthia and Garchomp was absolutely no less than that between Leon and Charizard.

The existence of Mega Evolution as an enhancement method further brought out the potential of Cynthia and Garchomp.

Her coat fluttering, Cynthia placed her hand on her slender waist, gazing intensely at Johan with a curve on her lips.

"Come on, Johan!"

Johan could naturally feel Cynthia's aura change—that oncoming confidence, that heroic bearing—this was the Champion Cynthia that people truly adored!

"Chomp!!"

Garchomp roared at Johan without any courtesy, the resulting air waves making Johan feel as if his cheeks stung.

This guy...

Johan bared his teeth slightly.

Its "hostility" toward him was really great.

But no matter, Ninetales would teach it a lesson for him.

Whether it was Lance's Dragonite or Cynthia's Garchomp, his Ninetales was their absolute "nemesis."

"Ka-kaah!"

Just as Johan was about to grab Ninetales' Poké Ball, Gengar floated out from Johan's shadow on its own, grinning and sticking out its tongue, bouncing on the ground like a balloon with an eager expression.

When Johan held Ninetales' Poké Ball, Gengar looked over.

One person and one Pokémon stared at each other.

"Ka-kaah?"

The purple fatty tilted its head, dark mist floating to form a question mark.

Eh?

Johan blinked, confusion passing through his eyes.

Hm?

One person and one Pokémon stared at each other for three to four seconds.

Then they spoke almost simultaneously.

"Ka-kaah?" (You're not thinking of not sending me out, are you?)

"Gengar, you're not thinking of battling, are you?"

Johan & Gengar: "..."

This scene made the entire audience burst into laughter.

"Internal conflict! Internal conflict! Teacher Johan and his Gengar are having internal conflict!"

"Is this an ace? Is Gengar really Teacher Johan's ace? This coordination..."

"Haha—Gengar is too cute!"

One person and one Pokémon, staring wide-eyed at each other.

Johan indicated that Garchomp was a Ground and Dragon-type Pokémon with quadruple weakness to Ice, and Fairy-type could also nullify Dragon-type moves—Ninetales would "demolish" it!

Gengar indicated that ace versus ace was perfectly fine, right?

Johan further indicated that this wasn't about ace or not, and besides, who said anyone in their family was an ace?

Gengar laid it all out: I don't care! I'm going up! This "good brother" Garchomp can only be "killed" by me! Don't you trust me?

Good... good brother?

The opposing Garchomp, hearing Gengar's words, narrowed its already fierce eyes and bared its sharp, cold teeth.

Heh...

Feeling Garchomp's gaze and aura change, both Johan and Gengar stiffened simultaneously and swallowed in unison.

Gulp—

"Ka-ka-kaah!" Gengar stood up and waved its hands.

Forget it, forget it, I won't compete! You guys handle it!

With that, it prepared to dive into the shadows but was grabbed by the quick-handed Johan by its head spike, who said "fiercely":

"Then it's decided—you, Gengar!"

He forcefully threw Gengar toward the field.

"Ka! Kaah—" Gengar, drawing out a long note, landed in the field.

"Tea-Teacher Johan's substitute Pokémon is—his... his ace, Gengar!!" Even the commentator wasn't confident after the scene just now... Was it really an ace?

"Chomp! Chomp!"

Garchomp called out to the Gengar that appeared in the field, almost word by word.

"Ka-kaah..."

Gengar got up and dusted off its bottom, giving Garchomp a sheepish smile.

Listen to my explanation, actually... actually Johan is the real "villain"! I'll help you teach him a lesson together!

Garchomp looked disdainfully, glancing sideways at both Gengar and Johan.

Cynthia smiled gently.

Who said the relationship and bond between trainer and Pokémon had to be obedient submission and command like limbs?

But after smiling, Cynthia's expression immediately became serious again.

Perhaps...

In the entire Pokémon world, she was the person who understood Johan and Gengar's strength best.

But even she couldn't say she understood completely.

"Garchomp!"

Cynthia raised her arm, golden hair flying, taking out her Key Stone-embedded lipstick and saying boldly: "Mega Evolution!!"

"Chomp!!"

Garchomp raised its head and roared freely. Seven-colored rainbow light connected from the Mega Stone on its body to the Key Stone in Cynthia's hand, turning the power of bonds into substance as evolution light suddenly appeared.

Terrifying air waves rippled outward. Crimson scythes cut through the evolution light, and the more menacing-looking Mega Garchomp appeared in everyone's sight.

"Chomp—!!"

The audience couldn't help but be awed by Garchomp's presence, subconsciously forgetting to breathe.

"Here it comes!" Flint excitedly waved his fist. "Big sister's invincible Mega Garchomp!"

If anyone understood Cynthia's Garchomp's strength most clearly, it definitely wasn't Johan, not Lucian, not Steven, but him—"the explosive-haired fire player" Flint!

Over a hundred consecutive defeats, and more than seventy percent of those battles were lost to Cynthia's Garchomp!

Being hung on trees time after time was his "proof" of understanding Garchomp!

At the same time.

Having completed Mega Evolution, Cynthia showed no hesitation and immediately shouted: "Use Dragon Rush!"

Mega Garchomp spread its two menacing scythes and roared again, shooting up like a cannonball. When it reached the highest point, it immediately dove straight down like a jet fighter, trailing long air streams.

Blue-purple Dragon-type energy formed a giant dragon coat, completely enveloping Mega Garchomp as it dove down with fierce momentum!

"Stop playing around."

Seeing Garchomp's Mega Evolution, Johan's expression tightened. After "scolding" Gengar, he said: "It's attacking!"

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar immediately put away its previous playful expression.

No matter what.

Mega Garchomp was an existence that Gengar had to take seriously. That overwhelming presence—one careless moment could lead to a crushing defeat.

Cynthia's Mega Garchomp was worthy of the word "legendary"!

"Shadow Sneak!" Johan called softly.

"Ka-kaah~"

Gengar stuck out its tongue and gently swiped in front of itself. A crack immediately opened in the void, and Gengar dove in with a fish leap.

Boom!!

Mega Garchomp fell like a meteor, causing a huge explosion in the center of the field and raising large clouds of dust.

But obviously, it hadn't hit Gengar.

However, Mega Garchomp wasn't discouraged. Standing on the ground, it opened its crimson eyes and looked around sharply, searching for where Gengar might appear.

"Garchomp!" Cynthia's eyes also swept across the field.

"Chomp!!"

Found it!

The void rippled slightly—there was Gengar, sneaking around carefully with a shifty, thief-like appearance.

Hehe~

Gengar covered its mouth and chuckled wickedly.

Just then, Cynthia's voice suddenly came again: "Use Earthquake!"

No matter where Gengar would come out from, or how it would emerge—

"Chomp!"

Found you!

Mega Garchomp roared and swung both scythes, slamming them into the ground with half their length buried in the field.

The next second.

Rumble rumble—

Earth-colored Ground-type energy surged, with Mega Garchomp as the center. The stadium floor began terrifying vibrations, waves rippling outward in rings!

The terrible tremors swept over, and Gengar, who had just emerged from the void, showed surprise.

Really found?

Gengar pushed off the ground with its stubby legs and floated up.

Only then did Cynthia and Garchomp finally locate Gengar.

"Garchomp!"

"Chomp!"

Mega Garchomp whipped its head around, crimson eyes locking onto the floating Gengar. It stomped the ground hard, and the originally scattered earthquake aftershocks actually converged toward Gengar.

At the same time.

The ground beneath Gengar bulged up as earth-colored Ground-type energy surged out.

Really should have switched Gengar's ability back to "Levitate," Johan couldn't help but complain internally.

If it had the "Levitate" ability, Gengar wouldn't need to worry about these Ground-type moves—

But in the current situation...

Staring intently at Gengar, Cynthia caught sight of the black-suited young man unbuttoning his jacket and taking out that brilliantly shining Key Stone from the corner of her eye.

Key Stone!

Cynthia took a deep breath. Johan's Gengar was one of the few Pokémon that had already reached legendary level in its normal state. If it Mega Evolved—

After a brief moment of distraction, Cynthia's expression sharpened.

Let us see how strong you really are!

"Garchomp, full power Earthquake!!"

Rumble rumble—

The entire Wyndon Stadium shook with "rumbling" vibrations.

"Gengar—"

Johan held the Key Stone.

Although he and Gengar could perform Bond Evolution without relying on the Key Stone, doing so would consume more energy from both him and Gengar—there was no need to do that unless absolutely necessary.

The Key Stone in his hand burst with brilliant light, connecting him and Gengar.

"Mega Evolution!!"

"Ka-kaah!!"

Dazzling white light emerged from Gengar's body. Its form sprouted sharp spikes, the golden eye on its forehead slowly opened, and a smile full of eeriness and evil appeared on its face as large amounts of dark mist immediately poured out.

Some people even faintly heard horse neighing from within the dark mist, quite amazingly.

Mega Gengar!

Hum—

The dark mist instantly spread like a curtain, covering the entire field in an instant. The ground raised by Mega Garchomp's Earthquake was also swallowed by the dark mist before reaching Gengar.

Although it couldn't completely block the earthquake's shockwaves, it bought Gengar time to create distance.

"What move is this?"

"Did you guys hear horse neighing?"

"This couldn't be a Ghost-type field move, could it?"

"Teacher Johan, is this move legal?"

Cynthia, also witnessing this scene, had a serious expression.

Mega Gengar, Johan's strongest combat power, had finally appeared...

That rolling dark mist like a curtain gave her an extremely strong sense of oppression, as if... as long as that dark mist existed, Mega Gengar was an invincible being.

But...

When Garchomp and I are together, we're also invincible!

"Garchomp, Crunch!"

Boom!!

From the earth layers raised by the earthquake, the fierce Mega Garchomp burst out, lunging to bite at Gengar.

When did Garchomp burrow into the earth?

All the audience was stunned.

Too focused on Gengar's Mega Evolution, they had all ignored Mega Garchomp's movements.

Dig plus Crunch?

Swish swish—

Crimson scythes swung, opening a gap in the dark mist created by Gengar.

"Gengar!" Johan suddenly smiled.

Didn't Cynthia want to see us going all out?

Then—

He suddenly raised his hand: "Dark Void!"

"Ka-kaah~"

Mega Gengar faced the lunging Garchomp directly and playfully winked at it.

Tired? Take a rest~

It threw the black hole in its hand toward the approaching Garchomp.

Endless drowsiness accompanied by an increasingly unresponsive body.

"Garchomp, hold on!!" Cynthia's voice came.

"Chomp, chomp!!"

A glimmer of struggle flashed in Mega Garchomp's increasingly drowsy eyes. It forced itself to resist the drowsiness frantically assaulting its mind and still lunged at Gengar.

Bang—

Mega Gengar was sent flying, its bottom sliding on the ground for some distance before stopping.

"Ka-kaah! Ka-kaah!"

The grimacing Gengar quickly got up and patted its bottom.

Hiss—hiss—

Going to smoke! Going to smoke!

The playful scene made the stunned audience laugh.

Bang—

At the same time, Mega Garchomp crashed to the ground, snoring gradually rising.

Truly worthy of our little Cynthia... Johan secretly admired.

He knew all too well how strong Gengar's hypnotic ability was, or he wouldn't normally restrict Gengar's use of hypnosis.

It wasn't an exaggeration to say that Mega Gengar's Dark Void would make even the legendary "first-class gods" take a nap if they were hit, let alone ordinary Pokémon.

He hadn't expected Cynthia and Garchomp to actually endure until the attack finished through sheer willpower before falling asleep.

"Gengar, Dream Eater!" Johan said softly.

"I thought Teacher Johan was already inhuman enough before, but I didn't expect Gengar's entrance to be the killing blow."

"Is Cynthia really Teacher Johan's girlfriend? I feel like I might have a chance again."

"I can already see Teacher Johan's 'funeral'..."

The audience was already feeling uncomfortable with the tactics Johan and Gengar were displaying.

However—

Cynthia obviously hadn't given up.

As Garchomp's dreams were devoured by Gengar, becoming Gengar's stamina, Cynthia said sternly: "Garchomp, Sleep Talk!"

"Chomp—!!"

The sleeping Garchomp suddenly rose.

Though its eyes were closed, its presence was undiminished as it roared skyward. At the same time, a clanging metallic sound emerged from around its body, and Mega Garchomp's aura rose again.

Swords Dance!

"Sleep Talk rolled Swords Dance? Miss Cynthia's luck has arrived!"

"What does this represent? This represents heaven wanting Miss Cynthia to beat up the 'heartless man'!"

The audience cheered.

In Johan's mind, scenes suddenly flashed.

It seemed like long ago, when Cynthia was discussing tactics with him, they talked about... how to deal with sleep teams.

At that time, Johan hadn't considered that he would ever battle Cynthia.

So he had jokingly said to have every Pokémon master Sleep Talk.

And consciously train their move selection while using Sleep Talk.

He hadn't expected this would be used against him...

Wait!

Could it be that I'm the biggest traitor in our team?

Gengar: Aha! Case solved!

As the metallic clanging gradually faded, Johan immediately gathered his scattered thoughts and quickly said: "Gengar, Thief!"

"Ka-kaah!"

Gengar grinned wickedly and reached toward Mega Garchomp.

Give it here!

Two "bats" representing physical attack boosts flew from Mega Garchomp and landed in Gengar's hands.

For a moment.

Garchomp's rising aura fell back, while Gengar gained two levels of physical attack boost!

Seeing this scene, the audience was stunned briefly before immediately "cursing."

"Holy crap! Teacher Johan, you really %￥#@&%..."

"Inhuman! Completely inhuman!"

"Referee! I want to report! This Gengar isn't legal! I want to report!"

Hypnosis plus Dream Eater was already unbearable enough, and now it could steal stat boosts too?

Report! Must report!

"Cough." Johan avoided looking into Cynthia's eyes and spoke again: "Gengar, Icy Wind!"

Whoosh—

Eerie, cold frost swept over. Mega Garchomp's body was immediately covered with a layer of frost, its speed drastically reduced.

However, the cold air finally woke Garchomp from its slumber.

But just as it woke up, it saw—

Several ghostly blue Will-O-Wisps appeared before it.

Bang! Bang! Bang!

Mega Garchomp immediately entered the burn status.

"I seem to see Teacher Johan's old battle style..."

Some old fans suddenly realized—this was Teacher Johan's real battle style!

What hot-blooded, type disadvantage, overwhelming—was that Teacher Johan?

This! This was the real Teacher Johan!

The legendary "Gentleman" returns to the arena!

"Garchomp, Dragon Rush!!"

"Gengar, final Shadow Ball!!"

Cynthia and Johan spoke simultaneously again.

When all the curtain-like dark mist gathered in Gengar's hands, forming a black sphere.

When Garchomp launched its final charge despite all its debuffs.

When the two Pokémon collided again.

Boom—!!!

Terrifying roaring, raging aftershocks.

"Ka-kaah~" Gengar grinned wickedly and stuck out its tongue.

"Chomp..." Mega Garchomp, breathing heavily, had its Mega Evolution light fade as it glared fiercely at Gengar, expressing only one meaning.

We're not friends anymore!

Gengar: Σ(дlll)

Bang!!

Garchomp crashed heavily to the ground and fell unconscious.

After a brief silence.

The referee immediately raised the flag and announced the result.

"Garchomp is unable to battle!"

Then both flags crossed and waved, representing the end of this battle.

"All six of Cynthia's Pokémon have lost the ability to battle!"

"Therefore, the winner is—Johan!"

The venue immediately erupted in cheers.

There were cheers, and also... "curses."

Johan rubbed his nose lightly.

This victory somehow didn't feel quite satisfying...

# 799: Lance VS Giovanni!

"The first contestant to advance to the finals of the Pokémon World Master Tournament has been born!"

"That is our Teacher Johan!!"

The commentator's somewhat hoarse voice spread throughout the venue.

Although there had been some "cursing" earlier, when the final result was announced, the audience immediately burst into tsunami-like cheers.

"Teacher Johan is awesome!!"

"Teacher Johan even 'cut down' Miss Cynthia—if he doesn't take the championship, it won't make sense, right?"

"Johan-bao, charge!!"

In the contestant viewing area.

After a brief silence, everyone still offered cheers and applause.

Flint clapped while smacking his lips, "Big sister lost, but Teacher Johan is still amazing..."

Lucian looked somewhat relieved, "No matter what... losing to Teacher Johan is much better than losing to others."

Aaron's ahoge suddenly stood up as he sighed deeply, "What a pity..."

Flint and Lucian were already used to Aaron's reactions being several beats slow.

"However, big sister still showed the spirit of our Sinnoh League," Lucian said, ignoring Aaron.

Although she was beaten 6-1, obviously, Cynthia's 6-1 against Johan was much more difficult than Nemona's 6-1 against Johan.

Not only did ace Gengar and Ninetales both appear, but the Mega was also used on ace Gengar.

Teacher Johan barely held back at all.

Flint shrank his neck and suddenly felt somewhat afraid, "It's just that... Teacher Johan seemed a bit 'ruthless,' things might get bad..."

Gengar's final tactics made even Flint feel suffocated watching.

Lucian glanced at him sideways, "Don't 'might get bad'—do you think Teacher Johan and big sister could actually have a falling out? Instead of worrying about this, you should worry about yourself."

"Worry about myself?" Flint was stunned.

"Big sister and Teacher Johan will definitely be fine, but if big sister feels uncomfortable, among all of us, who do you think is most likely to..."

Flint: Σ(дlll)

The match came to an end. Amid cheers, under falling confetti and ribbons, Johan and Cynthia walked to the center of the field.

Cynthia's golden hair cascaded down as she placed her hand on her slender waist, watching Johan approach with a somewhat unnatural expression and slightly constrained movements, and smiled slightly.

She lowered her eyes, and a flash of melancholy passed, but when she looked up again, she had returned to her heroic appearance.

"Cough—"

The two shook hands. Johan coughed lightly, not knowing what to say for a moment. So Cynthia playfully blinked and said somewhat mischievously:

"How come someone who won looks like they're not very happy?"

"Haha, haha—" Johan laughed awkwardly, but seeing Cynthia's condition still made him breathe a sigh of relief.

He had really been afraid he used too much force.

"Well, there's still one more match, right? It's not easy..." Johan said.

"Mm..." Cynthia said thoughtfully, "Take the championship?"

Johan was speechless, "It's not that easy to take."

Cynthia's gaze flowed as she smiled lightly, "Including my portion?"

Johan was startled, then also smiled and nodded:

"Yes!"

Including Cynthia's portion!

"Let's go."

After the handshake ended, Johan simply didn't let go of Cynthia's hand and pulled her toward the contestant tunnel.

Cynthia's lips held a smile and she didn't resist.

Seeing this scene, the originally lively venue became silent, but the next second erupted in even more enthusiastic cheers.

Johan VS Cynthia—this much-anticipated match came to an end.

This battle became a topic of conversation for many people.

But the focus wasn't on Johan and Cynthia's battle itself—much of it was...

Full admiration for Teacher Johan.

After "brutally beating" his girlfriend, he could immediately comfort her and leave the field hand-in-hand—such an existence was truly... a model for our generation!

Of course, jokes aside, the vast majority of fans of both sent their blessings to the couple.

While joking around, many people finally realized the terrifying nature of Teacher Johan's strength.

Gengar and Ninetales—two powerful beings that had reached "legendary" level in their normal states!

Plus Mega Dragonite and Tinkaton when facing special opponents...

That was at least three Pokémon already at "legendary" level, right?

Wouldn't that mean—

Teacher Johan could actually be considered a legend that had already surpassed Champions?!

This way.

Miss Cynthia losing to Teacher Johan seemed much more acceptable.

Just—

With Teacher Johan's strength, was there still any suspense in the final?

With the thought of... finding Teacher Johan a "worthy opponent," the audience began looking forward to the upcoming semifinal from the other group.

This included Johan and Cynthia, who had just finished their match.

"Comfortable?"

On the sofa, Johan smiled while massaging Cynthia's slender, pale calves.

"Mm..." Cynthia, holding her laptop, nodded.

It had to be said—

If Johan stopped being a trainer and teacher and became a "masseur," he should be able to earn quite a bit of money.

Using aura power for massage was somewhat extravagant though.

On the other side.

Gengar had a happy expression, massaging Garchomp's shoulders from behind.

"Ka-kaah?"

Comfortable?

"Chomp!"

Garchomp crossed its arms and snorted white air from its nose.

This scene... made Johan unable to tell who had actually won the match and gained "family status."

"What do you think—Lance or Mr. Giovanni, who's more likely to advance?" Cynthia asked while browsing through contestant information and online predictions.

"Mr. Giovanni," Johan answered without thinking.

"Hm?"

Cynthia looked over the laptop screen at Johan with some surprise.

Cynthia didn't know much about Giovanni, but she had battled Lance quite a few times.

Moreover, in online predictions, those supporting Lance, or rather those who thought Lance could advance, were clearly more numerous.

Although Giovanni had been a "dark horse" in this "Pokémon World Master Tournament," surprising everyone by reaching the semifinals.

Lance had been famous early on, and everyone had witnessed his strength.

Taking a step back, Lance was at least an Elite Four of the Kanto League and Champion of the Johto League!

While Giovanni was "merely" a Gym Leader from the Kanto region.

"Why?" Cynthia couldn't help asking.

Johan shrugged.

Could he say that even Lance at peak condition wouldn't be Giovanni's match, as had been shown in the special manga?

After thinking, Johan said: "Many people know Lance's strength, but how many have seen Giovanni's true power?"

Cynthia recalled.

Back then, Johan and Giovanni had worked together during the Hoopa incident.

Giovanni had single-handedly helped Johan block the legendary ancient Pokémon Groudon.

Perhaps in the entire Pokémon world, only Johan had sufficient understanding of Giovanni's strength.

So since he said so...

"But that's just my opinion—it doesn't mean Lance has no chance," Johan quickly added.

If Giovanni didn't care about the championship, or if Lance had some explosive performance, Lance's chances of victory existed too.

Cynthia rolled her eyes at Johan.

That final addition was like saying nothing.

But overall, Johan seemed more optimistic about Giovanni?

Wyndon City, Rainbow Rocket Corporation building.

Matori stood seriously at the office door, stopping all those who wanted to report to Giovanni with an extremely serious expression.

During this time, she had to let Giovanni prepare well for battle—she would handle all matters big and small! Matori silently encouraged herself.

Inside the office...

Giovanni rarely wasn't wearing a suit, dressed in a white shirt with two buttons undone at the collar, looking relaxed.

Sitting before the large floor-to-ceiling window, overlooking the prosperous city before him.

Beside him, a Beedrill rarely seen by others vibrated its wings, accompanied by slight air tremors, hovering at his side.

Giovanni smiled slightly.

"Soon," he said softly.

The steel "forest" of towering buildings ultimately wasn't quite suitable for Beedrill.

Recently, he didn't know why.

His mind kept recalling the days when he was still a "Bug Catcher" adventuring in Viridian Forest with Beedrill.

That forest was the place where he and Beedrill felt most comfortable.

"Soon..."

Giovanni repeated once more.

After battling Lance and "testing" Johan's strength, he could probably truly retire.

By then.

He could open a farm outside Viridian Forest, grow things with his Pokémon, and most importantly, work hard to gain Silver's recognition as his father.

The prerequisite was that he had to battle Johan!

Although his prestige within Team Rocket was unmatched and no one would resist his orders, but...

There were still many "troublemakers" within Team Rocket who might not fully acknowledge Johan. Once he retired, those people couldn't possibly always follow his orders without developing ulterior motives.

Consider it paving the way for Johan, letting more people within Team Rocket recognize Johan's strength.

Whether he lost or won.

Consider it a "handover ceremony" between old and new.

So...

Lance, you'll just have to be "wronged" a bit.

"Siipaa..."

Beedrill pushed a plate of small cookies to Giovanni's side with its stinger.

"Thank you."

Crunch, crunch...

Johto region, Blackthorn City, deep in the Dragon's Den at the Dragon Sanctuary.

Because there was sufficient rest and preparation time, plus Johan's Mega Dragonite appearance in the battle against Nemona, both Lance and the Dragon Clan took it extremely seriously.

Bang!!

In the Dragon Sanctuary's field, two Dragonite collided, and under the impact force, they quickly separated again.

"Let's stop here."

An elder with hands behind his back, smiling, spoke up.

"Draaa~"

"Draaa~!"

Both Dragonite stopped their movements. One landed beside Lance, while the other exhaled and flew into the flower bushes, picking up a small flower to admire carefully.

Lance still wore his blue Champion outfit with wine-red cape, patting Dragonite's arm in praise.

"How was it?" the elder asked Lance.

Lance shook his head, "Vaguely feels like there's something there, but it's too unclear."

After saying this, he sighed again, "Teacher Johan is really amazing..."

Their Dragon Clan wouldn't say Dragonite were everywhere, but each generation had at least one, yet no one had ever discovered Dragonite's Mega Evolution.

On this point, the elder was quite open-minded.

Long-established families weren't just the Dragon Clan, but before Mega Stones and Mega Evolution were confirmed, how many families possessed information about Mega Evolution?

Saying it was because there were no Mega Stones—did their Dragon Clan have Dragonite's Mega Stone?

So it wasn't that their Dragon Clan was inadequate—Teacher Johan was truly exceptional.

But Lance's mention of "having that feeling" also made the elder understand that Lance, with a specific goal and direction, also had a chance to discover Mega Evolution with his Dragonite.

Just...

This Master Tournament wouldn't be in time.

"Are you confident?" the elder asked with a smile.

"No," Lance was very straightforward.

As an Elite Four of the Kanto League and Champion of the Johto League, Lance was one of the few who knew Giovanni's identity.

But Team Rocket's changes, from a former investigator's perspective... were positive, developing in a good direction.

Hearing Lance's answer, the elder was speechless, "You little brat are quite frank."

Lance patted Dragonite and showed a smile and confidence, "But no matter who my opponent is, I'll give it my all! Not having confidence doesn't mean I don't have faith in myself and my partners!"

He swept his cape and said spiritedly:

"Now and in the future, I will defeat all opponents!"

The elder nodded slightly, "Maintain this state, Lance! When you look back at yourself now in the future, you'll be proud of this moment, just as I feel now."

It doesn't matter who the opponent is.

Run forward with everything you have, don't fear falling, don't fear setbacks—the thorns and obstacles crossed along the way are the most brilliant "medals" on the road to success!

Wyndon Stadium, brilliant fireworks decorated the night sky. Hundreds of thousands of spectators cheered freely amid incredible excitement as the host's voice rang out passionately.

"Ladies and gentlemen!"

"Welcome once again to the Pokémon World Master Tournament, second semifinal battlefield!"

"The two sides about to battle are—"

"From the Johto League, Champion, the 'Dragon Master' Lance!!"

"And from the Kanto League, Gym Leader, known as 'Giovanni of the Earth,' Gym Leader Giovanni!!"

Although the host's voice was loud, at this moment it was still inevitably drowned in the tide-like cheers.

Under everyone's anticipation.

Spotlight beams focused on two places.

Rustle rustle—

Dressed in neat blue outfit, wine-red inch hair and black-red cape, the resolute-faced Lance walked out of the contestant tunnel.

"Lance!!"

"Dragon Master Lance!!"

"Dragon Tamer!!"

From the other contestant tunnel.

Giovanni still wore his black suit, hands in pockets, his leather shoes making crisp sounds against the smooth floor.

"Gym Leader Giovanni!!"

"Giovanni of the Earth!"

"Boss boss go! Boss boss mighty! Boss boss..."

Giovanni's "support group" was even more exaggerated than Lance's.

Seeing Giovanni approaching, Lance seemed to feel invisible pressure, and behind Giovanni, he seemed to glimpse a massive "Earth Legion"!

Looking at the spirited Lance, Giovanni also seemed to see his younger self—that momentum of charging forward regardless of everything was a symbol of youth.

Two holders of "Viridian Power" stood on the same battlefield for the first time.

"Interesting, too interesting..."

Johan watched Giovanni and Lance.

If it had been when he first came to this world, he never would have imagined that the legendary Team Rocket leader Giovanni would compete on the same field as Dragon Master Lance.

These two would actually have a match?

Street fights were their true calling!

This "Pokémon World Master Tournament" was truly interesting.

"Crunch, crunch—"

Tyranitar sat on the ground beside Johan, glanced at the field, and munched on some diamonds.

How could matches be as interesting as eating?

"Watch carefully, Tyranitar." Johan lightly patted the armor of the dark green "Godzilla" beside him, "The essence of 'Earth Mastery' might be in this match."

Earth Mastery?

Tyranitar's eating motion paused slightly.

"Tar!"

It nodded heavily.

I know! I'll watch carefully!

With that, its eating speed became even faster, and the rate at which diamonds disappeared made Johan's eye twitch wildly.

It felt like they'd need to go to Princess Diancie for "restocking" again soon.

Fortunately... they didn't need to buy from jewelry stores, or selling everything probably wouldn't be enough for Tyranitar's consumption.

Just then.

The referee's voice came from the field.

"Please, both contestants, get ready!"

---

---

---

# 800: Mega Evolution VS Terastallization!

At the referee's signal, both threw out their Poké Balls.

"Go, Gyarados!"

Lance's opening move was still his signature red "Intimidate" Gyarados!

The fierce-looking Gyarados clad in red scales, just by raising half its body, already exceeded three meters in height and let out a deafening roar!

"Roar—!!!"

Ordinary Gyarados could reach 6.5 meters in length, but Lance's Gyarados, having evolved due to special electromagnetic interference, was not only shiny but also superior to ordinary Gyarados in physical abilities and talent.

The "Intimidate" ability could cause considerable trouble for physical attack-type Pokémon right from the start.

Not to mention when facing Giovanni, known as "Giovanni of the Earth"—Water-type's advantage over Ground-type went without saying, and Flying-type's immunity to Ground-type moves was even more severely advantageous.

Speaking of which...

Lance really had quite an advantage purely in terms of type matchups against Giovanni.

After all, "Dodo Bird" wasn't called that for nothing.

At the same time.

Giovanni also threw out his Poké Ball, and what entered was—

"Tar!!"

With fierce aura no less than Gyarados, even more exaggerated than Gyarados, the dark green armored Tyranitar landed on the field with a "bang."

Facing Gyarados's "Intimidate," Tyranitar showed no sign of fear and instead glared fiercely back at Gyarados with gleaming teeth.

With Tyranitar's entrance, large sandstorms immediately rose on the field, swirling upward.

"Tar?"

Tyranitar sitting beside Johan looked at the green big guy that entered and was stunned, even stopping its eating motion.

Johan smiled slightly.

Although he didn't know if Giovanni's Tyranitar had any blood relation to his own Tyranitar, presumably...

Through watching the battle, his Tyranitar should be able to learn something, right?

Look, the little guy had half-closed its eyes and seemed to be taking it seriously.

Just as Johan's thought passed, Tyranitar gently tugged on Johan's clothes with its huge, thick palm.

"Tar!"

Hey! It's not as tall as me!

Johan: "..."

Suddenly felt a bit tired.

On the field.

"Battle begin!"

As the referee's flag fell, another "Pokémon World Master Tournament" semifinal officially began!

"Gyarados, Dragon Dance!"

"Roar—!!"

The red Gyarados roared skyward, its momentum rapidly climbing, its already fierce appearance becoming even more menacing.

Lance struck first, but very aggressively chose Dragon Dance as his opening move, which couldn't help but surprise people.

Really bold.

But from this, one could also see that Lance's attacking desire today was... full!

Giovanni's choice, however, was—

"Come back, Tyranitar."

A red beam shot out. After the roaring Tyranitar gave Gyarados one more fierce glare, it disappeared from the field along the red light.

"Mr. Giovanni immediately chose to switch Pokémon!" Even the commentator's voice carried surprise.

Just purely... to raise a sandstorm?

That was one aspect.

On the other hand, although Tyranitar seemed unaffected by Gyarados's "Intimidate," that was only how it appeared.

However.

Both sides' opening moves made Lance's choice seem extremely wise—not only did he gain Dragon Dance enhancement for free, but he also seized the initiative during his opponent's switch.

Clair in the audience curved her lips.

Everyone thought Lance wasn't good at tactics, but only she knew that Lance made thorough preparations before each battle, including watching his opponent's battle videos.

Sandstorm teams were the tactical system Giovanni had used most consistently since participating in the tournament.

"...The substitute is—Rhyperior!!"

Ground and Rock-type Pokémon, Rhyperior?

When everyone saw that massive figure covered in orange brick-like stones with a pointed horn on its head appear on the field, they couldn't help but be puzzled.

Huh?

Although Tyranitar was double weak to Water and got "Intimidated," wouldn't that still be safer than the quadruple Water-weak Rhyperior facing Gyarados?

Are you sure this won't get one-shotted by Gyarados that just finished Dragon Dance?

Even Johan was somewhat surprised.

But after some brief thought, Johan seemed to understand something.

"Gyarados, Aqua Tail!" Lance waved his hand and launched a fierce attack without hesitation.

"Roar—!!"

The red Gyarados roared again, using the speed from Dragon Dance to rapidly approach the just-entered Rhyperior.

At the same time, the "rushing" sound of water rose as its tail, wrapped in surging water flow, slammed heavily toward Rhyperior!

Giovanni's expression remained unchanged, his eyes slightly narrowed as he said in a deep voice: "Rhyperior, take it!"

Take it?

All the audience were stunned.

Although there was a sandstorm ongoing, sandstorms could only cause minor effects on Gyarados, and theoretically, sandstorms enhanced Rock-type Pokémon's special defense, not physical defense...

Moreover, Gyarados had received Dragon Dance's physical attack enhancement.

The next second.

The audience got their answer.

Bang!!

Rhyperior roared and extended its extremely thick arms, directly facing the red Gyarados's tail wrapped in surging water flow.

Dull sounds accompanied by the "rushing" of splashing water.

"Ca-caught it?"

"Really caught it?"

When they saw the two grooves Rhyperior had slid on the ground, when they saw Rhyperior standing tall with raised arms, most people's faces showed amazement and disbelief.

Indeed.

Johan, sitting in the audience, lightly stroked his chin and nodded slightly.

This scene seemed to be captured by the director, and immediately a filming Rotom flew to his face. He appeared in a corner of the big screen as a commentator.

"Can Teacher Johan explain this for us?" the commentator's voice came.

Hm?

Commentary?

Johan was stunned.

My commentary... charges fees!

But after a brief pause, he still spoke quickly: "Because Mr. Giovanni's Rhyperior has the 'Solid Rock' ability."

[Solid Rock: When receiving super effective attacks, the attack power is reduced to three-quarters!]

That is to say.

Although Ground and Rock-type Rhyperior was quadruple weak to Water, with the "Solid Rock" ability, it was actually triple weak to Water.

Furthermore, Rhyperior was inherently a Pokémon that excelled at physical defense. Forcing Lance and Gyarados to use physical attacks under sandstorm was also attacking where Rhyperior's defense was strongest.

So...

Rhyperior wouldn't be one-shotted.

Many in the audience understood.

Johan quickly added: "If I'm not mistaken, Mr. Giovanni's Rhyperior should be carrying 'Weakness Policy.'"

As soon as he finished speaking.

Hum—

Rhyperior on the field immediately surged with fierce air waves, sand swirling at its feet as its momentum began rapidly climbing.

"It really is 'Weakness Policy'!" the commentator exclaimed in surprise.

More than just "Weakness Policy"...

Giovanni's voice came at just the right time: "Rhyperior, Rock Polish!"

Bang—bang—

The collision of rock against rock and the grating friction sounds.

With Rhyperior's base speed, using Rock Polish?

Acceleration-type Rhyperior—haven't seen that before, right?

Soon.

Rhyperior seemed extremely familiar with this move. When the "Weakness Policy" effect ended, Rock Polish was already complete!

Thus...

"Stone Edge!" Giovanni raised his hand.

Thud! Thud! Thud—

Sharp rock pillars rose from the ground.

Gyarados, having spent considerable effort breaking free from Rhyperior's tail grab, directly took the full brunt of Stone Edge!

Bang!!

The massive impact force instantly sent Lance's red Gyarados flying, the intense pain nearly knocking it unconscious.

"Gyarados, hold on!!" Lance clenched his fist and shouted.

Watching this scene, the audience gasped.

The quadruple Water-weak Rhyperior wasn't one-shotted by Gyarados's Aqua Tail, but instead Gyarados was nearly one-shotted by Rhyperior's Stone Edge?

Although Gyarados was indeed double weak to Rock-type.

"Roar, roar..."

The red Gyarados ultimately persevered under Lance's shouts, breathing heavily and looking warily at the opposing Rhyperior.

Giovanni's face showed some surprise for the first time.

It held on?

Obviously "hold on" wasn't shouted for nothing.

"Come back first, Gyarados." Lance exhaled lightly, taking the Poké Ball and recalling Gyarados.

The "Intimidate" ability Gyarados was very important to his team. If it completely lost battle ability right from the start, that would be big trouble.

Fortunately, it held on.

"Gyarados held on! Champion Lance chooses to switch Pokémon!"

Johan clicked his tongue.

Is this what battles between two "cheaters" look like?

Both using "lock HP hacks" like they cost nothing.

Having "Viridian Power" makes you so great?

"...The substitute is—Kingdra!"

Lance's second Pokémon, Water and Dragon-type Kingdra!

Upon entry, under Lance's command, Kingdra helped Gyarados finish the final blow.

"Rhyperior is unable to battle!"

The referee waved the flag and announced the result.

The first Pokémon to lose battle ability in this match appeared.

Lance took the first victory!

However, Giovanni, having lost one Pokémon, remained calm, hands in pockets. After recalling Rhyperior to his waist, he immediately threw out his third Poké Ball.

"Mr. Giovanni's third Pokémon is—Nidoqueen!!"

"Nido!!"

Nidoqueen, clad in light blue armor-like shell, roared at Lance's Kingdra.

"Ka-kaah—!!"

Gengar in front of Johan's eyes lit up upon seeing Nidoqueen, floating to the front and cupping its hands like a megaphone, shouting loudly to cheer for Nidoqueen.

Having once played together, they now both stood on the "Master Tournament" stage.

"Kingdra, Rain Dance!" After finishing off Rhyperior's last "HP," Lance, seeing Nidoqueen, commanded again.

The sandstorm's presence was very influential.

Moreover, Lance now somewhat mastered half a "storm" tactic—rainy weather matched well with his Pokémon.

"Oowu—" Kingdra's melodious voice spread throughout the venue.

The sandstorm gradually dispersed, clouds gathered in the sky, and water vapor accumulated.

While Kingdra was using Rain Dance, Giovanni also gave commands.

"Nidoqueen, Ice Punch!"

Somehow, Nidoqueen had appeared before Kingdra, its raised fist covered in frost.

Bang!!

A heavy strike.

Ice crystals scattered.

None fell on Kingdra's body, but Kingdra... shot out like a cannonball.

It flew past Lance in an instant and crashed into the stadium barrier.

This punch...

The entire venue fell completely silent.

Was this that "ordinary" Ice Punch?

The director seemed to have learned, as the filming Rotom appeared before Johan in the audience again.

I'll block him in the bathroom if Leon doesn't pay appearance fees... Johan thought viciously while calmly explaining: "Nidoqueen's ability is Sheer Force, and it's carrying 'Life Orb'—of course its explosive power isn't weak."

But ultimately, it wasn't STAB.

Kingdra hadn't completely lost battle ability.

The sandstorm had dissipated, and rain fell like continuous threads.

"Kingdra, Hydro Pump!" Lance took a deep breath and shouted.

Under rain, Lance's Kingdra's speed soared—the "Swift Swim" ability doubled Kingdra's speed in rain!

A surging water column headed toward Nidoqueen.

Then—

Giovanni raised his arm: "Nidoqueen, Surf!"

Nidoqueen showed everyone what it meant to... ride the waves!

"Earthquake!"

"Nido!!"

Taking advantage of Surf to approach Kingdra, enduring Kingdra's Hydro Pump and heavily stomping the ground, stirring up terrifying tremors across the field.

"Kingdra is unable to battle!" The referee waved the flag.

The score became 1:1!

However, the sandstorm was gone, but the rain continued.

Lance's third Pokémon was—Dragonite!

He unexpectedly sent out his ace Dragonite as his third Pokémon?

No!

Soon people realized.

Although both were Dragonite, this one didn't seem to be Lance's ace?

As heir to the Dragon Clan, having two or three Dragonite was quite reasonable, right?

"Dragonite, Hurricane!"

Under rain, an exaggerated storm swept over.

Giovanni looked at the Dragonite in the sky and said to Nidoqueen: "Hold steady."

"Nido!"

Nidoqueen nodded heavily and stomped the ground hard.

Hurricane howled over, but Nidoqueen seemed to draw strength from the earth, standing unmoved under the storm.

When I stand upon the earth...

"Avalanche!"

Just as Dragonite's Hurricane ended, Avalanche appeared out of thin air above it and crashed down heavily.

[Avalanche: When hit by an opponent's move, this move's power doubles!]

Avalanche's base 60 power suddenly became an exaggerated 120, comparable to Blizzard!

Plus Nidoqueen's own ability and item enhancement...

"Draaa—"

Dragonite cried out in pain and was smashed down from the sky.

Lance's pupils contracted: "Dragonite, hold on!"

Just before crashing to the ground, Dragonite finally regained the ability to flap its wings from Avalanche's extreme cold.

"Extreme Speed! Aqua Tail!"

Dragonite, wrapped in white light, rapidly approached Nidoqueen, its water-wrapped tail striking heavily and sending Nidoqueen flying.

"Nidoqueen is unable to battle!"

Giovanni then sent out his fourth Pokémon, Aggron!

With Stone Edge, it took out Lance's Dragonite and once again stirred up sand and dust, raising a sandstorm.

The score returned to a 2:2 tie.

Lance's fourth Pokémon was a Pokémon from ancient times—Aerodactyl!

Also making use of the sandstorm's benefits.

But...

Aerodactyl couldn't match the terrifying defense of Aggron. The score became 2:3.

Aggron violently swept through two!

This was also the first time Giovanni had taken the lead in score.

Because someone had lost three Pokémon, they entered halftime break for field replacement.

Also giving both sides more thinking time.

The next Pokémon choice was crucial for Lance.

Having lost more than half his Pokémon, if he lost one more, he would fall into a very big disadvantage. Given Giovanni's currently displayed strength... once the score reached 2:4, a comeback would become extremely difficult.

So...

Lance made a choice that surprised many people.

Charizard!

Although Lance's Charizard couldn't be considered his signature, Charizard was indeed Lance's first Pokémon, and the bond between them couldn't be measured by what outsiders called signature or not.

Therefore, at the crucial moment, Lance trusted Charizard.

Even though when facing Giovanni, Charizard had very obvious type disadvantages.

But Charizard responded to Lance, using the Ground-type move Scorching Sands to inflict "Burn" status on Aggron.

Then with Brick Break, it reverse-typed and one-shotted Aggron!

The score became 3:3.

No matter how you looked at it, they seemed evenly matched.

Giovanni then sent out Dugtrio, but Lance and his Charizard had obviously entered some kind of state, ultimately using Charizard's Solar Beam to send Dugtrio out of the field.

4:3!

Now it seemed Giovanni had fallen into disadvantage.

But Giovanni remained unhurried and once again sent out the one that had appeared earliest but hadn't appeared since—Tyranitar!

"Tyranitar!"

Giovanni lightly patted his chest. Where the Team Rocket "R" symbol should have been, there was now a special pin embedded with a Key Stone!

"Mega Evolution!"

"Tar!!!"

He actually chose Tyranitar for Mega Evolution?

Others might think—what was strange about pseudo-legendary Tyranitar being used for Mega Evolution?

But...

Lance recalled his barely surviving Charizard and sent out his final trump card.

"Dragonite!!"

"Soar through the sky!"

Terastallization!

"Draaa—!!"

Transparent crystals built up as Dragonite instantly completed Terastallization, its type changing to—

"Tera Water?!"

"Eh? When did Champion Lance change Dragonite's Tera type?"

"It's actually not Tera Normal—now this is interesting..."
